Chapter 1401: The Heavenly Beast Performs A Great Feat

The ancient stone slab guarded at the center of the formation was just like the Nine-Star Red Bead Li Fan had previously seen within the ruins of the Xuanhuang World-Protecting Immortal Formation.

The difference was that the Nine-Star Red Bead had long since disappeared without a trace, while this ancient stone slab was still clearly present.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes, trying to make out the contents engraved within the formation and upon the stone slab.

The stone slab itself remained completely motionless, yet the lines engraved upon it seemed to be constantly changing, appearing blurred and indistinct.

This immediately reminded Li Fan of the copper coins from the Beiin Immortal Realm that he had seen before.

'It seems this is a recording method unique to the Immortal Realm. But the contents engraved upon this stone slab...'

'Are even more complicated!'

Li Fan's heart stirred, but he did not rush to investigate further. He keenly sensed that the formation before him seemed to possess a certain misleading effect. What could be observed through the formation might not necessarily be the truth.

With the help of the Final Dissociation Disc, Li Fan began analyzing the formation before him.

'There is no immortal spiritual qi. It seems that for the sake of concealment, it does not even use the most basic spiritual energy. Then what exactly is supporting this formation and allowing it to continue operating for all these years?'

After some research, Li Fan became quite interested.

With his current attainments in formations, he could absolutely be considered a grandmaster of formations. He was even capable of integrating and deducing Immortal-grade formations according to his own needs. Yet he had never encountered a formation of this type.

'It is not that the formation's energy consumption is so low that some single object can sustain it. The might of this formation...'

Li Fan pondered for a moment, then cautiously transformed spiritual energy into a small bird and attempted to send it into the formation.

There was no obstruction whatsoever.

However, the instant the spiritual bird crossed the boundary of the formation, it vanished into thin air without the slightest warning.

Li Fan's pupils suddenly contracted.

Because he could not sense in the slightest how the bird he controlled had disappeared.

Li Fan snapped his fingers lightly.

In the next instant, thousands upon thousands of birds surged toward the formation in a dense swarm.

Under Li Fan's exquisite control, the total amount of spiritual energy contained within all the birds was negligible. It remained below a certain threshold, ensuring that it would not provoke a stronger defensive response from the formation.

Their fates were all exactly the same.

Li Fan carefully savored the process of the birds disappearing.

'The spiritual energy was not "consumed". Rather, the foundation that maintained the existence of the spiritual energy was extracted...'

After the entire flock disappeared, Li Fan faintly grasped something.

This formation actually gave him a strange sense of familiarity.

Li Fan then used actual physical objects for further experiments.

Stones, magic treasures, vegetation, and birds.

The results were almost identical.

Anything that entered the formation completely vanished without a trace.

Li Fan's expression gradually became solemn.

'Everything I have tested so far belongs to the realm of the "mortal". What if it has something to do with the "immortal"?'

Golden source power essence transformed into a tiny swimming dragon and crashed against the formation. The air seemed to twist as if scorched by flames. Then the golden light gradually faded.

The result was the same.

Afterward, Li Fan took out a mass of True Immortal flesh and blood.

Countless golden threads pierced into the mass of flesh. The True Immortal flesh and blood was like a puppet controlled by strings, swaying from side to side as it slowly walked into the formation.

The golden threads Li Fan used to control it snapped at the waist.

Yet the True Immortal flesh and blood within the formation underwent a change completely different from that of the mortal objects before it.

The stone slab that had been lying silently within the formation suddenly stopped flickering for an instant.

In that brief moment, Li Fan vaguely saw what appeared to be a bolt of lightning illuminating the stone slab before striking the mass of True Immortal flesh and blood.

The strange and mysterious aura of the True Immortal flesh and blood suddenly vanished.

It was no longer counted among the immortals. In an instant, it was transformed into a mortal object.

Then it dissipated within the formation.

The entire change occurred in the blink of an eye. At first glance, it looked as though the True Immortal flesh and blood had met the same fate as all the mortal objects used for testing and could not endure within the formation for even an instant.

But Li Fan keenly sensed what had truly happened.

He repeated the previous operation, continuously using True Immortal flesh and blood to probe the formation, hoping to seize that fleeting opportunity and see the lightning flash clearly.

'That light that appeared upon the stone slab seems to be one of the patterns engraved by the Xuanhuang Heaven, one of the patterns that once existed on the Ascension Platform? It can actually transform an immortal into a mortal?'

Li Fan's heart was deeply shaken.

However, he also understood that what he was using for the experiment was not a genuine 'immortal'. It was merely flesh and blood that had unconsciously revived from a True Immortal corpse.

It could not possibly be compared to a true True Immortal.

Nevertheless, the terrifying regenerative and defensive abilities of True Immortal flesh and blood had already been verified on Tianyang's Immortal Puppet body. It could ignore almost all mortal methods of attack and could even partially resist the influence of True Immortal seal script.

Yet such a transcendent and terrifying existence had absolutely no ability to resist before the lightning pattern on the stone slab.

'If it can transform an immortal into a mortal, then why can't it transform a mortal into an immortal?'

'The Ascension Platform, the Ascension Platform...'

His previous speculation seemed to have received some form of confirmation.

Looking at the stone slab not far away, Li Fan's mood gradually became excited.

After continuing his experiments for some time, he had nearly consumed one tenth of his entire stock of True Immortal flesh and blood, yet he still gained nothing.

Only then did Li Fan reluctantly abandon this crude method.

The lightning-like pattern on the stone slab appeared for far too short a time. Moreover, what was displayed through the formation was already subject to a certain degree of distortion.

Trying to see it clearly through this method was no different from wishful thinking.

'Previously, I destroyed the world several times, completely reducing the Xuanhuang Realm to nothingness. Yet I never noticed the existence of this formation and stone slab.'

'This formation should not be fixed in this location. Instead, it is probably like a living creature. When it encounters a situation it cannot resist, it strategically relocates and retreats...'

'It requires no external energy source to operate and can even relocate itself in an emergency. This formation is truly extraordinary!'

Li Fan sighed in admiration.

'It cannot be forcibly broken. It seems I will have to let the little beast make a move.'

With a thought from Li Fan, blue light flashed beside him.

Supreme Qiushui appeared beside Li Fan, carrying the little Heavenly Beast.

'I greet the Great Lord.'

Qiushui remained as respectful as ever.

When the little Heavenly Beast saw Li Fan, however, it bared its teeth at him.

Qiushui immediately slapped it on the head.

Energy surged.

This was not merely a symbolic gesture. It was a full-powered strike.

The little Heavenly Beast was instantly struck dizzy and disoriented.

In its ordinary form, even if it were a jade pendant from the Immortal Realm that had transformed into human form, such a blow would have left it seriously injured.

But in this life, the little Heavenly Beast had already devoured four Immortal Domain Go pieces in succession. With the laws of the Immortal Domain now upon it, its physical body had grown to an unimaginable degree.

It merely let out a pitiful cry before recovering.

Under Qiushui's pressure, the little Heavenly Beast shrank its head toward Li Fan, as if bowing in greeting.

'Indeed, everything has its bane.'

Li Fan secretly nodded.

Perhaps because they were both manifestations that had taken the shape of living beings, after spending so much time together, the little Heavenly Beast had gradually come to regard daoist boy Qiushui as something akin to its parent.

Under Li Fan's orders, Qiushui constantly fed it Immortal Domain Go pieces and took it wandering all over the Star Sea, hoping that the little Heavenly Beast's keen intuition might lead it to discover something. These actions further cemented Qiushui's status as its "parent."

Thus, the little Heavenly Beast obeyed Qiushui almost without question.

With a slight movement of his mind, Li Fan used Qiushui to command the little Heavenly Beast to crawl toward the stone slab surrounded by the formation.

In front of this formation, the little Heavenly Beast's keen senses seemed to have completely lost their effectiveness.

It was practically blind, apparently unable to detect the existence of that strange formation at all.

Only after slowly crawling to the edge of the formation did it suddenly become alert and stop.

It stared suspiciously at what lay ahead, its eyes filled with wariness.

The little Heavenly Beast was unwilling to continue forward, but Qiushui kept urging it on.

After hesitating for a moment, mysterious silver starlight began to faintly emanate from the little Heavenly Beast's eyes.

After carefully observing and perceiving the situation for a long while, the little Heavenly Beast finally continued forward, albeit extremely reluctantly.

'Just as I expected. The little Heavenly Beast was originally an Immortal Realm object that had taken the shape of a living creature. It possesses a certain resistance to this formation. After devouring four Immortal Domain Go pieces, its physique underwent another qualitative transformation. It should be able to withstand the formation's attacks.'

Li Fan's eyes flickered.

The little Heavenly Beast crawled very slowly.

It stared at the stone slab not far ahead, and a trace of confusion flashed through its eyes. It seemed to feel an inexplicable sense of familiarity toward the stone slab.

As a result, it became even more resistant.

Once again, Qiushui had to coax it with affection and entice it with benefits before the little Heavenly Beast finally continued forward.

Li Fan quietly waited for the little Heavenly Beast to retrieve the stone slab.

At the same time, he continued examining the strange formation before him.

After such a long time, Li Fan had finally begun to understand where that sense of familiarity came from.

It originated precisely from the formation's principle of severing the foundation of an object's existence.

Throughout the fissures and depressions scattered across the Darkest Star Sea, dark rivers of vitality flowed. A layer of energy that extracted the force of life enveloped them, severing the foundation upon which the existence of things depended. This life extracting power was precisely why vitality gathered within those fissures.

The decaying greenary surrounding the Thousand-Eyed Desolate God on the Xuan Immortal Ark likewise devoured the principles governing the existence of whatever it came into contact with.

And even...

The invisible High Wall surrounding the Darkest Star Sea.

It could likewise be viewed as directly erasing the foundation upon which the concept of "before" existed.

'All of these can be regarded as the absence of elements necessary for existence.'

'And they share the same fundamental principle as the formation before me.'

Li Fan's eyes flickered as a storm of thoughts swept through his mind, and countless possibilities emerged in an instant.

'They should all be methods that once belonged to the Immortal Realm.'

'It is even possible that the regions beyond the High Wall now possess such divine abilities as well.'

'According to the clues I have gathered before, when Xuantian King arranged these hidden measures, he should have still been only a Half Immortal. Without immortal spiritual qi, how exactly did he accomplish all of this?'

The little Heavenly Beast remained inside the formation.

As it continued to trigger the formation, the formation's operation also allowed Li Fan to see it more clearly.

Even the symbols on the stone slab at the center of the formation gradually became more and more visible.

Among the increasingly numerous patterns that flashed past, Li Fan suddenly caught sight of a pattern extremely similar to the Inch Apart, yet Worlds Away Grand Formation.

'Because I am observing it through the formation, what I see has undergone distortion, so it does not correspond perfectly. But this further confirms my speculation.'

'This stone slab replicated by Xuantian King indeed contains a complete engraving of the Immortal Ascension Platform from the Immortal Realm.'

'This engraving encompasses almost everything. Every individual aspect corresponds to some kind of power that originally belonged to the Immortal Realm.'

'For example, Inch Apart, yet Worlds Away. And also Immortal Becoming Mortal, Mortal Becoming Immortal.

Li Fan's emotions surged.

The farther forward the little Heavenly Beast crawled, the greater the pressure it seemed to endure. Its movements became increasingly slow, until it was almost completely unable to move.

It was practically only one step away from the stone slab.

But the little Heavenly Beast rolled its eyes and simply gave up!

No matter how much Qiushui urged it at this point, the little Heavenly Beast remained completely motionless.

Through the formation, Li Fan could see the countless patterns flashing across the stone slab, their transformations becoming increasingly frequent and almost constant.

He knew that the little Heavenly Beast was not deliberately playing tricks or trying to demand a higher price.

It had genuinely reached the absolute limit of what it could endure.

Muted explosions seemed to resound from within the little Heavenly Beast's body. Small portions of its flesh were being stripped away by the power of the formation.

A trace of fear flashed through the Heavenly Beast's eyes. It wanted to give up and retreat.

Qiushui was helpless as well. Even offering three Immortal Domain Go pieces as a reward was useless.

At this moment, Li Fan suddenly formed a hand seal.

The little Heavenly Beast, which had been preparing to retreat, suddenly froze.

Then, completely against its own will, it began crawling forward again!

A trace of the innate savagery of a ferocious beast flashed through the little Heavenly Beast's eyes. It continuously let out low growls, expressing its immense fury.

Yet it was completely unable to break free from the restraint of Li Fan's hand seal.

This hand seal was something Li Fan had secretly learned from the technique Mo Rubin used to control the Heavenly Beast's Eye.

Whether it was the ancient Xuantian Sect's guardian divine beast or the little beast that had only recently been born, their true bodies were both jade pendants brought down from the Immortal Realm by Xuantian King and transformed into living beings.

In truth, they could be regarded as the same living being.

Naturally, a control method effective against the Heavenly Beast of the Xuantian Sect would also be effective against the little beast before him.

Li Fan had already secretly tested this beforehand.

Otherwise, he would never have allowed the little beast to devour four Immortal Domain Go pieces so casually.

Under Li Fan's merciless coercion, the little Heavenly Beast ignored its increasingly severe injuries and finally crawled to the stone slab.

Using its comparatively frail body, it carried the stone slab onto its back.

With the slab resting on its back, it let out a low growl and slowly began retreating.

Countless patterns constantly shifted across the surface of the stone slab.

The flesh of the Heavenly Tian Beast was stripped away inch by inch, disappearing into nothingness.

In the end, only an empty shell remained, along with the Heavenly Beast's Eye protected by the silver starlight ahead.

Carrying the stone slab on its back, it continued forward slowly but with unwavering determination.

No one knew how much time passed.

Eventually, it crawled out of the area covered by the strange formation.

The moment it escaped the formation, the little Heavenly Beast completely exhausted its strength and could no longer move.

Qiushui hurriedly went forward to receive it.

Li Fan, meanwhile, stared at the stone slab on the Heavenly Beast's back.

All the patterns on it had gone dark.

The sacred and solemn aura had disappeared at the same time.

It was as though it had become an ordinary worldly object, utterly unremarkable.

Li Fan casually tossed an Immortal Domain Go piece to the little Heavenly Beast, which had rendered a great service but was now on the verge of death.

He himself then lifted the stone slab into the air and began examining it carefully.

Chapter 1402: The Xuanhuang Ascension Immortal Domain

All the constantly shifting patterns on the stone slab gradually settled and sank inward.

It looked just like an ordinary mortal stone slab, completely unremarkable.

But Li Fan had personally witnessed the divine might of this stone slab earlier. How could he possibly be deceived by the illusion it was currently displaying?

'The key is how to activate the power of the stone slab.' Li Fan pondered deeply.

The stone slab in his hand had completely ceased responding to any stimulation from the outside world. Whether it was spiritual energy, source power essence, True Immortal flesh and blood, or even the obliterating wind calamity, none of these forces could provoke the slightest change from it, even when brought close to test its reaction.

Li Fan could even vaguely sense that when faced with all these probing forces, the stone slab truly possessed no defensive power whatsoever.

If these forces advanced another step and actually touched the stone slab, they would probably tear it to pieces in an instant.

'This...' Since this concerned whether he could successfully achieve Dao Integration and ascend to immortality in this life, Li Fan's eyes flickered. In the end, he temporarily abandoned the idea of testing it by directly destroying it.

His gaze swept over the stone slab again and again. Recalling the astonishing display of divine might it had shown earlier at the center of that strange formation, a thought suddenly emerged in Li Fan's mind.

'Could it be that this stone slab really is an ordinary mortal object under normal circumstances, exactly as it appears now? Only when placed inside that unknown formation can its various mysteries be activated?'

Li Fan could not help looking toward the strange formation nearby. After the stone slab had been removed, it seemed to have disappeared into the world.

'It took so much effort to remove it. Could I really have to put it back again?'

Li Fan's expression shifted between uncertainty and determination.

At this moment, he suddenly found himself missing Cat Treasure.

'If Cat Treasure's replication ability were here, I would not have to agonize over this.'

There was no way he could simply return the stone slab to its original position. Given the little Heavenly Beast's current condition, barely clinging to life, it would absolutely be unable to pass through the formation again and retrieve the stone slab.

'From the looks of it, the stone slab and the formation activate each other. Neither can do without the other. Although I cannot replicate the stone slab, I can replicate the formation.'

'Even if I can only reproduce a small portion of it...'

Li Fan temporarily set aside this stone slab, which the Xuantian King had replicated from the Immortal Realm's Immortal Ascension Platform, and instead focused his attention on observing and studying the mysterious formation buried a million miles beneath the Xuanhuang Realm nearby.

Without the stone slab's support, the formation became hidden. But it had not truly disappeared.

At the very least, unlike the High Wall's 'formlessness', Li Fan could still vaguely sense the existence of the formation.

With a thought, countless golden threads shot out and pierced into the area covered by the formation. They transformed into countless tentacles belonging to Li Fan, assisting him in sensing and analyzing it.

Within his sea of consciousness, the Final Dissociation Disc suddenly shone brilliantly.

It was not because its load had exceeded its limits. Rather, it had vaguely sensed an entirely new type of formation structure. Extremely rarely, it actually displayed emotions of longing and excitement.

Li Fan stood silently, synchronizing everything he had comprehended with the Final Dissociation Disc.

At the same time, he adjusted his method of observation according to the feedback from the Final Dissociation Disc.

Again and again.

Even though its core was already capable of deducing Immortal techniques, the Final Dissociation Disc still struggled greatly to analyze this formation.

Time passed second by second.

Only six hours remained before the moment when Li Fan would attempt to achieve Dao Integration in the branch simulation.

In truth, Li Fan could ignore the result of the branch simulation. He did not necessarily have to attempt his breakthrough within these twenty-four hours.

But doing so would also mean facing more unexpected variables.

Within the period covered by the branch simulation, everything would unfold exactly as Li Fan had personally experienced it. The actions of the Immortal Ancestor, the Heavenly Doctor, the Grand Mage, the Hunger Immortal, and the others were all within his control. This allowed Li Fan to focus wholeheartedly on pushing the Xuanhuang Realm toward ascension and transformation without worrying about interference from the outside world.

But if he delayed until beyond the time covered by the branch simulation...

Anything could happen.

There would simply be too many variables, making every step like walking on thin ice.

'If I had not vaguely sensed the existence of some [once lost, forever lost] characteristic during the attempt to ascend to immortality, I would not need to be so cautious. After all, with [Truth], I can still turn the tide. Even if some unexpected change occurs, I should at least be able to preserve my life.'

'If what I sought were merely True Immortality, then so be it. After hundreds or thousands of attempts, sooner or later I would be able to become a True Immortal.'

'But what I seek is eternal life!'

Li Fan's gaze was deep, revealing not the slightest emotional fluctuation.

'True eternal life! True Immortals who died like ants when the great calamity arrived; even the nameless True Immortal whose leg bone was sealed and forged into an Immortal Ascension Heavenly Pillar...'

'None of these are my ultimate goal!'

'To roam freely through eternity without being bound by anything. That is what I have painstakingly sought all this time!'

Having comprehended the deepest desire within his own heart, Li Fan's countless anxieties and unease vanished completely the moment this thought arose.

All distracting thoughts concerning the countless possibilities dispersed like smoke.

At this moment, only an utterly pure and incomparably resolute will remained in his mind, the determination to pursue the Dao, ascend to immortality, and swear to attain eternal life.

The Final Dissociation Disc also sensed its master's determination at this instant.

Its radiance suddenly surged to an unprecedented level.

It no longer cared about the limits of what it could withstand and performed deductions without reservation. Countless formation unit data rapidly transformed, while cracks began appearing across the surface of the Final Dissociation Disc.

Li Fan also remained completely indifferent to the imminent collapse of the Final Dissociation Disc as it staked everything on this final attempt.

His mind was completely focused on comprehending the formation.

Throughout his hundred reincarnations, from He Zhenghao's [Hundred Veins Formation Diagram] to Zhang Zhiliang's [Unlimited formation]; from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's [Tianxuan Spirit Locking Grand Formation] to the ancient Xuantian Sect's [Floating Across Starry Sky Grand Formation]; even the Immortal-grade formation [Primordial Mystic Spirit]...

From ancient times to the present, from mortal formations to Immortal formations, Li Fan had already witnessed countless formations.

And with the supreme treasure Final Dissociation Disc by his side, constantly deducing and innovating day and night, Li Fan's attainments in formation arts had long since become unrivaled throughout the Star Sea.

Although the strange formation before him was a type of formation Li Fan had never encountered before, it was still ultimately within the category of a 'formation'.

Through his joint deductions with the Final Dissociation Disc, Li Fan gradually began to understand the manner in which this formation existed.

His eyes flickered. He also recalled the [Dao Refining Medicine King Scripture] passed down by Sun Piaomiao, and fell into deep thought.

'What is a formation?'

'Back then, Zhang Zhiliang of the Congyun Sea said that a formation was a standardized form of laws. But in my view, a formation is a shortcut for connecting numerous laws.'

'The reason we cultivators can use mortal bodies to move mountains and fill seas is because we can invoke the power of the laws of Heaven and Earth. The most elementary formations can be viewed as a tool for doing precisely this.'

'As for more profound formations, those are Immortal formations. They connect, fuse, and alter the laws of Heaven and Earth, allowing one to transform the rotten into the miraculous at will!'

Countless sparks of inspiration suddenly erupted within Li Fan's mind, as if pushing him into a profound and unfathomable realm.

'The great laws of the world have existed since time immemorial and circulate eternally. The reason ordinary formations require energy to operate is because, in order to invoke the power of laws through a formation, one must first establish a connection to those laws.'

'But this formation before me...'

A strange glint suddenly flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

'It uses the Dao itself as the formation!'

'It eliminates the process of establishing a connection and instead relies upon the natural circulation of the Great Dao itself to drive the formation!'

'At the same time, the Great Dao has no physical form. If the subtle balanced structure of [using the Dao as the formation] is disrupted, then the formation will naturally return to the Great Dao and disappear from the mortal world!'

Li Fan's thoughts spun faster and faster.

What stood before him was no longer an ordinary formation.

It was an unparalleled divine ability that used unfathomable methods to gently pluck the Great Dao laws of Heaven and Earth from existence, then knead and reshape them!

After comprehending this point, Li Fan felt as though he were directly facing a portion of the Great Dao itself. His mind inevitably trembled.

At this moment, the Final Dissociation Disc, which had been probing the [Dao Formation] while operating under an overloaded state for such a long time, finally reached its limit.

Countless cracks appeared one after another, like ravines across the earth, covering its entire surface in an instant.

Ding!

With a crisp sound, the Final Dissociation Disc that had accompanied Li Fan for so long shattered!

It dispersed into countless points of starlight that remained suspended within Li Fan's soul.

Without the assistance of the Final Dissociation Disc, Li Fan's vision instantly became blurred.

He could no longer peer into the true essence of the [Dao Formation].

However, no trace of disappointment appeared on Li Fan's face.

His gaze shifted away from the Dao Formation and toward the stone slab beside him.

'If this formation is a manifestation of the Great Dao, then this stone slab is the key that activates different Great Daos.'

'In truth, there really is nothing special about this stone slab. It is simply that the patterns engraved upon it happen to correspond with the [Daos] fused together within the Dao Formation.'

The aura surging around Li Fan completely subsided and became calm.

'A gentleman is not born different from others. He is merely skilled at making use of external things.'

'Even though I cannot completely comprehend the essence of the Dao Formation for now, that does not prevent me from...'

'Using it!'

'Using the Dao as the formation...'

Li Fan softly repeated these four words.

His figure flickered, and in an instant he left the depths of the earth and arrived beneath the vast sky of the Xuanhuang Realm.

'Use the Dao as the formation.'

Li Fan silently repeated these four words within his heart.

'Transform the mortal into the immortal.'

His expression became unreadable as he looked toward the stone stele standing within the You Clan's small world.

On the stone stele, the character representing the transformation between immortality and mortality had been accelerated by Li Fan through multiple methods. It was now only a hair's breadth away from fully manifesting.

Li Fan took a deep breath.

Using his finger as a brush, he did not infuse it with any energy. He simply imitated and copied the form.

With a slight application of force, he left only a faint mark upon the stone slab.

And even that mark seemed as though it could disappear at any moment.

Li Fan was completely focused at this moment. Even though the characters of the Immortal Mortal Transformation were still incomplete, it did not stop him from imitating them as best he could.

While copying the True Immortal seal script, Li Fan was also observing every movement within heaven and earth.

To his slight disappointment, even after he had completely written out all the known characters of the Immortal Mortal Transformation onto the stone slab, there was still no unusual phenomenon.

'Could it be that it has to be complete?' Li Fan could not help frowning.

Looking at the remaining You Clansmen in the small world, Li Fan considered whether he should throw all of them into the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal at once, thereby causing the characters of the Immortal Mortal Transformation to fully manifest.

'Or perhaps... it has to be done somewhere else.'

After pondering for a while, Li Fan did not act immediately.

Instead, he carried the stone slab to the center of the Star Sea.

'Ascend!'

With a thought from Li Fan, the flesh and blood of the True Immortal began burning fiercely, transforming into an endless source of power for the Ascension Formation.

Holding the stone slab aloft with one hand, the Star Sea within Li Fan's vision rapidly became smaller.

Yet what was extremely strange was that no matter how much Li Fan's momentum rose, the stone slab suspended in his hand remained exactly the same size.

At first, compared to the entire Star Sea, it was merely an insignificant speck that could be ignored.

But as Li Fan's momentum continued to rise, amid the contrast between change and invariance, the stone slab gradually became almost comparable to the entire Star Sea.

'The Great Dao is projected from above to below. Whether mortal or immortal, everyone can glimpse a portion of the Great Dao. It is simply that the scenery each person sees is different. The difference in cultivation realm makes no difference to the Great Dao itself. The Great Dao one glimpses will not undergo any obvious change.'

'The only thing that can influence it to a certain extent is one's own comprehension of the Dao.'

'The same applies to the Great Dao, and to the Key of the Dao as well.'

Having ascended to the limit of the mortal realm, the energy within Li Fan's body was being consumed at an extremely terrifying rate. Only by borrowing the infinite power of the Star Sea from daoist boy Qiushui could he barely maintain this state for a short period.

Holding up the stone slab consumed twice as much power.

Without the assistance of the Star Sea, Li Fan probably could not remain in this elevated state for even a moment.

Steadying his mind, Li Fan continued with what he had left unfinished on the stone slab, though it was extremely difficult.

With his hand, he began engraving the characters of the Immortal Mortal Transformation.

As before, they only left behind faint marks.

But unlike before...

Li Fan's actions actually caused a certain identical symbol within the stone slab to respond.

Or rather, to resonate.

Just as it had when previously activated by the Dao Formation, patterns began appearing on the surface of the stone slab in a regular manner.

However, rather than countless different patterns continuously changing, only a single pattern appeared.

Buzz...

As the stone slab underwent this astonishing transformation, a corresponding phenomenon also emerged within the Star Sea.

At the center of the Star Sea, a muffled sound suddenly rang out. It was like the sound of paper being pierced by the tip of a brush.

A profoundly mysterious principle of the Great Dao descended upon the center of the Star Sea in response to the stone slab's summons.

It was as if, while standing atop a mountain peak, someone had suddenly lifted him up another step.

Li Fan felt the entire scene of the Star Sea within his vision suddenly contract.

Countless lines curled inward and converged into a single point.

The spiritual energy, flesh and blood, and even divine soul throughout his entire body seemed to be scorched by raging flames. They became blazing hot and boiled violently.

Like fine metal being forged in raging fire, like sand being sifted by tremendous waves.

All impurities had to be removed, leaving only the essence.

However, the consumption caused by this refining and extraction was simply too terrifying.

Even though Li Fan cultivated the New Method and could continuously grow new flesh and blood through his Cave Heaven, and even though he had mastered the Limitless Fullness and Emptiness Method, allowing his source power to remain inexhaustible and his divine soul to remain undying, he still could not withstand such an enormous level of consumption for long.

Li Fan felt the changes within his body with some alarm and decisively made a decision.

The phantom of the Wooden Sword left its sheath and, carrying the stone slab with it, plummeted downward at the speed of light.

The phenomenon within the Star Sea stagnated for a moment.

Then, amid complete silence, a tremendous roar erupted like the collapse of the heavens, reverberating endlessly and lingering for a long time.

At the same time, like waves splashing up when a massive stone falls into water, rolling black qi began emerging from the center of the Star Sea not long after Li Fan departed.

Li Fan naturally knew the consequences of attempting the Ascension here, in this special region at the center of the Star Sea.

But he also knew that the black qi of the Dao Annihilation Calamity would not spread quickly.

It would not affect his plans in this life to push the Xuanhuang Realm toward ascension and sublimation.

He had a reason that made it necessary for him to act here.

'The center of the Star Sea, the pinnacle of mortal momentum.'

'The combination of these two conditions creates the highest point that can be reached within the Star Sea apart from a True Immortal.'

'It is one step closer to the Great Dao itself.'

'It was precisely because of this additional step that the function of the stone slab, the Key of the Dao, was finally able to manifest properly.'

After returning to the normal Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan stroked the stone slab in his hand.

All the various marks on its surface had already faded away.

Just as the Dao comes and goes, leaving behind no trace.

But the speculation in Li Fan's heart had already been verified.

'The Dao Formation is a formation created through supreme means, extracting strands of numerous Great Dao laws and then fusing them together.'

'The stone slab is the key that activates the laws within the Dao Formation.'

'If the True Immortal seal script is the concretization of the Great Dao, then the Dao Formation has already dismantled countless True Immortal seal script characters and incorporated them into the operation of the formation.'

'Strictly speaking, it is actually similar to compressing and unfolding True Immortal seal script characters to form fragments of an Immortal Domain.'

The stone slab felt extremely strange to the touch. It was like metal and wax at the same time, cold yet carrying a trace of softness. But when truly compressed, one could still feel its indestructible nature.

Li Fan gently stroked it and closed his eyes, sensing it carefully.

'This Key of the Dao existed within the Ascension Platform in ancient times. Presumably, the process by which ancient cultivators successfully ascended must have involved the corresponding laws engraved within this Key of the Dao.'

'Activating the Key of the Dao is certainly no easy matter. Perhaps the ancient Immortal Realm had a simpler method. But the most primitive and fundamental method is to engrave upon it the True Immortal seal script representing the laws themselves.'

'The True Immortal seal script resonates with the inscriptions within the Key of the Dao. The inscriptions of the Key of the Dao then mobilize the corresponding Great Dao laws represented by those True Immortal seal script characters...'

A perfect cycle had already formed in Li Fan's mind.

'It is like using the correct password to log into an account and obtain the authority to use the Key of the Dao.'

'Then, using the Key of the Dao to mobilize the corresponding law power.'

'In the fragile lower realm Star Sea, the destructive power manifested by True Immortal seal script and the Great Dao itself is actually not much different. But certain special Great Dao laws are different.'

'And the Key of the Dao happens to contain one of them.'

Li Fan's eyes suddenly opened, their sharpness fully revealed.

'Today is the day I will achieve Dao Integration and ascend to immortality.'

...

The final moment of the Three-Pronged Branch Simulation had arrived.

Li Fan proceeded step by step, following the memories from the simulation as he catalyzed and pushed the Xuanhuang Realm toward sublimation and transformation.

However, now that he possessed memories allowing him to know what had not yet happened, Li Fan still made some minor adjustments in advance.

Among the Eighteen Bronze Figures that surrounded the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital, Li Fan had secretly embedded the Inch Apart, yet Worlds Away Formation into the core of each bronze man, in addition to concealing the Obliterating Wind Calamity and the Decaying Greenary within them.

The destination connected by the formations was none other than the center of the Star Sea.

If the Grand Mage dared to reveal himself and successfully suppress the Obliterating Wind Calamity and the Decaying Greenary, the Inch Apart, yet Worlds Away Grand Formation would immediately activate and teleport him to the vicinity of the rolling black qi that heralded the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Judging from the sensitivity that black qi had displayed toward Immortal level power in Li Fan's previous life, there was no way the Grand Mage could possibly survive.

Amid the rumbling of the Xuanhuang Realm, numerous blessed lands and Cave Heavens, resembling organs within the Xuanhuang Realm, began to evolve.

Every region enveloped by a halo contained the new laws of heaven and earth that had emerged during the Xuanhuang Realm's ascension and transformation.

At the very moment these blessed lands and Cave Heavens descended, a black shadow would always silently blend into them.

Hiding within and comprehending the laws.

All of these black shadows were strands of divine sense split off by Li Fan.

Apart from personally comprehending the newly born laws of the Xuanhuang Immortal Domain, their greater purpose was to induce and detonate the Xuanhuang Mutation within the blessed lands and Cave Heavens at a critical moment.

Combined with the measures Li Fan had secretly planted within the Xuanhuang Realm beforehand, even if the Xuanhuang Realm successfully transformed into an Immortal Domain, it would still be turned upside down at a single thought from Li Fan.

Li Fan did not need to be capable of directly contending with the Xuanhuang Immortal Domain.

Instead, he would borrow its own strength to strike against it.

Using the opponent's own methods against them.

Everything proceeded exactly as it had in the branch simulation.

After the evolution of the Cave Heavens was complete, four great immortal pillars rose from the four corners of the Xuanhuang Realm.

At the same time, Li Fan continuously formed hand seals and cast spells.

In the center of the Xuanhuang Realm, the phantom transformed from the Nameless True Immortal Stone Stele appeared simultaneously.

Five peaks stood side by side, supporting the heavens. It was a magnificent sight.

Next, Great Celestial Venerable Wuming appeared.

The three Immortal Artifacts, the Great Sun Golden Bell, the Primordial Five Elements Wheel, and the Taixu Blue Heaven Lamp, were successively fused into the Xuanhuang Realm by him.

Yet even the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm itself failed to notice that strands of strange power had entered the Xuanhuang Realm along with these Immortal Artifacts.

Strange was not merely an adjective.

It was the name of an actual existence called [Strange].

Its inspiration came from the Heaven-Rending Arrow, preserving and refining the disharmonious force produced when worlds merged.

After falling under Li Fan's control, it was like a targeted medicine. It quietly followed the Immortal Artifacts during their fusion and was injected into the Xuanhuang Realm.

With the amplification of the three Immortal Artifacts, the overall balance between the Xuanhuang Realm and the cultivators' power gradually shifted.

Yet this did not affect Li Fan.

In fact, by relying on the Strange power under his control, Li Fan could even mobilize the power of these newly fused Immortal Artifacts to a certain extent.

However, Li Fan did not reveal this.

He continued pretending that he was barely able to endure it, allowing his figure to sink slightly.

Everything was ready.

Next, Li Fan held the jade command tablet and began wielding his brush across the vast lands of the Xuanhuang Realm.

With a single stroke, he wrote the two characters, "Creation".

Having already experienced this once before, Li Fan's movements were even smoother this time.

Before long, the sacred power of Creation began to spread throughout heaven and earth.

The four great Immortal Pillars rose once again, soaring straight into the heavens.

The blessed lands and Cave Heavens overflowed with radiant light, and the laws contained within them became increasingly sacred.

The living beings of the land reaped the benefits without effort.

Without labor or cultivation, the strength of their souls and bodies advanced by leaps and bounds with each passing day.

Everything that had happened in the branch simulation unfolded once again in perfect synchronization.

Li Fan, who had long been prepared for this, circulated the Sitting Heaven Art and transformed the seemingly endless power of feedback within his dantian into a phantom of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Because he had begun from the transformation of the Xuanhuang Realm itself, he received the entire feedback of its ascension power.

When the Xuanhuang Realm could advance no further, the phantom of the Xuanhuang Realm gathered within Li Fan's dantian had already reached the equivalent of thirty-six times the total power of the Xuanhuang Realm at its ascension threshold.

Even Li Fan, who had already experienced this once before, was still somewhat shaken by it.

The feedback from a complete ascension from beginning to end was vastly greater than that obtained by assisting halfway through.

'This is still not the limit.'

'The greatest feedback will arrive after the Xuanhuang Realm officially ascends into an Immortal Domain.'

'I truly wonder what kind of scene will unfold when that time comes.'

Within Li Fan's thoughts, the Xuanhuang Realm's journey of ascension had already reached its end.

Next, if no external factors interfered, its fate would be the same as in the branch simulation.

It would plummet violently downward, before being swallowed by the endless black qi of Dao Annihilation.

Perhaps sensing the fate that was about to befall it, the Xuanhuang Realm became both frightened and anxious, frantically sending pleas for help toward Li Fan.

After all, Li Fan was the one who had personally brought about all these changes, and he was currently the only lifeline upon which the Xuanhuang Realm could rely.

Without showing the slightest hesitation, Li Fan demanded an enormous amount of authority from the Xuanhuang Realm.

It was practically equivalent to a contract of selling oneself.

The authority he demanded was even greater than that possessed by the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

Faced with this transaction of selling it the very rope with which it would be hanged, the Xuanhuang Realm had countless grievances and was deeply unwilling.

But under the pressure of life and death, it had no choice but to agree.

The transaction between Li Fan and the Xuanhuang Realm was completed in an instant.

Just as the Xuanhuang Realm finished ascending and was about to begin its descent, Li Fan made his move.

He supported the collapsing heavens and earth, turning the tide with his strength.

The momentum of the Xuanhuang Realm had already reached the pinnacle of the mortal world.

Using the newly acquired authority, Li Fan's body, divine sense, and will had all merged with the Xuanhuang Realm.

There was no need for him to ascend once again.

Li Fan's phantom stood tall, his form transforming into a colossal manifestation of Heaven and Earth.

Once again, he donned that mysterious black and gold robe.

A crown rested upon his head, its surface shimmering with starlight.

However, this time Li Fan no longer concealed his expression.

Instead, he deeply imprinted his face into the minds of every living being within the Xuanhuang Realm, regardless of whether they were conscious or not.

Even the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm was deeply imprinted with the majestic visage of Li Fan, the savior.

Although Li Fan had already transformed into a giant who towered over heaven and earth, the stone slab he had been holding up remained exactly the same size in his eyes.

Li Fan understood immediately.

With a thought, he began mobilizing the remaining immortal spiritual qi within heaven and earth.

He divided it into three streams.

The first stream was used to begin writing the characters of the Immortal Mortal Transformation upon the stone slab.

The second stream surged into Li Fan's body.

His body was still mortal and therefore incapable of utilizing immortal spiritual qi.

After entering his body, the immortal spiritual qi was absorbed by the scattered fragments of the Final Dissociation Disc within Li Fan's divine soul.

Countless fragments glittered like a vast cluster of stars.

Within every single fragment, nourished by the immortal spiritual qi, it seemed as though a new Final Dissociation Disc was being born.

As for the third stream, Li Fan sent it toward the Little Heavenly Beast, which was on the verge of death and still unconscious as it recovered from its injuries.

Immortal spiritual qi seemed to be an extraordinary tonic for it.

Even though it was unconscious, the moment the immortal spiritual qi approached, it instinctively raised its head and greedily yearned for it.

What surprised Li Fan somewhat was that daoist boy Qiushui, despite also possessing the form of a living being, avoided the immortal spiritual qi as though it were some kind of poison.

The immortal spiritual qi that countless living beings yearned for seemed to be toxic to him.

The Xuanhuang Realm's Heavenly Dao was filled with countless grievances over Li Fan's reckless consumption of its already scarce immortal spiritual qi.

Yet under Li Fan's intimidating authority, it did not dare reveal the slightest trace of dissatisfaction.

Because as Li Fan continued writing upon the stone tablet, a vortex suddenly appeared in the sky above the Xuanhuang Realm.

Within it seemed to be an endless array of scenes.

The vortex firmly anchored the Xuanhuang Realm, which had already begun to show signs of falling.

'Condense!'

Li Fan gave a low shout within his heart.

Countless You Clan members, tiny as ants, were successively absorbed by the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal.

The characters of the Immortal Mortal Transformation were finally completed.

Li Fan also wrote the final stroke upon the stone slab.
