Next

Chapter 1403: Proving The Dao Integration Realm And Attaining Half Immortality

The brush fell, stunning the wind and rain. The written characters were formed, and the heaven and earth was set in place.

Following the same method as before, Li Fan successfully copied the complete True Immortal seal script character representing the Immortal Mortal Transformation that had manifested on the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal, and engraved it onto the Dao Key Stone Slab.

At that very moment, within the vortex high above the firmament, it seemed that some indescribable existence had descended.

Amid the upheaval, the Xuanhuang Realm, and even everything in the world, seemed to be forcibly frozen in place.

Even Li Fan, who was presiding over and driving the ascension of the Xuanhuang Realm, was no exception.

Countless bolts of lightning flashed across the sky of the Xuanhuang Realm at the same instant.

With every flash of thunder, the Xuanhuang Realm trembled slightly, as though it had been struck by a massive hammer.

Boom, boom, boom...

The Xuanhuang Realm, which had originally been stitched together from numerous worlds, began to undergo even further fusion under these tremendous blows.

The white mist that devoured essence and served as the boundary between the various prefectures became increasingly thin under these strikes. In the end, it dissipated completely.

The distance between heaven and earth also drew closer with each successive strike.

Even more terrifyingly, every flash of lightning was accompanied by the annihilation of an area within the Xuanhuang Realm. It seemed that those regions could not withstand the enormous pressure of the world's ascension into an Immortal Domain and were reduced to dust.

The void left behind by the destruction and disappearance of each region was quickly compressed and replaced by other regions.

...

A transformation that would normally require hundreds of millions of years of accumulation within a world was unfolding in the Xuanhuang Realm in an instant.

At this moment, it was as though the entire world were being tempered in thunder and raging flames, impurities being burned away while the essence was retained.

The aura permeating heaven and earth became increasingly sacred, while the overall size of the Xuanhuang Realm was gradually shrinking.

Although Li Fan's body had been immobilized by the tremendous force of the world's ascension, his thoughts remained active.

He stared intently at the vortex in the sky, and sure enough, he sensed within it the primordial and ancient aura of the Great Dao of Immortal Mortal Transformation.

Compared to the Immortal Mortal Transformation seal script character itself, the aura of the Great Dao was even more incomprehensible, instinctively causing one to feel awe and reverence.

It was precisely under the influence of this Great Dao aura that the Xuanhuang Realm had been able to break through the critical point of ascension and gain the qualifications to continue advancing toward an Immortal Domain.

For a cultivator to transform from mortal to immortal was already unimaginably difficult. How much more difficult would it be for an entire world?

Even with Li Fan's numerous preparations and the extremely profound accumulation of the Xuanhuang Realm, the formal process of ascending into an Immortal Domain was still fraught with danger, like a small fishing boat tossed about in a violent storm, seemingly on the verge of capsizing at any moment.

Within the vortex, countless bolts of lightning descended like hammers, striking continuously without end.

The bloated body of the Xuanhuang Realm was being shaved away inch by inch.

...

'Something is wrong.'

'The consumption caused by this world's Immortal Mortal Transformation is far too enormous.'

Li Fan faintly sensed the problem, and his expression became extremely solemn.

'Just like my previous experiment...'

In the blink of an eye, Li Fan recalled the records from the ancient cultivation world regarding cultivators successfully overcoming their tribulations and subsequently ascending.

'There is no visible tribulation lightning, nor is there any infusion of immortal spiritual qi to assist in the transformation. Everything depends solely on one's own foundation to endure the Immortal Mortal Transformation.'

'Under normal circumstances, this should be completely impossible.'

Another terrified distress signal from the Xuanhuang Realm reached his ears. However, under the torment of the ascension tribulation, it was already incredibly weak.

Li Fan's interests were temporarily tied to those of the Xuanhuang Realm. He quickly made his decision.

He drew upon the remaining power of the Xuanhuang Realm and temporarily recovered his ability to move under the suppression of the aura of the Great Dao of Immortal Mortal Transformation.

With a thought from Li Fan, daoist boy Qiushui transformed back into the form of the azure sword.

Sword light spread like rain, appearing throughout every region of the Xuanhuang Realm. Using the sword itself as a medium, it began absorbing power from the boundless Star Sea.

At the same time, more than a hundred fountains suddenly erupted from the earth of the Xuanhuang Realm for no apparent reason.

This was the vitality of the fissures and depressions in the Darkest Star Sea that Li Fan had stolen and transferred here.

The two continuous streams of power were like two powerful restorative medicines, desperately sustaining the Xuanhuang Realm's precarious life.

But within the vortex high above, the flashes of lightning became increasingly rapid.

The intervals between flashes grew so short that they formed an uninterrupted purple radiance, quietly illuminating the Xuanhuang Realm below.

As the distance between heaven and earth continued to shrink, the sky seemed to overturn and press downward.

Of the five Immortal Pillars, only the one formed from the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal at the center was still capable of supporting it.

The other four were all being crushed from top to bottom under the terrifying pressure, gradually turning into dust.

Thus, the shape of the heavens became like a dome, high in the center and low on all four sides.

Seeing that the Immortal Pillars naturally formed by the Xuanhuang Realm itself were insufficient to support the collapsing heavens, Li Fan narrowed his eyes and continuously formed hand seals.

The little Heavenly Beast, which was sleeping and being nourished by immortal spiritual qi, suddenly let out a heaven-shaking roar.

Its body involuntarily flipped over.

Then, in an instant, it expanded by tens of millions of times, its four feet pointing toward the sky, resembling Immortal Pillars as it supported the constantly descending firmament.

The enormous pressure of the falling sky was borne by the little Heavenly Beast.

Even with its extraordinary physique and the several Immortal Domain Go pieces it had swallowed, it was unwilling to be treated as expendable material.

A dangerous red light flashed through its eyes as it struggled incessantly.

It growled and roared, seemingly attempting to break free from Li Fan's control.

"Please, Wooden Sword Heavenly Wonder!"

Li Fan's expression was solemn as he loudly chanted.

Within the ruins of the Sword Tomb of the Heavenly Sword Sect long ago, the wooden sword surrounded by countless swords suddenly let out a spontaneous hum.

Sword light flashed.

Moments later, it flew into Li Fan's hand.

This Wooden Sword had originally been created by Mr. Bai, and it was the manifestation of the Sword Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm.

During the ascension of the Xuanhuang Realm, this Sword Dao had undergone transformation along with it.

Now that it had fallen into Li Fan's hand, it had already incorporated certain supreme aspects of the Sword Dao that transcended the mortal world.

Slash!

The sword moved according to his will.

In the blink of an eye, the Wooden Sword Heavenly Wonder transformed into a streak of white light and swept around the four corners of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Roar!

Amid the little Heavenly Beast's agonized roar, its four limbs fell away one after another.

'Go!'

Li Fan controlled the power of heaven and earth within the Xuanhuang Realm and fused the four limbs of the little Heavenly Beast into the four great Immortal Pillars.

Having absorbed some of the laws of the Immortal Domain, the four limbs of the Heavenly Beast possessed a carrying capacity far beyond anything in an ordinary mortal cultivation world.

The descent of the heavens continued for some time.

Finally, after the four limbs of the Heavenly Beast completely merged with the Immortal Pillars of the Xuanhuang Realm under the extreme pressure from outside, they barely managed to hold back the collapsing heavens.

Li Fan breathed a slight sigh of relief and looked at the Wooden Sword Heavenly Wonder in his hand.

Although it had relied on its sharpness and its Sword Dao characteristic of advancing without hesitation to forcibly sever the limbs of the Heavenly Beast, cutting through the Immortal Domain laws contained within those limbs had also inflicted unimaginable damage upon the Wooden Sword.

The Wooden Sword gradually shattered inch by inch, transforming into streams of fluorescent light that dissipated into heaven and earth.

The Sword Dao no longer existed within the Xuanhuang Realm.

For it to recover naturally might require ten million years, or even longer.

With the four limbs of the Heavenly Beast providing additional support, the Xuanhuang Realm's critical situation greatly improved.

Although the overall size of the realm continued to shrink, at the very least, the distance between heaven and earth would no longer decrease.

However, the fundamental problem remained unresolved.

The world's Immortal Mortal Transformation consumed far too much energy.

Even with the dual infusion of vitality from the fissures and power from the Star Sea, the expenditure still far exceeded the supply.

Li Fan also realized that it was not because the energy required for the Xuanhuang Realm to ascend into an Immortal Domain exceeded the infinite power of the Star Sea.

Rather, Qiushui alone was simply too inefficient at absorbing energy.

There was an infinite amount of power available, but it could not be fully drawn out.

As his thoughts raced, Li Fan already had an idea.

He bowed slightly, his crown hanging low and concealing the expression on his face.

Li Fan's voice resounded throughout the Xuanhuang heavens and earth.

"Please, all the Great Celestial Venerables of past generations!"

His voice was like an imperial decree. Once his words were spoken, they became reality.

Amid the violent upheaval of the Immortal Mortal Transformation, figures gradually appeared throughout heaven and earth.

The current Great Celestial Venerable, Wuming, also rose into the air of his own accord, taking his place at the final position.

Starting from Wuming, the farther back in time they went, the more indistinct the figures of the Great Celestial Venerables became.

The first was the phantom of Mr. Bai. His features could still be faintly discerned.

The one before him could only be seen as a vague silhouette, while his face was nothing more than a blank void.

Standing before this blank-faced phantom was another figure.

Although his face could not be seen clearly, Li Fan recognized him at a glance.

That should be the imprint left behind by the Xuantian King.

Before the Xuantian King, there were four more.

However, each was more indistinct than the last.

The first generation Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable had been reduced to nothing more than a faint outline.

Eight phantoms.

All of them represented Great Celestial Venerables who had once existed in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Although they had already passed away for various reasons, each had still left behind a unique imprint within the Xuanhuang heavens and earth.

In the past, Heavenly Dao Bai had once relied on Li Fan's Holy Embryo to reappear in the mortal world.

And now, Li Fan, who had obtained almost complete control over the Xuanhuang Realm, was taking advantage of the Xuanhuang Realm's life and death crisis to forcibly summon all eight Grand Celestial Venerables at once.

"The Xuanhuang Realm is now in peril. I ask all of you Great Celestial Venerables to lend me your strength."

Li Fan's expression was solemn as he bowed once again to the eight figures.

Then, the Taijia Divine Art erupted into action.

It seems to have sensed the earthshaking changes currently taking place on the Xuanhuang Realm's side. The consciousness of the Star Sea deliberately avoided him and did not respond to Li Fan for a time.

Li Fan persisted without giving up. At the same time, he transmitted the Taijia Divine Art to the Great Celestial Venerables of successive generations.

Thus, eight figures joined the ritual of communicating with the Star Sea one after another.

Anyone capable of becoming a Great Celestial Venerable of the Xuanhuang Realm was an unparalleled genius of their era. The First Generation Great Celestial Venerable had even possessed a True Immortal body.

Although they had already passed away, the imprints they had left behind still retained some of their original characteristics. With all eight joining forces to perform the ritual, the Infinite Star Sea was forced to establish a connection, no matter how unwilling it was.

"Equal Heaven!"

Under Li Fan's direction, the eight figures jointly cast the Equal Heaven Divine Art toward the consciousness of the Star Sea.

An immeasurable and boundless radiance of brilliant starlight suddenly illuminated the Xuanhuang Realm.

The stillness forced upon the world by the ascension into an Immortal Domain was briefly broken, allowing everything to resume its operation.

"Great Celestial Venerables, please offer yourselves to the Dao!"

Li Fan's expression was solemn as he bowed to each of them in turn.

First came Wuming.

Without the slightest hesitation, Wuming used his body as a passageway, absorbing and receiving the infinite power of the Star Sea into the Xuanhuang Realm.

Then came Heavenly Dao Bai.

One after another, the illusory figures of the Great Celestial Venerables flew toward various parts of the Xuanhuang Realm, guiding down the infinite power of the Star Sea.

Until finally, it was the First Generation Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

His indistinct outline transformed into brilliant blue light under the infusion of the infinite power of the Star Sea. The giant figure formed from starlight did not immediately fly toward the position where it was supposed to go.

Instead, it somewhat inexplicably looked toward the stone slab that Li Fan had been holding aloft.

The illusory figure of the Great Celestial Venerable did not remain motionless for long.

Moments later, it arrived beside Li Fan in an instant.

With a single finger, it lightly touched the stone tablet.

Everything happened far too quickly.

The First Generation Great Celestial Venerable's movement was so swift that even Li Fan, who was empowered by the strength of the Xuanhuang Realm, failed to react.

His heart jolted. He immediately summoned the [Truth] interface, preparing for whatever might happen next.

To his surprise, however, after the First Generation's illusory figure pressed a single finger against the stone slab, it made no further move.

Nor did any strange phenomenon occur.

After remaining in this stalemate for several breaths, the Great Celestial Venerable's illusory figure withdrew its finger on its own. Then, as if it had finally fulfilled some long held obsession, it went to the position where it was supposed to be.

Li Fan was slightly stunned at first. After a moment of thought, he understood.

"This is not the original body of the Dao's Key stone slab from the Immortal Ascension Platform of the past. It is merely a replica left behind by the Xuantian King."

"...Fortunately."

"If that were not the case, this illusory figure, which still possesses a trace of the First Generation Great Celestial Venerable's instincts, might have brought me a catastrophe."

Li Fan secretly felt fortunate.

With the limbs of the Heavenly Beast supporting the Xuanhuang Realm, and the eight Great Celestial Venerables connecting it to the Star Sea and continuously providing assistance through its infinite power, the Xuanhuang Realm, which had originally been on the verge of collapse, finally began to stabilize amid the extreme turbulence of crossing the boundary between the Immortal and mortal realms.

Within the vortex where the Great Dao manifested, the dense purple lightning that had appeared one after another began to decrease in frequency.

The tremors shaking the heavens and earth of the Xuanhuang Realm also gradually weakened.

Empowered by the strength of the Xuanhuang Heaven and Earth, Li Fan could clearly see even with his mortal eyes.

Within the seemingly unchanged Xuanhuang Realm, some unknown and mysterious force was repeatedly tracing over everything, stroke after stroke.

Nothing seemed to have changed from the inside out. Yet everything had become more solid, more vivid, and closer to its origin.

Joy arose within Li Fan's heart.

He knew that the critical stage had been successfully endured. Now came the moment when the transformation and ascension would descend.

"However, this is not the time to celebrate prematurely."

Li Fan stood quietly and continued waiting for the right moment.

Until the vortex in the sky began to shrink, and the Great Dao evolution of the Immortal and mortal transformation was about to end.

Li Fan moved again.

"Great Celestial Venerables, please mend Heaven!"

His expression was sincere as he simultaneously transmitted his intention.

During the process of balancing the power of the Star Sea and helping the Xuanhuang Realm overcome its crisis, the illusory figures of the Great Celestial Venerables of successive generations had already become existences similar to Star Spirits.

In the past, the Xuanhuang Realm had existed within the Star Sea. Thus, the Great Celestial Venerables could perhaps serve both purposes.

But now, even if the Xuanhuang Realm transformed and ascended into an Xuanhuang Immortal Domain, how could the Great Celestial Venerable of an Immortal Domain remain subordinate to the Star Sea?

...

Li Fan's meaning was extremely clear.

It was also genuinely in accordance with the interests of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Having recovered from the brink of death and now seeing hope of proving the Dao and becoming an Immortal Domain, the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm also cooperated with Li Fan and issued the order.

Of the eight Great Celestial Venerables, all except Wuming were originally transformed from world imprints.

Thus, they offered little resistance. Their indistinct figures slowly dissolved.

They disappeared into the infinite power of the Star Sea.

Even if Star Spirits were immortal and indestructible, once the vessels on which they depended no longer existed, they could only disappear from Heaven and Earth.

This was not merely a simple matter of ending the summoning.

The Xuanhuang Realm was completely erasing the primordial imprints that several Great Celestial Venerables of the past had left behind within Heaven and Earth.

This also meant that from this point onward, the Xuanhuang Realm would never again be able to summon the illusory figures of the Great Celestial Venerables.

The Star Spirits dissipated, and the passage connecting to the infinite power of the Star Sea gradually closed.

But the consumption caused by the Immortal and mortal transformation was also rapidly decreasing.

With Qiushui providing support, it was already sufficient.

After sending away the seven Great Celestial Venerable figures who posed the greatest threat, Li Fan looked toward the final one, Wuming.

The Great Celestial Venerable Wuming possessed neither knowledge nor consciousness. He merely followed the natural operation of the Heavenly Dao.

Now that the time had come for him to make the correct choice, Wuming first established the succession trial for the next Great Celestial Venerable.

Then, the Wuming Flame suddenly blazed fiercely from within his body.

Just like the previous generation Great Celestial Venerable Su Bai, he had chosen to die by his own hand.

When the flames upon Great Celestial Venerable Wuming completely engulfed him and then vanished, the Xuanhuang Realm's path of ascension and transformation, with the eight Great Celestial Venerables as sacrifices, finally reached its final moment.

It was about to cross that boundary between the Immortal and mortal realms.

Li Fan suddenly gained an insight.

The instant the Xuanhuang Realm ascended into an Immortal Domain, all the various agreements it had previously entered into with Li Fan would become nothing more than waste paper.

They would no longer count.

Even mortals make agreements and contracts with one another.

How could an Immortal and a mortal possibly remain bound by such things?

As inspiration suddenly struck him, taking advantage of the final moment during which he still controlled the power of the Xuanhuang Heaven and Earth, Li Fan summoned the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death.

The next instant...

The vortex in the sky completely healed.

Amid a deafening roar, the Xuanhuang Heaven and Earth resumed its operation.

Stripes of seven colored light appeared throughout every corner of Heaven and Earth, constantly leaping and flickering across the world.

The Xuanhuang Realm, which had originally been compressed to its absolute limit, began to rebound and transform after reaching its lowest point.

It began growing at a speed visible to the naked eye.

In an instant, the Xuanhuang Realm once again became vast, with high heavens and distant earth.

In a single instant, billions of miles passed. From Li Fan's field of vision, after only a few breaths, he could no longer clearly see the giant pillars supporting the heavens at the four corners of the world.

...

Li Fan took in the astonishing changes occurring within the Xuanhuang Realm.

Relying on the final trace of the Xuanhuang Realm's remaining power, he vaguely sensed that far beyond the heavens, in an extremely distant place, the Hunger Immortal within the Immortal Ruins seemed to have awakened.

However, she inexplicably stared at the Immortal Domain that had appeared before her and cautiously chose not to emerge rashly. She merely observed from afar.

Meanwhile, within the Daoyuan Prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm, the Grand Mage and that illusory figure remained in a state of slumber.

It was just that the illusory figure, whose voice of transmitting the Dao had not sounded for a long time, once again began spontaneously chanting.

The sleeping Grand Mage furrowed his brows tightly. His mouth repeatedly opened and closed, likewise uttering the sound of transmitting the Dao.

When the two voices met, however, they did not overlap.

Instead, they actually canceled each other out.

In the end, everything fell into silence.

Outside the Xuanhuang Realm, the Heavenly Doctor and the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor had just broken through the High Wall that Li Fan had established.

Looking at the Xuanhuang Realm that had appeared before them, both familiar and unfamiliar, they stood there dumbfounded.

What surprised Li Fan was that the face of the Immortal Ancestor began to flicker and transform.

It seemed as though something was about to break out of its shell.

...

Within the Xuanhuang Realm, countless changes occurred one after another as it formally ascended into an Immortal Domain.

Yet in truth, the vast majority of Li Fan's attention was focused on the Celestial Spirit Heaven and Earth before him, or rather, Ink Death.

As the Xuanhuang Realm transformed from mortal to immortal.

Ink Death also completed the transformation from mortal to immortal before Li Fan's eyes.

Originally, it had merely possessed the vague shape of a human body formed from dense black ink.

After transforming into a Celestial Spirit Heaven and Earth, its appearance became far more detailed and concrete.

It had even gained a long robe that billowed in the wind.

Ink Death's face did not possess ordinary human features. Instead, constantly shifting and changing black symbols wandered across its face.

As Li Fan and the Celestial Spirit Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, stared directly at each other, an instinctive and frenzied warning arose within Li Fan's heart.

Perhaps because Li Fan was the absolute greatest contributor who had single handedly driven the Xuanhuang Realm's ascension and transformation, Ink Death, which was purely an embodiment of the Law of Destruction, did not immediately choose to attack.

But that did not mean Li Fan would hesitate.

'Xuanhuang.'

'World Destruction Transformation.'

Li Fan silently recited the words in his heart.

The bombs he had secretly implanted in the Xuanhuang Realm long ago were detonated in an instant.

When the Xuanhuang Realm had once merely been an ordinary cultivation world, the World Destruction Transformation had already been terrifying.

Now that the Xuanhuang Realm had ascended and transformed into an Immortal Realm, the World Destruction Transformation that erupted within it was even more indescribably horrifying.

The world had transformed into an Immortal Domain, yet the countless living beings within it had not even had time to enjoy the blessings brought by the ascension of all things.

The terrifying disaster had already descended.

Cold winds howled, freezing heaven and earth, space and time in an instant. All living beings within it had no time to react whatsoever before becoming part of the ice crystals.

Terrifying bolts of lightning descended from the sky. Wherever the electric radiance swept, everything was transformed into pure nothingness. The lightning born from heaven and earth itself seemed determined to destroy heaven and earth.

...

The Xuanhuang Realm, which had ascended into an Immortal Domain, had only just stabilized.

Yet it immediately welcomed the terrifying transformation brought about by the descent of countless tribulations.

The Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm was both furious and shocked, yet it could not understand what was happening.

The Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, was also stunned.

Because it keenly sensed that the vast majority of these world destroying calamities were all coming toward it.

No longer concerned with the cultivator before it, Ink Death attempted to join forces with the other Heaven and Earth Immortal Spirits of the Xuanhuang Realm to suppress and calm the various world destroying changes.

'Immortal Descent, All Things Return to Void.'

An utterly indifferent voice interrupted Ink Death's movements.

It was as though it had suddenly been transported into a completely unfamiliar region. The scene of the Xuanhuang Realm before Ink Death's eyes suddenly changed.

In its place was an endless storm of destruction.

Li Fan, concealed within the phantom of a Wooden Sword, had billions upon billions of brilliant stars simultaneously shine within his mind.

Each one was deducing a portion of a magnificent Immortal Formation.

As these billions upon billions of stars united and carried out their deductions together, they brought the true Immortal Formation into existence.

[Final Dissociation Disc, Immortal Formation]!

With the assistance of immortal spiritual qi, the Final Dissociation Disc, which had originally shattered into countless particles, was formally reborn from the flames.

Under the miraculous effects of immortal spiritual qi, every single particle once again transformed into an entirely new Final Dissociation Disc.

Billions upon billions of stars shone brightly, and the newly born Final Dissociation Discs stood like countless True Immortals, radiant and resplendent.

The deduction ability of the Immortal Formation Final Dissociation Disc had already somewhat exceeded Li Fan's understanding.

With merely a slight movement of his thoughts, the Final Dissociation Disc had already begun endlessly iterating the formation according to Li Fan's requirements.

It did not even require Li Fan to personally arrange the formation.

Drops of immortal spiritual power dispersed from the billions upon billions of cores of the Immortal Formation Final Dissociation Disc.

They spontaneously evolved into a formation.

Thus, a genuine Immortal-grade Grand Formation was born.

Compared with the formations Li Fan had previously arranged, its might was like heaven and earth apart.

Even at the very instant it was generated, the Immortal Formation Final Dissociation Disc was already iterating and improving it.

From the moment the formation began to the moment it was completed, it had already evolved through dozens of generations of versions.

It seemed to possess the might to transform all things into complete nothingness.

In the past, even a phantom of the Hunger Immortal had been able to casually suppress the [Immortal Descent, All Things Return to Void] Grand Formation with a wave of its hand.

But now...

Within this Immortal grade formation, Ink Death, the Heaven and Earth Celestial Spirit, was visibly being struck and diminished at an astonishing speed.

'Hmm?'

'This Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth is weaker than I imagined?'

Seeing this, Li Fan felt little joy. Instead, he was somewhat caught off guard and surprised.

While controlling the Immortal Formation, he observed the Xuanhuang Immortal Domain at the same time.

Only now did Li Fan discover something abnormal.

Although the Immortal Domain had clearly descended, the black qi of the Dao Annihilation Calamity at the center of the Star Sea seemed to show very little reaction.

Its reaction was completely different from the previous life, when the true Immortal Domain had unfolded.

Within the newly born Xuanhuang Immortal Domain, no new immortal spiritual qi was being generated either.

It had merely crossed the barrier between mortals and immortals.

Yet it had not undergone the qualitative transformation that came with becoming truly immortal.

For some reason, all of this made Li Fan think of the Xuantian King from long ago.

'He had clearly ascended and become an immortal, yet there was no immortal spiritual qi.'

'Half Immortal, Half Immortal...'

'Could it be that after exhausting everything I had, what I transformed was merely a Half Immortal Domain?'

Li Fan could not help feeling somewhat irritated.

However, he quickly calmed down.

A Half Immortal was still an immortal.

From the Xuantian King ten thousand years ago to the Xuanhuang Realm today, both had ended up in the same state.

Perhaps the problem was not that his attempt had gone wrong.

Rather, after the destruction of the Immortal Realm, something had gone fundamentally wrong with the entire cultivation path of the past.

Sensing that the cautious Hunger Immortal seemed to have finally decided to come and investigate, Li Fan no longer hesitated.

The Immortal Formation Final Dissociation Disc operated at full power.

Under the All Things Return to Void Grand Formation, the Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, was blasted by endless violent winds.

Bit by bit, it was blown into nothingness.

At the same time that Ink Death dissipated between heaven and earth, an incomparably immense force descended upon Li Fan.

Li Fan had finally stepped into the Dao Integration Realm.

But this was somewhat different from the ideal state he had planned.

Before he even had time to think about it, the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm sensed that Ink Death had disappeared, and that Li Fan was actually the culprit.

It instantly went mad.

In its eyes, Li Fan had accompanied it all the way from mortality to the present. He was someone who was both teacher and friend.

Yet now, when the Xuanhuang Realm was facing a world destroying crisis, Li Fan had chosen to turn his blade against it.

This was a complete and utter betrayal.

The Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm, driven to the extreme by rage, ignored the world destroying disasters occurring throughout its own body.

Countless Immortal Spirits of Heaven and Earth descended around Li Fan.

They were determined to kill him.

Within the All Things Return to Void Grand Formation, Li Fan felt little fear.

Taking advantage of the final moments of this life, he observed the Xuanhuang Immortal Domain that had only half successfully completed its ascension.

The sun in the sky, the Five Elements power flowing through the world, and the azure light of the Taixu Blue Heaven Lamp protecting the Xuanhuang Realm.

There were also the four Immortal Pillars supporting the heavens.

In this so called Xuanhuang Immortal Domain, there were actually only these few places where traces of immortal spiritual qi existed.

'Perhaps as time passes, this immortal spiritual qi will slowly spread and permeate throughout the realm.'

'It will gradually transform this place into a true Xuanhuang Immortal Domain.'

'But...'

'It still cannot compare with the Immortal Realm I once witnessed, the glorious Immortal Realm of the past.'

Li Fan let out a sigh in his heart.

'Indeed.'

'With the Immortal Realm destroyed, how can one become truly Immortal?'

A sense of utter boredom suddenly arose within Li Fan's heart.

The All Things Return to Void Grand Formation was gradually being broken through by the Immortal Spirits of Heaven and Earth that charged forward without fear of death.

Li Fan no longer lingered.

He silently recited, "[Truth]."

When [Truth] was activated once again, the dejection in Li Fan's heart immediately vanished without a trace.

Because the way [Truth] activated was clearly different from before.

Next

Chapter 1404: Dao Concealment In Stasis

In the past, whenever he activated [Truth], a layer of illusory water mist would envelop the surroundings.

Everything in the world would become unreal.

When Li Fan's consciousness awakened once again, he would already have returned to the predetermined anchor point.

But now...

When Li Fan silently thought of [Truth], it was no longer a thick curtain of water that appeared to obscure the surroundings.

Instead, the entire world entered a state of stillness with a single thought from Li Fan. The only difference was that the world had changed from its former myriad colors into monotonous black and white.

Li Fan had never observed the world from such a perspective before. He stared blankly at the scene before him.

The ruined Myriad Things Return to Void Grand Formation, along with the remaining force of the wind, seemed to have frozen in midair.

Countless Immortal Spirits of Heaven and Earth surrounded him, each with a different Form.

Farther away, he could vaguely see a thin, withered figure descending into the world.

It was none other than the Immortal Ruins' Comprehending Hunger Immortal.

...

Everything before his eyes seemed to be no different from what had existed before [Truth] was activated. The only difference was that everything had fallen into a black and white state of stillness.

Li Fan carefully observed for a long time. Suddenly, his heart stirred.

His figure then began to drift, and he actually started moving within this frozen world!

It was an extremely novel experience.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and attempted to touch a nearby Immortal Spirit of Heaven and Earth.

Even though it had taken on a black and white form, Li Fan recognized it at a glance.

It was an old acquaintance, Crimson Flame.

Compared with when he had been merely a Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Crimson Flame's expression was now even more lifelike, almost indistinguishable from that of a real cultivator. Yet his eyes remained tightly shut, while the clothing manifested around his body was tattered and ragged.

Compared with the other Spirits of Heaven and Earth, he seemed somewhat out of place.

The instant Li Fan touched the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Crimson Flame's body suddenly flashed.

It transformed into countless particles of light and scattered with the wind.

Li Fan also sensed that the range of this frozen world had simultaneously shrunk by a considerable amount.

For the moment, he paid no attention to what was happening in the distance. Instead, he narrowed his eyes and savored the scene that had just appeared in his mind.

Apart from a few fragments of the purest Crimson Flame Laws, there were also a handful of scattered memories.

They were the past experiences of a blind cultivator who had attained supremacy through the Dao of Fire.

The power displayed by this blind cultivator in those memories left Li Fan deeply shaken.

An endless, cold, desolate starfield stretched as far as the eye could see.

The blind cultivator sat within it, comprehending the Dao.

No one knew how many years passed.

Gradually, tiny points of fire gathered around the blind cultivator.

The cold starfield slowly became warm.

Then it became incomparably hot.

In the end, the entire starfield was incinerated by an invisible flame, becoming a void and hollow place where even coldness no longer existed.

At the end of the scene, that blind cultivator seemed to disappear from the world as well.

Li Fan had now comprehended the Dao through the Spirit of Heaven and Earth.

Although he was only at the Dao Integration realm, his actual strength was already comparable to a Half Immortal.

Thus, he could truly comprehend just how terrifying the strength displayed by the blind cultivator in those memories was.

'The phenomenon caused by merely comprehending the Dao is already this terrifying.'

'A True Immortal... It is definitely a True Immortal!'

Li Fan's heart trembled violently.

But soon, he realized something even more astonishing.

'Wait, what I just saw...'

'A True Immortal comprehending the Dao and disappearing into the world.'

'Was that the scene of him achieving the realm of the Nameless True Immortal?'

Li Fan was shaken to the core once again. His body even trembled slightly and uncontrollably.

His thoughts raced.

After several breaths, Li Fan vaguely realized why all of this had happened.

'The Spirit of Heaven and Earth were formed by the laws of the Xuanhuang Realm through the imprints left behind by cultivators who had once appeared throughout history.'

'After the Xuanhuang Realm ascended into an Immortal Domain, this characteristic was preserved. The Immortal Spirit of Heaven and Earth were likewise formed by the laws of the Xuanhuang Immortal Domain through deeper imprints left within heaven and earth.'

'They are one with the Dao, and can even merge with the Dao. Naturally, those who can resonate most deeply with various laws are the Nameless True Immortals who once proved the Dao through the Dao itself.'

'Once they enter the Nameless realm, they disappear from heaven and earth forever. The only thing that the Xuanhuang Immortal Domain can perceive is the final scene before they proved the Dao...'

Li Fan's expression became solemn.

After a Nameless True Immortal proves the Dao, they become the Great Dao itself.

If his speculation was correct, then what he had just absorbed was the Dao-proving insight once possessed by a Nameless True Immortal!

Even if he had only managed to capture such a tiny fragment from the countless particles of light, for Li Fan, who had only just taken half a step into the Immortal realm, it was absolutely an enormous benefit.

He carefully savored everything that had emerged within his mind.

After his brief excitement, Li Fan suddenly felt that something was wrong.

'Above the True Immortal realm, one proves the Dao and enters the Nameless realm. Each one's Dao path is different.'

'The Dao of Flame has already been occupied by someone. What use could it possibly have for me?'

'Unless...'

'That blind Nameless True Immortal is already dead.'

The more Li Fan thought about it, the more likely he felt this possibility was.

He quickly looked toward the surrounding Spirits of Heaven and Earth.

Those like Crimson Flame, who had evolved into forms resembling human cultivators, were extremely rare.

There were only three such cases.

'If I absorb them, I should likewise be able to sense the corresponding Dao path and witness the scenes of the Nameless True Immortals once attaining supremacy.'

Li Fan was just about to act when his expression suddenly changed.

During the brief moment he had been thinking, the effective range of the frozen black and white world had once again shrunk drastically.

Only a spherical region with a radius of nine point nine zhang remained around him.

There happened to be no Spirit of Heaven and Earth within it.

A wave of helplessness immediately surged through Li Fan's heart.

'I see. Apart from absorbing objects within the frozen world accelerating the collapse of its effective range, simply lingering here for too long will also...'

'The area retained after activating [Truth] does not exist indefinitely. From the looks of it, its duration is still very short.'

Li Fan now fully understood the new changes that had occurred to [Truth] after he had advanced to Dao Integration, or rather, after he had achieved the Half Immortal realm.

When he activated [Truth], everything in the world would no longer immediately become completely illusory.

Instead, a vast region centered around Li Fan would remain in a black and white state of stillness.

As for exactly how large this region was, Li Fan had not yet had the chance to verify it this time.

However, it should be extremely vast.

At the very least, the entire Xuanhuang Immortal Domain within his field of vision had been enveloped by it.

Li Fan also speculated that both the effective range and duration of the black and white stasis region might be positively correlated with his own strength.

He had only just entered the Immortal realm, so the effect he could maintain was naturally extremely limited.

'This black and white stasis region can temporarily be called the [Dao Concealment] Zone.'

'Within the Dao Concealment Zone, everything other than me seems to exist in an overlapping state of being both real and false.'

'Maintaining the Dao Concealment Zone also requires some kind of energy related to [Truth].'

'When I touch or sense the objects within the Dao Concealment Zone, it is equivalent to dragging them out of the state of being both real and false and into the same state of [Truth] as myself.'

'The energy maintaining the Dao Concealment Zone is therefore consumed in large quantities, causing its range to shrink drastically as well...'

Even though there was barely any room left for him to stand, Li Fan still did not immediately leave the Dao Concealment Zone.

He had already sensed that while inside the Dao Concealment Zone, comprehending the transformation between [Truth and Falsehood] was countless times easier than in the normal world.

Every additional moment he remained here brought him immeasurable benefits.

'When the true becomes false, even the false becomes true.'

Li Fan continued pondering and comprehending the profound meaning of [Truth].

At the same time, he looked around at the boundary of the Dao Concealment Zone, nine point nine zhang away.

As usual, it was completely shrouded in layers upon layers of water mist.

"Truth is falsehood. Falsehood is truth."

Li Fan furrowed his brows tightly, doing his utmost to comprehend the most ethereal and profound principle in this world.

Not long afterward, the range of the Dao Concealment Zone shrank once again.

Rolling water mist swallowed everything except Li Fan.

Li Fan's consciousness also slowly sank into darkness.

However, this time, Li Fan did not completely fall into a deep sleep and lose all awareness.

He remained half asleep and half awake. In his hazy state, he seemed to see an endless radiance erupting from within the darkness.

Amid the blazing white light, countless events, countless people, and countless objects emerged one after another.

When Li Fan's consciousness finally became completely clear...

Anchored to one year, the peak of the Jieli Mountain!

Li Fan's expression was somewhat dazed.

He extended both hands and carefully examined them.

Everything he had experienced from [Truth] until now gave Li Fan an extremely unreal feeling.

His thoughts were still immersed in comprehending the transformation between truth and falsehood.

A full day and night passed before Li Fan finally could no longer capture even the slightest ripple of inspiration. Only then did he temporarily stop his meditation.

Only now did Li Fan turn his attention toward [Truth]'s notification.

[This simulation has ended.]

[You may choose one of the following options to retain:

One item personally possessed during this simulation.

,

Your cultivation realm during this simulation.

,

The simulated memories of one person who had a close relationship with you during this simulation. These memories may be inherited by that person.

,

Abandon the above options and accelerate the charging progress.]

,

...

"I have already attained the Half-Immortal realm, yet there was still no prompt about an additional interference variable or an extra inheritance option."

"Looks like even my achievement of the Half-Immortal realm was completely within [Truth]'s expectations."

Li Fan spoke with some self-mockery.

"However, the newly born Xuanhuang Immortal Domain does not count as an additional interference variable. Yet the descent from beyond the High Wall does?"

Li Fan fell into brief contemplation.

Then he shook his head and returned to his senses.

"Thinking too much is useless. Now that I have attained the Half-Immortal realm, I am completely invincible throughout the Star Sea."

"This lifetime, I will turn my gaze beyond the High Wall."

"Whatever mysteries lie there, I will find out for myself!"

After experiencing countless twists and turns, he had finally attained Dao Integration and the Half-Immortal realm.

Although it was far from perfect, he had ultimately become an immortal who stood above all mortal beings.

Of [Truth]'s four options, he would naturally choose to inherit his cultivation.

However...

Li Fan slowly walked to the edge of the cliff on Jieli Mountain and raised his head to look at the frail Great Xuan Small World, and even the Xuanhuang Realm.

He could not help sighing.

"If I inherit my cultivation here, I am afraid the Great Xuan Small World would immediately be torn apart by the phenomenon accompanying ascension to immortality."

"Even the Xuanhuang Realm, formed from the fusion of countless worlds, might be shattered into pieces by this tremendous disturbance, with its fragments falling one after another into the Immortal Ruins."

"Forget it."

Under normal circumstances, regardless of what happened to a bound item during a reincarnation, whether it was damaged or sublimated, it would not affect the binding space of [Truth].

Once [Truth] was activated, they would still return to the state they had possessed when they were first bound.

But now...

With a thought from Li Fan, the Final Dissociation Disc was immediately taken out.

Within his sea of consciousness, countless stars seemed to rise, dazzlingly brilliant.

[Final Dissociation Disc, Immortal-Rank], had astonishingly returned to the present together with Li Fan!

"Just like that leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal before."

Li Fan looked toward the [...leg bone] within [Truth]'s binding space and pondered.

"It seems that only things at the level of True Immortals are qualified to be carried along by me through the transformation between truth and falsehood."

Previous

Next

Chapter 1405: The [Truth] Signs Of The Doom

Li Fan carefully observed the [Dissociation Disc, Immortal Rank].

In his previous life, Li Fan had already deeply experienced the terrifying power of the Dissociation Disc after it formally advanced into an Immortal Artifact.

With a single thought, an Immortal Formation could be formed.

Once the Immortal Formation was complete, it could slay Immortals and gods.

Ink Death was also among the strongest of all the Spirits of Heaven and Earth. After following the Xuanhuang Realm through its transformation from mortal to Immortal, the strength of Ink Death in the state of a Immortal Spirit of Heaven and Earth could be imagined.

A mass of ink-black color covered Li Fan's eyes. Countless writhing black specks also emerged from the fingertips of his right hand.

Li Fan extended his right hand and gently pressed that strand of ink-black color against the restrained mass of True Immortal residual power in his left hand.

The True Immortal residual power that had been wreaking havoc across the Star Sea was completely incapable of resisting the corrosive power of the Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death. It was swallowed by the dense blackness and completely erased from existence.

The ink-black tide continued surging forward. When it finally receded, there was nothing left between Li Fan's hands.

"It can annihilate True Immortal residual power. Even when facing a True Immortal, I should not be at too great a disadvantage. This is precisely the advantage of advancing through the new method. As long as the power of the Immortal Spirits of Heaven and Earth I devour is sufficiently strong, I can instantly turn it into my own strength."

"With the power of Ink Death protecting my body, even though I, a [Half Immortal], lack the methods that Immortals possess, such as immortal spiritual qi, I am still more than capable of dominating and crushing my way through the Star Sea."

"But my true trump card is still the Immortal Rank Dissociation Disc."

Within Li Fan's mind, countless Dissociation Discs simultaneously radiated brilliant light. From every star, threads of silvery-white starlight were released, flowing and converging together.

They appeared within Li Fan's palm.

"The Ink Death of the Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth is already this powerful. Yet before the Dissociation Disc, which can arrange genuine Immortal Formations, it can still be killed like an ant in an instant."

Li Fan's current Half Immortal realm had been achieved through merging with the Dao of Ink Death. In other words, immediately after achieving Dao Integration, before upgrading his other divine abilities to the level of a Half Immortal, Li Fan himself was actually not much stronger than the Ink Death of the Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth.

Likewise, before an Immortal Formation arranged by himself, he would be completely powerless to resist.

"It is like a child holding an Immortal treasure. Offense and defense are completely disproportionate."

"And the reason for this ridiculous situation..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly and attempted to absorb the silver starlight in his palm into his body.

Yet the starlight remained completely unmoved.

"Immortal Spiritual Qi. But I cannot use it?"

The reason the Dissociation Disc displayed such unimaginable capabilities was because, during the critical moment of his Dao Integration in his previous life, Li Fan had extracted a strand of immortal spiritual qi obtained by the Xuanhuang Realm from the inheritance of the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable and transferred it to the Dissociation Disc.

Only after obtaining the immortal spiritual qi did the Dissociation Disc undergo its true transformation.

Moreover, this transformation seemed to have been even more complete than that of the Xuanhuang Realm and Li Fan himself.

To verify the speculation in his heart, Li Fan's thoughts shifted slightly, and countless stars within the Dissociation Disc began to shine.

Immortal power flowed forth, instantly forming a miniature Immortal Formation before Li Fan.

From the outside, it looked like a silver ring about the size of a palm. In reality, its core was the [Illusory Xuanhuang, Multitudinous Living Beings] Formation, combined with the laws contained within the [Restrain] character art. Once the ring flew out, it could easily suppress any existence below the Immortal level.

The stars of the Dissociation Disc continued to shine.

One silver ring after another appeared accordingly.

They lined up before Li Fan.

Only after a full ninety-nine silver rings had been formed did the Dissociation Disc reach its limit and have no choice but to stop.

The energy contained within each of these silver rings was greater than that of the mortal Illusory Formation that Li Fan had painstakingly arranged in his previous life to encompass the entire Xuanhuang Realm.

After all, the difference between the Immortal and mortal realms could not be measured in ordinary terms.

Even after advancing into an Immortal Artifact, arranging ninety-nine Immortal Formations at once seemed to have reached its limit.

Li Fan stared at the Dissociation Disc, which seemed to have exhausted its immortal spiritual qi.

Yet he soon saw that within the countless sub-discs resembling stars, new strands of immortal spiritual qi were slowly regenerating.

Li Fan was certain that during this process of depletion and rebirth, the Dissociation Disc had not absorbed any energy from the outside world.

"In a certain sense, it is infinite."

Li Fan pondered this thoughtfully.

At the same time, a trace of regret and deep confusion rose within his heart.

"After the Wuming succeeded the Great Celestial Venerable, I once used the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao to observe and inspect the four remaining Immortal Artifacts."

"They indeed contained Immortal Spiritual Qi, but every bit consumed was permanently lost. They could never achieve what the Immortal Rank Dissociation Disc can do, with immortal spiritual qi that can be drawn upon endlessly and used without exhaustion."

"What exactly is going on?"

Li Fan's eyes flickered as his thoughts surged.

After pondering for a long time, he temporarily reached a conclusion.

"Perhaps when the Immortal Realm bestowed the Immortal Artifacts upon the Xuanhuang Realm, it deliberately imposed restrictions upon them."

"But now that the Immortal Realm has been destroyed, newly born Immortal Artifacts are no longer constrained by the upper realm. Thus, they have regained the characteristics that Immortal Artifacts originally possessed."

"But..."

Li Fan once again felt that something was not quite right.

If immortal spiritual qi alone was enough to recreate Immortal Artifacts in their prime on a large scale, then the successive Great Celestial Venerables of the Xuanhuang Realm in ancient times should have long ago destroyed all their old Immortal Artifacts and forged new ones.

"Perhaps it has something to do with the Final Dissociation Disc itself."

After a long while, Li Fan suddenly understood something.

"The Final Dissociation Disc was formed through the fusion of the Final Disc and the Dissociation Disc. Both of them were secondary bodies of the Tianxuan Mirror."

"The Tianxuan Mirror was one of the Immortal Artifacts that the Xuantian King brought back from the Immortal Realm."

"In a certain sense, the Final Dissociation Disc itself is a descendant of an Immortal Artifact."

"Moreover..."

Images of how he had obtained the Final Dissociation Disc flashed through Li Fan's mind.

Gu Xinghan, a disciple of the Medicine King Sect who possessed the Dissociation Disc transformed from Li Fan himself, had coincidentally obtained the Final Disc.

The two gradually merged after coming into contact with each other.

After Gu Xinghan died, the object was buried deep underground along with him, where it endured thousands of years.

It could be said that the birth of the Final Dissociation Disc was itself an exceedingly rare and extraordinarily difficult occurrence.

"Perhaps simply fusing two secondary bodies is still not enough. They must also undergo the tempering of time, perhaps even the destruction and rebirth of an entire world, before they can meet the conditions necessary to advance into an Immortal Artifact..."

Only then did Li Fan truly understand just how fortunate he had been to obtain the Final Dissociation Disc back then.

Although he did not understand the underlying principle, regardless of the reason, Li Fan had now mastered this Immortal Artifact.

He was naturally extremely satisfied with the power of the Dissociation Disc. The only thing troubling him was that he could not absorb or use the immortal spiritual qi contained within and generated by the Immortal Artifact itself.

Li Fan made another attempt to transfer the immortal spiritual qi within the Dissociation Disc into other objects.

For example, the Myriad Beasts Condensed Crystal and the Dao Deviation Stone, among others.

Yet without exception, the silver starlight passed straight through them without stopping in the slightest.

Those objects that had been infused with it seemed completely unaware that any immortal spiritual qi existed within them.

"It seems that this infinite immortal spiritual qi can only be used by the Dissociation Disc itself."

"It is not that I cannot use it. Nobody can."

Li Fan shook his head slightly and pondered inwardly.

"No wonder the Xuantian King, despite plundering so many treasures from the Immortal Realm, still found it so difficult to advance from the Half Immortal realm."

"The relationship between a artifact and another artifact, or a artifact and an immortal, is not the same."

"I am afraid that even the immortal spiritual qi within the Faceless Immortal, the True Immortal, could not be transferred to the Xuantian King for his use. Otherwise, given the friendship between the two of them, and considering the infinite nature of immortal spiritual qi, the Faceless Immortal should not have been unwilling to lend him a hand."

Suddenly, Li Fan recalled another matter concerning the Xuantian King that he had learned from Ji Yuzhen's memories.

"But it is said that the Xuantian King was able to extract immortal spiritual qi from the Great Mage and refine and absorb it. How should that be explained?"

"The shattered memories of the Dharma King of the Xuantian Sect cannot be completely trusted."

"But they have nevertheless given me a warning. After returning to the Xuanhuang Realm in this life, I must be wary of that mysterious Grand Mage..."

Li Fan stood within the Star Sea, his thoughts taking a long time to finally settle.

It was only then that he reopened the [Truth] panel and examined the specific changes that had occurred after his advancement to Dao Integration and his strength reaching the Half-Immortal realm.

Name: Li Fan

Realm: Immortal

Physical Age: ...

Psychological Age: ...

Virtualization Charging Progress: 50%

Anchoring Charge Progress: 0%

Current Number of Anchors: 3

Currently Available Anchors: 1

Bound Items: [Nameless... Leg Bone], [Descends into Mortal World], [Final Dissociation Disc, Immortal Rank], Dao Deviation Stone, Xuantian Mirror Origin (Fragment), Taiyan Ark, Ten Thousand Beasts Condensed Crystal, Ten Thousand People Blood Essence.

...

That was far less than he had imagined.

How could such a lifespan be worthy of the legendary Immortal?

Moreover, according to Li Fan's observations, this constantly changing number was not changing at a uniform rate.

Instead, in most cases, after a certain period of time passed, it would suddenly plunge and decrease.

Only on extremely rare occasions would it increase.

Li Fan stared intently at this incomparably profound string of numbers.

Suddenly, an inexplicable insight surged into his heart.

'An Immortal's lifespan certainly cannot be a mere few million years.'

'Yet according to [Truth]'s judgment, I can only survive for this long at most...'

A golden light suddenly flashed in his eyes, and Li Fan instantly realized the truth.

'It is because this is the lifespan of the [Mortal World], not mine!'

As if he had discovered some earth-shattering secret, even Li Fan, who had already become a Half-Immortal, could not help but feel his heart pounding violently.

'When that constantly changing number reaches zero, the entire Mortal World will simultaneously reach its end.'

'Every living being within it will be plunged into eternal ruin along with it!'

'If I am within it, and I do not activate [Truth], then the same will happen to me!'

'Wait!'

Li Fan pondered this, and his heart suddenly began pounding even faster.

'In the past, my psycholgical age did not change along with T[ruth].'

'It was determined solely by what I personally experienced.'

'Then, once my mental age has already merged with this string of numbers and disappeared...'

Li Fan's expression became somewhat grim.

'Even if I repeatedly activate [Truth], the countdown to the death of this [Mortal World] will not change at all?'

'The arrival of this true world-ending calamity cannot be reversed simply by me using [Truth]?'

He had only just ascended to immortality and had barely had time to feel excited.

Next

Chapter 1406: Illusory Formation Plays A Trick On The Heavenly Doctor

Not only had Li Fan entered the True Immortal realm, the Virtualization Charging Progress had directly reached 50 percent. Its charging speed had also increased dramatically.

It would take less than a year to complete the remaining 50 percent of the charge.

And even more importantly...

"The charging limit has also increased from 200 percent, doubling all at once to reach 400 percent. This was previously a value that could only be achieved after first absorbing the bowing figurine. Now, for me, it has become the norm. Moreover, the defensive strength of [Truth] is also completely different from before. It will no longer be as fragile as paper in front of a True Immortal."

"As expected, the strength [Truth] can display is closely connected to my own strength. The moment I stepped into the Immortal realm, [Truth] underwent corresponding changes. And this is only what can be observed on the surface."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and carefully sensed the changes, nodding with great satisfaction.

At the very bottom of the [Truth] panel were the anchors and bound objects.

There were three established anchors.

The first anchor was the moment Li Fan had initially entered this world. It had been fixed the moment he crossed over, when he was nothing more than a complete mortal.

The second anchor was at the summit of Jieli Mountain, when the Canghai Pearl served as a Foundation Establishment treasure and he possessed late Foundation Establishment cultivation.

The third anchor was also at the summit of Jieli Mountain, with late Foundation Establishment cultivation. However, in that case, [Truth] itself was the Foundation Establishment treasure.

"The order in which the bound objects are displayed on the panel has also undergone a subtle change. It is no longer arranged according to the order in which they were bound. Instead, it seems to be arranged according to their level of status?"

Li Fan looked at the list of bound objects, with [...leg bone] at the very top and "Ten Thousand Peoples Blood Essence" at the very bottom. He pondered thoughtfully.

After a moment, Li Fan closed the panel.

Without much hesitation, he placed the final empty anchor at this point in time.

The fourth [Truth] anchor was established.

Location: Center of the Darkest Star Sea.

Cultivation: Half-Immortal.

"After entering the Half-Immortal realm, it is equivalent to beginning from an entirely new stage. The Immortal Realm has long since been destroyed, and finding the path beyond the Half-Immortal realm will not be easy. My strength will not increase greatly for the time being. It seems that for a very long period, each new reincarnation will need to begin from this anchor..."

"The main objective of this life is to collect and integrate every usable resource within the High Wall, then begin attempting to explore beyond the High Wall."

"Immortal Ancestor, Heavenly Doctor, Grand Mage, Hunger Immortal..."

"Heh heh."

Li Fan let out a cold laugh.

"Right, there is also the Xuanhuang Realm and the Xuan Immortal Ark. The things I could not obtain from these two places before because my strength was insufficient can finally be plundered all at once."

"After becoming a True Immortal, my perspective is naturally different. Perhaps in this life, I will be able to discover even more interesting things within the Xuan Immortal Ark, as well as the true secret behind the relics left behind by Sun Piaomiao."

Li Fan's expression became inscrutable as his gaze drifted toward the distant end of the Darkest Star Sea, where the Xuan Immortal Ark was located.

The Immortal aura had already been fixed at the highest point of the Star Sea. Looking down from above, the Darkest Star Sea, which he had once considered vast and boundless, now appeared to Li Fan like nothing more than a small pond that could be seen at a glance.

"Still, I cannot become too arrogant."

"I should wait until [Truth] finishes charging before I begin acting."

After a long while, Li Fan slowly withdrew his gaze and thought to himself.

"Besides, [Truth]'s charging speed has already become extremely fast."

With that thought, Li Fan did not return to the Xuanhuang Realm. Instead, he remained directly at the center of the Star Sea.

While waiting for [Truth] to finish charging, Li Fan himself did not remain idle.

He used this free time to reorganize everything he had learned and everything he knew from the past.

In particular, the True Immortal seal script.

When he had still been a mortal, after experiencing countless reincarnations, Li Fan had felt that he had already studied the True Immortal seal script almost to its limit. Even though there was still a gap between his understanding and the characters that represented the Great Dao itself, he had believed that the difference was not particularly great.

But now, looking back upon them from the perspective of an Immortal...

Only then did he realize that he had been completely wrong.

Although the patterns he saw were clearly the same, what he saw from the perspective of an Immortal was fundamentally different from what he had seen as a mortal.

Far too many details had been ignored and overlooked when he was still at the mortal level.

Now, when Li Fan recalled the True Immortal seal script he had written in the past, he could not help shaking his head repeatedly.

"It was practically a child's doodle!"

"Come to think of it, those True Immortal seal script characters that seemed to have caused the calamities of the Darkest Star Sea, which appear to have originated from Immortal He Gan's imitation copies, are not particularly beautiful either. If I study them for a little longer, surpassing him will not be difficult."

Li Fan sneered mockingly.

"Based on what I currently know of True Immortal characters, setting aside their specific power and considering only their degree of authenticity, they can be divided into three grades."

"The first consists of the imitation copies of Immortal He Gan. Characters such as [Ancient] and [Chaos] belong to this category."

"The second consists of the authentic True Immortal seal script left behind by True Immortals of the former Immortal Realm. Characters such as [Hunger] and [Return] belong to this category. Whether in terms of beauty or practicality, they are superior to Immortal He Gan's handiwork."

"As for the third grade, these are the characters closest to the Great Dao itself. They were manifested through the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal. [Restrain], [Innate], and [Immortal and Mortal Transformation] are among them..."

Once again, Li Fan's mind conjured the greatest contributor to his advancement into the Half-Immortal realm.

Even after becoming an Immortal, he still felt that this single character contained endless mysteries.

He became completely immersed in it, unable to pull himself away.

A year passed in the blink of an eye.

Anchored in Year Two, when [Truth] completed its charging, Li Fan also awakened from his seclusion.

One year was still insufficient for him to completely reorganize everything he had learned.

Fortunately, he could divide his attention among multiple tasks. While organizing and improving his own understanding, he was not delayed in handling other matters.

After achieving the Half-Immortal realm, the first thing Li Fan naturally wanted to do was meet some old acquaintances, the Heavenly Doctor and the Immortal Ancestor.

Li Fan had already vaguely locked onto the locations of these two Supreme experts within the Star Sea.

Just as expected, they were diligently wandering through the Remnant World Great Wall beneath the High Wall, gathering resources for the revival of the Heavenly Law Realm.

A flicker of light passed through Li Fan's eyes.

With a single step, he crossed half of the star sea.

He first arrived before the Heavenly Doctor.

It did not give the Heavenly Doctor any chance to notice. A silver halo descended directly from above, striking down upon him. It was none other than one of the ninety-nine immortal formation rings that Li Fan had previously refined using the Final Dissociation Disc.

The Heavenly Doctor was traveling through the many remnant worlds, searching for suitable materials, when he suddenly felt his vision blur.

When he regained his senses, the vast and desolate scenery of the Remnant World Great Wall, filled with endless deathly silence, had inexplicably vanished.

What appeared before the Heavenly Doctor was instead a world brimming with vitality, and one that he was extremely familiar with.

The Heavenly Law Realm!

Cloudwater Heavenly Palace, Heavenly Law Pavilion, Heavenly Sword Valley...

Looking at the homeland within this world, the homeland he had yearned for day and night yet had long since been separated from by life and death, the Heavenly Doctor suddenly froze.

He descended upon the earth and personally reached out to touch every blade of grass and every tree. His cloudy eyes were filled with nostalgia and disbelief.

But worthy of being the Heavenly Doctor, he quickly awakened from the beautiful dreamscape Li Fan had woven for him.

Or rather, Li Fan had deliberately left behind a flaw.

Because at this moment, within the Heavenly Law Realm before the Heavenly Doctor, there was not a single person!

Only the newly born "world" itself existed.

Nothing else!

The Heavenly Doctor's expression gradually became solemn. Countless golden threads shot out from his body as he attempted to comprehend the truth behind this inexplicable scene that had suddenly appeared before him.

But what shocked him was that his golden thread probing technique, which had always worked without fail in the past, became dim inch by inch shortly after leaving his body.

They dissipated in midair.

The Heavenly Doctor took a deep breath and forcibly suppressed the turmoil in his heart. While slowly flying through the realm, he carefully searched every corner of the Heavenly Law Realm.

Yet he could not find the slightest flaw.

And apart from him, there was not a single other living creature.

After repeating this process three times, the Heavenly Doctor finally became certain of what had happened to him.

The shock on his face could no longer be suppressed. Even the Heavenly Doctor's aura began to become somewhat disordered.

The scene before him was indeed what he had always dreamed of. Yet the Heavenly Doctor understood that all of this could never possibly be real.

It was merely a false image created to trap and confuse him.

The Heavenly Doctor attempted to escape from this illusion.

Yet no matter how much time passed, all his efforts remained futile.

The Heavenly Doctor looked toward the sky and opened his mouth slightly, seemingly wanting to say something.

But in the end, he made no sound.

There was no trace of surrender or despair on his aged face. Instead, his gaze became increasingly resolute.

This time, he did not wander around aimlessly.

Instead, he stood suspended in the void and began carefully observing the "illusion" surrounding him.

The Heavenly Doctor's mind became immersed in comprehending the "world" before his eyes.

At times, his brows furrowed tightly. At other times, realization dawned upon him.

No one knew how much time had passed when a voice suddenly sounded beside his ear.

"Can you understand it?"

It seemed like a question, yet it also sounded like mockery.

Like a sudden clap of thunder, it instantly awakened the Heavenly Doctor.

A figure dressed in white had appeared beside him at some unknown point.

It was a slender young man who looked at him with a faint, enigmatic smile.

Not the slightest trace of aura could be sensed from his body, yet his eyes seemed like an endless and boundless Star Sea, impossible to fathom.

Even the Heavenly Doctor, who had already reached the Supreme realm, the absolute limit that the Star Sea could contain, could not help but feel his heart issue frantic warnings the moment this young man appeared.

Suppressing the terror in his heart, the Heavenly Doctor cupped his hands and replied without arrogance or humility, "Senior, I can understand one or two parts of it."

The young man was none other than a phantom projection that Li Fan had constructed within the formation.

He smiled slightly.

"Not bad. I did not expect that, even with the vitality of the lower realm having withered to such an extent, someone like you could still emerge."

"Very impressive!"

Li Fan clapped his hands, seemingly full of admiration.

"When I awoke, I discovered that my place of seclusion had suddenly been surrounded by the High Wall. I originally intended to come here and investigate, but unexpectedly discovered the two of you..."

"This world was created by me for you. What do you think? Does it suit your wishes?"

These few short sentences contained an astonishing amount of information.

The Heavenly Doctor's thoughts raced as he analyzed the unfathomable young man before him.

Naturally, the Heavenly Doctor could not possibly judge the other's strength based on his apparent age.

"He can trap me without making a sound. And that invisible pressure..."

"The lower realm. Awakened."

"He should undoubtedly be a True Immortal."

"Not the one who Comprehending the Dao within the Immortal Ruins."

"When did another True Immortal appear in this Star Sea?"

The Heavenly Doctor's heart was filled with doubt and alarm.
