Next

Chapter 1407: Xuanhuang Made Into A Slice

"Moreover, the Heavenly Law Realm was destroyed a very long time ago. Even my own memories of it have become somewhat hazy. Yet this person before me can actually reconstruct it..."

"And even directly states that it was created for me? Does he truly possess the power to see through the hearts of others?"

"The methods of a True Immortal are truly unfathomable."

In an instant, countless thoughts flashed through the Heavenly Doctor's mind.

In the end, they all turned into a bitter smile.

"Senior, why bother playing..."

However, the young True Immortal before him seemed to truly be able to see through his thoughts. He merely raised his hand slightly, interrupting the Heavenly Doctor's words.

"That this Venerable plays tricks on you should be considered your honor."

"However..."

"If you do not like it, then destroy it."

The Heavenly Doctor saw the faint smile that had previously lingered on the young True Immortal's face instantly disappear.

His gaze became incomparably indifferent.

It was genuine indifference, the kind that regarded all things as mere ants.

It was even several times colder than the attitude the Heavenly Doctor himself had shown toward the countless beings of the Xuanhuang Realm.

The sense of intimacy that had never truly existed between the two, despite their earlier conversation and laughter, also vanished.

The Heavenly Doctor felt as if he had suddenly seen an unfathomably deep chasm stretching across the space between them.

On the other side of that chasm, the young True Immortal stood upon land that continuously rose beneath his feet until it towered into the clouds.

The young True Immortal stood high above, looking down upon the Heavenly Doctor.

As if an ancient primordial beast had locked onto him, the Heavenly Doctor's heart suddenly skipped a beat.

Then he saw the young True Immortal wave his hand.

The homeland he had dreamed of day and night, the Heavenly Law Realm before him, instantly crumbled inch by inch into ashes.

Even though he knew in his heart that the Heavenly Law Realm before him was false.

Even though he knew that the person standing before him was a True Immortal.

When he watched the Heavenly Law Realm being destroyed once again before his very eyes, the Heavenly Doctor could not help but develop the urge to fight that lofty young True Immortal to the death.

But ultimately, it was merely an impulse born from a moment of heated emotion.

Moreover, it seemed that the young True Immortal had once again sensed the thoughts in the Heavenly Doctor's mind.

Within the void left behind by the destruction of the Heavenly Law Realm, the scene suddenly changed again.

It was none other than the present Xuanhuang Realm.

However, unlike the scene within the Heavenly Law Realm, countless figures moved about within the Xuanhuang Realm.

They appeared to be genuine living beings, cultivating and living within the world.

The young True Immortal smiled once more, and in the Heavenly Doctor's eyes, the barrier between himself and the True Immortal instantly disappeared.

As he walked across the land of the Xuanhuang Realm, he casually conversed with the Heavenly Doctor, who was being forced to follow behind him.

"Originally, you did not have the qualifications to speak with me."

"I would not have any interest in conversing with any mortal either. Even if you have already stood at the peak of the lower realm, so long as you have not taken that final step, this remains true."

The Heavenly Doctor's expression changed slightly. He opened his mouth, seemingly wanting to argue.

But in the end, he could only say helplessly, "This junior naturally understands."

The young True Immortal nodded, seemingly quite satisfied with the Heavenly Doctor's response.

Then he sighed softly and repeated with deep emotion, "Originally."

"But back then, far too many True Immortals died in the great catastrophe. How many survived throughout the entire mortal world? It has truly become far too lonely."

"Not good. Not good."

Li Fan clicked his tongue in lament.

"Therefore, for existences like you who have the possibility of becoming my kind, I will slightly change my attitude. Perhaps I might even lend you a hand and help you break through the boundary between immortals and mortals..."

"It is not impossible."

After Li Fan finished speaking, the Heavenly Doctor's heart once again trembled uncontrollably.

Before he could determine whether the young True Immortal's words were sincere or merely casual teasing, Li Fan pointed toward the Xuanhuang Realm manifested by the immortal formation and said calmly:

"Your method is quite ingenious among mortals. But in my eyes..."

"It is truly laughable."

"Thousands of years of painstaking effort. If you had spent it cultivating yourself, perhaps you would have already ascended to the immortal realm. Once you became an immortal, what could possibly remain beyond your reach?"

Watching the young True Immortal's disdainful and domineering bearing, the Heavenly Doctor wanted to speak several times.

But when he thought of the True Immortal's cultivation, he swallowed every word.

The young True Immortal, however, had no intention of letting him off so easily on this matter.

Li Fan's expression became enigmatic, his tone distant and indistinct as he slowly said:

"Bringing the dead back to life, how could something like that be so difficult?"

"Even creating something from nothing requires nothing more than a single thought!"

His white robes fluttered as Li Fan appeared ghostlike before the Heavenly Doctor.

He extended his right hand and gently pressed it against the top of the Heavenly Doctor's white-haired head.

Boom!

The vivid Xuanhuang Realm before the Heavenly Doctor's eyes suddenly froze.

After pausing for a brief moment, it rapidly shrank.

In the blink of an eye, it transformed into a palm-sized, ice-sealed crystal.

It was as if they had returned once more to the void of the Star Sea, with endless darkness surrounding them.

The Heavenly Doctor merely stared blankly at the "Xuanhuang Crystal" before him.

Trembling, he reached out and picked up the Xuanhuang Crystal.

It was merely a few inches in size, yet it seemed to genuinely contain an entire world within it.

As he touched this sacred crystal, the Heavenly Doctor faintly sensed something.

He did not need to rely on the Xuanhuang Realm in reality. Even with nothing more than this crystal before him, which contained an entire world, the Heavenly Law Realm could be brought back to life.

"Such methods..."

The Heavenly Doctor's mind was in complete turmoil.

But Li Fan's voice once again sounded beside his ear.

"The temperaments of True Immortals differ from one another."

"The one guarding outside the present Xuanhuang Realm..."

"Heh. She will not be as friendly as I am. In her eyes, even if she were the only one left alive in this world, it would not matter."

The Heavenly Doctor recalled that True Immortal he had encountered within the Immortal Ruins, the one who had disregarded the lives of all living beings in the lower realm Star Sea and nearly annihilated the entire Star Sea while comprehending the Dao.

He could not help but faintly agree with Li Fan's words.

"Therefore, if you truly wish to save your homeland, then go and become a True Immortal!"

"This world crystal will be considered a gift to you."

Li Fan's voice gradually faded.

After a wave of distortion, the scene before the Heavenly Doctor's eyes returned to the endless Remnant World Great Wall.

The young True Immortal had already disappeared.

Only the Xuanhuang Realm crystal in his hand remained, reminding the Heavenly Doctor that everything that had just happened had not been an illusion.

The Heavenly Doctor once again examined the miniature Xuanhuang Realm in his hand.

It seemed to have resumed its operation.

The countless living beings within the world continued to live their lives without noticing anything.

They even completely ignored the line of large characters floating across the sky.

"By the way, my patience is not particularly good."

"One thousand days is the deadline. If by then you still show no signs of breaking through..."

"Hehehe."

The young True Immortal's incomparably cold laughter echoed within the Heavenly Doctor's heart.

The Heavenly Doctor's breathing suddenly stopped.

It was as if he were once again facing that young True Immortal's incomparably cold gaze.

Clenching the Xuanhuang Realm crystal tightly, the Heavenly Doctor looked at the countdown in the sky and felt a weight he had never experienced before.

From that brief contact just now, the Heavenly Doctor had already gained a profound understanding of this young True Immortal's unpredictable temperament and unfathomable methods.

Not to mention the immortal formation that had suddenly trapped him, whose flaws he could not find at all.

Just this ability to create an entire Xuanhuang Realm from nothing and then present it in crystallized form was already beyond what the current Heavenly Doctor could imagine.

"One thousand days to become a True Immortal?"

The Heavenly Doctor furrowed his brows tightly and began considering his options.

"Perhaps my only hope is that invisible High Wall..."

The Heavenly Doctor could not help looking toward the nearby empty void.

Over the long years, through his unremitting comprehension, he had already come extremely close to truly understanding the mysteries of the High Wall.

It was only because of the inexplicable warnings he had sensed, as well as his efforts to revive the Heavenly Law Realm, that he had not devoted all his attention to comprehending the High Wall.

But now, it seemed that he had no other choice.

At a time when the Heavenly Doctor was still struggling with his decision, Li Fan's true body had already arrived before the Immortal Ancestor.

Even within the complete Immortal-grade illusion formation, the Immortal Ancestor was still able to quickly detect its flaws and continuously search for a way to break out. Li Fan could not help but frown slightly.

'As expected of the Heavenly Demon. Its resistance to illusion formations is even stronger than the Heavenly Doctor's.'

As for the Heavenly Doctor, Li Fan had naturally withdrawn the Immortal-grade formation's restrictions himself.

There was little practical meaning in simply toying with this Star Sea Supreme.

Allowing him to display his proper value was what Li Fan preferred to see.

And things were indeed developing as Li Fan had anticipated. Under the deterrence of the Immortal-grade formation and the complete Xuanhuang Realm crystal, the Heavenly Doctor had temporarily been deceived.

The Immortal-grade formation had naturally been created by the Final Dissociation Disc.

As for that Xuanhuang Realm crystal...

It was a creation formed from the feedback power that erupted within Li Fan's dantian in his previous life when the Xuanhuang Realm ascended into an Immortal Domain after the Sitting Heaven Art was activated.

Originally, during the ascension process, the vast feedback power had already been equivalent to dozens of times the total energy of the Xuanhuang Realm at its ascension threshold.

And after the Xuanhuang Realm completely ascended into an Immortal Domain, the feedback power within Li Fan's dantian had become even more astonishing.

At present, an ever shifting mass of Xuanhuang mist floated within Li Fan's dantian.

It was like images continuously flickering across an Immortal Realm copper coin.

Within the mist, light and shadows constantly changed. Every instant, it seemed as though a Xuanhuang Realm was being born anew.

Of course, Li Fan knew this was probably only an illusion.

The reason the Xuanhuang mist continuously flickered like images passing rapidly before one's eyes was because the total quantity of feedback power was simply too enormous. Arranged sequentially, something static had seemingly become dynamic.

Although the quantity was extraordinarily vast, it was still nowhere near the realm of the 'infinite.'

According to Li Fan's own estimation, the energy currently gathered within his dantian was roughly equivalent to one hundred thousand times the total energy of the Xuanhuang Realm under ordinary conditions.

It was an enormous power that even Li Fan found completely inconceivable.

At first, he had even experienced a terrifying illusion that the Xuanhuang mist might suddenly explode inside his body.

However, after his initial shock, Li Fan discovered that this was actually not much different from the infinite power of the Star Sea.

It was even somewhat inferior.

Quantity could not accumulate indefinitely and transform into a qualitative change.

Gradually, Li Fan stopped paying much attention to it.

He separated a fragment of it and transformed it into a Xuanhuang Realm crystal, giving it to the Heavenly Doctor as a small gift.

To Li Fan, this did not even qualify as a simple gesture.

Moreover, in order to maintain control over the Heavenly Doctor at all times, Li Fan had secretly concealed a trace of residual Immortal-grade formation power within this Xuanhuang fragment.

It could erupt again at any moment and trap the Heavenly Doctor.

Li Fan's thoughts returned from the Heavenly Doctor to the Immortal Ancestor within the formation. He was not nearly as casual with him.

Judging from the various signs observed throughout his previous reincarnations, the Immortal Ancestor was very likely the reincarnation of the Xuantian King, after which he had been possessed and controlled by the Heavenly Demon.

The Heavenly Demon was itself an extraordinary existence that had stolen the creations of Heaven and Earth. Its Dao of Heart Demons had fused with the Heavenly Dao of a world. Even in the ancient cultivation world, such an existence was exceedingly rare.

Not to mention the Xuantian King...

As a Half-Immortal himself, Li Fan understood the might of an 'Immortal' far more profoundly.

Thus, he became increasingly cautious.

At the end of his previous life, when the Xuanhuang Realm successfully ascended into an Immortal Domain, the strange behavior displayed by the Immortal Ancestor remained vivid in Li Fan's memory.

How could Li Fan possibly underestimate him?

This was also why, on the Heavenly Doctor's side, only a phantom projection of the Immortal-grade formation had been sent, while here with the Immortal Ancestor, Li Fan's true body personally remained in command.

Li Fan's expression was solemn. In response to the Immortal Ancestor's unique situation, the Final Dissociation Disc was also gradually fine-tuning the Immortal-grade formation.

At this moment, the world within the Immortal Ancestor's eyes had been divided into two halves.

On the left was the Xuanhuang Realm he knew extremely well.

On the right was the homeland of the Heavenly Doctor, Tian Jue, and other venerable figures of the Heavenly Law Realm.

The two worlds flickered like lamps swaying in the wind, constantly changing positions.

Gradually, the previously calm expression on the Immortal Ancestor's face began to show immense pain.

It was as though something was trying to break free from within his body.

Yet the power of this division seemed insufficient.

In the end, it withdrew once again.

Seeing this, Li Fan activated the Immortal-grade formation once more, accelerating the frequency at which the two worlds switched places.

At the same time, he shot out strands of golden threads that silently pierced into the Immortal Ancestor's body.

Li Fan had once witnessed the Heavenly Doctor's method of controlling the Immortal Ancestor.

With his current level of insight, it was not difficult to reverse the process and decipher the method of control.

He simply needed to experiment again and again.

In any case, with the Final Dissociation Disc presiding over the situation, there was no need to fear that the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor would escape.

The constantly shifting dual worlds and the relentless assaults of the golden threads stimulated the Immortal Ancestor, causing him to unleash a heaven-shaking roar.

Yet he was truly like a bird trapped inside a cage.

No matter how desperately he struggled, he could not change his fate.

'Found it.'

After a long while, Li Fan's eyes lit up.

He exerted a slight force and pulled on the golden threads.

At the other end of the golden threads, a transparent figure was slowly dragged out.

The moment the transparent figure emerged, the Immortal Ancestor's physical body seemed to lose its foundation.

It immediately collapsed to the ground.

After leaving the Immortal Ancestor's body, the legendary Heavenly Demon also became calm.

It silently floated in midair.

It seemed to have fallen into a deep sleep.

Yet the Heavenly Demon itself possessed even greater resistance to the illusion formation than it had while possessing the Immortal Ancestor.

The Final Dissociation Disc rapidly operated, analyzing the Heavenly Demon's flaws.

No matter how extraordinary it was, it was ultimately still a mortal existence.

It would not take long before its defenses were finally breached.

Li Fan, however, first examined the Immortal Ancestor's body.

'It possesses its own independent consciousness.'

'It was simply suppressed by the Heavenly Demon for far too long, leaving it extremely weak. For the time being, there are no signs that it will awaken.'

'Then let me take advantage of its weakness and investigate it.'

'Let me see the true identity of the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor, the existence who has suppressed the Xuanhuang Realm for several thousand years.'

With a trace of anticipation, Li Fan manipulated the illusion formation.

The scenery within the formation began to evolve on its own according to the Immortal Ancestor's memories.

On a vast plain, cooking smoke slowly rose into the sky.

"Goudan, come home for dinner!"

Next

Chapter 1408: The Final Answer To The Mystery Of The Immortal Ancestor

The thin, frail-looking little boy appeared to be no more than seven or eight years old. He was lying on the roof of a thatched hut, gazing up at the sky.

Hearing his mother's call, he was just about to get up when he accidentally lost his balance and fell straight from the roof.

Fortunately, he landed directly on a haystack below and escaped injury.

Goudan seemed to be accustomed to such things. He patted himself off, straightened his clothes, and ran toward home.

At the dinner table, his mother served Goudan food while scolding him.

"Did you go play at the eastern end of the village again? I told you to stay away from that madman!"

Goudan wolfed down his food and said indifferently, "I think Crazy Uncle is a good person. He's not nearly as frightening as everyone says."

Goudan's father was accustomed to keeping silent and had not spoken a word since the meal began. Upon hearing his son's words, he immediately put down his chopsticks and slapped Goudan on the head.

"What the hell do you know? Do you know what happened to all those people in the village who refused to believe in such things? Stay away from him when I tell you to!"

Under his father's iron fist, Goudan did not dare argue. Yet he was still unconvinced inside and simply lowered his head to continue eating.

After that, Goudan indeed stopped openly going to Crazy Uncle's house.

But whenever he had the opportunity, Goudan would secretly bring some tasty food and go visit Crazy Uncle.

"Crazy Uncle" was the name Goudan had given him himself. The villagers simply called him the madman.

He was also a member of the village. His parents had died when he was young, and he had grown up eating whatever food the villagers were willing to give him. When he was young, he had been perfectly normal. But as he gradually entered middle age, he became increasingly deranged.

He often muttered strange words.

Things like, "I saw many versions of myself," and "I am not myself at all." Sometimes, he would even point at someone in the village and scream in terror, "Aren't you already dead?"

Strangely enough, eight or nine times out of ten, the villagers whom he cursed would suddenly die violent deaths not long afterward.

Immortals and gods truly existed in this world. The villagers firmly believed that the madman must somehow be connected to those mysterious and unfathomable powers. To avoid bringing further disaster upon themselves, the villagers decided to join together and drive him away.

However, there seemed to be something in the village that he cared about deeply. After sensing the villagers' hostility, the madman suddenly erupted and attacked them.

Goudan did not know exactly how many people died that day. Afterward, however, the villagers chose to avoid the madman entirely and keep their distance from him.

Only fearless youngsters like Goudan dared approach him.

The madman spent almost all his time in his family's dilapidated ancestral house. The place was dark and gloomy, cut off from sunlight. He would curl up in a corner and constantly mutter something under his breath.

At first, Goudan had accidentally wandered inside while playing hide-and-seek with his friends. The gloomy and terrifying atmosphere inside had frightened him.

However, when he heard the madman's muttering, something about it deeply captivated him.

Although he could not understand what those strange syllables meant, he found himself inexplicably drawn to them.

Only when the shouts of his companions repeatedly came from outside did Goudan reluctantly leave.

From that day onward, whenever Goudan's mind became hazy and distracted, the madman's murmuring would echo in his ears.

Finally, one day, Goudan could no longer resist the temptation in his heart.

He returned to the madman's little dark house.

This time, he even brought a roast duck as a polite gift.

Perhaps the aroma of the food briefly awakened the madman's sanity. The madman stopped muttering and unceremoniously snatched the roast duck away, devouring it ravenously.

After eating his fill, the madman, during one of his lucid periods, merely warned Goudan to stay away from him.

How could Goudan, having been tricked into giving away a roast duck, possibly be willing to leave?

He stubbornly stayed. He even began asking the madman about the strange phrases he was always muttering.

The madman remained silent at first.

Then, from among the ruins of the house, he rummaged out a pile of wooden tablets.

He told Goudan that these were ancestral tablets.

He himself did not know what he was saying when he went mad. However, this strange illness had accompanied their Xuanyuan Clan since ancient times.

Fortunately, only one person in each generation would suffer from this strange affliction. As the Xuanyuan clan gradually multiplied and spread, very few members still remembered this hereditary illness.

"Let this curse end here."

The madman placed the ancestral tablets on the ground in their proper order.

When it was his own turn, however, he placed only an empty wooden tablet.

Then, under Goudan's astonished gaze, every ancestral tablet spontaneously burst into flames without any wind. In the cramped wooden house, they were reduced to ashes in an instant.

Goudan then asked about the inexplicable deaths of the villagers.

Crazy Uncle revealed a pale smile.

"Deaths that are destined to happen. What do they have to do with me? When the rooster crows, the whole world becomes bright. Does that mean the great sun in the sky is really brought out by the rooster's crowing?"

"Goudan, you must understand. Everything in this world has already happened countless times. What is happening now is merely another rehearsal."

"For example, you..."

Crazy Uncle stared intently at Goudan.

Then, slowly, an expression of utter terror appeared on his face.

After the fear came, he once again began to lose his mind.

Before completely losing his sanity, he casually threw Goudan out of the door.

From that day onward, Goudan vaguely believed that some enormous secret was hidden within Crazy Uncle.

Although young Goudan could not understand what Crazy Uncle had said, he firmly believed that the secret hidden within him was very likely connected to the legendary immortals.

For this reason, Goudan went to great lengths to find out his real name from his father.

Xuanyuan Sheng.

Everyone in the village was surnamed Xuanyuan, but Goudan still thought Crazy Uncle's name was especially impressive.

As the days passed, Crazy Uncle's madness grew increasingly severe.

Even bringing food to comfort him no longer worked.

Goudan could only climb onto the roof of Crazy Uncle's house, close his eyes, and listen to the increasingly bizarre sounds coming from below.

Every time he listened to Crazy Uncle's eerie voice, which sounded terrifying to outsiders, Goudan slept exceptionally soundly.

This continued until Goudan was nine years old.

One night, a thunderous explosion suddenly erupted throughout the village.

The villagers were awakened and discovered that the explosion had come from Xuanyuan Sheng's house.

By the time everyone arrived, they found that, for some unknown reason, the entire place had already been reduced to a flat wasteland.

Crazy Uncle's death did not cause much disturbance.

In the eyes of most members of the Xuanyuan Clan, the disappearance of Xuanyuan Sheng, the troublemaker, was actually a good thing.

Only Goudan was deeply saddened by Crazy Uncle's death.

While no one was around, he secretly went to the site of the incident and searched carefully.

Unfortunately, apart from a single charred ancestral tablet, he found nothing else.

The wooden tablet had been burned until only one corner remained.

Goudan could not read, so he took it to an educated adult in the village and asked what was written on it.

Only then did he learn that it was the character "Hong."

"Xuanyuan Hong."

Goudan had an excellent memory. He vaguely remembered that when Crazy Uncle had arranged the ancestral tablets on the ground in their proper order, this particular tablet seemed to have been solemnly placed in the very first position.

Knowing that it was an ancestral tablet, Goudan did not dare keep it for himself.

After respectfully kneeling and paying his respects, Goudan set fire to the remaining wooden tablet and burned it completely.

Xuanyuan Village had lived in peace with the outside world since ancient times.

After Xuanyuan Sheng's death, the village returned to its former tranquility.

Goudan gradually grew older, and the villagers stopped calling him "Goudan."

Instead, they began calling him by his real name, Xuanyuan Tuo.

Xuanyuan Tuo's life followed the same path as everyone else in the village.

Planting, farming, harvesting. Getting married, taking a wife, having children.

Yet during his spare time, the strange murmuring of Crazy Uncle from his childhood would occasionally return to his mind.

No matter how much time passed, those mysterious sounds remained deeply engraved in his mind.

They never faded.

As Xuanyuan Tuo grew older, his life seemed to be moving toward its predetermined end.

Until the year Xuanyuan Tuo turned fifty.

Xuanyuan Tuo's son died from an illness.

His wife could not endure the pain of watching her own child die before her and passed away not long afterward.

In less than half a year, Xuanyuan Tuo lost two of his closest family members.

He was devastated.

He felt that life was no longer worth living and repeatedly thought about ending his own life.

But at the critical moment, the mysterious melody that had accompanied him throughout his life sounded once again.

It scattered the dark thoughts filling his mind.

Xuanyuan Tuo sat there in a daze for several days, and the grief in his heart gradually subsided.

He had no intention of marrying again or having more children.

Instead, another thought arose within his heart like a tiny flame.

Then, like a wildfire spreading across the wilderness, it grew uncontrollably.

Having personally witnessed the deaths of one loved one after another, Xuanyuan Tuo had gained a profound understanding of how fragile life was.

He was already more than fifty years old.

Perhaps it would not be long before he followed the dead into the grave.

The thought filled Xuanyuan Tuo with immense fear.

After going without food or water and sitting motionless for two days, Xuanyuan Tuo finally made his decision.

He would cultivate immortality and seek eternal life.

For an old farmer approaching fifty, seeking immortality and asking about the Dao seemed like nothing more than a complete joke.

Xuanyuan Tuo himself knew how absurd his decision was.

Therefore, he did not tell anyone.

He simply packed his belongings in silence, left Xuanyuan Village, and embarked alone upon the path of seeking immortality.

Fortunately, the Immortal Dao was flourishing in this era, and the immortals were not people who secluded themselves from the world. On the contrary, in almost every major city, one could occasionally see immortals soaring through the skies.

After enduring all manner of ridicule, Xuanyuan Tuo finally managed to find out the location of the nearest cultivation sect.

Guiyuan Sect!

After traveling through countless hardships, Xuanyuan Tuo finally arrived outside the Guiyuan Sect.

To his astonishment, several hundred people like him had gathered there. Most were children accompanied by attendants. Someone like him, with one foot already in the grave yet still dreaming of cultivating immortality, was truly one of a kind.

After making some inquiries, Xuanyuan Tuo finally learned why everyone had gathered outside the mountain gate. It was because the Guiyuan Sect had not yet reached the time for accepting disciples. The sect protecting formation was currently activated and enveloped the entire mountain range. This immortal paradise was completely inaccessible to mortals.

Xuanyuan Tuo then asked when the next disciple acceptance ceremony would be held. The answer was three years from now.

He had originally intended to obediently wait outside the mountain gate like everyone else. Yet for some reason, when Xuanyuan Tuo looked at the sect's protective formation, which everyone else feared like a tiger, he vaguely did not find it particularly frightening.

With his heart stirring restlessly, the usually bold Xuanyuan Tuo decided to take the risk and try.

As it turned out, things really were just as he had expected. He encountered no danger along the way and somehow, almost without realizing it, successfully walked all the way to the entrance of the Guiyuan Sect.

When he met an immortal cultivator, Xuanyuan Tuo was overjoyed. He wanted to join the immortal sect, only to be mercilessly ridiculed by the other party.

Fortunately, just as he was about to be forcibly driven away, an immortal cultivator surnamed Xu, perhaps taking pity on him, gave him a book containing an introductory cultivation technique.

The immortal cultivator also promised that if Xuanyuan Tuo could cultivate spiritual energy within a year, he could come back and find him.

Although this journey had not resulted in him formally becoming a disciple of the immortal sect, he had at least obtained an immortal cultivation technique.

Xuanyuan Tuo carefully kept the book close to his body, then bypassed the people who were still waiting outside the mountain gate. For the sake of safety, he even traveled a long distance to a mortal town farther away.

Xuanyuan Tuo himself could not read. In order to cultivate the technique, whenever he had the opportunity along the way, he asked others to teach him.

Although he was already old, his mind was not dull in the slightest. By the time he safely reached his destination, Xuanyuan Tuo could already recognize most of the characters in the introductory cultivation technique.

Without an immortal master to guide him, Xuanyuan Tuo could only study it by himself.

The Immortal Dao was profound and mysterious. Even an introductory cultivation technique was not something that an ignorant old mountain farmer who had never seen the wider world could successfully cultivate on his own.

Moreover, Xuanyuan Tuo was already at the age when one understood the Mandate of Heaven. He had long since passed the optimal age for cultivation.

On top of that, he still had to earn money during the day to maintain his basic livelihood.

In the blink of an eye, the one year deadline was almost upon him. Yet Xuanyuan Tuo possessed an immortal cultivation technique but had gained absolutely nothing from it.

'Even if I manage to cultivate spiritual energy now, I will not make it back in time.'

Xuanyuan Tuo was almost completely despairing.

'Cultivation is simply far too difficult for ordinary people like us.'

He suddenly recalled the young immortal cultivator he had seen looking up at the city skies a few days earlier. Before even reaching twenty years of age, that young man had already been able to freely ride the wind.

How unfair was the Heavenly Dao!

Despair, jealousy, fear...

Xuanyuan Tuo collapsed onto the ground and gazed up at the heavens.

'Why can people in this world not all cultivate?'

'Why must such restrictions exist between heaven and earth?'

'You damn heavens!'

Such curses were exceedingly commonplace. At every moment, day and night, they were being uttered in countless places throughout the world.

Yet as Xuanyuan Tuo cursed inwardly, the familiar murmuring chant of his Crazy Uncle, Xuanyuan Sheng, once again rang beside his ears.

Only this time, it did not disappear quickly.

Instead, it repeated again and again until it completely filled his mind.

In a daze, Xuanyuan Tuo seemed to return to his childhood.

Within that gloomy little dark room, his Crazy Uncle stood amid a room filled with the memorial tablets of the Xuanyuan ancestors, a ferocious expression on his face.

'It has appeared again! It cannot be burned away, and its lingering spirit refuses to disperse!'

'Then let us die together!'

Suddenly, raging flames erupted from Xuanyuan Sheng's body.

He then charged toward the densely arranged ancestral tablets.

Dark flames gradually spread from Xuanyuan Sheng's body into those memorial tablets.

Amid Xuanyuan Sheng's maniacal laughter, the raging fire gradually devoured everything.

In the end, only one corner of the tablet bearing the name Xuanyuan Hong remained.

And then Xuanyuan Tuo found it!

[Xuanyuan Hong]!

The three characters appeared ghostlike within his mind. Their gilded color gradually seeped with droplets of blood-red.

Then they exploded with a bang!

Xuanyuan Tuo consequently fell unconscious.

When he awoke, he stared blankly at the sky, then looked at himself in the mirror.

Something seemed to have changed.

When he looked back at the introductory cultivation technique bestowed upon him by the immortal cultivator of the Guiyuan Sect, Xuanyuan Tuo suddenly found it incomparably simple and easy to understand.

In fact...

It was almost too crude.

Xuanyuan Tuo was no longer in a hurry to complete his cultivation and return to join the Guiyuan Sect.

Instead, he began pondering the question he had been thinking about before.

'Why can’t everyone in this world cultivate?'

Xuanyuan Tuo naturally knew that all things were born of Heaven and Earth, and that every person was different.

But was there some way to allow everyone in the world to cultivate, regardless of differences in innate talent?

The murmuring voice that had always accompanied him had already ceased to sound. For a time, Xuanyuan Tuo was somewhat unaccustomed to the silence.

At the same time, countless images began continuously surfacing in his mind.

Some were scenes of countless plants and beasts.

Some were faces that were both familiar and unfamiliar.

Others were fragments of conversations whose meanings he could not understand.

'How confident are you that you can accomplish this journey?'

'I can only do everything within my power and leave the rest to fate.'

'That does not sound like something you would say.'

'The difference in strength is simply too great. I can only risk my life in a final struggle.'

'Forget it. Go, then. I do not know whether this will bring fortune or disaster.'

...

Xuanyuan Tuo pressed his fingers against his brow.

It seemed that he had forgotten some extremely important mission.

The chaotic memories could not affect Xuanyuan Tuo's state of mind.

Instead, relying on the countless fragments of memories that had suddenly surged into his mind, Xuanyuan Tuo continued delving deeper into the question he had been pondering.

How could everyone cultivate?

More and more images surfaced in his mind, becoming increasingly chaotic.

Gradually, they pieced together the life of another powerful cultivator known as the Heavenly Doctor Venerable.

Yet through the obsession in his heart, Xuanyuan Tuo forcibly suppressed everything for the time being.

He continued thinking mechanically.

He sat motionless in the courtyard for thirty three days.

On this day, Xuanyuan Tuo, who had become extremely emaciated, with only a thin layer of skin hanging over his bones and looking like a skeleton, suddenly stood up.

His eyes were as brilliant and bright as the stars in the heavens.

He raised his head and gazed at the heavens, murmuring softly.

“I wish to become an immortal. Why should I seek the permission of Heaven? If Heaven refuses to give it to me, then I shall take it myself!”

“To follow Heaven is to remain mortal. To defy Heaven…”

“...is to become Immortal!”

Xuanyuan Tuo's originally hunched chest instantly straightened.

With the body of a mortal, he gazed directly at the vast heavens.

Although he possessed no extraordinary aura whatsoever, he seemed completely undaunted.

He continued murmuring.

“Therefore, the path to immortality is…”

“Absorb the spiritual essence of Heaven and Earth to command its Qi;

“Borrow the wonders of Heaven and Earth to establish the Dao Foundation;

“Glimpse the laws of Heaven and Earth to refine the Golden Core;

“Seize the essence of Heaven and Earth to form the Nascent Soul;

“Draw out the marrow of Heaven and Earth to obtain its soul;

“Sacrifice the Spirit of Heaven and Earth to integrate one's body with the Dao;'

“Defy the Principles of Heaven and Earth to prove Longevity!”

Xuanyuan Tuo spoke faster and faster. By the time he uttered the final sentence, he had exhausted all his strength and collapsed helplessly to the ground.

Yet he did not get up. He simply lay there and burst into laughter.

Far too weak, Xuanyuan Tuo had finally found the Dao he sought, and soon sank into a deep sleep.

The spiritual energy between Heaven and Earth was spontaneously drawn toward him. It surged in torrents, forming a vortex that lifted Xuanyuan Tuo into the air and surrounded him.

Only after a long while did the phenomenon of spiritual energy pouring into his body gradually disappear.

Xuanyuan Tuo slowly descended to the ground. His body, already more than fifty years old, was once again filled with vitality. Although his appearance remained aged, his body now contained a tremendous vitality.

After an unknown amount of time had passed...

While Xuanyuan Tuo was still asleep, his face suddenly convulsed violently.

He abruptly opened his eyes.

Yet there were no pupils within them, only an eerie expanse of white.

His voice had also become even older and deeper.

“The Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao is even more difficult to deal with than I expected.”

“I nearly lost myself completely and was almost unable to recover my original consciousness.”

“Cough, cough. I miscalculated.”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1409: Altering Reality

“Xuanhuang Realm, worthy of being a great realm favored by the Immortal Realm. The power of its Heavenly Dao is far beyond that of the Heavenly Law Realm.”

“This [Heavenly Soul Reincarnation] method completely severs the connection with the former world, leaving behind only a remnant thought to reincarnate. I previously tested it more than ten thousand times in the Heavenly Law Realm and never once failed.”

“Although I had already anticipated that I might encounter some unexpected circumstances in the Xuanhuang Realm, the degree of danger has still far exceeded my expectations.”

“Fifty whole years, and there has still been no awakening...”

It seemed to have taken some time to determine the condition of the current body.

After remaining silent for a long while, the aged voice finally sounded again.

It was filled with uncertainty and suspicion. “Strange. The Heavenly Soul Reincarnation uses the [Heavenly Soul] Great Dao of the Heavenly Law Realm as the sacrifice, allowing me to reincarnate and enter the Xuanhuang Realm, becoming a living being of this world. In this way, I can deceive the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“According to the original plan, after awakening, I would first diagnose the Xuanhuang Realm, then create a new cultivation method, causing chaos to arise from within. But now, my original consciousness has yet to awaken, yet this body has already created a new cultivation method?!”

“Defying the principles of Heaven and Earth to prove longevity...”

The Heavenly Doctor became increasingly suspicious. “This cultivation method is practically identical to what I have been thinking about. In fact, the ingenuity of many of its details is even superior!”

At that moment, as if sensing the Heavenly Doctor's words, Xuanyuan Tuo's eyelids suddenly twitched.

The Heavenly Doctor's voice also began to weaken.

“No. The Heavenly Soul Reincarnation is superior even to the possession method. I have already awakened. Why does this body's consciousness still exist?”

“The Xuanyuan Clan?!”

Amid the Heavenly Doctor's startled cry, Xuanyuan Tuo finally awakened completely from his unconscious state.

The Heavenly Doctor fell completely silent.

After awakening, Xuanyuan Tuo seemed completely unaware of the Heavenly Doctor's existence within his body.

He was simply overjoyed as he examined his cultivation, which had already reached the Qi Condensation Realm.

After a long while, Xuanyuan Tuo rose into the air on the wind. Amid the startled cries of the mortals in the city, he shot into the sky and departed.

Within the Immortal Formation, light and shadows shifted.

Next, relying on the faint, inexplicable sensations within his mind, Xuanyuan Tuo continuously searched the Xuanhuang Realm for rare cultivation treasures.

In merely half a year, he established his Dao Foundation with [Xuantian Myriad Spirits], achieved the Golden Core Realm through [Heavenly Evolution Method], and advanced to the Nascent Soul Realm with [Primordial Origin Cave Heaven].

Then, relying on the transformation of Heaven and Earth's [Myriad Phenomena], he stepped into the Soul Transformation Realm.

His cultivation advanced by leaps and bounds, unstoppable.

It was not until he reached the peak of Soul Transformation that he finally stopped at the step of [Sacrifice the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Integrate with the Dao].

There were countless Spirits of Heaven and Earth within the Xuanhuang Realm.

How to choose one became Xuanyuan Tuo's greatest difficulty.

Before the creation of the new cultivation method, cultivators generally regarded the Spirits of Heaven and Earth as projections of the will of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. They were therefore regarded as ideal mediums for comprehending the laws of the Heavenly Dao.

Some cultivators had indeed attempted to capture Spirits of Heaven and Earth. At most, however, they could only use them to assist in comprehending the Dao. There had never been a method of refining and transforming the power of a Spirit of Heaven and Earth into one's own cultivation.

“The Spirits of Heaven and Earth are born after receiving a heavenly mandate. After descending into the world in the form of human phantoms, although they appear to be independent individuals, they remain connected to the great network of the Heavenly Dao.”

“The new cultivation method I envisioned is precisely meant to exploit this connection and steal the power of Heaven and Earth.”

“Every Spirit of Heaven and Earth corresponds to a particular law of Heaven and Earth. My target is...”

Xuanyuan Tuo looked toward the Xuanhuang Heaven and Earth.

The scenery within the realm became completely blurred, transforming into enormous interwoven networks extending both horizontally and vertically.

His gaze swept across the vast network as Xuanyuan Tuo carefully searched for his target.

No one knew how much time had passed.

Xuanyuan Tuo's gaze suddenly froze.

Complex emotions flashed across his face.

There was confusion, as well as surprise.

“For some reason, I feel an inexplicable sense of familiarity.”

“Moreover, the law corresponding to that Spirit of Heaven and Earth does not seem to have originated naturally within the Xuanhuang Realm. Compared with the other laws, it seems somewhat out of place...”

“Could it have come from the upper realm?”

Xuanyuan Tuo remained silent for a long time before finally making his decision.

After spending another half a year and employing every means at his disposal, he finally drew out this extraordinary Spirit of Heaven and Earth.

Its entire body was enveloped in golden light, its aura sacred and inviolable. It looked down at Xuanyuan Tuo below as though gazing upon an insignificant ant.

“Even if you originated from the Immortal Realm, after merging into the Xuanhuang Realm, you have already fallen into the mortal world. You are about to die, yet you remain completely unaware!”

A year ago, Xuanyuan Tuo had still possessed the mentality of an old farmer who had lived for more than fifty years.

But as his cultivation and realm continued to rise throughout this year, while more and more memories continuously emerged within his mind, Xuanyuan Tuo's bearing also became increasingly extraordinary.

Even those with profound connections to the upper realm were no longer worthy of his attention. He regarded them merely as prey.

Xuanyuan Tuo struck down with one palm.

The space surrounding the golden Spirit of Heaven and Earth instantly froze. Countless scenes from the Xuanhuang Realm transformed into sinister phantoms and surged forward.

It was as though a world had been created around the golden Spirit of Heaven and Earth, after which countless other Spirits of Heaven and Earth evolved and surrounded it.

Xuanyuan Tuo was like a commander leading three armies, sitting firmly in the center without moving and allowing his subordinates to fight.

Although the golden Spirit of Heaven and Earth was powerful, after Xuanyuan Tuo used unfathomable methods to sever its connection with the Xuanhuang Heaven and Earth, it ultimately became a tree without roots and water without a source.

In the end, it was slowly worn down to death.

It transformed into a streak of golden light and was swallowed by Xuanyuan Tuo.

“Spirit of Heaven and Earth, [Golden Authority]!”

The dazzling golden radiance gradually receded, and Xuanyuan Tuo exhaled slowly.

Golden Authority had existed in the Xuanhuang Realm since the very beginning of its creation. It had been created by an Immortal and possessed the ability to govern and create the myriad things of the Xuanhuang Realm. Originally, it had been deeply concealed within the Xuanhuang Realm, making it impossible for mortals to detect.

However, after the destruction of the Immortal Realm, the ancient formation that had enabled the Golden Authority Law to function had also been destroyed.

Only then did Xuanyuan Tuo discover its traces.

After integrating with the Dao through [Golden Authority], Xuanyuan Tuo's strength underwent another qualitative leap.

Ordinary Longevity Immortal cultivators were absolutely no match for him. Only the Sect Masters of the Ten Great Immortal Sects, with their profound foundations, might be able to pose some threat to him.

Xuanyuan Tuo knew that it was time to begin his path of transmitting the Dao.

It was also the final step.

“Use human momentum to seize heavenly momentum.”

“Use the Human Dao to determine the Heavenly Dao.”

“When more and more cultivators throughout the world cultivate my new method, and eventually Heaven and Earth are overturned, so that this becomes the only cultivation method under Heaven, that will be the moment I prove the Dao!”

“From this day onward, I will turn the Xuanhuang Realm upside down!”

Carrying this conviction, Xuanyuan Tuo arrived at Congyun Marsh in the borderlands of the Xuanhuang Realm.

There, he began his first act of transmitting the Dao.

When he manifested the Dharma and Heaven Manifestation and proclaimed to the world, [Defy the Principles of Heaven and Earth to Prove Longevity], it did not actually cause much of a stir.

The new cultivation method was simply too shocking and unbelievable. Very few cultivators were willing to believe him and abandon their existing cultivation to start over.

Xuanyuan Tuo did not mind.

He simply continued spreading the new cultivation method throughout the lands of the Xuanhuang Realm.

When the first batch of mortals who had been unable to cultivate successfully became cultivators, they became Xuanyuan Tuo's most fanatical believers.

They did not know Xuanyuan Tuo's name. They respectfully addressed him only as the [Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor].

The believers spontaneously established the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, helping one another and spreading the new cultivation method.

Within only a few years, the new cultivation method had already developed into a prairie fire, sweeping across the entire world.

Such a drastic change naturally attracted the attention of the lofty Ten Great Immortal Sects.

Slaughter ensued.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had only just begun to take shape. How could it possibly contend with the Ten Great Immortal Sects that had dominated the Xuanhuang Realm for thousands of years?

The Ten Great Immortal Sects did not even need to act personally. Their subordinate sects alone had already inflicted heavy losses upon the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

However, cultivators of the new method possessed advantages that old method cultivators could never match.

First, the cultivation speed of the new method was simply too fast. Even if they were slaughtered almost to extinction, it would not take long before large numbers of new method cultivators appeared once again.

Second, once new method cultivators reached the Nascent Soul realm, their ability to preserve their lives was simply too powerful. As long as their souls did not suffer fatal damage, their physical bodies could recover instantly no matter how severe their injuries were.

With these two factors combined, although many new method cultivators died, most of them were cultivators from the Qi Condensation Realm to the Golden Core Realm.

As for those at the Nascent Soul Realm and above, they were instead able to survive time and time again.

Through their continuous struggles, their experience became increasingly rich.

On the other hand, among the cultivators of the old cultivation system, raising a qualified combatant required an enormous amount of time and resources. Every death meant one fewer.

As one side declined while the other grew stronger, the situation gradually began to turn around.

Moreover, Xuanyuan Tuo could sense that even within the Ten Sects, some people had already begun cultivating the new cultivation method. They were no longer united as one. How could they possibly sweep away all their external enemies in a single campaign?

Everything seemed to be progressing according to plan, moving in a favorable direction.

Yet the ominous premonition in Xuanyuan Tuo's heart grew increasingly intense. It was as if a great calamity could descend upon him at any moment.

Even though Xuanyuan Tuo carefully concealed his movements and no longer openly transmitted the Dharma in broad daylight, the sense of crisis in his heart did not diminish at all.

Xuanyuan Tuo therefore knew that he must have already attracted the attention of the major figures within the Ten Sects.

Only after personally facing them could one truly understand just how desperate it felt to stand against a colossal existence like the Ten Great Immortal Sects.

Xuanyuan Tuo tried every possible method, but it seemed that he would always arrive at only one conclusion.

Death.

But Xuanyuan Tuo did not want to die.

Just as the despair in his heart was about to drown him, that ethereal murmuring voice that had accompanied him since childhood suddenly appeared once again after many years.

Ever since he had embarked upon the path of cultivation, this voice had disappeared from Xuanyuan Tuo's life. Now that it had returned, it brought back everything he had experienced over the years.

Amid the hazy and mysterious murmuring, Xuanyuan Tuo seemed to experience everything from his past once again.

Moreover, he could observe everything around him from the perspective of a third party.

An inexplicable sensation even arose within Xuanyuan Tuo's heart.

He seemed to be able to manipulate and alter the thoughts of these people to a certain extent.

When he saw a young cultivator in his memories who had once gathered around him and listened to him transmit the Dharma, an inexplicable surge of immense joy suddenly welled up within Xuanyuan Tuo.

He knew that this young man was the key to his survival.

His very soul seemed to rejoice and leap with excitement. Accompanied by the murmuring sounds, Xuanyuan Tuo approached, touched, and invaded.

Even though it was merely an illusion within his memories, under the mysterious power of the murmuring voice, Xuanyuan Tuo felt as though he had truly entered the other person's soul.

He learned everything about him.

Chen Tianhai. The number one direct disciple of the Taiyan Sect.

The Nine Volumes Derivation of Heavens. The Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

'There is actually such a divine object in this world?'

Xuanyuan Tuo's mind trembled violently, and he almost awakened from that mysterious state.

This Dao Deviation Stone Mother was something he had never even heard of in the countless scenes continuously emerging within his mind.

'If I can borrow the power of this Dao Deviation Stone Mother to perform deductions, I will certainly be able to find that one thread of hope for survival.'

Hope ignited within Xuanyuan Tuo's heart.

But even Chen Tianhai, the number one direct disciple of the Taiyan Sect, did not have the qualifications to use the Dao Deviation Stone Mother. As an outsider, and moreover the sworn enemy of the Ten Sects of the Immortal Dao, wanting to make use of it...

It was simply a pipe dream.

'Perhaps I can manipulate and influence Chen Tianhai and accomplish all of this.'

Xuanyuan Tuo suddenly understood.

To pry into the thoughts and feelings of an illusion from the memories of the past was already an utterly inconceivable matter.

But to influence the future reality through an illusion from the past was something even more unheard of.

Yet for some reason, an inexplicable confidence arose within Xuanyuan Tuo's heart. If he could make use of the murmuring voice within his mind...

This would not be difficult.

Xuanyuan Tuo truly lived up to his reputation as a peerless genius.

Even though he had no understanding whatsoever of the principle behind the murmuring voice, that did not prevent him from gradually discovering how to use it through instinct alone.

In a daze, he established some kind of connection with the Chen Tianhai in reality.

He could faintly sense Chen Tianhai's thoughts at every moment.

Thus, Chen Tianhai's memories were silently altered.

It seemed that back when he had been watching Xuanyuan Tuo transmit the Dharma and expound upon the Dao, Xuanyuan Tuo had inexplicably glanced at him.

And throughout the years that followed, Xuanyuan Tuo's gaze from that moment had remained with him.

It became his inner demon, making him restless day and night.

For Chen Tianhai to have been selected by the Taiyan Sect as its number one direct disciple, his talent and state of mind were naturally beyond reproach. He was an absolute prodigy.

With tremendous willpower, Chen Tianhai forcibly suppressed the unease in his heart.

Xuanyuan Tuo did not become impatient. He merely waited patiently for the opportunity he sensed.

Once the seed had been planted, the day it blossomed and bore fruit would naturally come.

Sure enough.

Not long afterward, Fang Dingge, the Sect Master of the Great Dao Sect, entrusted Sect Master Xingji of the Taiyan Sect with a request before departing to hunt down and surround Xuanyuan Tuo with six Longevity Immortals. He asked him to use the Dao Deviation Stone Mother to perform deductions.

Daoist Xingji performed five hundred million deductions, and every single deduction ended with Xuanyuan Tuo's death.

After receiving the answer, Fang Dingge set out to search for and hunt Xuanyuan Tuo.

'Six Longevity Immortals. What an enormous force.'

Xuanyuan Tuo's heart turned ice-cold.

Feeling death gradually approaching, Xuanyuan Tuo remained shocked but did not panic.

He continued using the mysterious power of the murmuring voice to corrupt and influence Chen Tianhai's state of mind.

As fate would have it, Xuanyuan Tuo's life was not yet meant to end.

Daoist Xingji temporarily left the Taiyan Sect to suppress an upheaval in the Indeterminate Prison.

In the end, Chen Tianhai was unable to resist the temptation in his heart and secretly activated the Dao Deviation Stone Mother to perform deductions.

He did not know how many times he urged the Stone Mother to perform deductions.

Finally, amid countless impossible outcomes, he found the one and only path to survival.

Chen Tianhai seemed to foresee the deaths of the six Longevity Immortals. His mind was shaken, and he became utterly dazed.

Xuanyuan Tuo, meanwhile, seized the final opportunity and rushed directly toward the small world revealed by the deductions of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

He arrived at his destination just before the six Longevity Immortals of the Immortal Sects surrounded him.

The six Longevity Immortals arrived one after another.

Even though Xuanyuan Tuo possessed heaven-defying divine abilities, he was quickly and severely wounded under the combined assault of the six Longevity Immortals.

Just as he was about to die, the foreign races within the small world, which had suffered catastrophic casualties from the battle between the seven cultivators, launched an unexpected counterattack.

Those beings whom the six Longevity Immortals regarded as mere ants and had never taken seriously began chanting and summoned a gate.

It enveloped all seven people, including Xuanyuan Tuo.

The gate opened wide, and endless darkness swallowed everyone.

Within the Dark Sea, relying on the circumstances revealed through the deductions of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, Xuanyuan Tuo used the revived will of a True Immortal to survive and killed the six Longevity Immortals one by one.

But because his injuries were ultimately too severe, he lost consciousness and collapsed within the Dark Sea.

During this time, the Heavenly Doctor, who had been lying dormant and waiting for an opportunity all along, finally regained control of the body once again.

'What exactly was that murmuring voice?'

The Heavenly Doctor, who had watched everything from beginning to end, was also deeply shocked by that mysterious and unfathomable power.

The Heavenly Doctor had thought that this body was already doomed.

Yet he had never expected Xuanyuan Tuo to actually find a path to survival within the impossible.

To influence and alter reality through memories of the past.

Such an ability absolutely could not exist within the mortal world.

'More importantly, Xuanyuan Tuo and I are actually beings who share the same soul. Yet that murmuring voice exists only within his memories.'

'Although I know that such a thing exists, I am completely unable to recall or sense it in any specific way.'

The Heavenly Doctor's mind trembled violently, and he remained unable to calm himself for a long time.

Next

Chapter 1410: Tianxuan’S Demon-Carving Sound

While shocked by this unknown and overwhelming power, the Heavenly Doctor also became deeply wary of the original owner of this body.

Thus, taking advantage of Xuanyuan Tuo's severe injuries and unconscious state, the Heavenly Doctor gradually began tampering with his body.

The Heavenly Doctor knew that Xuanyuan Tuo was protected by the power of that mysterious murmuring sound. Therefore, he did not use ordinary methods. Instead, relying solely on the connection between their two souls within one body created by the [Heavenly Soul Reincarnation] technique, he used himself as the medicine and slowly infiltrated Xuanyuan Tuo's body.

In a certain sense, he was Xuanyuan Tuo, and Xuanyuan Tuo was him. It was only because of the mysterious power enveloping the Xuanyuan clan's bloodline that Xuanyuan Tuo, who should have lost his independent consciousness, was still able to retain control over the body.

The stronger Xuanyuan Tuo became, the stronger the Heavenly Doctor became alongside him.

As Xuanyuan Tuo's injuries gradually recovered, he slowly awakened and escaped from the Dark Sea.

The mysterious disappearance of six Longevity Immortal realm experts naturally caused a tremendous uproar.

However, the Ten Great Sects possessed profound foundations. Although this loss could already be considered a serious blow, it was still insufficient to bring them to ruin.

Xuanyuan Tuo faintly sensed that he would soon face the Ten Great Sects' true trump card.

Yet for some reason, Xuanyuan Tuo seemed to have overestimated the Ten Great Sects. Until he achieved the Longevity Heavenly Sovereign realm, the Ten Great Sects never managed to organize any effective counterattack. They merely withdrew their forces and consolidated their own sect territories.

They allowed the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance to gradually devour the small sects and minor factions surrounding the Ten Great Sects.

Then, when Xuanyuan Tuo finally attained Longevity Immortal, the laws of heaven and earth in the Xuanhuang Realm were permanently changed.

The old cultivation method ceased to exist, and the new cultivation method became the only path available to all cultivators.

Xuanyuan Tuo's lifelong ideals and ambitions were finally achieved. Anyone in the world who wished to cultivate could do so without obstruction.

Although he had already foreseen that, as the number of cultivators continued to increase, the resources required to advance through the new cultivation system would eventually become a constraint upon their cultivation, Xuanyuan Tuo had already prepared a solution.

After absorbing the power of [Golden Authority], he could manipulate the Xuanhuang Realm, continuously generating all kinds of wondrous objects, Cave Heavens, and even the essence of heaven and earth.

Within, the Xuanhuang Realm would continuously evolve, creating an endless cycle of life.

Without, it would annex and absorb the surviving cultivation worlds in the star sea, replenishing its resources.

As the number of new method cultivators increased, Xuanyuan Tuo's own strength would continue to rise.

Even if the Immortal Path had already been severed, he could still reach the pinnacle of the Star Sea. At that time, he would repair the ancient formation left behind by the Ancient Xuantian Sect and take the Xuanhuang Realm away from the range enveloped by the Immortal Ruins.

After reaching a relatively safe region for a period of time, he would then use the power that defied the principles of heaven and earth when a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign attained the Dao to continuously transform the Xuanhuang Realm.

According to Xuanyuan Tuo's calculations, as long as a dozen or so Longevity Heavenly Sovereigns worked together to construct and transform the Xuanhuang Realm, then, combined with the activation of the ancient formation, they might be able to cross the High Wall.

Xuanyuan Tuo, the Dharma-Transmitting Heavenly Sovereign, was continuously accumulating and increasing his own strength. At the same time, he was secretly observing the cultivators within the Xuanhuang Realm who were capable of shouldering great responsibilities.

He did not care about aptitude. He only looked at their temperament and character.

Those who passed his tests would secretly receive his assistance, allowing them to comprehend the method of reversing the principles of heaven and earth and directly attain the Longevity Immortal realm.

Xuanyuan Tuo had indeed selected several promising young people worthy of being entrusted with great responsibilities.

But before he could take action, a calamity suddenly descended upon the Xuanhuang Realm. The two cultivation methods could no longer coexist.

Such a drastic change in heaven and earth was truly beyond Xuanyuan Tuo's expectations.

He possessed the Dao of [Golden Authority]. In principle, the entire Xuanhuang Realm should have been under his control. Yet such a change had inexplicably occurred.

Xuanyuan Tuo understood that the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao must have somehow awakened a power capable of opposing the Dao of Golden Authority.

The Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

This name instantly appeared in Xuanyuan Tuo's mind.

Xuanyuan Tuo did not harbor much hostility toward this newly ascended Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable. He even wanted to seek him out and have a conversation.

Unfortunately, this Great Celestial Venerable had concealed himself extremely well by relying on the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.

Meanwhile, Xuanyuan Tuo also had to divert his attention to deal with the mess left behind by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance after the calamity of the two cultivation methods being unable to coexist descended.

The Ten Great Immortal Sects possessed profound foundations, and their disciples practiced many different cultivation methods. With their existing order still intact, they would not completely collapse in an instant.

Although the number of cultivators in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was expanding at an astonishing rate, the vast majority practiced the [Heavenly Net Divine Art] taught by Xuanyuan Tuo.

This cultivation technique, personally derived by Xuanyuan Tuo and encompassing countless principles, with a single method reaching through heaven, became the death warrant of everyone in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance when the calamity arrived.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had only been established for a short time. It was originally a loose alliance jointly formed by new method cultivators. Aside from Xuanyuan Tuo himself possessing tremendous prestige, few others could truly command the respect of everyone.

Under the descending calamity, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance fell into a horrifying massacre among its own members.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, which the Ten Great Sects regarded as their greatest threat, collapsed on its own before the Ten Great Sects even needed to attack.

After this incident, Xuanyuan Tuo deeply understood the importance of a profound foundation.

When the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance first rose, its momentum had indeed appeared overwhelming. But its accumulation was insufficient, and its ability to withstand risks was simply too weak. The slightest storm was enough to break it.

While attempting to rescue and preserve the vitality of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, Xuanyuan Tuo also tried to find that Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

But because he had created the new cultivation method, without the power of [Golden Authority], he and the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao could be said to be mortal enemies.

Naturally, that Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable also regarded him with irreconcilable hatred.

'He has misunderstood me far too deeply. This is truly a case of judging someone by their actions rather than their intentions.' Xuanyuan Tuo felt somewhat regretful.

But as an unparalleled expert, he was too proud to step forward and explain himself.

Even the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable could not stop the Dharma-Transmitting Heavenly Sovereign, who had already completely succeeded in reversing the principles of heaven and earth.

However, not long afterward, two more unexpected things happened to Xuanyuan Tuo.

The first was that Xuanyuan Tuo, already the strongest expert of the current era, would occasionally experience an inexplicable sense of alarm and unease.

His heart would suddenly fill with anxiety and sorrow.

After carefully investigating the matter, Xuanyuan Tuo was horrified to discover that these emotions did not originate from himself.

They came from another existence within his body.

Another consciousness that he had been unable to detect for a very long time.

The second matter was that the calamity of the two cultivation methods being unable to coexist had yet to pass when another disaster, known as the Immortal-Mortal Miasma, quietly descended upon the world.

The arrival of the Immortal-Mortal Miasma was accompanied by the death of that unknown Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

This matter shocked Xuanyuan Tuo even more than discovering the existence of another consciousness within his body.

Because Xuanyuan Tuo understood that the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable had used his own life as the price to execute a method that reversed the principles of heaven and earth.

By simultaneously attaining the status of both the New and Old Method Heavenly Sovereigns, he had also suffered a backlash and perished.

Xuanyuan Tuo simply could not understand his actions.

Yet Xuanyuan Tuo knew that someone capable of simultaneously becoming the Heavenly Sovereign of both the New and Old Method could certainly not be an incompetent or foolish person.

There had to be a reason why he had done this.

Xuanyuan Tuo was utterly unable to understand it.

Until later, when he discovered that even after cultivators expelled and overcame the Immortal-Mortal Miasma, it would still remain within the bloodlines of their descendants.

It had become part of the laws of heaven and earth, preventing mortals from cultivating.

But if mortals could overcome this suppressive force and embark upon the path of cultivation, it would be equivalent to completing a minor reversal of the principles of heaven and earth.

Although it was still far from being enough to influence the fundamental laws of heaven and earth, when the passage of time was extended, countless mortals would continuously embark upon the path of cultivation.

The birth of every single cultivator was equivalent to shaking the foundation of the Dharma Transmission.

'It cannot kill me, but it has eliminated a large portion of the accumulation brought to me by every cultivator who practices the new cultivation method.'

'Originally, every cultivator born within the new cultivation system would become part of the foundation of my cultivation. As this accumulated over time and continued to increase, one day it would surely push me toward an unknowable sacred realm.'

'But now...'

'It is cutting off my path to the Dao!'

Even a clay figurine has three parts of temper.

Having finally realized what had happened, Xuanyuan Tuo could not help but fly into a rage. But that Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable was already dead. Where could Xuanyuan Tuo go to vent his anger?

Just as Xuanyuan Tuo's mind was more unsettled than ever before, his consciousness was suddenly pulled into a pitch black space. He lost control over his own body.

Floating before him was an aged old man with a head full of white hair.

Although the man's appearance was completely different from his own, when Xuanyuan Tuo looked at him, it was as if he were looking into a mirror.

"Heavenly Doctor!"

For some inexplicable reason, Xuanyuan Tuo called out the man's name.

The Heavenly Doctor did not waste words with him.

Instead, blood tears flowed from his eyes.

As if possessed, he repeatedly muttered, "Return to the Heavenly Law Realm quickly. Return to the Heavenly Law Realm quickly!"

Xuanyuan Tuo immediately understood. This was the reason he had been feeling restless and uneasy in his heart recently.

Xuanyuan Tuo tried to communicate with him.

But the Heavenly Doctor, whose heart was consumed by thoughts of the Heavenly Law Realm, could not hear a single word.

Xuanyuan Tuo wanted to suppress him, but to his horror, he discovered that the other was almost exactly like a copy of himself. His strength was nearly identical to his own.

With the support of his overwhelming obsession to return to his homeland, he was even somewhat stronger than Xuanyuan Tuo.

Gradually, he seized control of the body.

The appearance of the body used for transmitting the Dao also slowly changed into the Heavenly Doctor's original appearance.

It was as if he had been sealed inside a small dark room. Xuanyuan Tuo could only vaguely sense what was happening outside.

He saw that when the Heavenly Doctor finally returned to the Heavenly Law Realm after enduring countless hardships, the living beings of the Heavenly Law Realm had already been slaughtered almost to the last.

The horrific scene was so brutal that even Xuanyuan Tuo found it difficult to accept.

His homeland had suffered a great calamity, and the Heavenly Doctor descended into madness.

A terrifying storm seemed to rage within his heart, severing the connection between Xuanyuan Tuo and himself.

Xuanyuan Tuo accumulated his strength, attempting to seize back control of the body.

He did not know how much time had passed when another transparent figure, apart from the Heavenly Doctor, appeared in this place.

What caused Xuanyuan Tuo an overwhelming sense of danger was that this transparent figure seemed to possess neither awareness nor consciousness.

Yet it could attach itself to and erode his consciousness.

This was a strange power Xuanyuan Tuo had never encountered before.

Although he desperately resisted, he ultimately could not prevent it from eroding him step by step.

Just before this transparent body completely occupied Xuanyuan Tuo's consciousness, that ethereal whispering voice once again surfaced in his mind.

With the help of this miraculous power, Xuanyuan Tuo could barely maintain his awareness.

In the dark space, there was no way to know how much time had passed.

After an unknown length of time, Xuanyuan Tuo finally sensed that the opportunity had arrived.

The body seemed to have entered a strange space.

And within this place, the mysterious whispering voice became clearer than ever before.

Relying on the strength he had accumulated for a long time, Xuanyuan Tuo launched a full counterattack and unexpectedly gained control of his body for a brief instant.

Although it lasted only an instant, after understanding his current state, Xuanyuan Tuo still came up with a way to deal with it.

The space where the body currently resided seemed to be the interior of an immortal artifact. That was precisely why it had resonated with the mysterious whispering voice, which carried an Immortal-level power.

Xuanyuan Tuo softly murmured, leaving the whispering voice in his mind behind within the internal space of the immortal artifact.

Relying on this strange whispering power, which possessed the heaven defying ability to influence future reality through memories of the past, Xuanyuan Tuo was confident that he could make use of the later people who would continuously enter this immortal artifact space.

Through them, he would search for a way to escape his predicament.

After imprinting the whispering voice, Xuanyuan Tuo once again lost control of his body.

He was once more sealed within the boundless darkness of consciousness.

Time passed without him knowing.

During this period, he attempted to fight back several times. However, for some unknown reason, the power suppressing and sealing him became increasingly stronger. It seemed that he would never have another chance to turn the situation around.

But Xuanyuan Tuo never gave up.

Because the whispering voice he had implanted earlier was continuously gathering energy for his counterattack.

It would simply take a little longer for him to completely break free.

...

Back to the present.

As Li Fan watched the scenes reconstructed within the illusion formation based on the memories of the Dao transmitting body, his expression changed subtly several times.

Even though he believed he already understood the story between the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor quite well, he had never expected the truth to be so convoluted, and...

That fate could be so cruel.

However, what concerned Li Fan the most was the various strange phenomena surrounding the Xuanyuan Clan.

Not only that mysterious and extraordinary whispering voice.

There was also the crazy uncle Xuanyuan Sheng, whom Xuanyuan Tuo had encountered during his childhood.

"Goudan, you must understand. Everything in this world has already happened countless times..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and carefully pondered these words.

"Was it merely the meaningless babbling of a madman, or did it truly contain some deeper meaning?"

It was difficult for Li Fan not to associate these words with [Truth].

And according to Xuanyuan Tuo's memories, it was still uncertain whether Xuanyuan Sheng had actually died on the night he disappeared.

"The Xuanyuan Clan worships the memorial tablet of Xuanyuan Hong. It should be a contingency left behind by the Xuantian King."

"The Xuantian King actually possessed that heaven defying whispering power?"

"Or perhaps that is the true might that a Half Immortal should possess?"

Although Li Fan had already advanced to the Half Immortal realm, the only Immortal-level means he possessed was his mastery of formations.

In every other aspect, there was no qualitative difference from the methods he had possessed during his mortal cultivation.

"Imprinted within the Tianxuan Realm?"

Li Fan's gaze pierced through the Darkest Star Sea and fell upon the Xuanhuang Realm.

He had entered the Tianxuan Memorial Hall more than once.

He had also experienced various illusions of different Dao-transmitting experiences.

"It appears that one were experiencing the past of the Immortal Ancestor."

"In reality, one is being eroded by the whispering voice..."
