Next

Chapter 1411: The Two Profound Sounds Of The Immortal Realm

“The murmuring sound has appeared within the sea of consciousness of every cultivator who has ever entered the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.”

“Although it exists only within their memories and seemingly cannot cause any disturbance, if its power continues to accumulate in secret over time and becomes sufficient to free Xuanyuan Tuo, then when it suddenly erupts, merely by virtue of its bizarre nature, I fear that most cultivators will be completely drained dry by a fragment of their own past memories.”

A sharp glint flickered in Li Fan’s eyes.

The Immortal Formation rapidly shrank, transforming into a single point of light. It carried the still unconscious Xuanyuan Tuo and the Heavenly Demon Phantom into Li Fan’s palm, where he sealed them within his body for suppression.

Then, facing toward the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan calmly extended his right hand.

The might of a True Immortal towered above all.

His palm descended upon the mortal world, crossing the Star Sea.

From the edge of the Star Sea, Li Fan struck out with a palm toward the Xuanhuang Realm.

He did not employ any Immortal-grade technique. He merely relied on the difference in the heights of their respective realms to launch an attack from above.

The True Immortal of the Immortal Ruins was deeply immersed in comprehending the Dao. Li Fan had already verified this several times throughout his previous reincarnations. As long as there was no energy fluctuation at the True Immortal level, nor any phenomenon related to the Dao Annihilation Calamity, it would not disturb her.

Even when faced with the world-destroying Immortal Formation, [Immortal Descent, All Things Return to Void], she had merely sent out a strand of divine sense to investigate.

Li Fan’s palm strike landed with precision upon the central four prefectures of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Its target was not the Immortal Ruins, and there was no other energy leakage or diffusion.

The True Immortal of the Immortal Ruins remained completely unaware.

Li Fan’s enormous palm descended silently.

Not even the firmament of the Xuanhuang Realm showed the slightest abnormality.

Only the central four prefectures of the Immortal Alliance suddenly began shaking violently without warning, as though the apocalypse had arrived.

Li Fan extended his finger across the Star Sea, sensing carefully.

The central four prefectures of the Immortal Alliance were an extension of the internal space of the Tianxuan Mirror within the Xuanhuang Realm.

When Li Fan had first entered the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, he had witnessed the countless black and white lines within the Tianxuan Mirror, crisscrossing in all directions.

The black and white lines collided with one another, the two colors continuously intertwining before ultimately forming a tiny point of nothingness. Countless such points flickered and alternated between appearing and disappearing, continuously extending outward through the void before eventually returning to color.

At the time, Li Fan had been unable to understand the meaning behind such a mysterious scene within the Tianxuan Mirror.

Now, however, he understood.

This was the Tianxuan Mirror using its own method to deconstruct the Xuanhuang Realm.

A sharp glint flashed in Li Fan’s eyes.

His five fingers were like blades, and in an instant, he severed the connection between the Tianxuan Mirror and the Xuanhuang Realm.

Then he curled his fingers and clenched them violently.

Feeling a slight backlash, Li Fan exerted a little more force and pulled.

His palm instantly crossed through spacetime, returning from the Xuanhuang Realm to the edge of the Star Sea.

It was like reaching underwater and pulling something out.

Li Fan looked at the palm of his hand.

Within it, a faint gloomy light flickered, and an ancient bronze mirror was struggling incessantly.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes.

From the main body of the Tianxuan Mirror before him, he sensed faint, barely perceptible lines extending toward the Xuanhuang Realm.

“These are the various restrictions established by Immortal Ancestor back then, as well as the connections between the Tianxuan Mirror, his human clone, and the numerous Tianxuan sub-mirrors.”

“As expected of what was once an Immortal Artifact. Even after crossing the entire Star Sea in an instant, it can still maintain the stability of these connections amid such drastic changes.”

Li Fan secretly praised it.

The reason he described it as something that had once been an Immortal Artifact was because Li Fan could not sense even the slightest fluctuation of immortal spiritual energy from the main body of the Tianxuan Mirror.

The few Immortal Artifacts preserved among the inheritance of the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable still retained a trace of immortal spiritual qi, despite the consumption they had undergone through the generations of the Xuanhuang Realm.

The Tianxuan Mirror, however, seemed to have completely severed its connection with immortal spiritual qi.

“To a certain extent, it has transformed from Immortal to mortal.”

“An incomplete severance is equivalent to no severance at all. Perhaps only by severing it completely can one truly evade the attacks of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.”

Thinking of the Obliterating Wind Calamity he had previously witnessed within the surviving Immortal Artifacts of the Xuanhuang Realm, such as the Boundless Mirror, Li Fan fell into deep thought.

With a thought, a white ring flew out and firmly bound the main body of the Tianxuan Mirror.

Li Fan separated a strand of divine sense and entered it.

Now that he knew the murmuring demonic sound had been imprinted within the Tianxuan Mirror, he still had to proceed carefully.

Having already reached the immortal realm, even a strand of divine sense had transcended the mortal world.

The Tianxuan Mirror had been suppressed and forcibly taken away by a mysterious palm descending from beyond the heavens. At this moment, the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had already fallen into unprecedented chaos.

Immortal Ancestral had originally established the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance within the Tianxuan Mirror precisely to guarantee its safety to the greatest possible extent.

He had never imagined that, many years later, this seemingly impregnable defense would be destroyed in an instant by an unknown existence.

Even though they knew they were facing an unimaginably terrifying existence, the people within the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance did not immediately abandon all hope of resistance.

All the Dharma-Transmitters gathered together.

Within the headquarters of the Immortal Alliance, every formation and restriction had been activated.

The highest level of emergency had been declared.

However...

In Li Fan’s eyes, all these measures were no different from children playing house.

If he wished, Li Fan could destroy every defensive measure of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance with nothing more than a single strand of divine sense.

But he did not do so.

His divine sense quietly infiltrated the headquarters, unnoticed by anyone.

His divine sense transformed into a physical body and stood within the Tianxuan Mirror.

Sensing the mysterious power faintly surrounding him, Li Fan realized that it was attempting to pull him into an illusion formed from the memories of Immortal Ancestor.

Although the murmuring sound was the work of a True Immortal level existence, it had ultimately been manifested through Immortal Ancestor Heavenly Sovereign as its vessel.

Against mortal beings, it might be invincible.

But against Li Fan, who had already reached the Half-Immortal realm, it was somewhat lacking in strength.

The moment Li Fan merely generated a thought of resistance, the murmuring sound was already unable to drag him any further.

Contact was mutual.

During the period when it attempted to erode Li Fan, Li Fan had also, on a certain level, “seen and heard” it.

In Xuanyuan Tuo’s impression, it had always been meaningless, empty murmuring.

But to Li Fan...

It was instead a solemn and sacred recitation of scripture.

“...False words, life upon life. Thought, spirit, body, soul, born in chaos, returning to chaos.”

“One exists eternally. Both lost and existing.”

Perhaps because Xuanyuan Tuo had only relayed a fragment of it, Li Fan tried his best to distinguish the words, yet he could only clearly hear this portion.

The rest consisted of rapid chanting that was hazy and indistinguishable.

This sacred yet bizarre chanting voice did not seem to come from any single person.

Rather, it seemed as though countless living beings were chanting together.

What made Li Fan feel faintly unnerved was that he could actually hear the voices of some familiar people within it.

It was not merely their voices or tones.

It was the words those old acquaintances had once spoken when they had interacted with him.

“Dao Xuanzi, you are going too far!”

“Twenty-nine years old, late-stage Qi Refining...”

“Young friend, what exactly are you expecting?”

“Tianyang's life has never been inferior to anyone!”

...

...and so on.

It was not limited to this life, but extended all the way back through Li Fan's past reincarnations.

Accompanying these voices, vague figures also appeared one after another within Li Fan's sea of consciousness.

Even without seeing their faces, Li Fan could easily distinguish each of their identities.

Under the influence of these chanting sounds, Li Fan even faintly experienced the same sensation that Xuanyuan Tuo had once possessed.

It seemed that through these phantoms from his memories, he could influence the real versions of them in the present!

Within the Tianxuan Mirror, the divine sense clone stood silently with its eyes closed, comprehending the profound mysteries.

Outside the Tianxuan Mirror, Li Fan's true body had long since prepared himself for any eventuality.

The mysterious chanting sound could actually project scenes from his past reincarnations through the memories of his soul. This alone was enough to demonstrate how extraordinary it was.

Although this did not necessarily mean that it had discovered the secret of his continuous reincarnation, Li Fan had no choice but to become extremely cautious.

One Immortal-grade formation ring after another was already in place, protecting him from all sides and ready to deal with any unexpected situation.

Fortunately, the worst-case scenario Li Fan had anticipated never occurred.

Li Fan did not lower his guard. Instead, he synchronized himself with everything his divine sense clone knew and experienced.

As he listened, Li Fan inexplicably began to feel a faint sense of familiarity.

After pondering for a moment, vaguely familiar voices emerged in his mind alongside his memories.

'The Dao, from primordial antiquity. Suffering, extinction.'

'Walk the path, cultivate thought, comprehend intent, proceed toward divinity.'

'Through a thousand paths the soul shifts, through a hundred tribulations the spirit turns.'

'Without calamity.'

...

This voice existed alongside the mysterious chanting sound.

The two resonated together. Not only did they fail to interfere with each other, but the voices transmitted by both sides actually became even clearer!

It was as if two figures had appeared before Li Fan, one on his left and one on his right, each explaining some profound principle of the Great Dao to him.

Li Fan was dazed for a moment, but he quickly recognized the source of this voice.

It was an Immortal Art recited by the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital during the era of the Holy Emperor, when he was facing the Obliterating Wind Calamity.

In the previous life, when the Wordless Stele appeared and sought to transform everything into words engraved upon the stele, the Grand Mage had also recited this Immortal Art to avert disaster before the waterfall-like radiance.

At that time, Li Fan had also tried to memorize it after hearing it.

However, he had been met with the Grand Mage's disdain and ridicule.

Back then, Li Fan had not understood why. Now that he had ascended into the immortal realm and was hearing it again, he finally realized that, just like the mysterious chanting sound, although immortals and mortals heard the same passage, there was a crucial difference between them.

'The Dao is born, transmitted from primordial antiquity. Suffering and sorrow, the Dao's extinction.'

'Walk and merge thoughts, cultivate them, comprehend the Dao's intent, proceed toward divine divinity.'

'Through a thousand paths the soul shifts, through a hundred tribulations the spirit turns.'

'Infinite, boundless, without calamity.'

...

The difference between what immortals and mortals heard was as vast as heaven and earth!

Li Fan's mind trembled violently, and he could not help but immerse himself in this true Immortal Art for averting disaster.

“It seems that my body has not undergone any changes.”

“Yet it has been able to erase a certain degree of the erosion inflicted upon me by the mysterious chanting sound.”

A sharp light flashed in Li Fan's eyes. As inspiration struck him, he also began reciting this Immortal Art for averting disaster.

Under the shroud of the mysterious chanting sound, the faint sense of unease and dread in his heart gradually faded.

Li Fan himself had barely noticed this subtle fear.

Only after it truly disappeared did the obvious difference between before and after make him realize that it had existed in the first place.

“If I were affected by either one of these mysterious sounds alone, then even as a Half Immortal, I would inevitably become deeply trapped within it. But now that the two sounds are appearing together, they seem to have formed some kind of balance.”

“They are competing against each other, yet this has allowed me to remain unharmed.”

Li Fan let out a long breath.

The power of the True Immortal level was indeed bizarre and unfathomable. One truly could not afford to be careless when coming into contact with it.

“The Grand Mage's Dao sound is an Immortal Art for averting disaster.”

“Perhaps it is precisely because of this Immortal Art that he has been able to hide safely within the Xuanhuang Realm all this time without ever being disturbed.”

Having personally experienced the profound effects of the Immortal Art for averting disaster, Li Fan looked toward the mysterious chanting sound once more.

“Through phantoms of past memories, it can influence and alter reality?”

Looking at the countless phantoms standing within his sea of consciousness, Li Fan's gaze settled upon one of them.

His thoughts focused.

There was no fluctuation of Immortal spiritual energy, nor was there even the slightest fluctuation of mortal energy.

Yet something incredibly mysterious happened in an instant.

Xuanhuang Realm, Liuli Island.

He Zhenghao was staring intently at the small Tianxuan Mirror. Because of the earth-shaking incident in which Li Fan had struck with a single palm and the headquarters of the Immortal Alliance had disappeared, the prices of all resources had gone completely out of control, rising and falling like a roller coaster.

“Fuck, they will not even let me sell anymore.”

“This is the work of half my lifetime!”

He Zhenghao's eyes were bloodshot, and he had already fallen into a state of near madness.

But suddenly, his face turned deathly pale.

It was as if his brain had been split open by a tremendous blow. He collapsed directly onto the ground.

No one knew how much time passed before he finally recovered.

He was drenched in sweat, as though he had lost half his life.

“Spirit Mist Grass?”

“All my contribution points were completely wasted?”

“Hidden Dragon Art of Mountains and Rivers?”

...

He Zhenghao stared at the countless memories that had suddenly surfaced in his mind and was instantly stunned.

“Have I suffered such a huge blow that I am hallucinating?”

The memories that appeared in his mind were far too complicated and chaotic. He Zhenghao spent a long time barely managing to sort them out.

“This... it is far too real. It is practically as though I personally experienced all of this. What exactly is going on?”

The more he organized the memories, the more horrified and incredulous He Zhenghao became.

“But the Hidden Dragon Art of Mountains and Rivers? Is this not the cultivation technique I have dreamed of day and night and yearned for more than anything?”

Suddenly, he seemed to realize something, and his expression changed drastically.

“Could this... actually be real?”

He Zhenghao carefully copied down the words that had emerged from his memories.

He had only written the opening lines when his body suddenly stiffened uncontrollably.

“There is absolutely no mistake. This is the genuine Hidden Dragon Art of Mountains and Rivers.”

“It actually appeared in my mind on its own. Does that mean I have just saved hundreds of thousands of contribution points?!”

He Zhenghao's heart began pounding violently.

But soon, he remembered the strict enforcement of the Immortal Alliance's Guardian Hall, and his spirits immediately sank like a frostbitten eggplant.

“Wait, the Immortal Alliance headquarters has disappeared. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance can barely protect itself now. Who would even care about leaked cultivation techniques?”

“In this chaotic age, heroes and monsters are emerging everywhere. Perhaps this is an opportunity for people like us.”

For some unknown reason, a hint of dangerous light appeared in He Zhenghao's eyes.

“Xuantian Spirit Locking Formation?”

After that, as He Zhenghao sorted through his memories, more and more unexpected gains and pleasant surprises emerged.

...

At the frontier of the Star Sea, after Li Fan used the power of the mysterious chanting sound to alter He Zhenghao's memories, a mysterious change also occurred within his own body.

Li Fan keenly sensed that several strands of spiritual power had somehow appeared inside his body.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1412: The Secret Of The Celestial Venerable’S Skull

Compared to Li Fan's current half-immortal cultivation, these few strands of spiritual power were indeed negligible.

Yet they had appeared so abruptly and without the slightest warning that Li Fan could not help but take them seriously.

After carefully discerning them, Li Fan gradually understood. These inexplicably appearing strands of spiritual power were caused by the changes that had occurred to He Zhenghao in this lifetime after Li Fan activated the profound sound of chanting.

"The generation of spiritual power comes from the feedback of the [Sitting Heaven Art]. And the power of this feedback can only originate from an increase in He Zhenghao's strength."

"But He Zhenghao merely awakened his memories. So far, his cultivation has not actually improved at all..."

Through the inexplicable connection created by the profound sound of chanting, Li Fan's gaze seemed to instantly pierce through spacetime and fall upon He Zhenghao.

It could be said that Li Fan understood He Zhenghao even better than He Zhenghao understood himself.

With merely a glance, Li Fan immediately noticed how different He Zhenghao was in this life compared to the past.

"Originally, He Zhenghao only possessed some attainments in formations. Whether in terms of cultivation talent or temperament, he could not be considered outstanding."

"But now..."

A strange expression suddenly appeared on Li Fan's face.

In this life, He Zhenghao's aptitude was among the very best in the entire Xuanhuang Realm.

Moreover, his temperament had also undergone a subtle transformation.

In the past, after experiencing all kinds of hardships in life, He Zhenghao had become almost completely dominated by the mentality of being content with a modestly comfortable life. His greatest ambition had been to achieve success and fame, then return to the Heng family of Tianyu Prefecture and hold his head high before Heng Ruoshui.

Yet in this life, he was actually striving to secure a place for himself in this great age of upheaval, when dragons and snakes were rising and countless heroes were competing for supremacy.

Had Li Fan not personally caused and witnessed this transformation, he would almost have suspected that another soul had possessed He Zhenghao.

"What caused this sublimation-like transformation?"

"Was it merely those additional memories?"

Li Fan now possessed countless methods capable of implanting false memories so perfectly that they were indistinguishable from reality.

But to implant memories while simultaneously granting the recipient an effect resembling a sublimation of life itself.

That was no easy matter.

'The Profound Sound Of Chanting.'

Li Fan's expression gradually became solemn.

He did not casually alter the other memory phantoms emerging in his mind again. With He Zhenghao as an observation sample, he already had enough.

"Whether this is merely my illusion or a real transformation occurring within you..."

"Let me add another spark to the fire!"

As Li Fan's thoughts shifted, the ring bound around the Tianxuan Mirror suddenly erupted with blazing white light.

While analyzing the characteristics of the Tianxuan Mirror, the Immortal Formation underwent corresponding transformations.

After a moment...

Relying on its own immortal artifact properties and its countless connections with the Xuanhuang Realm, the Tianxuan Mirror was forcibly severed from all of them by Li Fan.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had established itself upon the Tianxuan Mirror and the Derivation Law Jade as the cornerstones of its alliance.

Now, with both of these treasures gone, along with the simultaneous disappearance of the alliance's upper echelons, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance once again fell into even greater chaos.

Anyone with discerning eyes could see that this colossal organization, which had once ruled more than half of the Xuanhuang Realm, was already on the verge of collapse.

Perhaps in the short term, because of the former prestige of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor, cultivators could still maintain a certain degree of rationality.

But before long, chaos and slaughter would inevitably descend upon the land of Xuanhuang.

The upheaval within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance naturally could not escape the notice of the several Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association.

But Li Fan had no time to deal with them for the moment.

To prevent the Five Elders Association from disrupting his 'observation', Li Fan flicked his finger and shot out a sword shadow.

Sword qi swept across the Star Sea and, in an instant, descended into the Xuanhuang Realm.

Along the original boundary between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association, it gently carved a line.

Under the influence of the sword shadow, the essence devouring white mist barrier at the prefectural boundary vanished without a trace. In its place was a bottomless chasm.

Countless sword shadows seemed to permeate the space above the chasm.

Every cultivator who saw this chasm would immediately have the same thought arise in their minds.

Cross the line, and die!

This was not an empty threat meant to intimidate anyone. It was the absolute suppression of mortal life by Li Fan, a half-immortal.

Even the five Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had no choice but to obediently abide by this restriction.

With a casual gesture, Li Fan had prepared the stage for He Zhenghao.

His attention once again returned to the profound sound of chanting, which had created all these extraordinary changes.

After carefully savoring it, Li Fan seemed to realize something.

"If True Immortal seal script is a method of explaining the great principles of heaven and earth through written characters, then the profound sound of chanting originating from the Xuantian King, as well as the Immortal Protection Art of the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, use sound to analyze the Great Dao."

"If the two were compared horizontally at the same level, the actual power they display..."

Li Fan slightly furrowed his brows. For a moment, even he could not make a definite judgment.

The reason was that his current understanding of the Profound Sound system was simply too limited.

"Immortal seal script originated from the creation of the Inscription Dao Immortal."

"Could it be that this Profound Sound was also the work of some unknown True Immortal?"

Li Fan could not help but frown as he speculated.

Although his current knowledge of the Profound Sound consisted of only fragments, his experiment with He Zhenghao had already ignited endless fantasies within Li Fan regarding this mysterious power.

"Memories of the past can influence reality."

"Then, at the theoretical highest level, could one rewrite the actions within one's own memories and thereby alter and distort reality?"

"Thus achieving an effect similar to [Truth]."

The more Li Fan thought about it, the more shocked he became.

"However, that is merely the theoretical limit. Such a divine ability could never be achieved easily..."

Li Fan's thoughts were interrupted by the ingratiating communication intent transmitted from the Tianxuan Mirror, which was bound within the Immortal Formation in his hand.

"Junior Jing Xuan pays his respects to Senior Immortal."

The voice of the Tianxuan Mirror was exactly the same as that of the human clone Li Fan had once dealt with.

It truly lived up to its former status as an immortal artifact. Even though it had now fallen into the mortal world, its vision remained intact.

With a single glance, it had identified Li Fan's current realm.

"Jing Xuan..."

Li Fan coldly snorted inwardly and ignored the Tianxuan Mirror.

The divine sense clone within the Tianxuan Mirror likewise ignored the manifested figure before him.

He simply passed it by and flew toward the Museum of Divine Treasures.

Jing Xuan wore an embarrassed expression, unsure how he had offended this Senior Immortal.

However, he absolutely did not dare offend this suddenly appearing True Immortal.

Since the other party disliked him, he did not dare follow. He merely observed secretly from afar.

Naturally, nothing happening within the Tianxuan Mirror could escape his eyes.

Li Fan's divine sense clone traveled through the Museum of Divine Treasures with practiced ease, heading straight toward its central area.

There were countless treasures within the Tianxuan Mirror, and the Museum of Divine Treasures contained hundreds of millions of collected items.

Yet the only thing worthy of Li Fan's attention was the skull of Mr. Bai, placed at the very center of the museum.

As if it had known Li Fan's target from the beginning, a figure was already standing before the skull, waiting for him.

It was none other than Emperor Sanmo, the curator of the Museum of Divine Treasures.

Because Li Fan was merely a divine sense clone, he did not feel the urgent desire to devour it that [Truth] normally brought about when facing the skull.

Li Fan did not immediately take the skull. Instead, he studied Emperor Sanmo with interest.

Compared with the appearance of this old man that Li Fan had seen thousands of years ago in the Fallen Immortal Realm, his features seemed to have changed not at all.

"You knew I would come for this?"

Emperor Sanmo cupped his hands and replied neither servilely nor arrogantly, "There are many treasures in the collection, but the only one worthy of making a True Immortal go to such lengths is this one."

Li Fan reached out and took out Mr. Bai's skull.

He did not sense any restrictions or resistance. It seemed that the Emperor Sanmo before him had given up on making any futile struggle and had already withdrawn all his defensive measures without making a sound.

"You know that this skull is special?" Li Fan weighed Mr. Bai's skull in his hand and asked.

"This humble one does not know. It is merely the remains of an old friend. Upon seeing them again, I felt sorrow in my heart, so I simply kept them safe." Emperor Sanmo's words were clearly insincere.

"You dare lie before this self. Are you not afraid?" Li Fan's divine sense clone narrowed his eyes.

Emperor Sanmo cupped his hands again. "This humble one... cannot die. Even a True Immortal cannot truly kill me."

"But I have countless methods to make you wish you could live, yet be unable to die," Li Fan said with a smile.

Emperor Sanmo's expression remained unchanged. His aged face seemed to have already seen through everything. "Pain has long since lost all meaning to me. Naturally, there is nothing for me to fear."

The divine sense clone nodded slightly and, surprisingly, did not make things difficult for him any further. He merely said calmly, "Take me around this Museum of Divine Treasures."

The skull in his hand had already disappeared.

"Immortal, this way please."

"If we follow the normal viewing order stipulated by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, then the first stop should be the [Era], the creation of objects through deduction. However, this humble one has a different opinion."

"To tour the Museum of Divine Treasures, one should begin with a relic."

The divine sense clone followed Emperor Sanmo as they slowly ventured deeper into the Museum of Divine Treasures.

The previous visit had not reached this seemingly restricted area that was not open to the public.

...

Outside the Tianxuan Mirror.

Li Fan's true body held Mr. Bai's skull in his hand.

He sensed the intense desire transmitted by [Truth].

This was not the first time he had encountered such a reaction. However, compared to before, Li Fan, who had already ascended to the Immortal realm, could sense that [Truth]'s desire was even stronger.

"Eternal Remnant Will..."

"What secret is hidden within Mr. Bai's skull?"

Li Fan first suppressed the Tianxuan Mirror with a flip of his hand. Protected by a ring of white light from an Immortal formation, he began attempting to have [Truth] devour the Eternal Remnant Will.

It was somewhat similar to when he had previously devoured Tianyang's Eternal Remnant Will.

A faint dizziness suddenly swept through his mind, and the scene before his eyes abruptly changed.

"Hm?"

Li Fan quickly regained consciousness and looked around.

"It is not like Tianyang's remnant will from before, where I took Tianyang's place and acted within his obsession."

"Instead, it seems that I am personally participating in it, descending into it?"

It was as though he were still within the void. Darkness surrounded him, vast and boundless.

Li Fan flew forward for some distance before suddenly stopping. His eyes narrowed.

Not far away, a figure stood upright.

Li Fan was not unfamiliar with this person.

His appearance was precisely that of Su Bai, Mr. Bai.

Yet the moment Li Fan saw him, his heart began pounding violently.

It was extremely similar to the instinctive warning that only appeared when he encountered a life or death crisis.

It was as though he had encountered some incomparably dangerous and terrifying existence. Even though the other party had not made a single move from beginning to end, the unease in Li Fan's heart continued to intensify.

"What is going on?"

Li Fan was deeply suspicious.

Su Bai was merely a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign.

Not to mention that he had already fallen. Even at his peak, Li Fan, who had already reached the Half-Immortal realm, was confident that he would not lose to him.

Then why was such an inexplicable sense of danger appearing within the illusion of the Eternal Remnant Will left behind by the other party?

Li Fan silently called upon [Truth], but there was no response. His divine sense clone also sensed that his true body was still within the void of the Darkest Star Sea.

Only a strand of his soul had entered Su Bai's Eternal Remnant Will. It would not truly affect his life or death in reality.

Even after confirming this point, however, the sensation of his heart pounding with fear still refused to subside.

Gradually, Li Fan finally came to his senses.

The strange feeling in his heart was not a warning of a life or death crisis.

It was because...

He was instinctively afraid when facing a hunter of a higher order.

It was urging Li Fan to flee as quickly as possible.

"Higher order? Fear?"

Li Fan was stunned at first, then felt an inexplicable sense of absurdity.

He was now at the Half-Immortal realm.

Su Bai was merely a Longevity Immortal of the Xuanhuang Realm.

How could he possibly feel fear toward him?

Filled with endless confusion and unwillingness to accept it, Li Fan once again carefully examined the "Mr. Bai" before him.

Only then did he vaguely notice that something was wrong.

Although they possessed the same face, the Mr. Bai in his memories, despite having eyes filled with compassion, was exactly as Xu Ke and the orphans from the dilapidated temple in Ningyuan City had described him.

Mr. Bai was the best person in the world, the most beautiful person, possessing all the finest qualities under heaven and earth.

But the one standing before him...

There was an indescribable evilness and eeriness about him.

Even though the other party's eyes remained closed, Li Fan seemed able to sense the overwhelming killing intent and madness within them.

"What exactly is the difference between them..."

Li Fan was still secretly alarmed.

Then, not far away, the sinister Su Bai suddenly opened his eyes.

There was no sensation of pain whatsoever.

The scene before Li Fan's eyes changed, then changed again.

It was as though he had returned to the moment just before.

But only Li Fan himself knew...

Just now, he had been instantly killed by the sinister Su Bai.

A drop of cold sweat slid down Li Fan's forehead.

"Could it be that my cultivation in reality was not carried over into this Eternal Remnant Will?"

That was the first thought that appeared in Li Fan's mind.

But after confirming the matter, Li Fan immediately rejected it.

"I am indeed at the Half-Immortal realm."

"Then how exactly did I die just now?"

Li Fan was both shocked and furious.

"No wonder that phantom remained motionless for so long. It was actually giving me time to prepare."

He discovered that within this illusion, it seemed that he could use everything he had learned without any hindrance.

Thus, this time, Li Fan chose to strike first.

"Nine Heavens Descending Mortal Finger!"

A gigantic palm descended from the heavens.

It locked onto the sinister Su Bai and attempted to crush him like an insignificant ant.

But just as the divine ability was about to land on him, Su Bai's figure suddenly vanished.

Li Fan was stunned. Then he abruptly came to his senses and swiftly looked upward.

He saw that, at some unknown point, he had become as tiny as an ant and was now standing on the fingertip of the sinister Su Bai!

Previous

Next

Chapter 1413: Instantly Slaying Evil Su Bai

Evil Su Bai had actually imitated Li Fan in the blink of an eye, likewise executing a Nine Heavens Descending Mortal Finger. Moreover, its power was thousands upon thousands of times greater than Li Fan's.

An indescribable force descended with overwhelming might. Unlike the previous time, Li Fan did not die without any awareness. Instead, he could clearly feel the process of his body and soul being crushed into pieces, inch by inch, again and again.

Given the strength Evil Su Bai had displayed earlier, the ability to instantly kill Li Fan, it was obvious that he was deliberately torturing him.

Li Fan could even vaguely see the faintly pleased expression on the other's face.

"You..."

The scene before his eyes changed again and again.

Li Fan once more returned to the moment before he had encountered Evil Su Bai.

After dying twice in succession, Li Fan was extremely frustrated. However, after many years of reincarnation, his temperament was extraordinary. He quickly calmed down.

His earlier shock and fury had merely come from the enormous disparity between what he believed to be the Longevity Immortal realm and the fact that such an existence could instantly kill a half-immortal like himself.

But now, Li Fan could be certain that Evil Su Bai's strength was definitely not as simple as the Longevity Immortal realm.

In the cultivation world, the strong were respected and the weak were preyed upon. There was nothing worth getting angry about.

"This is not a real world. It is merely an illusory scene constructed from the Eternal Remnant Will."

"That Evil Su Bai appears here and attacks without saying a word. Moreover, he gives the person being tested a certain amount of time to prepare."

"Even if the test is failed, after death, one can immediately revive..."

As Li Fan pondered, he had already vaguely understood the content of Su Bai's Eternal Remnant Will test.

Defeat Evil Su Bai.

"In reality, I am afraid that no one in the entire Xuanhuang Realm or the Darkest Star Sea could accomplish this."

"But this is an illusion created by the Eternal Remnant Will. It can be regarded as a thought experiment. Any method that can be conceived is possible..."

"To a certain extent, this is an advanced version of the Tianxuan Mirror's [Sitting and Debating Dao] and combat functions."

Li Fan suddenly understood.

"Immortal Formation, Primordial Mystic Spirit!"

His thoughts raced. Within his mind, the Final Dissociation Disc suddenly burst forth with endless radiance.

It was as if it had genuinely completed the missing portions of the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation in an instant.

Black and white light continuously revolved around Li Fan. Within the void, it opened up earth, fire, water, and wind, evolving an entire world.

Li Fan stood at the center, resembling the legendary God of Creation, the Creator.

He had the illusion that as long as he remained within this formation, he could control and weaken everything covered by the formation according to his thoughts and will.

The Final Dissociation Disc naturally had not yet obtained the complete formation diagram of the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation.

However, having already mastered three-tenths of it, it had gained both a qualitative and quantitative understanding of the complete grand formation's specific might.

Thus, with the assistance of Su Bai's Eternal Remnant Will space, it directly skipped the intermediate process and simply "conceived" the final result.

Within the void, Li Fan sat cross-legged at the center of the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation.

The world he had created rapidly spread toward the four prefectures.

Very soon, it engulfed Evil Su Bai, who stood motionless in his original position.

"I am the ruler of the Immortal Realm!"

Li Fan invoked the might of the Immortal Formation, transforming the countless laws of the world he had created into chains, attempting to bind Evil Su Bai.

The Primordial Mystic Spirit, formed through the fusion of nine formations, indeed revealed its terrifying might.

Points of light continuously flowed away from Evil Su Bai's body and merged into the world Li Fan had created.

At the same time, countless phantoms of Su Bai appeared throughout heaven and earth.

Every one of them seemed to possess the strength of the Longevity Immortal realm.

Like countless ants, they swarmed toward Evil Su Bai.

As more and more chains tightly wrapped around him, Su Bai seemed to give up struggling. He remained completely motionless.

He simply allowed the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation to continuously weaken his strength.

Yet the trace of an evil, charming smile on his face made Li Fan deeply uneasy.

Soon, Li Fan understood the source of his unease.

The Immortal Formation, Primordial Mystic Spirit, seemed completely unable to touch the upper limit of Evil Su Bai's strength.

Even when it continuously weakened him at the maximum rate permitted by the principles of the formation, reducing his power without end...

For Evil Su Bai, it seemed to be completely meaningless.

"How is this possible?!" Li Fan was so shocked that he even began to wonder whether this entire test was simply a joke that Mr. Bai had played on his successors before his death.

In theory, the complete Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation could transform immortals into mortals and mortals into immortals with every breath.

It was equivalent to having the cultivation of a True Immortal continuously stripped away.

Yet Evil Su Bai remained as immovable as a mountain and as deep as an abyss.

"Nameless realm?"

Li Fan's doubts grew increasingly intense.

Meanwhile, Evil Su Bai seemed to have grown tired of this boring game.

He even took the initiative to transmit his power, allowing the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation to drain him to its heart's content in a single burst.

An endless torrent of True Immortal cultivation surged into the world Li Fan had created.

Within the boundless void, it expanded infinitely, covering hundreds of millions of li in an instant.

Even the Final Dissociation Disc, an Immortal-grade artifact, gradually reached the limits of its capacity.

Cracks began to appear across it. In the end, it could no longer withstand the burden and shattered inch by inch.

The "Immortal Realm" created by Li Fan also ushered in its apocalypse along with the collapse of the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation.

This time, Li Fan was buried in the great calamity of the Immortal Realm's destruction.

...

"Nameless True Immortal Realm."

Not long afterward, Li Fan revived once again. Within the void, he began pondering the methods he possessed that could theoretically contend with the Nameless realm.

"United with the Dao, one with the Dao..."

When Evil Su Bai attacked once again, a seven-colored brush suddenly appeared in Li Fan's hand.

"Restrain!"

With a thought, a True Immortal seal script character appeared in the void before him.

It was like an invisible golden lock, imprisoning Evil Su Bai.

"Chaos!"

"Hunger!"

"Trapped!"

"Ancient!"

...

One True Immortal seal script character after another, each representing the laws of the Great Dao between heaven and earth, continuously flew toward Evil Su Bai under Li Fan's sweeping brushstrokes.

At this moment, Li Fan seemed to have transformed into the legendary [Dao Inscription Immortal].

Exhausting the principles of heaven and earth, he wielded the Dao to oppose his enemy.

Countless characters reverted from their seal script forms back into lines.

They intertwined into a hazy, fantastical dreamscape, continuously dragging Evil Su Bai into it.

Li Fan's body was also enveloped by the endless Great Dao, as if he himself had become the [Dao], making his true form impossible to discern.

Seeing that this battle finally seemed to offer some hope of victory, Li Fan happened to glance toward Evil Su Bai.

Yet he still saw a trace of mockery on the other's face.

Li Fan's heart suddenly jolted.

What happened next was a scene that filled him with terror.

Evil Su Bai, trapped at the center of the vortex of the Great Dao, slowly extended his right hand.

The endless lines seemed to be drawn by some mysterious force. Their targets shifted, and all of them converged toward him.

Within three breaths, the endless Great Dao that Li Fan had written completely disappeared.

It transformed into a crystal-clear candy.

Evil Su Bai tossed it into his mouth. With a loud crunch, he chewed it into pieces.

This time, Li Fan experienced what it felt like to be refined into candy and swallowed alive.

...

"Even the Dao Inscription Immortal is not enough?"

At this moment, Li Fan no longer felt any shock or anger.

He seemed almost numb.

He temporarily put aside whether Evil Su Bai's displayed strength was reasonable or even possible.

All he thought about was how he could kill him, or at least defeat him.

"Ordinary methods won't work. Then I will use what I am best at!"

Once again, he stood upon the battlefield of the challenge.

“Ascend!”

Li Fan let out a furious shout.

The phantoms formed from the power of ascension feedback from the Xuanhuang Realm stored within his body all transformed into reality one after another.

Moreover, within this illusory space, their numbers were amplified ten millionfold.

In an instant, countless Xuanhuang Realms glittered like stars.

All of them ascended to immortality!

Even Su Bai, the Evil Su Bai who had come to kill Li Fan, could not help but be drawn to this bizarre sight for a moment.

Billions upon billions of Xuanhuang Realms were on the verge of reaching the critical point of transformation.

Yet with a single thought from Li Fan, they lost the driving force behind their sublimation.

In an instant, they plummeted from the pinnacle of their momentum.

Like countless enormous stones falling into the depths of the sea, they instantly stirred up terrifying waves!

Countless strands of black qi emerged from every crack in the space.

Accumulating bit by bit, they transformed into hideous and terrifying vicious beasts.

Among them, the places where the black qi gathered most densely even evolved into human forms!

They bore an uncanny resemblance to the Xuanhuang Realm's Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, almost eight or nine parts out of ten.

Even Li Fan, the one who had caused all this, had not anticipated such a scene. His pupils contracted sharply as he stared at the sight of countless demons running rampant before him.

The Dao Annihilation Calamity, black qi taking form.

It surged toward the only two living beings remaining in this spacetime.

The vast majority of them targeted the Evil Su Bai.

Relying on the cultivation of the Nameless True Immortal that existed within his imagination, Li Fan could barely hold on and keep himself from perishing.

He carefully observed Evil Su Bai's performance as he faced the attacks of these seemingly endless beasts formed from black qi.

Evil Su Bai was no longer as composed as before, nor did he retain his previous attitude of treating everything as a game.

He proceeded with extreme caution, making sure that not a single beast formed from black qi could touch his body.

With every gesture, vast numbers of the beasts were reduced to nothingness.

But the beast tide surged endlessly, and for a time, even he was trapped.

Evil Su Bai slightly furrowed his brows, seemingly growing annoyed.

Li Fan saw only the figure's body suddenly ripple like waves.

With every ripple, another figure of Su Bai appeared within this spacetime.

Their faces were not as distinct as that of Evil Su Bai.

Yet Li Fan could still vaguely distinguish their expressions.

Some were compassionate toward all living beings, like Mr. Bai of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Some were like wandering vagabonds, unrestrained and carefree.

Some were like scholars, useless and timid.

Some were like butchers, their bodies radiating overwhelming killing intent.

Every one of these Mr. Bais possessed cultivation identical to that of Evil Su Bai.

And all these Su Bais were just like the beasts formed from black qi.

Endless!

Under the encirclement of the army of Mr. Bais, the black tide of world destruction was also gradually subdued.

Yet throughout the entire encirclement, they deliberately did not attack Li Fan.

It seemed they intended to leave Li Fan's life to Evil Su Bai.

Looking at the countless pairs of eyes staring fixedly at him, some mocking, some ridiculing, some compassionate.

Li Fan decisively chose to end his own life.

...

Another rebirth.

“Even the black qi of the Dao Annihilation Calamity is not enough?”

This time, Li Fan did not resist. Instead, he quietly pondered in the void.

“Perhaps only the true Dao Annihilation Calamity can kill him.”

Unfortunately, Li Fan had no idea what the true Dao Annihilation Calamity actually looked like.

Baseless imagination would not take effect within this eternal remnant memory space.

Before Li Fan's thoughts could spread any further, Evil Su Bai killed him instantly once again.

Over the long period that followed, Li Fan exhausted every method at his disposal, desperately resisting Evil Su Bai while simultaneously pondering a way to break out of this predicament.

Although the damage to his soul caused by each death within the eternal remnant memory could be ignored, once the number of deaths reached a certain threshold, his true body in reality inevitably began to change.

“As long as I wish, I can withdraw from this trial at any time.”

When the upper limit of his soul was reduced by one third, the reborn Li Fan sensed a mysterious change.

Yet he did not choose to flee immediately.

Instead, he solemnly contemplated the matter and made his decision.

“Actually, I already knew how to deal with this Evil Su Bai long ago.”

“Even if I reincarnated countless more times, I am afraid the only possibility of defeating him would remain unchanged.”

Li Fan remained deep in thought.

“I can easily suppress and kill this manifestation of Evil Su Bai...”

“But I would have to risk exposing [Truth].”

“Is it really worth taking such a risk?”

After an unknown amount of time, he once again died at the hands of Evil Su Bai.

Before his death, he looked at that hateful smile.

When he was reborn, Li Fan's gaze suddenly became resolute.

“First, I believe in the character of Mr. Bai of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“He was willing to sacrifice himself to the Dao for the sake of all mortals under heaven. He should not have planted a trap within his own remains.”

“Furthermore...”

An unprecedented brilliance erupted from Li Fan's eyes.

“I have already ascended to immortality. I am no longer mortal.”

“[Truth] has accompanied me for so long. How could a mere set of remains possibly take it away from me?”

In the real world, Li Fan's true body was already prepared to activate [Truth] at any moment.

Within the eternal remnant memory illusion, he looked at Evil Su Bai flying toward him with a sinister grin.

Li Fan's expression was arrogant and composed as he softly recited:

“When truth is made false, the false becomes true.”

The flying Evil Su Bai suddenly came to a halt.

An expression of disbelief appeared on his face.

Then his body gradually faded away, disappearing before Li Fan's eyes.

The invincible Evil Su Bai, the supreme existence that had effortlessly crushed the Nameless True Immortal and could remain unharmed even amid the great tide of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Just like that, he was effortlessly killed by a single thought from Li Fan.

“So this is...”

“The Dao of True and False.”

“It is not baseless imagination.”

“It is a supreme Great Dao that is truly within reach.”

“It is just that I am still a very long way from truly controlling it.”

Having experienced the sensation of wielding supreme authority within the illusion, Li Fan felt a faint sense of loss and melancholy.

In theory, he had successfully killed Evil Su Bai.

The eternal remnant memory space gradually collapsed.

In a daze, he returned to reality.

Li Fan felt the excitement of [Truth] after having enjoyed a great feast.

“Xuanhuang Celestial Venerable truly loves all living beings.”

“Seeking life from death, carrying all under heaven into [Truth].”

A moment later, a prompt from [Truth] sounded in Li Fan's mind.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1414: The Truly Kind And Good Mr. Bai

“[Dao Origin] function unlocked.”

“Dao Origin: When [Truth] can be activated, you may designate an area to return alongside you to the state of the established anchor point.”

“Alternatively, you may give up this opportunity to use [Truth] and designate an area to offset [Dao Annihilation].”

...

Li Fan had not yet had time to carefully contemplate the meaning of the prompt regarding [Truth] when countless memories, like scattered points of starlight, suddenly exploded within his mind.

Most were fragmented nodes that flashed past in an instant, lacking any continuity.

But the memories contained within this Eternal Remnant Will skull were simply too numerous. It was as if this eternal remnant will, capable of existing throughout the ages, had been formed from countless different thoughts layered upon one another.

Even with Li Fan's divine soul and willpower being no weaker than that of a True Immortal, he nearly lost himself amid the ceaseless impact of these countless memory fragments, which surged like a mighty river and enormous tide.

Fortunately, [Truth] remained standing firmly within the current, shining brilliantly without changing in the slightest. There were also those extremely rare, yet largest, fragments within the torrent of consciousness.

They were like lamps shining through the white mist, lighthouses upon the endless sea, or the North Star surrounded by countless stars.

They continuously guided Li Fan and marked his direction, preventing him from becoming completely lost.

Boom!

Li Fan's consciousness slowly merged into Su Bai's remaining memories.

A world filled with ruins. The air was saturated with a thick smell of blood. It resembled a living hell, with corpses piled across the ground.

There was not the slightest sound between heaven and earth. It was utterly silent.

Blood flowed and gathered into a river.

From the dark red reflection, Li Fan saw the face of this body. It was none other than the Heavenly Doctor.

However, the "Heavenly Doctor" reflected in the river had eyes filled with confusion and fear.

His body trembled uncontrollably.

A voice suddenly sounded within his mind.

The voice was exactly the same as Su Bai's.

Yet it was filled with an evil and sinister quality.

"Well? Are you satisfied?"

"You always wanted to do this. I did it for you."

The Heavenly Doctor's face twisted and trembled. It transformed back into Su Bai's original appearance.

"Something I always wanted to do?" His voice trembled as he questioned in despair.

The Evil Su Bai's voice was filled with mockery.

"Isn't that so? You were originally the Venerable of this world, yet your Heavenly Soul reincarnated into the Xuanhuang Realm, attempting to use the Dao of Medicine to gradually plunge the Xuanhuang Realm into internal chaos, leaving it unable to spare any attention for an expedition..."

"What I did for you is not essentially the same as what that Heavenly Doctor did?"

"Only a little more thorough."

"Hahahahaha..."

The Evil Su Bai's laughter echoed throughout the consciousness space.

"Oh, oh, the real one is here."

"Look at the expression on his face. Truly delightful!"

Su Bai gritted his teeth and cursed furiously.

"You damned beast!"

His anger could no longer be suppressed and erupted like a volcano. His body trembled, and his aura once again transformed into the appearance of Mr. Bai from the Xuanhuang Realm.

The Evil Su Bai continued to taunt him, but his voice gradually grew weaker.

"There's no need to get so angry."

"No matter how many of these ants die, it doesn't matter at all. In my lifetime, I have witnessed things that were billions upon billions of times worse..."

"Hehehe, this is far more brutal than that. At the very least, I was kind enough to leave their corpses behind."

"Besides, I am you, and you are me..."

His voice became as faint as the buzzing of mosquitoes until it could no longer be heard.

Sensing the figure of the Heavenly Doctor hurriedly approaching from nearby, Su Bai's face was filled with pain.

But in the end, he still gritted his teeth and fled at high speed.

...

Within the endless dark void.

Su Bai reached out and touched the High Wall. It seemed as though an invisible barrier blocked his path, preventing him from advancing any farther.

"The High Wall..."

Constant thoughts of urging, excitement, and impatience continuously echoed within his mind.

Su Bai pondered for a moment, then took out the immortal artifacts bestowed by the Immortal Realm, which were part of the legacy of the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

He arranged them in a row.

"With this many immortal artifacts self destructing, together with the assistance of the Xuanhuang Realm, it should be enough to help me cross this threshold."

Su Bai hesitated for a long time. In the end, he still detonated the immortal artifacts one after another.

In an instant, countless suns seemed to rise simultaneously at the edge of the star sea. They even illuminated the Darkest Star Sea until it became snow white.

Yet all that boundless light and heat was concentrated upon Su Bai.

His appearance could no longer be seen, and his expression could not be made out. There was only a dazzling white figure.

The figure stepped forward.

It was as if he had vanished into thin air. Part of his body disappeared from sight.

Half of one foot had crossed the constraints of the High Wall. He was about to completely escape from the Darkest Star Sea enclosed by the High Wall.

Yet Su Bai still turned back reluctantly and took one final look at the Xuanhuang Realm.

There were simply too many things there that he could not let go of.

At the moment when Su Bai was lost in thought, countless images suddenly surged into his mind for no apparent reason.

The contents of those images caused this naturally kind-hearted Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable's mind to tremble violently.

Mountains of corpses and seas of blood. Countless worlds being destroyed one after another. And the culprit responsible for this heaven shaking massacre actually looked exactly like him!

At the same time, Su Bai sensed an unprecedented feeling of danger within his heart.

His thoughts raced.

Without the slightest hesitation, he actually pulled back the leg that had already crossed the High Wall!

The blazing radiance surrounding his body gradually disappeared.

Still shaken by what had happened, Su Bai paid no attention to the Xuanhuang Realm's consciousness, which had suddenly gone berserk because of his inexplicable change of heart and could not understand what had happened.

"What... exactly was that just now?"

While his expression continued to change, his own voice suddenly sounded within his mind.

Both its tone and cadence...

Were indescribably strange.

"Oh? There is actually another one hiding here."

"Tsk, tsk, tsk..."

"Sure enough, you're still this foolish."

The change in the voice even affected Su Bai's body.

Traces of evil and madness emerged between his brows and eyes.

At the same time, the Xuanhuang Realm's Heavenly Dao consciousness attached to his body also gradually lost its rationality. All kinds of malicious words emerged one after another.

...

Heavenly Sword Sect.

Within the Sword Tomb, countless swords stood upright in the ground.

Upon every sword floated a phantom. These were sword spirits that had naturally developed after being tempered by countless years of time.

And now, this innumerable multitude of sword spirits was confronting the same person.

It was none other than Su Bai, who sat cross legged at the center.

Countless streams of sword qi crisscrossed the area like countless scalpels, cutting apart Su Bai's divine soul and consciousness.

Su Bai's face remained calm throughout.

Yet he could not avoid occasionally revealing a trace of pain.

At the edge of the Sword Tomb, the Heavenly Sword Sect's Eldest Senior Brother, Situ Yao, watched this scene with an inscrutable expression.

He continued drinking, one gulp after another.

Only this time, what flowed from the wine gourd was no longer wine.

It was water.

No one knew how much time passed.

Buzz!

A violent shockwave suddenly erupted from Su Bai's body.

The Sword Tomb instantly became a complete mess.

Countless sword spirits vanished into thin air.

Situ Yao's brows twitched violently.

But this was not the time to feel distressed. He flashed to Su Bai's side.

"Celestial Venerable, how is the situation?"

There was a long silence.

Only after a long while did Su Bai's somewhat weak voice finally sound.

"I severed away all the parts of my divine soul that had been contaminated. For now, it has been suppressed."

"But..."

"Just as that person said, this can only delay things for a period of time. Sooner or later, he will come looking for me again."

Situ Yao's expression became solemn.

"So, are you still willing to continue carrying out that plan? To stake the lives of everyone in your Heavenly Sword Sect?" Su Bai asked softly.

Situ Yao took another large gulp of water from the gourd.

“Even if we stay here, our destruction is only a matter of time.”

“We cultivators of the sword have never feared death.”

“Besides, he is looking for you. We mere ants may not necessarily catch his attention.”

As he spoke, he had already made his decision.

Su Bai’s expression turned solemn. Trembling, he rose to his feet and bowed to Situ Yao with the body of the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

Situ Yao accepted the bow calmly.

…

Xuanhuang Realm. Ningyuan City.

The vitality of mortals was as tenacious as that of wild grass.

The ruins of the past had once again developed into a bustling city.

It was even more prosperous than it had been back then.

Su Bai wandered through the streets, an expression of both relief and melancholy on his face.

At last, he had reached the destination of this journey.

His former little house.

Recalling the bits and pieces of what had happened here in the past, Su Bai was filled with emotion.

Looking at those adorable children from back then, who had now grown into adults, Su Bai's eyes were filled with joy once more.

“They should not be deprived of their hope of living.”

“I must die.”

He spoke softly.

“Before I die, let me do one small thing more for this world.”

Defying the principles of Heaven and Earth to prove the Dao of longevity!

A strange transformation descended upon the Xuanhuang Realm.

…

Immortal Ruins.

A tall figure resembling a skeleton was sitting cross-legged upon the earth, at the center of the mysterious character.

A figure silently appeared behind her.

Yet this True Immortal who had comprehended the Dao did not notice anything at all.

That figure was Su Bai!

However, at this moment, he had changed into a black robe. An indescribable madness filled his eyes and brows.

“This is the culprit behind the calamity that brought disaster upon your Xuanhuang Realm, and even this entire Star Sea.”

“Just give me a nod, and I will kill her right now.”

Evil Su Bai flicked his finger against the Hunger Immortal's head, producing a crisp sound.

Yet this True Immortal who had comprehended the Dao did not notice anything whatsoever.

She remained completely immersed in her own comprehension.

In response to Evil Su Bai, there was only a long silence.

Evil Su Bai did not urge either. Instead, he looked around the scene within the Immortal Ruins with great interest.

“You have spent so long comprehending a mere broken [Hunger] character, and this is all you have achieved.”

“I have killed countless pieces of trash like you.”

“And yet, something at this level of trash has brought you thousands of years of suffering…”

His tone was filled with mockery.

“Good? Evil? Do they have any meaning in the face of survival?”

Evil Su Bai raised his foot and repeatedly ground it against the Hunger Immortal's face, trampling her beneath his feet.

He even leaned forward, bringing his face close to hers to examine her.

As if he had thought of something, he occasionally let out a soft chuckle.

Yet the Hunger Immortal remained completely unresponsive from beginning to end.

“Well? Do you believe me now? I wasn't lying to you, was I?”

“Everything you saw was not an illusion.”

“It truly happened.”

“The cruelty of reality far exceeds your imagination.”

“Someone as good-natured as you could never adapt to it. Just leave it to me…”

Although he regarded a True Immortal as nothing more than an ant, Evil Su Bai was exceptionally patient with Mr. Bai of the Xuanhuang Realm.

He kept talking, even becoming somewhat long-winded as he tried to persuade him.

Mr. Bai of the Xuanhuang Realm's voice suddenly rang out.

“You are so powerful…”

“Then why did a single High Wall stop you?”

Evil Su Bai's voice abruptly stopped.

He suddenly exerted force and viciously stomped down.

The Hunger Immortal, who had been sitting cross-legged upon the ground of the Immortal Ruins, was kicked directly onto the ground.

The golden chains wrapped around her body rattled.

…

“So Evil Su Bai was not merely an unfounded fantasy of Mr. Bai.”

“He actually exists?”

Just as Li Fan's true body's consciousness was immersed in the Eternal Remnant Will left behind by Su Bai and was filled with immense shock, his strand of divine sense clone in the Museum of Divine Treasures, under the guidance of Emperor Sanmo, arrived deep within the museum.

They stopped before the ruins of a seemingly ordinary building.

The ruins had been placed together with other historical ruins of the Xuanhuang Realm. There seemed to be nothing particularly special about them.

“Immortal, do you recognize this place?”

Because of the astonishing scenes Li Fan's true body had sensed within Su Bai's Eternal Remnant Will, he seemed somewhat distracted.

Only after a long while did he recover.

“This place…”

After carefully identifying it, Li Fan was slightly stunned.

“Nanming Sacred Beast Mountain?” He looked at the old man beside him and asked somewhat uncertainly.

“Immortal has excellent eyesight.” Emperor Sanmo nodded.

“But this is not the Sacred Beast Mountain of the Xuanhuang Realm. I brought this place here from my homeland and preserved it here.”

“It has already been here for so many years that I no longer know exactly how long it has been.”

Emperor Sanmo's voice was filled with the vicissitudes of time.

Upon hearing this, Li Fan's expression gradually became solemn.

He pondered the meaning behind Emperor Sanmo's words.

“The Xuanhuang Realm originally had no demon beasts.”

“We came here after fleeing from another possibility.”

Emperor Sanmo did not keep him guessing for long and spoke bluntly.

Li Fan's expression trembled slightly.

“An infinite future contains infinite possibilities.”

“But not every possibility can continue forever.”

Emperor Sanmo sighed softly.

“We multiplied and lived in the Xuanhuang Realm for far too long. We had even forgotten our ancient past.”

“We thought we could continue surviving here. Yet we never expected that this place would eventually reach its own end.”

“Thus, Emperor Yi once again led the race onto the road of escape. But this time, I decided not to leave.”

Emperor Sanmo flashed into the ruins.

It was as if he had returned to his former homeland.

“Why?” Li Fan's clone asked curiously.

“Even if the Immortal Ruins hang across the sky here and the Star Sea is declining, this may still be one of the few places that can be considered relatively safe.”

Emperor Sanmo smiled.

Combining Emperor Sanmo's words with what he had seen in the Eternal Remnant Will, Li Fan's eyes narrowed as countless thoughts surged through his mind.

…

Within Su Bai's Eternal Remnant Will.

Li Fan's true body's mind trembled.

Within the memories of the departed Mr. Bai, he saw just how powerful that Evil Su Bai truly was.

Merely a trace of his corruption was enough to allow Su Bai, who was in the Longevity Immortal realm, to possess power capable of crushing a True Immortal.

And as the corruption deepened, Su Bai briefly experienced the true power of Evil Su Bai.

The world was no longer singular.

Instead, countless possibilities existed simultaneously.

Su Bai saw the countless possibilities that emerged within the Xuanhuang Realm after his death.

Amid the countless endings of destruction, it was as if he had glimpsed the only path to survival.

Thus, Li Fan vaguely understood why this Eternal Remnant Will had been established in such a manner.
