Chapter 1415: The Heavenly Doctor Realizes The Dao And Dies

"Outside the High Wall, the existence of the Evil Su Bai is truly too shocking."

"It is not merely the strength of the Evil Su Bai. It also involves the truth of the entire mortal world."

"Countless possibilities emerging together..."

"No, it is far more complicated than that!"

Li Fan watched countless memories of the departed Mr. Bai flash past like meteors.

He could not help but look again at the countdown of 7.36 million years on his [Truth] panel.

"In the memories of the Evil Su Bai, there was not only slaughter on an unimaginable scale. There were also scenes of a truly terrifying calamity descending."

"Even with the Evil Su Bai's unfathomably powerful strength, he could only flee in a sorry state before that world destroying calamity!"

"That was the instantaneous collapse of an entire world line!"

"The true Dao Annihilation Calamity!"

With the power of Mr. Bai's Eternal Remnant Will, and through the eyes of the Evil Su Bai, Li Fan personally experienced the terrifying nature of the true Dao Annihilation Calamity.

From the Xuanhuang Realm, to the Darkest Star Sea, to the Great Void Beyond Star Sea, and even the countless cultivation worlds. Including the Immortal Realm floating above the Infinite Sea.

This unimaginably vast and magnificent world, whose existence was beyond words, collapsed in an instant.

Without any warning, without making the slightest sound, everything was annihilated. Even the concept of nothingness itself ceased to exist.

Only by crossing into another possibility before the Dao Annihilation Calamity reached oneself was there any chance of surviving.

...

"So, the 7.36 million years is the countdown calculated by [Truth], marking the time remaining until all possibilities disappear?"

Li Fan's thoughts churned violently, and his heart could not calm down for a long time.

"The Evil Su Bai frantically slaughtered his other selves across different possibilities. After sensing the existence of Su Bai within the High Wall, he exhausted every possible method, all in an attempt to make Mr. Bai cross the High Wall once again and voluntarily submit to him..."

"It seems that, to him, his own existence across the other possibilities is extremely important."

"Every time the Evil Su Bai slaughtered himself in his memories, it was accompanied by slaughter on an unimaginable scale. It was a hundred times more cruel than what he had once done in the Heavenly Law Realm. Not even corpses, blood, or memories were left behind. Even nothingness itself was made to fill the world."

"Compared to the actions of the Evil Su Bai, my own world destroying actions are simply insignificant."

"Is this simply the nature of the Evil Su Bai himself, or is this the way all existences at his level beyond the High Wall conduct themselves?"

Li Fan took a deep breath.

"No matter which it is, the existence of the Evil Su Bai is even more terrifying to the Xuanhuang Realm than the Dao Annihilation Calamity."

"At the very least, there are no signs that the Dao Annihilation Calamity is about to descend. But if the Evil Su Bai beyond the High Wall were summoned here, I fear everything within the High Wall would instantly be reduced to nothingness."

"So Mr. Bai chose to die willingly, temporarily severing his connection with the Evil Su Bai. He even altered all the memories of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao that followed him, as well as everything related to the Evil Su Bai within the inheritance trials of the Fallen Immortal Realm."

"Only within his own Eternal Remnant Will did he preserve the crucial information."

The countless complicated memories slowly faded away.

Li Fan returned to reality. The skull in his hand transformed into specks of starlight and disappeared into heaven and earth.

"During the period when Mr. Bai was contaminated by the Evil Su Bai, his perspective was able to transcend a single possibility."

"Although I do not know exactly what he saw, it can almost certainly be determined that this information was left behind for those who came after him."

"No matter what method is used, the person who can pass the trials and, at least in theory, defeat the Evil Su Bai is the only one worthy of being entrusted with this."

"The secret behind how the Evil Su Bai repeatedly survived the Dao Annihilation Calamity and grew stronger throughout it!"

A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

"[Truth] absorbed Tianyang's Eternal Will and awakened the [Embodiment] function. This originated from Tianyang cultivating [Transformation of Embodiment into Object] almost to the extreme."

"And this time, the awakening of the [Dao Origin] function was likewise due to the cultivation method of the Evil Su Bai."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes.

"Dao Annihilation. Dao Origin."

...

Deep within the Museum of Divine Treasures, at the ruins of the Nanming Sacred Beast Mountain.

Li Fan's divine sense clone was still conversing with Emperor Sanmo.

"Our race will not always be so fortunate as to happen to arrive in a world where the Nanming Sacred Beast Mountain has remained completely intact."

"If we are unlucky and the energy sealed within the Sacred Beast Mountain there has already been completely exhausted, then it will truly be a dead end." Emperor Sanmo spoke with a cheerful smile.

Before Li Fan's eyes appeared the scene he had once witnessed within the Great Rift Vortex.

Emperor Yi had used the majestic divine face to extract a huge ancient bronze colored key from within the white serpent's body, then used it to activate the enormous energy within the Nanming Sacred Beast Mountain.

"So it is a consumable that can only be used once." Li Fan said thoughtfully.

Emperor Sanmo nodded.

"This is also why Emperor Yi allowed me and some of the other demon beasts not to follow him. Everyone is taking a gamble. After all, given the nature of our race, if Emperor Yi encounters an accident and his entire group is wiped out, then before long, another Emperor Yi will be born among the remaining members."

"Until now, that has not happened. It seems Emperor Yi has won his gamble again." Emperor Sanmo smiled once more.

"But who can guarantee that he will keep winning?" Li Fan asked in return.

Emperor Sanmo fell silent.

"Even jumping into another possibility cannot allow one to escape. I truly wonder where such a terrifying calamity originated." After a brief silence descended over the area, Li Fan sighed in a low voice.

"Immortal, why don't you listen to a story I have to tell?" Emperor Sanmo suddenly spoke.

"Not long ago... a strand of soul escaped from that device called [Era]. To be able to experience hundreds of billions of reincarnations within the Era while still maintaining a clear consciousness is an extremely rare characteristic, one unseen since ancient times."

Li Fan had also reincarnated within the Era. Hearing this, he immediately became interested.

"That sounds like excellent material for creating an Eternal Remnant Will." Li Fan thought to himself.

"How is he doing now?" Knowing that Emperor Sanmo would not bring this up without reason, Li Fan asked.

"He has completely disappeared from the world." Emperor Sanmo gently shook his head.

"He chose to hide himself within these ruins. The energy remaining within the ruins was capable of protecting his fragile soul. But there was ultimately too little of it, and it could not sustain him for long. He would rather die than be thrown back into the Era..."

"By the time I discovered him, he had already become far too weak."

"I could only help him maintain his vitality for a little longer. In the end, there was still nothing I could do to save him."

"However, during the final period of his life, I discussed many things with him. Based on the memories recounted by me and the entire race, he proposed a possible explanation for the cause of this terrifying world destroying calamity."

"Oh?" Li Fan's spirit was immediately stirred.

"But it is ultimately only a guess. Immortal, just listen to it as a story." Emperor Sanmo first gave this warning, then slowly continued.

"Everyone knows that above True Immortals, there are Nameless True Immortals. Nameless True Immortals merge with the Dao. From that point onward, no records of them remain in the mortal world, and they disappear without a trace. Since ancient times, no one knows how many have successfully proven the Dao and become Nameless True Immortals. But..."

Emperor Sanmo waved his hand, and a phantom appeared within the ruins before them.

Countless fine lines crisscrossed one another, supporting the entire world.

"Although reaching the Nameless realm and proving the Dao is difficult, and the probability is extremely small, when viewed across an infinite span of time, it is not actually such an ethereal and impossible matter."

"And every time a Nameless True Immortal is born..."

Emperor Sanmo repeatedly tapped his finger.

One of the vertical lines within the phantom before them disappeared.

Then another.

And another.

This continued for an unknown length of time.

The net of heaven and earth gradually became incomplete as more and more of it disappeared.

Slowly, it began to bend.

Li Fan's eyes suddenly narrowed.

In the end, the net of Heaven and Earth could no longer maintain its own form.

It collapsed with a thunderous crash!

Heaven and Earth itself collapsed along with it!

Emperor Sanmo waved his hand, and the phantom image before them disappeared.

“This is merely our most superficial understanding. It is not necessarily the truth closest to reality.”

“Perhaps every Great Dao of Heaven and Earth is far more important than we imagined. Even the absence of only a few Great Daos may be enough to cause the collapse of all possibilities.”

“Or perhaps, apart from an Nameless True Immortal merging with the Dao, there are other inexplicable factors that caused the Great Dao to disappear for no reason. That was what ultimately led to the unstoppable world destruction catastrophe…”

Emperor Sanmo's voice was faint and distant.

Yet before Li Fan's eyes, he seemed to see once again the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal that had been used to construct the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar.

“Perhaps this truth is already very close to reality.”

Emperor Sanmo continued, “It is precisely because countless possibilities collapsed in a chain reaction that escaping from one possibility to another became exceptionally easy.”

“Originally, even if we exhausted all the energy of the Nanming Sacred Beast Mountain, it still would not have been possible.”

“To put it simply, it can be seen as countless possibilities continuously collapsing. As a result, they are gradually becoming closer to one another…”

Li Fan listened intently.

Before his eyes, a scene seemed to emerge.

In the darkness, countless lines of light simultaneously lit up, arranged side by side, stretching endlessly into the distance.

But suddenly, some of them began to disappear from the darkness for no apparent reason.

Slowly, more and more lines of light vanished.

The ones that remained, under the influence of this inexplicable crisis, gradually drew closer together.

Thus, inevitably, they became twisted and chaotic.

“The Great Dao is annihilated.”

“The Great Dao crushes against itself.”

Li Fan recalled what he had seen in the memories of the Evil Su Bai, and a trace of coldness rose within his heart.

“What is the name of this soul? To have reached such a speculation, he certainly was no ordinary person.” Li Fan's thoughts stirred.

Emperor Sanmo did not conceal it either. He casually manifested another phantom image.

“This person's name was Qiao Sidao.”

“Half a year ago, he finally could not hold on any longer and perished completely.”

Li Fan looked at the person before him. His features carried a rather resolute expression. Li Fan nodded slightly.

“Half a year ago…”

…

The Darkest Star Sea. The High Wall.

The Heavenly Doctor seemed to have already forgotten Li Fan's threat and was wholeheartedly immersed in comprehending the High Wall.

His progress was even faster than he had anticipated.

Perhaps it was because he had continued making countless attempts throughout all these years.

Or perhaps, after seeing Li Fan, a True Immortal, and personally witnessing the true difference between immortals and mortals, he had gained another genuine insight in his heart.

In any case, the Heavenly Doctor vaguely felt that he might not be far from completely comprehending the secrets of the High Wall.

After realizing this, a subtle change appeared in the Heavenly Doctor's expression.

He even began to harbor some doubts about everything he had done over the years.

“Perhaps that True Immortal was right?”

“If I had devoted myself wholeheartedly to cultivation, perhaps I would have found a way to save the Heavenly Law Realm long ago.”

Such a thought flashed through his mind only briefly.

The Heavenly Doctor cast aside all distracting thoughts and once again immersed himself in comprehending the invisible High Wall.

Once sufficient accumulation had been achieved, sometimes a breakthrough could occur in an instant.

An instant passed.

Or perhaps millions of years had passed.

The Heavenly Doctor suddenly gathered up all the golden threads he had released.

For a moment, he seemed to have seen through the mysteries of the High Wall.

But at the same time…

An extreme sense of unease surged through his heart.

This feeling of unease had only appeared once before, when the Heavenly Law Realm had been slaughtered.

Even when he had directly confronted that True Immortal, he had never experienced such a change.

“Exactly what…” The Heavenly Doctor stopped comprehending the secrets of the High Wall.

Instinctively, he wanted to move away from the High Wall.

Suddenly…

He seemed to notice something, and his pupils contracted sharply.

At the location of the invisible High Wall ahead, in a place where nothing should have existed, the scene suddenly distorted for no apparent reason.

His heart began to pound violently again. The ominous premonition had reached its extreme.

A black thread suddenly appeared.

Without making the slightest sound, it pierced through the Heavenly Doctor's chest.

One became a thousand, a thousand became ten thousand, and ten thousand became hundreds of millions.

In an instant, they pierced through the Heavenly Doctor's body, turning him into a puppet controlled by countless strings.

The Heavenly Doctor's consciousness gradually became blurred.

Yet as he felt the countless threads piercing through his body, he unexpectedly experienced a strange sense of familiarity.

Perhaps it was merely an illusion. Before long, a figure appeared before him.

The Heavenly Doctor seemed to see his younger self. However, he was not wearing his favorite white robes. Instead, he wore a black robe.

His face was devoid of either joy or sorrow.

Creak…

The Heavenly Doctor's head was strangely twisted one hundred and eighty degrees.

From in front of his body, it was turned toward his back.

Streams of light and shadows floated out from the Heavenly Doctor's body and entered the body of that mysterious black figure.

The Heavenly Doctor's corpse slowly became ethereal and transparent.

And that black figure…

Or rather, the Heavenly Doctor in his youth, slightly furrowed his brows.

“Can you understand what you are seeing?”

His gaze turned toward the distance, toward the location where Li Fan's true body was!

…

At the same time.

Li Fan, who was immersed in the secrets of the Evil Su Bai beyond the High Wall, also suddenly felt an overwhelming sense of impending disaster, just like the Heavenly Doctor.

Moreover, this time it was stronger than ever before.

“Could it be because I triggered Mr. Bai's Eternal Remnant Will, causing the Evil Su Bai beyond the High Wall to sense me?”

Li Fan's heart was filled with extreme fear.

After thinking for only an instant, Li Fan did not hesitate at all and silently invoked [Truth].

At that very moment, Li Fan also noticed something extremely strange.

On his [Truth] panel, his original lifespan limit of more than three million years was actually fluctuating wildly.

It was not decreasing.

It was increasing!

In the instant Li Fan noticed it, his lifespan had already jumped from three million to tens of billions more. And it was still increasing at an utterly terrifying speed!

Next

Chapter 1416: When The Falsehood Becomes Truth

"What does this mean?"

"In the [Truth]’s deduction, the world-ending catastrophe that affects countless possibilities is showing a tendency to be reversed?"

Li Fan hurriedly activated [Truth] in his mind. As the seven words, [When the Truth Becomes False, False Becomes Truth], exploded before him, his heart was filled with astonishment and doubt.

"After I attained the True Immortal realm, the upper limit of my lifespan displayed on the [Truth] panel became the estimated lifespan of this mortal world. I am clearly facing a great calamity, yet the estimated lifespan of the mortal world is actually increasing instead of decreasing?"

A thousand thoughts flashed through his mind in an instant.

The [Dao Concealment] domain descended upon the mortal world.

Centered around Li Fan, everything within his field of vision in the Darkest Star Sea fell into complete Stillness, reduced to a world of only black and white.

But this time, things were different from before.

Within the range of Dao Concealment, apart from Li Fan, there was actually one other person who could still move!

In the completely motionless world, that person was exceptionally conspicuous. Li Fan noticed and locked onto him almost instantly.

That person also seemed to be looking toward Li Fan's location.

Their eyes met.

"Heavenly Doctor?"

Although compared with the Heavenly Doctor he was familiar with, this person's appearance, clothing, and temperament were all vastly different.

Li Fan still recognized him at first glance.

Recalling the existence of the Evil Su Bai outside the High Wall that he had glimpsed within Mr. Bai's Eternal Remnant Will, Li Fan immediately understood the source of the inexplicable sense of danger in his heart.

"Outside the High Wall, there is not only the Evil Su Bai, but also a darkened Heavenly Doctor?"

"How did he..."

Suddenly, Li Fan thought of the previous agreement he had made with the Heavenly Doctor inside the High Wall, the thousand day deadline. The Heavenly Doctor, whose life had been threatened by Li Fan, had devoted himself completely to comprehending the principles of the High Wall in order to break through as quickly as possible.

"Evil Su Bai could only be sensed when Mr. Bai had one foot outside the High Wall. Before that, I did not sense any Immortal level fluctuations. Yet the darkened Heavenly Doctor detected me before even crossing the High Wall?"

"Is this person's strength even greater than the Evil Su Bai's?"

Countless thoughts instantly flashed through Li Fan's mind.

Within the black and white, motionless region of Dao Concealment, the darkened Heavenly Doctor was still flying toward Li Fan's location.

Only, he seemed to be trapped in slow motion.

Li Fan could also see that the endless Remnant World Great Wall beneath the High Wall, at the frontier of the Darkest Star Sea, had become a complete mess, as if it had been swept by a colossal flood.

The invisible High Wall surrounding the Darkest Star Sea also seemed to have been blasted open.

Through that opening, the scene outside the High Wall could be faintly seen.

Unlike the withered vitality of the Darkest Star Sea, abundant life force was clearly gathered throughout various regions beyond the High Wall. Yet within this flourishing domain of vitality, there was an extremely incongruous and abrupt straight line of emptiness.

It was like a great road reaching the heavens, leading toward an unknown realm even farther away.

And at the end of this road, seemingly created by the reckless charge of some primordial ferocious beast from the ancient wilderness.

Li Fan's gaze was abruptly severed.

It was as if it had been blocked by a towering shadow, as if separated by a towering wall!

There was no time to think, nor could he afford to comprehend what was happening within the Dao Concealment region.

Li Fan's thoughts raced as he activated [Truth].

He now finally understood why the lifespan limit of the mortal world deduced by [Truth] had suddenly surged.

It was deducing the scenario in which [Truth] fell into the hands of that darkened Heavenly Doctor!

"Once the darkened Heavenly Doctor reaches his current level of strength, he can hold [Truth] and save the mortal world from its great calamity?"

Li Fan secretly gnashed his teeth in anger.

He did not believe that the darkened Heavenly Doctor would spare his life after seizing [Truth].

"The lifespan limit displayed on the [Truth] panel belongs to both the mortal world and me."

"It was not separated during the deduction, which means..."

"As the vessel of [Truth], I most likely cannot be separated from it. Instead, I will be refined into a state similar to the Derivation Law Jade, becoming a dharma treasure."

Li Fan's heart turned icy cold.

He urged [Truth] even more desperately, rapidly retracting the [Dao Concealment] domain.

The speed at which the black and white world returned to normal exceeded the speed at which the darkened Heavenly Doctor moved through the frozen world.

But what made Li Fan uneasy was that the mist outside Dao Concealment continued to churn and tremble.

It seemed as if some terrifying existence could emerge from it at any moment.

"[Truth]!"

Li Fan let out a final roar in his heart.

Even if the darkened Heavenly Doctor truly could save the mortal world after obtaining [Truth], Li Fan would never willingly hand it over.

Either let this mortal world be completely annihilated.

Or let Li Fan himself become its sole savior.

If they wanted him to sacrifice himself...

Not a chance!

The rolling white mist ultimately swallowed everything.

The familiar darkness was about to swallow Li Fan, and only then did the taut strings of his heart finally relax.

Li Fan's consciousness fell into a brief slumber.

...

Who knew how much time had passed, or perhaps it was only an instant.

The Heavenly Doctor, dressed in a black robe, stared at his empty hands, a trace of bewilderment flashing through his eyes.

Looking back at the long trail of destruction he had caused, his brows furrowed even more tightly.

With a single thought, his divine sense swept across the Darkest Star Sea.

When his divine sense passed over the True Immortal comprehending the Dao within the Immortal Ruins, it paused slightly.

Unexpectedly, a hint of hesitation appeared in his eyes. Then he reached out and lifted the skeletal Hunger Immortal.

He flew beyond the High Wall and passed through the long passage of emptiness.

When he reached the end and raised his head to gaze into the distance.

The Heavenly Doctor's body suddenly stiffened, and the hand holding the Hunger Immortal unconsciously loosened.

He stood there, completely stunned.

...

Endless darkness surrounded him.

Li Fan's consciousness drifted unsteadily, as though floating in water.

He was in a strange state between wakefulness and unconsciousness.

At this moment, Li Fan had neither awareness nor thought, relying entirely on instinct to observe his surroundings.

Not far away, a point of starlight seemed to ignite.

Then, countless stars successively began to shine together.

They connected into a line, piercing through the darkness.

Li Fan's consciousness suddenly plunged toward this line of light.

Faster and faster.

As the light completely filled Li Fan, it transformed into countless images of light and shadow, which exploded with a resounding boom.

Anchored at Year 1, Central Darkest Star Sea!

"Hu!"

Like a drowning person struggling desperately to raise their head above the water, Li Fan instinctively took huge gulps of air.

Li Fan was still immersed in the immense oppression brought by the darkened Heavenly Doctor in his previous life.

"Hm?"

Only after this feeling of fear gradually dissipated did a strange scene from the darkness just now vaguely flash through Li Fan's mind, the moment when a white line of light appeared.

"..."

Every time [Truth] truly returns to the beginning of the anchor point, it is indeed the best opportunity to comprehend the [Transformation of Truth and Falsehood].

Li Fan forcibly suppressed all distracting thoughts in his heart and experienced the supreme might of [Truth].

Yet for some reason, the image of that white line remained in his mind and refused to fade for a long time. As he stared at the process of countless points of light connecting together within the darkness, Li Fan's comprehension of the [Transformation of Truth and Falsehood] continued to soar at an incredibly terrifying speed.

Li Fan did not question its origin. He simply followed the flow and immersed himself in it.

After a long time, when the image in his mind gradually faded into darkness.

Only then did he suddenly open his eyes.

"False, yet also true."

Li Fan slowly uttered these three words.

After undergoing countless [Truth] simulations and personally experiencing the transformation between truth and falsehood time and time again.

At this moment, Li Fan finally understood the meaning of those seven characters that had always accompanied [Truth].

"When the Truth becomes False..."

"Perhaps this refers to the Dao Annihilation Calamity that affects every possibility. Countless world lines instantly turn from Truth to Falsehood, becoming annihilated and disappearing without a trace. It is not the so called void, but a kind of [Falsehood] that does not exist even on a conceptual level."

"Falsehood is also Truth. As for that..."

For a moment, Li Fan's expression became somewhat dazed.

"The supreme power of [Truth] can create new possibilities out of nothing within the false spacetime that has been swallowed by the Dao Annihilation Calamity!"

"This..."

Even though he had speculated countless times about the upper limit of [Truth]'s [Transformation of Truth and Falsehood].

At this moment, Li Fan was still completely stunned by what he had realized.

"Wait, perhaps it is not limited to this."

Suddenly, Li Fan seemed to think of something. He impatiently opened the [Truth] panel once again.

Ignoring the prompt that appeared regarding the four inheritance choices, Li Fan stared intently at the age limit displayed on the [Truth] panel.

7,365,864 years, 13 days, and 7 hours!

Chapter 1417: Saving Qiao Sidao

"It is exactly the same as what I saw in the previous life."

"So it really does activate. It is not merely creating something out of nothing and opening up a new possibility within a false spacetime. It also resets all the temporal nodes of the mortal world to the moment of the predetermined anchor point."

"Such power..."

Li Fan felt an unprecedented shock.

Especially after witnessing the existence of Evil Su Bai and darkened Heavenly Doctor, whose strength seemed to surpass even that of the Nameless True Immortal.

No matter how powerful their displays of strength were, before [Truth], all of it was mere illusion! With a single thought, it could turn the false into truth, and with another thought, turn truth into falsehood!

Based on the power of [Truth] inferred from this, theoretically speaking, even darkened Heavenly Doctor should be unable to move within the [Dao Concealment] domain after [Truth] was activated.

The only possible explanation seemed to be that Li Fan, as its vessel, was simply too weak, thereby limiting the effectiveness of [Truth].

"Weak?"

Li Fan, who had already advanced to the Half-Immortal realm, suddenly felt rather bitter about this. But in the next instant, he thought of how, beneath the Dao Annihilation Calamity, even countless True Immortals died like ants. Compared with that, the "weakness" of a Half Immortal was not so difficult to accept.

Recalling how his age limit had risen crazily when he encountered darkened Heavenly Doctor in his previous life, Li Fan's heart stirred.

The Transformation of Truth and Falsehood suddenly activated.

Having personally witnessed the entire process of [Truth] creating an entirely new possibility, Li Fan's comprehension of the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood had advanced by leaps and bounds.

He visualized his own Half Immortal cultivation from the previous possibility. As his thoughts moved, that cultivation descended upon his present body and was superimposed upon it.

Unlike before, when it could only last for an extremely short period, Li Fan could now maintain this false cultivation for nearly the time it took to burn a tea's worth of incense before it dissipated.

Even...

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and, with his entire mind focused, attempted to superimpose the false cultivation once again.

It could indeed be done.

However, the mental burden brought by this action instantly increased more than tenfold.

The duration for which the superimposed cultivation could be maintained also plummeted accordingly. Moreover, its fluctuations in strength became extremely unstable.

"Simply reproducing my own cultivation has already become this difficult. It is truly unimaginable to create an entire possibility out of nothing..."

Li Fan sighed as he looked at the numbers on the panel.

Three times the cultivation of a Half Immortal. Although it was still not enough to directly surpass a True Immortal in strength, it should be extremely close.

But...

7,365,864 years, 13 days, and 7 hours!

With such an increase in strength, the deduction of [Truth] could only delay the world calamity of the mortal world by one hour!

Better than nothing.

"It seems that my current strength is still far from being enough to interfere in this."

Li Fan's heart immediately became somewhat heavy.

Indeed, [Truth] could reset the entire mortal world again and again, preventing complete destruction from occurring.

But the mortal world's decline and collapse were part of the greater trend.

No matter how [Truth] attempted to turn the tide, the Dao Annihilation Calamity would eventually devour everything.

It was like endlessly pushing a boulder up a mountain, only for it to inevitably roll back to the foot of the mountain each time, an endless cycle with no escape.

Only by reaching the level of Evil Su Bai and darkened Heavenly Doctor did there seem to be a possibility of breaking the deadlock.

But the problem was that the appearance of these two was simply too abrupt and unreasonable.

They had completely exceeded Li Fan's expectations.

According to Li Fan's original plan, what came next should have proceeded step by step. He would rely on the combined strength of the Xuanhuang Realm and the Xuan Immortal Ark to slowly explore beyond the High Wall, searching for clues regarding the Faceless Immortal, Cat Treasure, and even the Fishing Pond.

But now, the sudden arrival of these two had almost completely disrupted Li Fan's plans.

In particular, the existence of darkened Heavenly Doctor made Li Fan feel an instinctive sense of lingering fear.

"Once the Heavenly Doctor within the wall is sensed, darkened Heavenly Doctor will descend instantly."

"Does that mean that, all these years, the entire Darkest Star Sea has been wandering on the edge between life and death? If the Heavenly Doctor had not remained concerned about his homeland and devoted most of his energy to reviving the Heavenly Law Realm, he would probably have attracted darkened Heavenly Doctor long ago!"

"Moreover, judging from the progress of the Heavenly Doctor's comprehension in the previous life, it seems that it will not take him much longer to completely unravel the secrets of the High Wall. Even without the variables I caused, once the Heavenly Law Realm successfully returns through possession, the Heavenly Doctor would eventually have to comprehend the High Wall. The day his resurrection succeeded would also have been not far from the day of his death. Truly ironic."

Li Fan's thoughts raced.

Suddenly, he recalled the life in which he became the Tianxuan Spirit Locking Formation.

At that time, he transformed into the Formation-Breaking Jade Disc and spent several thousand years. Anchoring to the year 2078, the world became utterly silent.

It was not until the year 3024 that long-lost sunlight finally returned.

"It seems that because of the existence of darkened Heavenly Doctor, under normal circumstances, the Xuanhuang Realm would ultimately reach a dead end."

"But after darkened Heavenly Doctor acted, neither the Xuanhuang Realm nor the newly born Heavenly Law Realm was completely reduced to dust. Even a thousand years later, there was still the possibility of life recovering..."

"It should be that darkened Heavenly Doctor was reminded of the scene before him and deliberately showed mercy."

Li Fan silently pondered.

Because of the two terrifying existences within the Wall, darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai, Li Fan's plans for this life also had to be changed substantially.

At the very least, he absolutely could not allow the Heavenly Doctor to approach the High Wall again.

"It is still unclear what exactly the situation beyond the High Wall is."

"In my previous life, within the black and white regions of Dao Concealment, I vaguely glimpsed the scene beyond the High Wall through the straight blank passage created when darkened Heavenly Doctor crashed through it."

"It seemed to be an ordinary star sea, filled with scattered stars brimming with life. But at the end of my vision, something blocked my sight."

"Could there be another High Wall beyond the High Wall?"

Li Fan could not help but feel suspicious.

"If I want to understand the current situation beyond the High Wall, perhaps I should start with that descendant of the Heavenly Sword Sect."

His attention returned to the inheritance options of [Truth]. After pondering for a moment, Li Fan chose to accelerate its charging speed.

The charging speed of [Truth] had always been closely related to Li Fan's strength.

After proving himself as a Half Immortal, this was the first time he had selected this option. Li Fan was pleasantly surprised to discover that things were different from before.

On the [Truth] panel, after his realm changed to Immortal, the moment [Truth] returned, the Virtualization Transformation Charging Progress was already at 50 percent. Then, when Li Fan chose to accelerate the charging again, the progress bar suddenly jumped.

It went directly to 99 percent!

"The required waiting time has been greatly shortened."

"Unfortunately, it is still not 100 percent."

"A one percent difference is like heaven and earth!"

If each selection to accelerate the charging could directly fill the progress bar completely, it would mean that Li Fan could activate [Truth] continuously.

Even if he encountered an enemy capable of crossing between possibilities in the future, he could escape calmly in an instant.

But now, precisely because of this one percent difference, he still had to endure a waiting period of at least around seven days.

"Until I can achieve one hundred percent charging instantaneously, I still need to act with extreme caution."

As Li Fan's strength increased, the existences he came into contact with also became increasingly difficult to fathom.

In particular, an existence like darkened Heavenly Doctor, who could cross between possibilities to pursue him and move freely within the Dao Concealment domain, posed far too great a threat to Li Fan.

"I wonder, if I advance to the True Immortal Realm, will I be able to suppress him?"

The cold and utterly indifferent gaze of the darkened Heavenly Doctor, so similar to his own, surfaced in Li Fan's mind once again. He could not help but fall into deep thought.

Seven days later.

The [Truth] was fully charged.

Li Fan's figure flickered, and he silently slipped into the Xuanhuang Realm.

His destination was naturally the hidden divine soul named Qiao Sidao within the Museum of Divine Treasures at the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

After hundreds of billions of reincarnations, he could still maintain his own independent consciousness. Moreover, based on Emperor Sanmo's description, he had even deduced the possible truth behind the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Li Fan was very interested in him.

"If possible, perhaps I can have him explore beyond the High Wall in my place."

"But I also need to be careful and guard against the possibility that another Qiao Sidao exists beyond the wall."

When Li Fan passed by Mr. Bai's skull, his footsteps paused slightly.

As expected, the [Truth], which had already absorbed energy once, no longer sent him any prompts.

As for the test phantom of Evil Su Bai within Mr. Bai's skull, Li Fan avoided it as much as possible.

He did not pay any more attention to it and headed straight toward the ruins of the Nanming Sacred Beast Mountain within the Museum of Divine Treasures.

"Come to think of it, although the [Truth] can generate possibilities out of thin air with a single thought, it still needs to consume certain strange objects within those possibilities..."

Li Fan's thoughts drifted.

The [Truth] seemed to sense his thoughts and sent what seemed to be a burst of mocking laughter, mixed with a hint of helplessness.

But afterward, no matter how Li Fan tried to sense it, there was not the slightest fluctuation.

When he arrived at the ruins of Nanming Mountain, Emperor Sanmo had already been waiting there for quite some time.

Just as in the previous life, he said very respectfully, "Greetings, Senior!"

Although Li Fan had not displayed any actual combat strength in this life, he had silently ignored all the restrictions at the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and entered the Museum of Divine Treasures without anyone noticing.

There was no doubt that this alone demonstrated his formidable strength.

From the changes in Emperor Sanmo's expression, Li Fan could tell that he did not possess the memories of the previous life.

Li Fan secretly relaxed.

He did not say anything, merely nodded before beginning to observe the ruins before him.

Just as Emperor Sanmo had said, faint traces of mysterious energy still remained here.

Within the already scant remaining energy, an extremely weak divine soul was hidden.

Under the concealment of the mysterious energy, even though Li Fan was already a half immortal, it would have been difficult for him to notice it without Emperor Sanmo's special reminder.

The appearance of the divine soul was no different from how Emperor Sanmo had described it in the previous life.

Li Fan reached out and directly extracted half of the life saving energy sustaining Qiao Sidao.

He had originally been barely surviving like someone clinging to life in a pool that was about to dry up. Now, with so much of the water in that pool inexplicably disappearing all at once, Qiao Sidao's divine soul immediately trembled violently, seemingly suffering immense pain.

Emperor San Mo hesitated, wanting to speak. However, faced with the faint pressure emanating from Li Fan, he ultimately chose to remain silent.

Li Fan had no intention of deliberately making things difficult for Qiao Sidao.

On the contrary, Li Fan was going to save his life.

Li Fan absorbed the mysterious energy into his body and carefully experienced it.

It was different from the spiritual qi of the Xuanhuang Realm, and even from immortal spiritual qi.

Yet Li Fan still felt a faint sense of familiarity.

This familiarity seemed to originate from the inherited memories he had obtained when he had transformed into the Black Bird in the Fallen Immortal Realm.

Beyond that, however, Li Fan knew nothing about it.

But this did not prevent Li Fan from using the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood on it.

He compressed the mass of energy and enclosed it within his Dantian.

After standing there for some time, once he had comprehended enough, the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood suddenly descended.

Amid those incomparably mysterious fluctuations, an identical small sphere instantly appeared in Li Fan's hand.

It emitted a strange copper green radiance, mixed with faint traces of silver light.

While the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood was still in effect, Li Fan lightly flicked his finger and injected the copper green sphere into Qiao Sidao's divine soul.

Like sweet rain after a prolonged drought, Qiao Sidao's extremely fragile divine soul visibly became somewhat stronger.

After waiting until Qiao Sidao had completely absorbed the energy sphere, Li Fan's thoughts shifted, and he canceled the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood once again.

His divine sense enveloped Qiao Sidao, observing every minute change within him.

Sure enough, just as Li Fan had expected.

Although the enhancement provided by the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood had disappeared, after the energy generated out of thin air during the duration of the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood had been absorbed, it did not completely dissipate along with the technique because it now had a vessel to contain it.

Instead, a small amount remained.

Although the proportion was not large, it was still of immense significance to Li Fan.

After resting briefly, Li Fan once again activated the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood and produced a second small sphere.

When Li Fan first produced the energy sphere, Emperor Sanmo had assumed that it was simply the energy Li Fan had extracted moments ago.

But now, when he saw Li Fan produce another one after the first energy sphere had been completely consumed, the usually composed expression on Emperor Sanmo's face could no longer remain calm.

Li Fan also noticed that from beginning to end, the other party seemed completely unaware of the effects of the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood.

Although the fluctuations of the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood were incredibly obvious to Li Fan himself.

"However, I still cannot let my guard down."

"Perhaps apart from me, the vessel of this technique, there are other mighty beings in this world capable of sensing the activation of the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood."

"From now on, whenever I use divine abilities, I must always pay attention to my own safety."

As these thoughts passed through his mind, Li Fan continuously activated the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood.

Under the uninterrupted infusion of large quantities of mysterious energy spheres, Qiao Sidao finally began to recover.

He awakened from his deep slumber.

"Uncle San, I..."

Qiao Sidao quickly realized that the situation before him was unusual.

His reaction was extremely quick. Despite his weakened state, he immediately expressed his gratitude to Li Fan for saving him.

Li Fan nodded slightly, but his movements did not stop.

Making use of the materials at hand, he used the collection of the Museum of Divine Treasures as raw materials and forged a temporary body for Qiao Sidao.

Only after placing Qiao Sidao's divine soul inside it did Li Fan finally stop.

Continuously using the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood was also a considerable burden for him.

He stood there and caught his breath for a moment, without revealing the slightest trace of fatigue.

At the same time, he waited for Qiao Sidao to adapt to his new body.

In less than half a day, Qiao Sidao had recovered from the brink of death.

All of Emperor Sanmo's efforts in the past had ultimately come to nothing. Yet now, this Senior had merely made a slight move and was able to turn something rotten into something miraculous.

How could Qiao Sidao fail to understand just how terrifying the strength of the person before him was?

He bowed slightly and sincerely expressed his gratitude.

"Thank you, Senior, for saving my life!"

As for Emperor Sanmo, he seemed to have yet to recover from the shock of what had just happened. His body continued to tremble slightly.

Li Fan extended his right hand and placed it on Qiao Sidao's head.

A strand of divine will entered Qiao Sidao's divine soul.

Faced with his life saving benefactor and the absolute gap in strength between them, Qiao Sidao's expression changed slightly.

But in the end, he did not resist.

He allowed Li Fan to examine the memories within his divine soul.

Chapter 1418: Primeval Dao Form

Fragility and strength, two completely opposite words, seemed to have achieved unity in Qiao Sidao.

Even though his divine soul, before being saved by Li Fan, had already been on the verge of collapse and could have perished at any moment, the vast sea of complicated memories he possessed still caused even Li Fan's expression to change slightly.

"If the strength of my divine soul were merely at this level, it would be completely incapable of supporting so many memories..."

Setting aside the special nature of Qiao Sidao's divine soul, Li Fan first roughly surveyed the countless scenes contained within his memories.

Li Fan was uninterested in most of the worlds deduced by the [Era] device. Even the various cultivation techniques and divine abilities that Qiao Sidao had personally encountered and experienced within them were of little use to Li Fan now.

What was more useful were the ancient secrets concerning the ancient era and the Immortal Realm that Qiao Sidao had occasionally learned by entering the Ten Great Immortal Sects under different identities.

"It is just unknown how reliable this information actually is. Are these merely settings that existed within the deduced worlds, or are they derived from fragments of clues passed down from the destruction of the myriad worlds..."

Looking through all the worlds Qiao Sidao had experienced across hundreds of billions of reincarnations, only two were worth Li Fan taking a closer look at.

One was the [Eternal Immortal Realm], and the other was [Chaotic Spacetime].

The Eternal Immortal Realm was the perfect world that emerged in the deductions after immortal cultivation civilization developed to its peak without a world-ending calamity. Not only did its inhabitants possess extraordinarily long lifespans, even those who did not cultivate could live for several thousand years. Within it was also a magnificent existence known as the [Immortal Spirit Tower].

The Immortal Spirit Tower was scattered throughout every corner of the world, continuously releasing pure spiritual energy day and night. Cultivation techniques, formations, alchemy, medicine, and any other knowledge one wished to learn could all be searched for and studied within the Immortal Spirit Tower, without any restrictions.

It was precisely within the Immortal Spirit Tower that Qiao Sidao accumulated sufficient knowledge and experience, allowing him to escape from the [Era] device in one fell swoop.

After all, it was merely a theoretical system within a deduction, and could not be completely applied to reality.

With Li Fan's current perspective, he could naturally see at a glance the inconsistencies in the various things Qiao Sidao had learned.

"It only resembles the real thing. Perhaps it can work within the deduced virtual world, but..."

Li Fan used an extremely powerful formation from Qiao Sidao's memories as a test.

The formation was called [Heaven Overturning and Earth Inverting]. Despite its ordinary sounding name, once the formation was completed, it could truly reverse the sky and earth of an entire world. Any living being incapable of flight would uncontrollably fall from the earth toward the sky. They would either starve to death during the fall, or crash violently into the boundary of the world and be reduced to pieces.

If it truly worked as described, then this formation was indeed rather interesting. But when Li Fan brought it into the Final Dissociation Disc for examination...

He obtained the result that this formation could not be formed at all.

"Perhaps in some possibility that is completely identical to the deduced world, this formation can operate normally. But in the present world, where the Great Dao is incomplete and different laws clash with one another, the operating environment simply does not meet its requirements."

Although it could not be used directly, Li Fan was not disappointed.

"It points toward a certain possibility, but it does not conform to reality. Yet with appropriate modifications, it can be made operational."

"Qiao Sidao merely lived within the virtual [Era] device. His understanding of reality comes only from what he experienced within the Era, so he may not possess this ability. But I am different."

Within Li Fan's sea of consciousness, the Immortal Formation Final Dissociation Disc flickered with light.

Formation data resembling luminous rivers was extracted from Qiao Sidao's memories and drawn into the Final Dissociation Disc.

Qiao Sidao's divine soul felt as though it were being subjected to the torture of being cut by a thousand blades, and he could not help but let out a muffled groan. If Li Fan had not helped stabilize his divine soul, this alone might have caused his soul to scatter and perish.

But Qiao Sidao was indeed worthy of being someone who had struggled his way out of endless reincarnations. After that, he remained silent throughout. He even seemed to gradually become accustomed to the suffering, and his expression slowly returned to normal.

It did not take too much time.

Through the transformation of the Final Dissociation Disc, the countless formations within Qiao Sidao's memories were all transformed from false formations into genuine formations that conformed to the environment of reality.

Li Fan immediately transmitted the conclusions deduced by the Final Dissociation Disc back to Qiao Sidao.

Qiao Sidao keenly sensed that the formations transmitted by Li Fan differed from what he had learned and understood. At first, his expression froze slightly, but then he became uncontrollably excited.

Qiao Sidao could still recall just how powerful he had become after experiencing countless reincarnations within the Era. He had been able to uncover the greatest mysteries between heaven and earth and break out of the deadlock.

Yet after arriving in the real world, the extreme contrast was difficult for him to accept for a time.

His divine soul was so fragile that it seemed as though even the slightest breeze could extinguish it. Qiao Sidao knew that this was the difference between the [Era] and reality.

But now...

Through the changes between the two formations transmitted by this mysterious and unfathomable senior before him, Qiao Sidao vaguely seemed to see exactly where this difference lay.

"Once one method is understood, all methods become accessible. As long as you give me some time, I should be able to completely transform everything I have learned."

Qiao Sidao greedily absorbed the knowledge transmitted by Li Fan, forgetting to thank him for a moment.

Li Fan did not mind. He could also see that Qiao Sidao was currently comprehending the Dao.

This divine soul had indeed not disappointed him.

It was truly useful.

Li Fan then turned to ask Emperor Sanmo, beginning the unfinished conversation between the two from his previous life.

"Exactly what level of strength is required to cross the boundary of feasibility?"

"And what exactly is the energy contained within Sacred Beast Mountain?"

As he spoke, Li Fan once again used the transformation between truth and falsehood to conjure a small ball of ancient bronze-colored energy. He tossed it toward Emperor Sanmo.

Li Fan's two questions had already caused this Longevity Immortal Beast to become somewhat tense after experiencing immense shock.

When he greedily absorbed the incomparably pure energy into his body and felt a sensation of revival spreading through every corner of his body, Emperor Sanmo became certain that this senior before him had definitely reached the legendary step.

Perhaps because this body was already too old and decayed, the extremely pure [Ancient Cang Primal Essence] he swallowed was actually slowly dissipating without stopping. In the end, only a small amount remained.

But even this small amount was of immense significance to him. At the very least, it could extend his lifespan by another ten thousand years.

More importantly, he had personally witnessed this senior before him continuously producing [Ancient Cang Primal Essence] as though performing a magic trick.

This divine substance, which was originally depleted with every use, if it could truly be obtained without limit and consumed without end...

In an instant, countless thoughts flashed through Emperor Sanmo's mind.

But ultimately, they all transformed into one most resolute thought.

No matter what, he had to remain by this senior's side.

For the sake of survival, whether for himself or for his entire race, Emperor Sanmo had repeatedly chosen to stand beside different figures.

At first, it was Emperor Yi. Later, it was the Beast Taming Sect. Later still, it was the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor.

The hope he had seen from all of them combined was still nowhere near as great as the hope he now saw in the person before him.

He was not someone who hesitated.

Once he made a decision, he immediately put it into action.

At this moment, when Li Fan asked, Emperor Sanmo knew that there was no point in concealing anything, so he told him everything he knew.

“Senior, according to the ancient memories passed down through our race, at the very beginning of the Primeval Era, even a [Primeval Dao Form] that had merged its body with the Dao could not easily leap across possibilities.”

“If we were to make an analogy, a Primeval Dao Form could be regarded as equivalent to the realm of a [Nameless True Immortal] among human cultivators. However, compared to human cultivators who comprehend the Dao, the great Primeval Dao Forms were themselves derivatives and incarnations of the Great Dao. Whether in terms of strength or comprehension of the Dao, they were innately superior by a level. Apart from certain supreme experts, most Nameless True Immortals were inferior to Primeval Dao Forms.”

Although Emperor Sanmo had already decided to completely submit to Li Fan, he did not embellish the truth because of this. He merely recounted everything exactly as it existed in the inherited memories.

Li Fan listened without expressing any opinion and gestured for Emperor Sanmo to continue.

“The existence of Primeval Dao Forms was both an advantage and a disadvantage. Among the Great Daos of Heaven and Earth, certain powerful Great Daos had never evolved into Dao Forms. Furthermore, because of their innate nature, Primeval Dao Forms were forever denied the opportunity to comprehend those Great Daos.”

“Human cultivators, however, were different. Any Dao naturally existing between Heaven and Earth could be refined and mastered by humans.”

Emperor Sanmo's tone suddenly became heavy.

“This was also the beginning of the disaster that befell our race.”

“In the original world of our race, the number of human cultivators was far too small to compare with us. Most humans existed merely as pets and food in our eyes. We monopolized the methods of cultivation. Only obedient humans were permitted to cultivate. They became our claws and fangs, helping us suppress other humans...”

Emperor Sanmo slowly recounted the secrets of the demon beast race.

Nearby, Qiao Shisi, who had been comprehending the Dao, could not help but become distracted and listen.

Even throughout the countless cycles of the Era, he had never experienced this part of history.

Upon hearing that humans had once merely been food for the demon beasts, he could not help slightly frowning.

He had received Emperor Sanmo's care for a long time, yet he had never heard him mention any of this.

Li Fan, however, remained completely expressionless.

“We were not a short sighted or unintelligent race. From those human cultivators who submitted to us, we saw the terrifying potential contained within the human body. Thus, we tightened our control over humanity even further.”

“According to the normal trajectory of history, theoretically speaking, there should have been no possibility of human cultivators rising in our homeland.”

“And in reality, that was indeed the case. Because the greatest variable came from beyond the possibilities of the world!”

When Emperor Sanmo reached this point, there was a trace of helplessness in his voice.

Li Fan seemed to understand something upon hearing this, and a sharp glint flashed through his eyes.

“Then, one day, the great disaster of our race descended for no apparent reason.”

“A [Nameless True Immortal] actually crossed possibilities and descended into our spacetime. After witnessing the miserable state of humanity, he began a crazed slaughter and revenge.”

“At first, our race possessed thirteen [Primeval Dao Forms]. Yet in the battle against that Nameless True Immortal, more than half of them were killed or severely wounded...”

Although Emperor Sanmo's tone remained calm, Li Fan could still detect the deep fear hidden within it.

“That Nameless True Immortal was also severely wounded and temporarily retreated. However, before leaving, he plundered the remains of five Primeval Dao Forms that he had slaughtered. If he refined the Great Dao contained within those remains, our race would inevitably face complete extinction.”

“The remaining Dao Forms exhausted countless possibilities, yet they could not find a path by which our race could emerge victorious. Thus, in their despair and helplessness, they had no choice but to choose escape.”

“However, as I said before, at the beginning of the Primeval Era, crossing possibilities was simply too difficult. Although we do not know how that inexplicable Nameless True Immortal who descended into our spacetime accomplished it, according to our deductions and calculations, even if all eight surviving Dao Forms sacrificed themselves, they would still be unable to allow most of our race to escape.”

“Everything in the world could be exhausted, yet it would only be enough for one person to escape!”

The figure of Emperor Yi immediately flashed through Li Fan's mind.

“Myriad Forms Returning to One?”

Emperor Sanmo nodded.

“Our race all evolved from the Primeval Dao Forms. At that time, if we wished to preserve even a thread of hope for survival, there was only one path, fusion and returning to the ancient origin.”

“To preserve the integrity of our surviving race as much as possible in the future.”

“Countless members of our race, some willingly and others unwillingly, entered that enormous furnace.”

“In the end, they were fused into [Emperor Yi].” Emperor Sanmo said calmly.

“The unfortunate part was that five of the most ancient and powerful Primeval Dao Forms had already been lost.”

“Afterward, another seven contributed themselves and dissipated while crossing possibilities.”

“Of the thirteen Primeval Dao Forms, only one remained. From that point onward, our race could never again achieve the glory of the Primeval Era.”

There was a trace of desolation in Emperor Sanmo's voice.

“Seven Primeval Dao Forms were required to leap across the timeline?” Li Fan did not care about Emperor Sanmo's emotions and narrowed his eyes.

“That was an old account from the Primeval Era. After the great catastrophe descended, the upper limit of power required to leap across possibilities gradually became lower.”

Emperor Sanmo waved his hand. Countless illusions appeared before them within the ruins of Nanming Sacred Mountain.

They were exactly the same as what Li Fan had seen back then in the Fallen Immortal Realm.

“As the saying goes, one must remain vigilant even in times of peace. After completing the escape, although we temporarily obtained safety, and Emperor Yi successfully began slowly separating the myriad forms of our race, the terror brought by that Nameless True Immortal still lingered in our hearts.”

“Therefore, we continued searching for a method that could allow us to escape from the realm of possibilities.”

“[Ancient Cang Primal Essence] was our discovery.”

Li Fan extended his hand, and the bronze colored energy sphere appeared once again.

“[Ancient Cang Primal Essence]?” Li Fan muttered thoughtfully.

“That is what our race calls it. In truth, human cultivators should have another name for it. This object was actually discovered by us within the ruins left behind by human cultivators. Yet I have lived within human society for tens of thousands of years, and I have never discovered any records concerning this object.”

Emperor Sanmo explained in detail.

“The divine face that sealed the Ancient Cang Primal Essence, as well as the key that opened the restriction, were both obtained by us from those ruins.”

“This ruin is within the Xuanhuang Realm?” A sharp glint appeared in Li Fan's eyes.

Emperor Sanmo nodded.

“Every possibility has slightly different names and actual locations. But at least among the possibilities we have experienced, its existence can always be found within roughly this region.”

“It seems that you have not escaped from calamity only once.” Li Fan stared intently at Emperor Sanmo.

“In the face of countless possibilities, time and the number of times have no great meaning.”

Emperor Sanmo looked toward the place where the Great Rift Vortex had long since become completely empty.

Chapter 1419: Dao Annihilation Ancient Past Advances

"Whether we can ultimately survive or not is what matters most," Emperor Sanmo said in an extremely solemn tone.

Within the Great Rift Vortex, winds surged and clouds churned, with torrential rain pouring down year after year. The traces of the former Nanming Mountain had long since vanished into the river of history.

After a long silence, Emperor Sanmo spoke again: "Aside from having to evade the pursuit of that Nameless True Immortal of the human race, the successive Heaven and Earth Annihilation Calamities have also forced us to flee continuously. Although we have been fortunate enough to always find Ancient Cang Primal Essences that can allow us to leap and escape, some of the facts we discovered have inevitably caused certain pessimistic emotions to arise within our hearts."

"To prevent these emotions from having a fatal impact on the race, Emperor Yi chose to split and evolve these emotions into separate forms, then allow them to develop and perish on their own..."

Li Fan naturally knew of those powerful demon beasts within the demon beast race whose personalities were all different, yet invariably strange. He keenly caught the key point in Emperor Sanmo's words.

"Facts?"

"Countless futures, infinite possibilities. This was originally our general understanding of all worlds. But through one leap and escape after another, we began to doubt this understanding. In theory, every leap should take us to an entirely new possibility. The point in time at which we descend into that possibility should also be random. For example, if our luck were good enough, we could theoretically arrive in a world where the Twelve PrimevalDao Forms had not yet been slaughtered and the race was still flourishing."

"Perhaps our fortune was simply insufficient, and we never encountered such a world. If that were all, it would not matter. But..."

Emperor Sanmo's expression became extremely solemn.

"What frightened us was that after every leap and descent, the point in time we arrived at was continuously moving further forward. Although we leaped across possibilities, it seemed that we could not reverse our position along the dimension of time."

"This is also the fundamental reason why I have always used the term 'possibility' rather than 'timeline' to describe it."

Li Fan and Qiao Sidao both immediately understood what Emperor Sanmo meant.

Qiao Sidao lowered his head in thought, concealing the fear in his heart.

Li Fan, meanwhile, thought of the fact that every time he activated [Truth], he reset the temporal point of the entire mortal world.

"The Dao Annihilation Calamity is not merely destroying countless possibilities. It seems to be swallowing the entire river of time of the mortal world from upstream."

"It is just like how, no matter how many times I activate [Truth], I can never return to before the first year I anchored."

"Taking this a step further, if the mortal world did not possess a miraculous object like [Truth], then would that not mean..."

"That no one could escape the calamity?" Li Fan's eyes flickered.

If Emperor Sanmo's words were true, then the scale and terrifying nature of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, as well as the importance of [Truth], were likely far beyond his imagination.

However, Li Fan had never crossed between possibilities on his own without relying on [Truth]. Thus, he could not determine whether the information provided by Emperor Sanmo was truly reliable.

It was even possible that the demon beast race was simply extremely unlucky, and every time they crossed into another possibility, they just happened to move continuously in the direction of the "future."

Countless thoughts flashed through Li Fan's mind in an instant. But since there was currently no way to verify them, there was no point in indulging in wild speculation. Thus, he returned the topic to the relic where the Ancient Cang Primal Essence had been discovered within the Xuanhuang Realm.

Li Fan asked Emperor Sanmo to describe the location of the relic and its details.

His heart stirred slightly. He drew a map of the original Xuanhuang Realm in the air before him, then pointed toward a certain location in the center.

"Is it here?"

Emperor Sanmo carefully examined it for a while before nodding.

Li Fan immediately had a vague answer.

The location of the Ancient Cang Primal Essence should be where the central hub and core of the ancient immortal formation in the Xuanhuang Realm was located. Its origins were even older than the Floating Across Starry Sky Grand Formation of the Xuantian King, tracing all the way back to the creation of the Xuanhuang Realm.

"My previous speculation was that the original purpose of creating the Xuanhuang Realm was to serve as an experimental ground for researching how to resist the Dao Annihilation Calamity. The extremely high degree of similarity between Ink Death and the black qi of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, as well as the countless figures resembling Ink Death that seemed to appear when the Dao Annihilation black qi spread like a flood in the life before last, undoubtedly confirmed my speculation from certain perspectives."

"However, my understanding of the Dao Annihilation Calamity had previously remained at the simple level of black qi. I had not realized that this great calamity could even affect countless possibilities."

"I have only come into contact with the Dao Annihilation Calamity for a short time. In the ancient Immortal Realm, there were even existences at the level of the Nameless True Immortal. They must have understood the Dao Annihilation Calamity just as well as the demon beast race, or perhaps even more deeply."

"It now makes sense that an experimental ground capable of leaping across timelines was left behind in the Xuanhuang Realm."

Li Fan could not help recalling the memory scenes of a True Immortal that he had once witnessed within True Immortal flesh.

"...Qionglang Star Region... annihilation... garrison..."

"...Target found."

Now that his understanding of the Dao Annihilation Calamity had reached a certain level, looking back at these words, Li Fan once again sensed information that he had previously been unable to perceive.

"The Immortal Realm was not completely powerless against the Dao Annihilation Calamity. The Dao Annihilation Calamity did not suddenly and violently swallow every existence across an entire possibility at once. Instead, different regions gradually fell one after another. Perhaps this was precisely the function of the Immortal Celestial Pillar, which was made from the remains of a Nameless True Immortal."

"After hearing that the Qionglang Star Region had been submerged by the Dao Annihilation Calamity, I did not sense any particularly strong feeling of panic from the memories of the True Immortal. Clearly, the people of the Immortal Realm at that time were not so pessimistic about the Dao Annihilation Calamity."

"I simply do not know what happened afterward that caused the Immortal Realm to suddenly collapse and be destroyed."

As his thoughts wandered, Li Fan thought of even more.

The Dao Annihilation Calamity completely swallowed possibilities. Even the lofty Immortal Realm had already disappeared within the Dao Annihilation Calamity. Under normal circumstances, the lower realm star sea should have vanished long ago as well.

Yet it had continued to exist for ten thousand years...

"Perhaps it has something to do with the living civilization I saw beyond the High Wall of the Darkest Star Sea."

Li Fan pondered silently.

The situation he faced now was much like when he had still been a mortal and left Great Xuan to arrive in the Xuanhuang Realm, only to suddenly learn of the existence of the True Immortals of the Immortal Ruins and even the invisible High Wall.

"Seeing too far only throws the mind into confusion."

"It is better to proceed step by step and slowly make our plans."

...

Li Fan fell into deep thought. Neither Emperor Sanmo nor Qiao Sidao dared disturb him.

After a long while, Li Fan finally spoke again.

He first said to Qiao Sidao, "You possess nothing but boundless theoretical knowledge, while your own strength is practically zero."

"In a chaotic era, cultivation is what guarantees one's ability to survive and establish oneself. You have reincarnated for so long. Surely you understand this principle."

"For now, organize what you have learned and transform it into something that can actually be used in reality. Before long, I will find a cultivation technique specifically suited to you..."

Qiao Sidao's expression became solemn, and he bowed.

"Thank you, Senior."

Li Fan then turned to Emperor Sanmo.

"Are you willing to work for me?"

He had merely asked a simple question, yet Emperor Sanmo had revealed so much, practically laying bare the secrets of his entire race. Li Fan, who was well versed in the workings of people's hearts, naturally already understood what the other party was thinking.

Clearly, after seeing the supreme might of Li Fan's [Transformation of Truth and Falsehood], he had developed the intention of submitting himself and seeking protection. Thus, he had asked so straightforwardly.

Emperor Sanmo also answered directly, bowing slightly. "I am willing to serve Senior and work for you."

Li Fan nodded slightly. "You possess countless years of inherited memories from the Beast Race and possess extraordinary wisdom. Your knowledge and experience are vast, and in some respects, you even surpass me. You are just what I need to offer advice and devise strategies for me."

"At this point, there is no longer any need to hide anything. I truly came to this world from another possibility." Li Fan spoke in a deep voice.

Qiao Shidao showed a hint of surprise.

Emperor Sanmo, on the other hand, looked as though this had been within his expectations all along.

After all, the sudden appearance of this True Immortal, combined with Li Fan's earlier question about "what level of strength is required to cross possibilities", clearly indicated that he was extraordinary.

"However, I did not come here by actively crossing possibilities." Li Fan's brows furrowed tightly, as though he had fallen into his memories.

"The world I came from shares many similarities with this world, yet there are also many differences. Although the Immortal Realm had been destroyed, it left behind numerous inheritances in the lower realms. Its overall strength was far greater than this place. There was not even any immediate danger of the Dao Annihilation Calamity descending. Who could have expected..."

Li Fan's expression darkened. "One day, two extremely terrifying experts suddenly descended. Their strength was even far beyond mine. With every movement, they completely destroyed my homeland. Even possessing the strength of a True Immortal, before those two, one would be no more than an ant."

"But for some unknown reason, those two powerful beings did not seem to be on the same side. Before long, they began fighting each other. Heaven and earth shook violently, and I could only flee in a miserable state by instinct. In a daze, I somehow arrived here..."

Li Fan's account was extremely dramatic.

From time to time, he even projected scenes before them.

The scenes of heaven and earth collapsing and the Star Sea being completely destroyed caused Qiao Shidao and Emperor Sanmo to change expressions slightly.

When Emperor Sanmo saw the Evil Su Bai and darkened Heaven Doctor that Li Fan displayed, his expression was filled with uncontrollable shock.

Li Fan had been waiting for this reaction. His tone immediately changed.

"Hmm? It seems that you know these two?"

Emperor Sanmo did not dare to speak recklessly. He carefully examined the images again before saying, "These two should be supreme experts capable of crossing possibilities through their own strength."

"In the history of this world, these two were also existences comparable to peerless geniuses. One of them is even still alive to this day. Moreover..."

Emperor Sanmo paused before continuing, "He also has a very close relationship with the actual ruler of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor."

He briefly introduced the lives and backgrounds of Su Bai and the Heavenly Doctor to Li Fan.

Upon hearing this, Li Fan's brows furrowed tightly. "Come to think of it, my world seems to have two figures with the same identities. However, with countless sects standing side by side and geniuses emerging one after another, they were not particularly conspicuous."

"Could those two powerful beings have been hunting their own counterparts in other possibilities?"

Li Fan's words caused Emperor Sanmo and Qiao Shidao to exchange glances, astonishment appearing on their faces.

After another long silence.

Li Fan looked beyond the Museum of Divine Treasures. "Before reaching the immortal realm, all are mere ants. The Heavenly Doctor of this world is not worth worrying about at all. What I am concerned about is whether those two powerful beings might also follow their connection and arrive here."

This question clearly exceeded Emperor Sanmo's understanding.

Even this Beast of Wisdom was unable to answer for a moment.

"Looks like I can only strike first and kill that Heavenly Doctor." Li Fan's voice gradually turned cold.

"I would not recommend that Senior do so." Qiao Shidao suddenly spoke.

Li Fan looked toward him, signaling for him to continue.

"Suppose those two powerful beings are constantly hunting their own counterparts for some reason. Judging from Senior's experience in your world, this is clearly not the first time they have done so. They obviously possess some secret technique that allows them to sense their own existence in other possible worlds..."

Qiao Shidao pondered for a moment, carefully organizing his words. "According to what Uncle San said, the timelines of all possibilities maintain a certain degree of consistency as they continue moving forward. In other words, their act of hunting their counterparts also requires the consumption of time. Even though they can cross possibilities."

"Since they cannot carry out the slaughter simultaneously, it means their hunts must have an order, occurring one after another."

"The world we are currently in is temporarily safe because we have not yet attracted their attention."

"If Senior's killing were to cause the Heavenly Doctor, at the moment between life and death, to sense his counterpart, then instead of solving the problem, it might attract a powerful enemy from beyond the possibilities." Qiao Shidao analyzed methodically.

Li Fan nodded slightly, quite satisfied.

Naturally, he had no intention of truly killing the Heavenly Doctor.

After all, he currently had a thousand ways to imprison him. Why take the risk and possibly attract darkened Heaven Doctor?

He had not even revealed that the invisible High Wall could, to a certain extent, isolate darkened Heaven Doctor's perception. Li Fan was doing this precisely to test how useful Emperor Sanmo and Qiao Shidao could be.

Judging from the results, they were quite satisfactory.

Emperor Sanmo also agreed. "Rather than taking the risk, it would be better to imprison him. Ancient Cang Primal Essence can serve as an energy source capable of crossing possibilities. This mysterious energy might be able to block, to some extent, perception coming from beyond the possibilities..."

This was something Li Fan had not considered before.

Better to believe it exists than to dismiss the possibility. Li Fan prepared to incorporate Ancient Cang Primal Essence into the formation used to seal the Heavenly Doctor.

The Final Dissociation Disc had already begun analyzing Ancient Cang Primal Essence.

Li Fan then looked toward Emperor Sanmo. "What do you think about the existence of darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai?"

"Why is it that in this world, where they have not even reached the True Immortal Realm, their counterparts in other possibilities are actually so powerful?"

Emperor Sanmo gave a bitter smile. "Perhaps there simply are not that many reasons behind the affairs of this world. It is just like that Nameless True Immortal who destroyed my race back then."

"Among ordinary nameless True Immortals, being able to fight evenly against one of the PrimevalDao Forms would already count as great strength. Yet that one was actually able to fight thirteen enemies alone..."

"Before a truly powerful being, there is no such thing as reason."

Qiao Shidao offered the same opinion. "Since ancient times, those who have been able to survive calamities were either heaven chosen lucky ones, or absolute powerhouses."

"I think these two belong to the latter.”
