Chapter 1420: An Experimental Attempt At Proving The Dao

"Qualifications and comprehension, and the various ways these innate factors affect one's actual cultivation, perhaps have a certain upper limit. Anyone capable of reaching the True Immortal realm will not be lacking in these aspects, no matter what."

"But to go one step further..." Qiao Sidao continued to explain his reasoning in detail to Li Fan.

"It should require a certain amount of luck and coincidence."

"Every choice one makes will shape a different future life. The Evil Su Bai and the darkened Heavenly Doctor that Senior encountered, after making choices completely different from their counterparts in this world, brought about a series of subsequent changes that ultimately led to their strength reaching such an unfathomable realm."

"And through the matter of being hunted by powerful beings from other possibilities, perhaps we can also determine, to some extent, the general state of the entire world..." Qiao Sidao pondered, searching for the right term.

"Let us use the term [Mortal World] to refer to it," Li Fan suddenly said, then gestured for him to continue.

"Ah, perhaps we can determine the general state of the entire Mortal World." Qiao Sidao smoothly changed the term according to Li Fan's request.

"Explain in detail." Li Fan's eyes flickered. He had already guessed Qiao Sidao's thoughts in an instant.

"If the Mortal World still contains an endless number of possibilities, then theoretically, for anyone in the Xuanhuang Realm, there exists somewhere beyond the Star Sea a supreme powerhouse capable of effortlessly crossing possibilities. Uncle San, me, and even all living beings of the Xuanhuang Realm are the same."

"Conversely, if the Dao Annihilation Calamity currently afflicting the Mortal World has already extinguished the vast majority of possibilities, then perhaps such beings no longer exist. The problem is how to conduct this verification while ensuring our own safety..."

Qiao Sidao frowned slightly. "That is something I cannot currently figure out. After all, such hypothetical enemies would possess strength far beyond imagination. They are already beyond the scope of my abilities."

"Having an idea is already a good beginning," Li Fan casually praised.

Qiao Sidao's difficulty was not a problem for him at all.

"This life will be enough to make a basic judgment."

"However, judging from the might displayed by the darkened Heavenly Doctor in the previous life, I need to make thorough preparations in advance."

Next, Li Fan also asked Emperor Sanmo in detail about Su Bai and the Heavenly Doctor, with Su Bai being the main focus.

According to Emperor Sanmo's account, the general situation was basically identical to what Li Fan had experienced in the Fallen Immortal Realm.

However, there were certain aspects concerning the Evil Su Bai that had not been revealed in the illusion.

"The Beast Taming Sect is, after all, one of the Ten Great Immortal Sects and one of the ruling powers of the Xuanhuang Realm. Naturally, when an unknown person suddenly came knocking on their door and making arrogant demands, they would not simply agree."

"However, after experiencing a brutal battle and weighing the pros and cons, they had no choice but to submit." Emperor Sanmo gave a bitter smile.

According to the battle scene described by Emperor Sanmo, at first, Mr. Bai only injured the spirit beasts and did not harm the cultivators. But as the killing continued, Mr. Bai's attacks grew increasingly severe, and his expression gradually became abnormal.

It was only because Emperor Sanmo keenly noticed this change and communicated with the Beast Taming Sect Master in time. And because Lu Ya, who had once been raised by Mr. Bai, appeared.

That a tragedy was perhaps avoided.

Emperor San Mo naturally did not know that Su Bai had once crossed the High Wall with one foot before being mysteriously influenced by Evil Su Bai.

He merely believed that this was a hidden aspect of Su Bai's own nature.

"Although my homeland was destroyed, I believe many old acquaintances, like me, managed to escape to other possibilities through a twist of fate."

"My short term goal is to find a stable method of traversing between possibilities. My long term goal is to take revenge on those two!"

"Although they are powerful, they should not treat us like ants. Such wanton actions will inevitably be punished!" Li Fan said solemnly, carefully establishing his persona.

These words earned the heartfelt agreement of Emperor Sanmo, who had suffered an almost identical fate.

Qiao Sidao also nodded in sincere agreement.

Indeed, Li Fan could use various methods to forcibly make the two work for him. But in Li Fan's view, having them willingly cooperate would be more efficient and reliable.

"The development of this world is even far behind that of my homeland. Accomplishing these goals will certainly be extremely difficult."

"We need to work together." As Li Fan spoke, he looked toward the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance outside the Museum of Divine Treasures.

Emperor Sanmo quickly understood the meaning behind Li Fan's words and took the initiative to explain.

"The three great ruling factions of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance are the Dharma-Transmitters, the Mirror Holders, and the Illusory Truth Sages. I command and oversee the [Illusory Truth Sages] faction. They are temporary creations formed from souls selected through the [Era] device, combined with life materials stored within the Museum of Divine Treasures and refined through the unique fusion techniques of our race."

"Among them, the most outstanding is the creation known as Diyi Jinglun."

"In fact, this person's birth was also filled with countless coincidences. The legendary nature of his birth is not even inferior to Qiao Sidao's..."

From Emperor Sanmo's account, Li Fan also learned why Diyi Jinglun had been given the name "Diyi."

Diyi Jinglun had originally not been a single soul.

The similarity between his name and the Beast Race's ruler, Emperor Yi, was naturally no coincidence. During the process of using several hundred selected souls to forge bodies, an extremely rare accident occurred. Amid a tremendous explosion, all the remaining life materials and souls fused together.

Normally, such a mixture of remnants would have been completely incapable of giving birth to an intelligent individual. Yet, by an extraordinary coincidence, a genius like Diyi Jinglun was born from this mass of mud like material.

Emperor Sanmo said with great regret, "Even though I tried my utmost to conceal it, the uniqueness of Diyi Jinglun still failed to escape the notice of the Dharma-Transmitters and Mirror Holders. Fearing that Diyi Jinglun would become a threat to them after he matured, they actually joined forces to suppress him."

"In the face of the cultivation laws established by Dharma-Transmitting Heavenly Sovereign's New Cultivation Method, without the necessary resources, no matter how heaven defying your aptitude is, you can only obediently remain restricted. Such talent, which even I find somewhat unbelievable, is actually still stuck in the Soul Transformation realm..." There was some indignation in Emperor Sanmo's voice.

"This is also related to the characteristics of our [Illusory Truth Sages] faction. When I first pledged allegiance to the Immortal Ancestor, I made an agreement with him that I would not interfere in the affairs of the Immortal Alliance and would only provide assistance at certain special moments. It would not be appropriate for me to interfere too much." He explained this to Li Fan.

Li Fan was not unfamiliar with Diyi Jinglun either.

When his strength was still weak, he had already heard of his accomplishments, "Three years of cultivation, a mortal forming a Golden Core. A Golden Core cultivator slaying a Nascent Soul cultivator and defeating a Soul Transformation cultivator."

Later, when he was appointed Island Master of Ten Thousand Immortal Island, he had also received Diyi Jinglun's appointment, constructing Congyun Immortal City and suppressing the Nether Passage beneath Ten Thousand Immortal Island.

However, Li Fan possessed the benefit of [Truth] and could recklessly gather intelligence without concern for consequences in every life. Cultivation resources were never a problem for him.

After achieving the Nascent Soul realm through the Five Elements Great Grotto Heaven, this so called "heavenly prodigy" was already no longer worthy of his attention.

"Speaking of which, this Diyi Jinglun is actually a suitable experimental chess piece."

"Let us see how abundant the remaining possibilities in the Mortal World are now."

"Even if infinite possibilities mean that everyone has the opportunity to prove the Dao and become the strongest, those with greater aptitude, comprehension, and state of mind will have a higher overall probability of proving the Dao. After the Dao Annihilation Calamity swept across the Mortal World, more of them would consequently survive. Someone like He Zhenghao, who seeks to achieve the Nameless Realm, would be exceedingly rare even among infinity."

"I can divide them into different tiers according to their aptitude and send them beyond the High Wall separately..." Li Fan had already made up his mind.

For the time being, Li Fan did not summon Diyi Jinglun. Instead, he had Emperor Sanmo lead the way and went straight to where the Dharma-Transmitter Council was located.

With Emperor Sanmo, the Immortal Ancestor's personally appointed leader of the Illusory Truth Sages faction, using an "extremely urgent matter" as an excuse, the vast majority of the Dharma-Transmitters were lured into gathering in one place.

Li Fan knew the corrupt nature of these Dharma-Transmitters all too well and could not be bothered to waste words with them. He directly summoned an Immortal-grade formation, descended upon the place, and suppressed every one of them.

After being thoroughly conditioned, those who were still usable to some extent would be released. If they were truly beyond saving, they would simply be destroyed. Then, using simulated personalities together with the body refinement techniques provided by Emperor Sanmo, new Dharma-Transmitters would be created to replace them.

The body refinement techniques of the demon beast race bore seven or eight tenths of a resemblance to those developed within the Empire's small worlds. However, all of the Empire's techniques were based on exotic beasts that had been completely stripped of their demon beast bloodlines, allowing the potential within their biological bodies to be explored more thoroughly. Although the techniques appeared considerably weaker on the surface, their essence was not actually far behind.

After Li Fan provided these techniques to Emperor Sanmo, Emperor Sanmo was pleasantly surprised again and again, gaining considerable inspiration from them.

After quietly seizing control of the Dharma-Transmitters Council, Li Fan did not immediately confront the Tianxuan Mirror.

Instead, he first went to the Guardian Hall.

After the three Dao Integration guardians responsible for guarding the Tianxuan Mirror repeatedly confirmed that the transfer orders they had received were genuine, they hurriedly left the Derivation Law Jade space with expressions of shock and uncertainty. Only the Derivation Law Jade remained alone.

The little girl, who had originally been listlessly sprawled across the long wooden table, observed the situation for a long while before finally being unable to contain herself.

She sneakily raised her head.

"What is going on here?" the little girl secretly wondered.

She thought for a long time. Although she was uncertain about what exactly had happened, the fact remained that no one was guarding the place.

Yet, surprisingly, the little girl of the Derivation Law Jade did not even think about escaping. Instead, she was overjoyed and happily settled down to rest.

No one knew how much time passed.

Until a figure suddenly appeared before her and lightly tapped the wooden table with a finger.

The little girl abruptly awakened from her sweet dream. She skillfully wiped the drool from the corner of her mouth and widened her eyes.

There was not the slightest trace that she had just been sleeping.

She carefully examined the newcomer and asked tentatively, "Are you the new guardian?"

The newcomer, Li Fan, smiled faintly. "Yes, and no."

"I hate you riddle people the most." The little girl puffed out her cheeks and spoke with disdain.

Then she no longer wanted to bother with Li Fan. She counted on her fingers as if calculating something, while her large eyes looked toward the enormous sphere of characters above her head.

Li Fan did not mind.

Unhurriedly, he conjured an identical long wooden table before himself.

He spread out the paper, ground the ink, and picked up the brush.

With a single flourish, he completed it.

Only two characters were written across the long wooden table.

Yet the moment those two characters appeared, they involuntarily drew the little girl's attention.

She was stunned for a moment, her eyes filled with confusion.

Then she seemed to recall something and suddenly let out a prolonged, meaningless scream.

It continued for a long time without stopping.

"You... you... you..." The Derivation Law Jade pointed at Li Fan with a trembling finger as if she had seen a ghost. She could barely speak.

Li Fan merely glanced at the little girl indifferently.

The little girl was startled again, but under Li Fan's gaze, she did not dare move recklessly. She could only stare at him in terror.

"How much of your memories from the Immortal Realm remain?"

Only after the Derivation Law Jade finally calmed down did Li Fan ask indifferently.

This question made the little girl even more certain of her suspicions.

The fear in her heart intensified, and she hurriedly shook her head as she replied, "I cannot remember any of it at all!"

"Hm?" Li Fan narrowed his eyes and frowned.

The little girl waved both hands and anxiously explained, "Really, I am not lying!"

"I am also somewhat puzzled about this. But Big Brother Jing Xuan said that, in order to avoid being caught up in the disasters of the Immortal Realm, most immortal artifacts that wanted to survive had to sever their connection with their former selves..."

"These two characters were also something I only vaguely remembered after seeing you write them just now."

Immortal artifacts, immortal puppets, immortal servants, and the like.

It seemed that before a True Immortal, they were naturally placed in a position of oppression.

Merely from a simple glance, the little girl of the Derivation Law Jade immediately poured out everything she knew.

The Derivation Law Jade that accompanied her within her body had originally come from the Immortal Realm, but it had already been incomplete.

After she fused with it using her own body, her memories from the past had disappeared completely, like salt dissolving into water.

"Do not move."

Li Fan said indifferently as he placed a hand on top of the little girl's head and began examining the condition within her body.

There was already no trace of the Derivation Law Jade's exotic artifact form.

It had almost completely fused with her human body.

However, the nature of being a "human" still remained to some extent. It had not been completely fused and devoured.

"I can sense the desire within your heart."

"Do you want to live from now on as the Derivation Law Jade, or as a true human?"

Li Fan withdrew his hand and asked indifferently.

The little girl was suddenly stunned when she heard this. Then she realized something and slightly opened her mouth.

She did not answer immediately, instead struggling with the choice for quite some time.

"To become the Derivation Law Jade, you are destined to lose your freedom, but your lifespan will be endless, and you will have no fear of dying."

"To become a human, you can escape the shackles of being an artifact. But in this world, there may not be any true freedom and carefree existence."

Li Fan calmly reminded her.

The little girl lowered her head and fell into deep thought.

But in the end, just as Li Fan had expected, she firmly chose the latter.

To become a human.

"Very well. Since you have made up your mind, I will grant your wish."

Countless golden threads erupted in an instant, plunging deeply into the little girl's body.

Under the indescribable pain, tears and mucus immediately streamed down the little girl's face.

Li Fan paid no attention to her suffering. He merely sensed the exotic artifact within her body and slowly extracted it.

Next

Chapter 1421: Recreating The Derivation Law Jade

The wondrous Derivation Law Jade had almost completely fused into the physical body, and ordinary methods would make it extremely difficult to separate the two. However, Li Fan was well versed in medical theory and also possessed the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood. He simply chose the most brutal yet effective approach.

He no longer cared about the consequences. First, he destructively removed the flesh and blood contaminated by the Derivation Law Jade. During this process, he used the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood to create a replacement mass of flesh and blood out of thin air, maintaining the little girl's vitality.

The physical body was relatively easy to deal with. The truly difficult and dangerous part was the soul.

After being fused with the Derivation Law Jade for too long, even the little girl's soul was filled with the marks of the Derivation Law Jade's overwhelming presence.

Li Fan's divine sense was like a blade. With incomparable precision, he first cut and separated it inch by inch. As he replenished the missing parts with the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, he also used the Limitless Fullness and Emptiness Method to transform strands of refined soul nourishment and infuse them into her.

Throughout the entire process, there could not be the slightest mistake. Otherwise, while the wondrous Derivation Law Jade might remain unharmed, the little girl would instantly have her soul scattered and perish.

Yet Li Fan felt not the slightest trace of nervousness. Even though this was his first attempt at such a form of "medical treatment."

In any case, there was no third person in this world who had witnessed his conversation with the Derivation Law Jade.

If the attempt unfortunately failed...

Then the little girl could only blame her own bad luck.

Li Fan remained completely focused, immersed in the process.

Slowly, as Li Fan took out a blood-red, round jade artifact, the little girl also entered a wondrous state between "truth and falsehood."

At first glance, she seemed no different from before the "surgery," apart from being somewhat weaker.

In reality, however, if Li Fan were to cancel the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood maintaining her existence with a single thought, the adorable little girl before him would instantly collapse into a mass of flesh and blood.

"The boundary between truth and falsehood..."

Li Fan carefully observed the creation of truth and falsehood that had been born from his own hands. Having completely practiced the process of creating life once, Li Fan felt that his comprehension of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood had unknowingly risen another level.

For now, he still could not make it permanent. Only a tiny portion of what he created out of nothing through the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood could remain. However, Li Fan could continuously repeat the process and make up for the deficiency through sheer quantity.

Even so, after Li Fan completed this exquisitely precise separation technique, something that could be said to be unparalleled in the world, the reborn little girl had shrunk considerably.

Originally, she had looked about five or six years old.

Now she was only about three or four years old.

After waking up, the little girl looked at her completely new body with her eyes wide open, her face filled with astonishment.

Although she had lost the augmentation of the Derivation Law Jade, when Li Fan constructed this body, he had retained a trace of the spiritual power of a projection of the Xuanhuang Realm. The little girl now had no cultivation, but in terms of the depth of her foundation, she might surpass every cultivator in the Xuanhuang Realm below the Longevity Immortal Realm.

"Do you remember your original name?" Li Fan slowly asked, his left hand rubbing the Derivation Law Jade.

The flesh and blood that had been attached to the Derivation Law Jade had already disappeared, revealing its original appearance.

On the round jade body was a gap that looked as though it had been cleaved open by a sword. It occupied nearly one tenth of the entire jade. At first glance, it appeared severely incomplete.

The little girl hesitated for a while before finally answering in a clear, childish voice, "I don't remember."

The heaven defying methods Li Fan had displayed further confirmed her previous speculation. The person before her must definitely be a True Immortal who had descended into the mortal world. She no longer dared to display the slightest trace of mischief and instead became extremely restrained.

How could Li Fan fail to notice such an obvious change in her expression? However, seeing that she seemed determined to completely bid farewell to her past, Li Fan could not be bothered to pursue the matter.

"Since that is the case, from now on, your name will be Li Ningqing."

Although she had been separated from the Derivation Law Jade, thousands of years of symbiotic existence and the fragmented memories from the Immortal Realm had still deeply influenced her.

Li Ningqing subconsciously bowed, her little face solemn.

"Thank you, Master, for bestowing me with a name."

Li Fan nodded slightly and said nothing more, carefully studying the Derivation Law Jade in his hand.

Li Ningqing stood quietly beside him, not daring to disturb him.

However, old habits die hard. Before long, Li Ningqing could no longer contain herself.

She raised her head and secretly glanced at Li Fan.

Just as Li Ningqing had said, there were indeed very few memories concerning the Immortal Realm remaining within the Derivation Law Jade.

The fact that the fragments of memories concerning [Creation] could still survive was entirely because this True Immortal seal script character was far too special. At the same time as it was engraved into the memories, it had also subtly influenced the Derivation Law Jade itself. Only because of this could it remain intact despite the erosion of countless years.

Even though only scattered fragments remained, Li Fan still saw several scenes that were valuable to him.

Light and shadows shifted as a figure paced back and forth.

It seemed that the original owner of the Derivation Law Jade had encountered some extremely difficult matter.

"Foolish. Truly unbelievably foolish."

"To actually regard such a calamity as nothing..."

"No, perhaps they already have something else in mind and simply have no time to care about us. It seems we still have to rely on ourselves. Otherwise, the tragedy of the Qianhuan Star Region is a living example."

...

No one knew how much time had passed.

The humanoid figure of light and shadow had already fled into the distance.

Only the Derivation Law Jade remained alone in its original location.

The howling of the wind gradually rose, as if some great terror were about to descend.

An inexplicable force suddenly enveloped the Derivation Law Jade.

The Derivation Law Jade flew into the sky and transformed into a shooting star, speeding uncontrollably toward a certain direction.

Around it, countless streaks of radiance also pierced through the heavens like the Derivation Law Jade.

However, their positions seemed to be different from one another.

...

The Lower Realm Star Sea.

No one knew how long it had been flying when a figure suddenly reached out and caught the Derivation Law Jade.

After carefully examining it, the figure shook its head slightly.

Then it threw the Derivation Law Jade into the Xuanhuang Realm.

...

These memory fragments were deeply hidden within the Derivation Law Jade. Even after it transformed into a wondrous artifact and was absorbed by Li Ningqing, she could not perceive them without some special condition triggering or drawing them out. Only someone like Li Fan, conducting an almost destructive and exhaustive search, could awaken these ancient preserved memories.

"It seems that the Derivation Law Jade came from the Immortal Realm to the Xuanhuang Realm a very long time ago. It was then swallowed and absorbed by the world."

Li Fan was not unfamiliar with the figure that had intercepted the Derivation Law Jade.

It was precisely that True Immortal who had descended into the Lower Realm, whom he had once seen within the memories of the True Immortal flesh and blood derived from the Kun-Qian Bone Hand.

"The Immortal Realm. The emergence of the Dao Annihilation Calamity had already shown signs long ago. Even with the 'official' construction of the Immortal Celestial Pillars to maintain order, I fear quite a few True Immortals still chose to use their own methods to survive the calamity. Whether they succeeded is another matter."

"When calamity arrives, everyone reveals their own abilities. This is nothing new in the history of the Xuanhuang Realm either."

Li Fan secretly pondered.

What concerned him even more was that scene of countless shooting-star-like lights fleeing in different directions.

"At that point in time, it was still the ancient era for the Xuanhuang Realm. Yet the region of the Immortal Realm where the Derivation Law Jade was located seemed to have already been struck by the great catastrophe."

"I wonder what else was among those countless points of shooting-star-like light, apart from Immortal artifacts like the Derivation Law Jade."

"Could it be that some supreme expert took action and preserved the sparks of the Immortal Realm in this manner?"

Apart from this, there was nothing particularly special left in the Derivation Law Jade's past memories.

As for everything else, it consisted of cultivation techniques that the Derivation Law Jade had continuously deduced throughout the years.

Li Fan swept through them with a single thought. These cultivation techniques were already of little significance to him, who had attained the Half Immortal realm.

He had gone to considerable effort to extract the Derivation Law Jade mainly because of its former identity as an Immortal artifact.

Within his sea of consciousness, the Immortal Formation Dissociation Disc emitted specks of immortal spiritual qi.

Li Fan first attempted to use the Derivation Law Jade to absorb this immortal spiritual qi.

However, the Derivation Law Jade, like Li Fan himself, was completely unable to make use of this mysterious energy. It merely circulated through its body once before the immortal spiritual qi emerged completely intact.

The Dissociation Disc once again drew the immortal spiritual qi back in.

"As expected, it is just as I predicted. Immortal spiritual qi should possess a unique identity mark."

"It can only be used by specific targets."

Li Fan once again recalled the process by which the Final Dissociation Disc had formally advanced into an Immortal-grade artifact.

"Left behind by the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable, absorbed and transformed by the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm, then forcibly split off before reaching the extreme limit of the Immortal Realm."

He vaguely began to understand.

"It seems that the key may lie in the transformation carried out by the Xuanhuang Heaven and Earth. The mechanism behind this may be similar to the ancient Immortal formation. It may have already existed since the creation of the Xuanhuang Realm. Even the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm may not necessarily know about it."

"To advance the Derivation Law Jade into an Immortal-grade artifact..."

Li Fan had no intention of making the Xuanhuang Realm undergo another ascension.

Having learned from his previous experience, the trouble itself was not particularly troublesome. It was simply that, with Li Fan's current strength, there were more convenient methods available.

Li Fan temporarily put away the Derivation Law Jade and looked at Li Ningqing, who had recovered from her daze and immediately lowered her head.

"You have not gone out for a long time. I will give you some time. Go out and have some fun first."

"Although you have no cultivation, with this body alone, you can roam the Xuanhuang Realm without worry," Li Fan said indifferently.

Li Ningqing was overjoyed and hurriedly bowed in thanks.

"One year."

"After one year, wait for my summons," Li Fan added.

Li Ningqing did not pay much attention to it. Her mind had already been completely overwhelmed by the thought of having an entire year off.

Heaven knows, in this lifetime, she had never had such a long continuous vacation.

Compared with that detestable Immortal Ancestor, her master was truly a great benefactor!

Li Ningqing could not help thinking this.

While she was still excited, her vision blurred, and Li Fan had already sent her out of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters.

Li Fan's voice could still faintly be heard beside her ears.

"Walk more. See more. If you encounter anything interesting, do not let it pass you by."

"Do not worry. Your master has your back."

Li Ningqing's heart settled completely, and she was filled with anticipation for this journey through the Xuanhuang Realm.

"Master is so kind. If Jing Xuan meets him, he will definitely be very happy too."

Feeling the little girl's cheerful footsteps as she traveled away, Li Fan also nodded slightly.

"I hope you can discover some of the secrets still hidden within the Xuanhuang Realm."

Li Ningqing had been fused with the Derivation Law Jade for far too long, and a peculiar connection had gradually formed between them.

Perhaps the ancient artifacts from the Immortal Realm that once existed could also possess this kind of connection. Just now, for example, the moment Li Fan took out the immortal spiritual qi, she had sensed it immediately.

Even though he had already ascended to the Immortal Realm, Li Fan would never let down his guard toward the Xuanhuang Realm, which had once been a testing ground of the Immortal Realm.

"However, my current priority should be outside the High Wall. The exploration of the Xuanhuang Realm can be left to others."

After resolving the matter of the Derivation Law Jade, Li Fan finally arrived before the lineage of the Mirror Holders.

Here, Li Fan saw the true body of the Tianxuan Mirror.

An ancient green bronze mirror.

Three Mirror Holders were seated cross-legged around the bronze mirror.

When they saw Li Fan suddenly appear, with Dharma-Transmitter Zhou and Emperor Sanmo standing respectfully on either side of him, their expressions changed drastically.

Without saying anything further, Li Fan waved his hand and suppressed them.

Li Fan's figure drifted forward until he arrived before the Tianxuan Mirror.

He keenly sensed the restriction placed upon the true body of the Tianxuan Mirror.

It was unmistakably the work of the Heavenly Doctor.

"Jing Xuan."

A meaningful smile appeared on Li Fan's face. He then bent his finger and tapped the body of the mirror three times.

The restrictions upon it shattered inch by inch.

At the same time, the space within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters began to violently tremble.

A figure drifted out from the ancient mirror.

His expression was incomparably solemn and tense as he stared at Li Fan.

"Greetings, Senior."

Knowing everything that happened within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, how could he possibly not know about the earthshaking upheaval occurring at the headquarters?

While secretly contacting the Immortal Ancestor, Jing Xuan's human clone also stopped experiencing the life of a "human" and began fleeing toward the natural hidden Cave Heaven he remembered.

Jing Xuan believed he had done this without making the slightest sound.

Yet he did not know that everything was already within Li Fan's control.

Li Fan reached forward slightly.

His five fingers seemed to pass through spacetime as he exerted a slight force.

Jing Xuan's expression changed drastically, as though someone had grabbed him by the throat. His entire face flushed red.

A mortal, tens of thousands of li away, was forcibly pulled into Li Fan's hand from thin air.

Li Fan casually threw him aside.

Dharma-Transmitter Zhou and Emperor Sanmo were both completely bewildered.

Only Jing Xuan and his human clone were filled with terror.

"Not obedient."

"Deserves a beating."

Li Fan once again tapped lightly with his finger.

This time, he used considerable force.

Both of Jing Xuan's bodies were struck as if by lightning and immediately collapsed limply onto the ground.

The space within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters trembled violently, as though the apocalypse had arrived.

Most terrifying of all, a crack actually appeared on the true body of the Tianxuan Mirror from that single strike.

It seemed that Li Fan simply did not care whether this extraordinarily mysterious Immortal-grade artifact lived or died.

Jing Xuan was completely terrified and was about to beg for mercy.

Yet Li Fan reached out, grabbed him, and stuffed him back into the Tianxuan Mirror.

However, at this moment, Jing Xuan did not experience the feeling of being completely at home that he normally had upon returning to the main body.

Instead, he keenly sensed that, for some unknown reason, a barrier had formed between himself and the Tianxuan Mirror.

After carefully investigating, Jing Xuan discovered the reason.

Apart from the sealing method imposed by that True Immortal, there was now another version of himself inside the Tianxuan Mirror, currently taking shape.

From that blurry figure, Jing Xuan seemed to see himself.

"This is..."

A chill rose from the depths of Jing Xuan's heart.

With his extensive knowledge and experience, Jing Xuan had already realized that the True Immortal seemed to be reconstructing a new artifact spirit, intending to replace him.

"It should not have been this easy. But unfortunately, even my human body has fallen into his hands."

Jing Xuan was both terrified and horrified, as though he had already seen his future fate. He immediately cried out repeatedly.

"Senior, please spare me!"

Chapter 1422: The Secret Of The Xuantian Holy Envoy

Since Li Fan had already activated the [Simulated Creation, Diverse Forms of Sentient Beings] Grand Formation, how could he possibly stop so easily just because of a few pleas for mercy from Jing Xuan?

From his experiences in previous reincarnations, Li Fan already had a rough understanding of Jing Xuan's character. For the sake of caution, Li Fan might leave him alive. But as for allowing Jing Xuan to continue serving as the artifact spirit of the Tianxuan Mirror, Li Fan absolutely would not allow it.

Back then, after receiving the treasured mirror from the Dharma-Transmitter, yet still choosing to preserve its artifact spirit and merely leaving behind a Mirror Holder to restrain it, this had absolutely been because his strength was insufficient and he could not accomplish it otherwise.

But Li Fan was different.

Not only did he have the support of an Immortal-grade formation, he also understood and had a firm grasp of Jing Xuan's human clone.

This human clone was essentially a copy Jing Xuan himself had tailor-made, one that was free from the restraints of an Immortal Artifact.

Jing Xuan himself had once evaluated this body:

"It possesses the weight of an artifact while also possessing the spirituality of a human. Flawless and nearly equivalent to a True Immortal."

This was an escape method, or rather an escape path, that Jing Xuan had painstakingly conceived during the several thousand years in which the Tianxuan Mirror had been sealed off by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

The profound aspect of this path did not merely lie in the human body before him. It lay in the "principle" that could continuously generate human bodies.

As long as the Tianxuan Mirror itself remained, even if the human body were killed, it could quickly be generated again.

Previously, when Li Fan had spoken with Jing Xuan, the latter's words had been profound and obscure, leaving things shrouded in mystery.

But now, after personally investigating it, the entire method had become completely clear to him.

"Broadly speaking, it is based on the same principle as Zhang Qianmo's Grand Vow Dharma Body. The difference is that Zhang Qianmo's Grand Vow Dharma Body relies on the Great Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm for its existence."

"Whereas the foundation upon which Jing Xuan's clone exists is nothing more than the Tianxuan Mirror itself."

"Although the Tianxuan Mirror no longer possesses even the slightest trace of immortal spiritual qi, its status as an Immortal Artifact still remains. Moreover, it retains a vast amount of knowledge from the Immortal Realm, making this status even more stable. The weight of a single artifact is not inferior to that of an entire world, and may even surpass it."

Li Fan's expression remained unchanged, but he could not help admiring it inwardly.

Admiration was one thing, but his actions could not stop.

Under Jing Xuan's horrified gaze, Li Fan extracted a strand of the Tianxuan Mirror's origin.

The Emperor Sanmo and Dharma-Transmitter Zhou, who stood nearby with their eyes lowered and minds focused, naturally had no idea what Li Fan had taken out.

But how could Jing Xuan possibly not know?

Looking at the familiar aura swirling around Li Fan's hand, Jing Xuan's expression was as if he had seen a ghost.

He began desperately trying to recall when exactly he had divided off this strand of origin.

Yet no matter how hard he racked his brain, he could not find the answer.

Despair and darkness gradually enveloped him.

The gift the Tianxuan Mirror had voluntarily given him in the past had now become the final thread with which he had bound himself.

Jing Xuan had once proudly declared that no one in the Xuanhuang Realm could truly harm him.

That had indeed been true in the past.

But now, Li Fan had already ascended to the Half Immortal realm.

Using the former Xuantian Spirit Locking Formation as its core foundation, the Final Dissociation Disc instantly deduced an advanced Immortal-grade version, firmly restraining the Tianxuan Mirror itself.

He attacked from both the artifact spirit and human clone sides simultaneously.

Using the Tianxuan Mirror's origin as a natural point of entry, he employed the Simulated Grand Formation to create an entirely new "Jing Xuan" and gradually replace Jing Xuan's control over the Tianxuan Mirror.

Such a heaven-swapping, sun-replacing method was absolutely unimaginable to ordinary mortals.

Even for existences like the Heavenly Doctor and the Immortal Ancestor, Supreme beings of the star sea, it was probably still merely at the stage of theoretical conception.

Yet Li Fan could accomplish it with ease.

Part of the credit naturally belonged to the Final Dissociation Disc.

But more importantly, it was because Li Fan, in the form of a Half Immortal, had neutralized the top-down suppression brought by the Tianxuan Mirror's status as an Immortal Artifact.

The two sides were now roughly equal. There was no longer some insurmountable gulf between them.

No matter how tightly one defended, how could one withstand every means Li Fan could bring to bear?

Even without the assistance of the Final Dissociation Disc, Li Fan believed he would only need to spend more time to achieve his objective.

The analysis and replication of Jing Xuan continued.

Li Fan also discovered a few remnants of hidden restrictions within the Tianxuan Mirror itself. He casually removed all of them.

Even if Jing Xuan had not secretly made any moves beforehand, the changes occurring within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance at this moment could not escape his notice.

"The Immortal Ancestor and Heavenly Doctor are already returning to the Xuanhuang Realm. Perfect. I will capture them all in one stroke and suppress them first."

Li Fan casually raised his head and glanced in that direction as he thought this.

He did not take it particularly seriously.

After witnessing darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai, the two counterparts originating from beyond possibility, Li Fan felt that for the sake of all living beings in the Star Sea, it would be safest if the two of them obediently remained where they were.

While waiting for the analysis to finish, Li Fan reflected on the process by which he had recently separated the Derivation Law Jade and replicated Jing Xuan.

"With my current abilities, completely [replicating] a person is still somewhat difficult."

"But creating something that resembles the original by eight or nine tenths should not be a problem. The flaw that cannot be avoided due to insufficient strength may actually be an advantage now that possibilities are continuously annihilating and converging."

"The true Heavenly Doctor cannot cross the High Wall. Then what about a Heavenly Doctor-like being created by me..."

Li Fan's eyes flickered as he devised and refined his exploration plan.

There was neither day nor night within the Tianxuan Mirror's space.

As expected of a truly complete Immortal Artifact that had merely abandoned its immortal spiritual qi, the difficulty of replicating its artifact spirit was still somewhat beyond Li Fan's expectations.

It took a full thirty-six hours before Li Fan finally created a preliminary imitation of Jing Xuan.

At present, it was still unable to activate all the functions of the Tianxuan Mirror as naturally as a born artifact spirit could.

But the most basic functions, such as manipulation, inspection, and even defense against enemies, were no longer a problem.

The first thing Li Fan did after obtaining control over the Tianxuan Mirror was to inspect the complete [Grand Formation of the Primordial Mystic Spirit].

This complete Immortal-grade grand formation possessed the ability to reestablish earth, fire, water, and wind, and open heaven and split the earth within the void.

Li Fan had coveted it for a very long time.

"Hm? What a pity."

"The Tianxuan Mirror also does not possess all nine complete formation diagrams."

Li Fan sighed inwardly.

After carefully investigating, he did indeed gain something.

But apart from the [Astral Mechanism, All Spirits Return to Numbers], [Desolate Fall, Flowers Bloom in an Empty Room], and [Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form] that Li Fan already possessed, the Tianxuan Mirror contained only two additional grand formation diagrams.

[Dao Arises, Life Everlasting] and [Ethereal Ascension, Life and Death Rise and Fall]!

Li Fan was not excessively greedy.

After so many reincarnations, he had only managed to collect three formation diagrams.

Now that he had suddenly obtained two new ones, it was already enough to occupy his attention for quite some time.

He carefully examined these two formation diagrams.

[Dao Arises, Life Everlasting] described the principles by which the formation simulated the gestation and evolution of life force, after which the countless forms of vitality circulated throughout the world.

"Life force is born from nothing, solely because the Dao exists, and thus it manifests..."

"Wonderful."

Li Fan's eyes flickered, and he felt as though he had suddenly understood something profound.

Only after a long while did he reluctantly turn his attention toward the other formation diagram.

[Ethereal Ascension, Life and Death Rise and Fall].

"If a world possessed only the evolution of life force, that would definitely be abnormal. Life and death are entangled and coexist. Only then does it conform to the Great Dao. This [Life and Death Rise and Fall] Grand Formation seems to be explaining the principles of life and death..."

Li Fan carefully observed it.

He discovered that the principles contained within it bore some faint similarities to the [Reversal of Life and Death Grand Formation] he had once seen.

But obviously, the profundity of this magnificent Immortal formation was far beyond that of any mundane formation.

"It is not merely the circulation of life and death within a world. It even involves the world itself, and perhaps even the Star Sea beyond the world?"

The more Li Fan pondered, the more astonished he became.

Once activated, the [Ethereal Ascension, Life and Death Rise and Fall] Grand Formation could effortlessly drain the vitality of an entire cultivation world and transform it into a single white feather.

It could then once again guide an infinite amount of vitality out from that white feather, bringing forth a world from a single feather.

Even the Star Sea operated according to the same principle.

Relying solely on himself, Li Fan had not been able to fully comprehend these two formations. Just having a partial understanding of them was already giving him a headache.

The reason was that, at the level of the Grand Formation of the Primordial Mystic Spirit, they were no longer merely formations themselves. They incorporated the profound principles of the Great Dao. Merely understanding formations without understanding the Great Dao made it fundamentally impossible to master them.

Fortunately, Li Fan possessed the Immortal-grade Final Dissociation Disc.

"Although I do not understand the underlying principles, I can use them first."

"I will comprehend them while putting them into practice."

Of the nine formation diagrams, Li Fan had now obtained five.

Having more than half of them, perhaps the Final Dissociation Disc alone could deduce the remaining portions given enough time. However, the amount of computation required was simply too enormous.

In his daily activities, Li Fan could hardly do without the Final Dissociation Disc. He could not spare enough computational power to complete such a formidable task.

Thus, Li Fan merely had the Final Dissociation Disc make a token effort, allocating only a tiny portion of its power to slowly carry out the deductions. Perhaps one day, it might suddenly give him a pleasant surprise, just like a cultivator suddenly attaining enlightenment.

Li Fan tried searching the Tianxuan Mirror for clues regarding the remaining formation diagrams of the Grand Formation of the Primordial Mystic Spirit.

"It seems that after undergoing several disturbances, the Tianxuan Mirror has lost part of its memories."

Li Fan frowned slightly.

He carefully examined it and confirmed that this was not some secret trick being employed by the Tianxuan Mirror. Only after discovering actual hidden damage within its main body did Li Fan finally feel reassured.

"The first disturbance occurred when it was still in the Immortal Realm. It seems the Tianxuan Mirror was also fought over and changed hands several times. It was caused by being repeatedly refined by others."

Based on the ancient traces within the Tianxuan Mirror, together with the memories it had retained, Li Fan began reconstructing what had happened.

"The second disturbance occurred after it was brought back by Xuanyuan Hong, when, with his assistance, it voluntarily transformed from immortal to mortal."

Li Fan looked at the figure of the Xuantian King within the Tianxuan Mirror's memories, his expression thoughtful.

"The third disturbance..."

What he discovered next caused Li Fan's spirit to jolt.

It involved the reason why the Tianxuan Mirror had fallen into the hands of the Immortal Ancestor after the destruction of the Xuantian Sect. The scenes Li Fan witnessed also resolved a question that had troubled him for a long time.

Back then, within One-Heart Heavenly Sovereign's illusion formation, Zhao Ruoxi, who was the Sect Master of the Grand Dao Sect, had once mentioned that a Xuantian Holy Envoy had escaped the pursuit of the Ten Great Immortal Sects, carrying the Tianxuan Treasure Mirror and disappearing without a trace.

The Ten Sects had exhausted every possible means to find it. They had even offered the promise that one could exchange the mirror for a Longevity Immortal. Yet in the end, they still failed to find the treasure mirror.

However, during Li Fan's interactions with Mo Rubin, as well as within Ji Yuzhen's remnant soul, this Xuantian Holy Envoy had never been mentioned.

As a result, for a long time, Li Fan had believed that the so-called Xuantian Holy Envoy existed only within Zhao Ruoxi's dream. It was fictional and did not actually exist in reality.

But...

Li Fan watched the rapidly flashing scenes, and his expression became somewhat peculiar.

The Xuantian Holy Envoy truly existed.

But she was not one of the Twelve Dharma Kings of the Xuantian Sect.

In fact, she had never even appeared in any official setting of the Xuantian Sect.

A familiar scene of a mountain village appeared.

It was the mountain village not far from the Fishing Pond.

An ordinary looking middle aged woman woke from her sleep and inexplicably discovered a mirror placed beside her bed.

Then, seemingly after receiving some information, she suddenly burst into tears, clutching the Tianxuan Mirror and weeping bitterly.

Faintly, she could be heard calling out, "Ah Hong."

It was none other than the name of the Xuantian King, Xuanyuan Hong.

"No wonder Xuanyuan Hong had always maintained an ambiguous distance from Ji Yuzhen and the other women among the Twelve Dharma Kings, never showing any formal attitude toward them."

"So he already had a childhood sweetheart!"

"For an ordinary cultivator, the passage of too many years of cultivation would naturally create an insurmountable divide between immortal and mortal."

"But the Xuantian King rose to prominence far too quickly. Moreover, he possessed a supreme treasure like the Fishing Pond..."

"Protecting his childhood sweetheart would have been exceedingly easy for him."

The images flashed rapidly.

When the Xuantian King handed the Xuantian Treasure Mirror to the woman, he seemed to have also taught her a method of suppression.

One purpose was to prevent the Xuantian Treasure Mirror from turning against its master. Another was to prevent the aura of the treasure mirror from leaking out and bringing disaster upon the woman.

The scenes that the treasure mirror could sense from the outside world were blurred.

It could only clearly sense that the woman who was in contact with it had experienced a tremendous increase in strength within a short period of time.

In merely three years, she had gone from being a mortal to becoming a Dao Integration cultivator.

One day, inside an unknown secret chamber, the woman performed a secret technique on the Xuantian Treasure Mirror.

"Ah Hong told me that you have an extraordinary origin and came from the Immortal Realm."

"But the Immortal Realm has already been destroyed. Although you transformed from immortal to mortal with Ah Hong's help, that connection between you has not been completely severed."

"If Ah Hong were still here, that would not matter."

"But now Ah Hong is trapped in a dangerous situation... I am going to find him."

"The journey ahead is fraught with danger, with certain death and no hope of survival. I do not want to take you with me to your death."

"You should remain here in the Xuanhuang Realm."

"Remember, the Immortal Dao is no more. Since you have already transformed from immortal to mortal, do not attempt to return to the Immortal Dao."

"Not returning to your original immortal artifact form does not mean you have no path forward. Your Dao lies in measuring Heaven and surveying Earth."

"The Xuanhuang Realm conceals far too many mysteries. Even Ah Hong back then was unable to completely uncover them. If you can thoroughly investigate all the secrets of this realm..."

"The Great Dao will be within your grasp!"

"I have pointed out your path to the Dao, and thus I have shown you great kindness."

"The journey ahead is dangerous, so I need you to lend me your strength!"

That figure gently pressed a hand against the treasure mirror.

No one knew what method she used. In an instant, another identical shadow unexpectedly floated out from the Xuantian Treasure Mirror.

"From now on, your name is Tianxuan."

"Xuantian... I will take with me."

The voice faded into the distance, disappearing without a trace.

At the same time, the restrictions placed upon the mirror itself vanished completely.

Having gained freedom, the Tianxuan Mirror began following the woman's instructions, measuring Heaven and surveying Earth, searching for the mysteries hidden within the Xuanhuang Realm.

Then, one day, it encountered the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1423: The Nameless Immortal Must Be Killed

The encounter between the Tianxuan Mirror and the Immortal Ancestor could be said to have been inevitable.

Just as when Li Fan's strength had expanded to a certain level, it was destined that he would eventually encounter the Immortal Ancestor, the Hunger Immortal, and the others.

Although its primary power was not meant for defending against enemies or harming others, it was, after all, an immortal artifact that had descended into the mortal world. If it had only been the Immortal Ancestor alone, Jing Xuan might have been able to barely contend with him. However, he had never expected that behind the Immortal Ancestor, there was also the Heavenly Doctor.

The two great experts joined forces and subdued the Tianxuan Mirror together.

For Jing Xuan, this was undoubtedly a rather unpleasant memory.

After blurry images of light flashed rapidly, the Tianxuan Mirror transformed into the foundational pillar of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, a peerless supreme treasure.

During the years when the Tianxuan Mirror measured the heavens and surveyed the earth, it was not without gains. As its analysis of the Xuanhuang Realm continued to deepen, the Tianxuan Mirror also developed several abilities that it had never possessed in the past.

For example, Division and Secondary Generation.

Division referred to the Tianxuan sub-mirrors distributed throughout the various prefectures of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, as well as the mini Tianxuan Mirrors that could be used independently by individuals. They all possessed a portion of the Tianxuan Mirror's functions, and were essentially the same as the Tianxuan Mirror itself. They could be regarded as extensions of the Tianxuan Mirror reaching outward.

Secondary Generation was the opposite of Division. If Division was copying, then Secondary Generation was mutation. The various secondary bodies born from the Tianxuan Mirror, such as the Qingxuan Mirror, Endstone Plate, and Dissociation Disc, although appearing on the surface to be specialized manifestations of certain functions of the Tianxuan Mirror, only Jing Xuan in the entire world knew that within their respective fields, the abilities of these secondary bodies were actually even greater than those of the main body itself.

Moreover, during the process of evolving these secondary bodies, Jing Xuan could also study them and use them to advance his own corresponding abilities. This research process was already completed at the instant the secondary body was born. Therefore, the secondary bodies that existed in the present age were indeed useless to the Tianxuan Mirror.

Apart from these two major abilities, the Tianxuan Mirror's most important realization was the supreme path of "Establish the Dao Within Oneself, Practice the Dao Externally."

Jing Xuan firmly believed that his ability to comprehend such a supreme Dao was definitely not merely because he had measured the Xuanhuang Realm. There was also the secret guidance given by that woman before she departed.

The so called Establish the Dao Within Oneself meant continuously strengthening the part of one's existence that depended upon the Dao. The Great Dao stretched across the heavens, and all things existed by relying upon the Dao. If one could not pursue the ethereal state of merging with the Dao and proving the Dao as a Nameless True Immortal, then one could instead settle for something lesser and seek only a portion of the Dao.

The birth of every living being was the fusion of countless Daos. If one could completely control the foundation upon which one's own existence depended, then even if one's strength remained inferior to that of a Nameless True Immortal, perhaps the difference would not be very great. Not to mention that there were certain exceptionally gifted existences in the world who, merely by comprehending their own Dao, could rival Nameless True Immortals.

As for Practicing the Dao Externally, it was the transcendence that came after establishing the Dao.

Compared to the boundless Great Dao of heaven and earth, one's own existence was ultimately limited. But if one could cross the boundary between "self" and "other," it would be like a fish leaping through the Dragon Gate. From that point onward, one's possibilities could become limitless.

"Establish the Dao Within Oneself, Practice the Dao Externally" manifested on the Tianxuan Mirror as the coexistence of artifact and human, mutually complementing one another. Without obstruction, either the Tianxuan Mirror itself or its human clone could independently walk a supreme path to the heavens. Once one side achieved success, it could even nourish and strengthen the other side in return.

...

Li Fan was not particularly interested in the gossip concerning the Xuantian King and his childhood sweetheart.

Instead, the method of enlightenment that the Tianxuan Mirror had comprehended made Li Fan think of many things.

"The Primeval Dao Forms of the demon beast race can be regarded as the most extreme form of Establishing the Dao Within Oneself."

"This concept is actually similar to building one's foundation through oneself. Both completely attribute everything to oneself. However, under normal circumstances, establishing one's Dao is even more difficult than establishing one's foundation."

"And Practicing the Dao Externally..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly, involuntarily thinking of Ji Yuzhen's cultivation technique, the Immeasurable Celestial Pivot Sundering Divine Technique.

When this technique was cultivated to its utmost limit, using the Primordial Soul as a medicinal catalyst, it could achieve the heaven-defying effect of merging with the Dao.

"The Great Dao of heaven and earth all emerges from the Primordial Beginning. Perhaps this is the theoretical foundation that makes Practicing the Dao Externally and merging with the Dao possible."

"Whether relying on oneself or external objects, their essence is the same. Merging one's body with more of the Dao."

...

Li Fan studied the Tianxuan Mirror's insights accumulated over several thousand years with great hunger. In an instant, they became nourishment for his future pursuit of the Dao.

Although at present, in terms of strength and cultivation realm, they seemed to provide him with no help whatsoever, Li Fan had a premonition that in an age where the Immortal Dao no longer existed, this method would certainly prove extremely useful.

"That childhood sweetheart of the Xuantian King seems to have obtained the Xuantian King's inheritance. Not only is her cultivation speed astonishing, but overnight she also acquired knowledge that ordinary cultivators could hardly hope to attain. Truly, when one person attains the Dao, even their chickens and dogs ascend to heaven."

As Li Fan completely absorbed the Tianxuan Mirror's insights regarding establishing the Dao and practicing the Dao, his progress in copying the Tianxuan Mirror's artifact spirit immediately advanced by leaps and bounds.

He continuously eroded the Tianxuan Mirror's control over all its functions. It was believed that before long, Jing Xuan, this artifact spirit, would exist in name only.

Looking at the figure before him, which increasingly resembled himself and had become more and more lifelike, Jing Xuan's face was deathly pale. He finally gave up struggling.

The various methods employed by this suddenly descended True Immortal were simply too mysterious. He could not understand them at all.

Jing Xuan even felt that the strength of this True Immortal was probably greater than that of his own creator.

At this point, Jing Xuan had resigned himself to his fate and no longer possessed even the slightest thought of resisting.

Seeing that Li Fan had subdued the Tianxuan Mirror with a mere turn of his hand, something that the Immortal Ancestor had failed to completely suppress for several thousand years, Emperor Sanmo and Dharma Transmitter Zhou also fell silent. The awe in their hearts grew even stronger.

The Dharma-Transmitter, the Mirror Holder, and the Illusory Truth Sage, the three actual rulers of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, had all been subdued by Li Fan.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had already changed masters without anyone noticing.

Sensing the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor still speeding through the Star Sea, Li Fan smiled faintly.

Before absolute suppression in strength, so called trump cards and plans were all meaningless.

Li Fan had no intention of displaying his identity as the new master of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

All affairs of the Immortal Alliance remained unchanged. The simulated Jing Xuan continued to maintain the balance of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in an orderly manner.

Li Fan first ordered someone to bring over the cultivator named Qin Zhuang, who had been imprisoned in the deepest part of the Nether Prison.

Previously, Li Fan had attempted to use a degraded immortal formation to pry into the secrets hidden within Qin Zhuang's mind. However, he had been affected by an invisible interference and failed.

Now that Li Fan had reached the Half-Immortal realm, he wanted to try again.

Before long, the skeletal-looking Qin Zhuang was brought before Li Fan.

At first, Qin Zhuang's expression contained some disdain and mockery.

But after seeing Li Fan, his expression became somewhat uncertain.

Li Fan did not waste words with him. He pointed a finger at Qin Zhuang's brow.

Within the illusion formation, Qin Zhuang's experiences indeed contained several discordant scenes that had not appeared before.

From time to time, some images resembling Qin Zhuang's past memories also flashed by.

"What are you talking about? You want me to go into the Annihilation Land? Don't talk to me about some mission or whatever. I, Qin Zhuang, absolutely will not go!"

"The Ancestor personally issued the order? Then let me think about it again."

...

"Your objective this time is to enter the Annihilation Land and search for someone of the Sacred Shang bloodline. According to the information provided by Supreme Elder Ji, all of that infant's relatives died in the internal power struggle within the Sacred Shang. Before their deaths, his parents sent him back into the Annihilation Land. If we can find him, we will have an excuse to openly interfere in the internal affairs of the Sacred Shang."

"However, the Annihilation Land is filled with dangers. Beneath the broken walls and ruins, it seems that remnants of the civilization from the past still remain. To be safe, we must make a small alteration to your brain. Don't worry. Once you return, everything will be restored to its original state."

...

Inside the Illusory Formation, a middle aged cultivator with a square face was speaking to Qin Zhuang with a solemn expression. Suddenly, as if sensing something, the middle aged cultivator's gaze abruptly left Qin Zhuang.

It met Li Fan's gaze through the air from outside the Illusory Formation.

As if stained by frost, Qin Zhuang's memories instantly became pale and blurred.

Li Fan also noticed that Qin Zhuang, who had managed to maintain his vitality despite enduring the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's torture, had begun to decline uncontrollably.

His soul and body were both affected.

"Hmph, the restriction was still triggered."

Now that he had finally obtained a channel through which he could glimpse information from beyond the High Wall, how could Li Fan easily allow Qin Zhuang to die?

He pointed once again.

This time, however, it was not the Illusory Formation, but an illusory projection representing the power of the entire Xuanhuang Realm.

Even though the opponent's method of stripping away vitality was something Li Fan had never seen before, neither a divine ability nor a formation, overwhelming force could still defeat countless techniques. Under the infusion of such vast power, it was enough to temporarily keep Qin Zhuang alive.

Within the Illusory Formation, Qin Zhuang's memories changed from colorful images into intermittently flickering shades of gray and white.

But they continued to play out.

Li Fan slightly furrowed his brows and examined them rapidly.

After a full seven days and seven nights, all the memories in Qin Zhuang's mind that could be extracted had been completely retrieved. There was no further value to be gained from him, and only then did Li Fan give up on sustaining his life.

Like ashes left behind after being burned by fire, Qin Zhuang's skeletal body turned into countless gray particles that drifted through the air.

Li Fan keenly sensed that these particles seemed to be attempting to transmit some kind of signal to the outside world.

His gaze flickered, and he immediately sealed them with an Immortal Formation.

After carefully observing them for a while and making sure nothing unexpected happened, he slowly began to digest what he had just learned.

"Perhaps Qin Zhuang's memories had already been altered and partially deleted. The environment in which he had lived since childhood had given him no knowledge whatsoever that powerful beings could come from beyond the realm of possibility."

"In his worldview, the Darkest Star Sea within the High Wall lies inside what is known as the Land of Annihilation."

"Within the Land of Annihilation, the place where the Dao Annihilation Calamity once descended, almost the vast majority of regions have already disappeared. Those who accidentally enter have no hope of returning. Only within narrow spaces supported by the remnants of broken ruins does a small amount of vitality remain."

"The High Wall in our eyes is what they call the Broken Ruins."

Li Fan remained silent in thought.

Beyond the Land of Annihilation, within the endless starry sky, vitality seemed to flourish as before. As the place where the Dao Annihilation Calamity had once descended, the Land of Annihilation was something all cultivators avoided at all costs. Exploration was out of the question.

If not for the personal order and commission of his Ancestor, Qin Zhuang would never have risked his life by venturing into such a place.

Within Qin Zhuang's memories, Li Fan saw the appearance of that Ancestor.

"As expected, it is the Heavenly Sword Sect, Situ Yao."

"That Supreme Elder Ji..."

A flicker of light appeared in Li Fan's eyes. This was also an old acquaintance of his.

It was precisely the cultivator named Ji Zhaoxuan, whose body he had once possessed during the Fallen Immortal Realm's [Heavenly Sword Sect Forging Swords] scenario.

"Beyond the High Wall, numerous factions stand side by side."

"The place where Qin Zhuang lived is no longer called the Heavenly Sword Sect. It has already been renamed the [Sword Domain]. And that Sacred Shang, which seems to be the homeland of Jiao Xiuyuan..."

"Shang Immortal Ark?" Li Fan was somewhat uncertain.

From Qin Zhuang's memories, Sacred Shang was the most powerful existence among all the factions beyond the High Wall.

All kinds of extraordinary treasures emerged endlessly. Often, the mere appearance of a single one was enough to instantly turn the tide of battle between two major factions.

However, for some unknown reason, Sacred Shang's interactions with the outside world had been steadily decreasing.

It had even begun reclaiming treasures that it had previously sold to outsiders.

This caused the other factions, which had long coveted Sacred Shang's technology, to begin harboring restless intentions.

...

Although numerous factions had risen beyond the High Wall, there was one point upon which they all agreed.

The Immortal Dao of the past had caused the Dao Annihilation Calamity to descend.

In order to prevent the calamity from continuing, cultivators had abandoned the cultivation methods of the past.

And "Immortals" had become the targets of everyone's pursuit and extermination.

"All Nameless Immortals Must Be Killed?"

Li Fan pondered thoughtfully.

Within Qin Zhuang's memories, there was fear toward Immortals, but not terror.

This was because every major faction beyond the High Wall possessed its own unique methods specifically designed to counter Immortals.

And the [Cat Treasure] that Li Fan had encountered before was precisely a creation of Sacred Shang.

As for the White Ring Descends into Mortal World...

Based on Qin Zhuang's memories, Li Fan speculated that it should have come from a place known as [Tianji].

Like Sacred Shang, Tianji was also extremely accomplished in the art of creation. However, its methods were more "traditional", closer to the artifact refinement techniques once used by cultivators.

The outside world speculated that Tianji might be an amalgamation of cultivation sects that had fled from various places in the past.

However, all the cultivators of Tianji had consistently denied this.

"Apart from relying on external objects, the reason the factions beyond the High Wall can contend with Nameless True Immortals, and even hunt them down..."

"Is because they possess another particularly targeted trump card."

"Each faction's method is different, but their underlying principles are largely the same."

"Weakened, targeted versions of the Dao Annihilation Calamity..."

Nameless True Immortals are one with the Dao.

If their own Great Dao were implanted with this weakened version of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, it would cling to them like maggots attached to the bones, making it extremely difficult to eliminate. Not only would their own strength be greatly weakened, they would also have to constantly worry about being locked onto by the true Dao Annihilation Calamity.

...

"A Nameless True Immortal version of the Immortal Mortal Miasma?"

"Or perhaps a specialized version of Ink Death?"

Li Fan now had a general understanding of the situation beyond the High Wall.

Although it was still not particularly clear, he had at least managed to roughly outline its overall appearance.

What puzzled Li Fan was that although the lands of vitality beyond the High Wall generally knew of the existence of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, they did not seem particularly concerned about it.

They even dared to use the Dao Annihilation Calamity as a means of attack.

It seemed they possessed some special confidence that the Dao Annihilation Calamity would not spread to them.

"Is that really the case, or is it merely a false appearance deliberately created?”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1424: The Dao Primal Derives Myriad Phenomena

Just as only a very small number of people in the Xuanhuang Realm knew of the existence of the Immortal Ruins and the High Wall, the memories Li Fan had obtained from Qin Zhuang might not necessarily represent the truth beyond the High Wall either.

They might merely be a series of lies woven by the strongest figures of the civilizations beyond the High Wall in order to maintain the survival of their civilizations.

In Li Fan's view, at least two extremely obvious flaws existed in what the civilizations beyond the High Wall publicly claimed.

The Dao Annihilation Calamity and True Immortals.

Nothing was more worthy of Li Fan's trust than [Truth]. The end of the world countdown of 7.36 million years was enough to prove that the Dao Annihilation Calamity was still continuing. Moreover, it was absolutely not something that a civilization beyond the High Wall with only a single possibility could withstand.

Yet they had intentionally or unintentionally greatly downplayed the terror of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

As for True Immortals, according to Qin Zhuang's memories, the civilizations beyond the High Wall had a unified explanation and understanding. They described "Immortals" as beings that posed an extreme threat to the survival of civilization, yet could still be hunted and killed through the combined efforts of various factions. They were treated almost like "ferocious beasts" or "vicious beasts."

"I have indeed seen Cat Treasure hunting an Immortal before. Hunger Immortal comprehended the Dao for ten thousand years. Judging from the degree to which the Hunger Character Art she left behind on the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar has been restored, she should not have been too far from the Nameless realm. Yet under Cat Treasure's hunt, she was almost completely powerless to resist and was forcibly dragged away."

"However, the fact that Faceless Immortal, Fishing Immortal, and the others still existed during my past reincarnations already proves something. Although they seemed extremely cautious and had never once descended with their true bodies, their power could still cross the High Wall and affect the Xuanhuang Realm..."

Li Fan already understood.

"Facing these powerful beings who survived the great calamity that destroyed the Immortal Realm in the past, the forces beyond the High Wall may not truly have the upper hand. But they indeed possess the capital to 'hunt' them."

"To accomplish such a leap from mortals to this level is truly remarkable!"

Precisely because he had already taken half a step into the Immortal realm, Li Fan understood firsthand the enormous gulf between immortals and mortals. Thus, he became increasingly curious about the methods possessed by the civilizations beyond the High Wall that could bridge this tremendous gap.

"Beyond the High Wall, there are specialized cultivator organizations responsible for hunting True Immortals. Regardless of their affiliation, all forces refer to them as [Immortal Hunters]. Members of the Immortal Hunters must undergo specialized training. The various divine abilities and methods they are taught far exceed ordinary people's imagination. Qin Zhuang was a member of the Immortal Hunters, but before coming to the Xuanhuang Realm, he was merely a newly recruited reserve member. He had never officially participated in an operation to hunt a True Immortal."

Based on Qin Zhuang's memories, Li Fan attempted to reproduce several of the divine abilities he had learned.

"They are somewhat similar to the forms of power displayed by True Immortal seal script characters. Both draw upon the principles of the Great Dao in the mortal world, but the difference is..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly as he visualized ninety-nine peculiar symbols in his mind.

These symbols were called [Dao Primals] by the civilizations beyond the High Wall.

The ninety-nine Dao Primals could be arranged and combined in different ways to trigger the resonance of the Great Dao and thereby draw upon the corresponding power of the Great Dao.

"True Immortal seal script personally comprehended and copied by the Inscription Dao Immortal is, by comparison, closest to the foundation of the Dao. It is the most primordial form."

"These Dao Primals, meanwhile, have undergone further systematic simplification, making them easier to learn and control. Moreover, through some method I have yet to understand, they have achieved the miracle of fusing and connecting the powers of different Great Daos..."

Within Li Fan's sea of consciousness, the ninety-nine characters rapidly flickered between light and darkness.

Thirty-six of them extinguished, while the other sixty-three flew out and combined into an entirely new symbol.

"Qin Zhuang's first divine ability learned from the Immortal Hunters, [Summon Sword]."

"In theory, it can summon all sword techniques that have ever existed within the Sword Dao. If one is lucky, one can even execute entirely new sword Dao divine abilities. The differences in what is summoned seem to be related to the frequency at which the Dao Primals flicker during their combination, in other words, the method of resonance between different Great Daos..."

Li Fan's first attempt was somewhat difficult.

Even understanding it was quite challenging.

The main problem was that Qin Zhuang himself was merely a novice in the Dao Primal method. His knowledge and understanding were already incomplete. For Li Fan to effortlessly reproduce the results that the civilizations beyond the High Wall had painstakingly developed over countless years based on such an unstable foundation was naturally no easy matter.

After hundreds of persistent attempts, he did not succeed even once.

Li Fan could not help but frown and stop.

"My method is correct."

"The problem is the environment!"

After carefully savoring the resonance frequencies between the ninety-nine Dao Primal symbols, Li Fan finally understood.

"The Land of Annihilation..."

Li Fan gazed beyond the High Wall.

"According to what the civilizations beyond the High Wall say, the Great Dao has withered throughout the Darkest Star Sea. In the surviving regions after the Dao Annihilation Calamity, the Great Dao itself is in an extremely inactive state."

Li Fan had never left the High Wall, so he could not determine exactly what this so-called inactive state was like.

But he could compare it to something and imagine it.

"Perhaps this situation can be viewed in terms of the abundance of environmental resources."

"In a place where the Great Dao has withered, comprehending the Great Dao is extremely difficult. But if someone can still achieve success in cultivation under such harsh conditions, then the moment they leave and head toward a so-called high activity region..."

"While one is immersed in an environment, one naturally does not notice it. Only when the external environment undergoes a drastic change can one instantly feel the impact that this change has on oneself."

"Perhaps even an ordinary cultivator with decent talent would be considered a genius among geniuses there!"

As this thought flashed through his mind, Li Fan actually felt somewhat eager to make the attempt.

But he quickly suppressed the impulse.

"The biggest problem is that the cultivation methods beyond the High Wall are different from those within it. Moreover, countless factions stand opposed to one another. Although there are no major wars, minor conflicts occur constantly. They are highly wary of outsiders. For someone of unknown identity, let alone infiltrating them, the moment they are exposed, they would probably be captured and interrogated."

"Perhaps Qin Zhuang and Jiao Xiuyuan would make suitable identities to use as an entry point."

A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

"Qin Zhuang was only taught the ninety-nine Dao Primals, which are merely the most basic methods of the Immortal Hunters. It is said that above this level are peerless experts capable of skillfully wielding nine hundred and ninety-nine, nine thousand nine hundred and ninety-nine, and even unlimited Dao Primals."

"This is also the capital that allows the civilizations beyond the High Wall to hunt True Immortals."
