Within Li Fan's sea of consciousness, two images appeared side by side.

On the left were the True Immortal seal script characters.

On the right were the ninety-nine Dao Primal symbols.

Li Fan attempted to correspond the True Immortal seal script characters that depicted the known paths of the Dao with the ninety-nine Dao Primal symbols.

But it was obvious that these Dao Primal symbols had undergone a special form of encryption and no longer possessed the characteristics of the original paths of the Dao.

Without systematic study, even with Li Fan's astonishing talent, he could not decipher them.

He shook his head slightly and temporarily suppressed this image in his mind.

"True Immortals and Immortal Hunters. Although their realms and manifestations of power are vastly different, at their core, they are still a matter of the degree of one's comprehension of the Great Dao."

"It is merely that beyond the High Wall, through these so-called Dao Primal symbols, the Great Dao is subjected to another layer of translation. While allowing mortals to grasp and utilize the power of the Great Dao, it also prevents anyone from merging their body with the Dao and stealing the Great Dao itself."

"The more I contemplate it, the more amazed I become."

"Whoever created these Dao Primal symbols probably understood the Great Dao no less deeply than the Inscription Dao Immortal of that era!"

Now that Li Fan was no longer completely ignorant of the world beyond the High Wall, and there was even a possibility of advancing further without stepping into the Immortal realm, he had already begun reconstructing his exploration plan in his mind.

"However, I must make every possible preparation."

"Aside from guarding against the various forces beyond the High Wall, I also have to be wary of other existences that lie beyond the known possibilities."

The possibility of making a leap was clearly beyond the understanding of ordinary people. Naturally, there was not the slightest record of it in Qin Zhuang's memories. Li Fan even suspected that, throughout the entire civilization beyond the High Wall, only a handful of people knew about this matter.

But one had to take things one step at a time.

Compared to darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai, Li Fan's current strength was indeed still far too weak.

"I still cannot find any immortal spiritual qi that I can use. The path ahead is cut off. Moreover, judging from the current situation, becoming a Half-Immortal only raises one's level of existence. If one truly proves the Dao and becomes a True Immortal, it would probably be extremely difficult to move forward beyond the High Wall. It may not necessarily be a good thing."

"First, I will use the Dao Primal methods from beyond the High Wall to improve my own strength." Li Fan made his decision.

"Besides, as long as I can ensure my safety, I still need to personally go beyond the High Wall at least once. I want to see exactly what these so-called regions where the Great Dao is active are like."

Learning the method of translating Dao Primal into the Great Dao did not mean that Li Fan had to start everything from scratch.

On the contrary, compared to most people in the civilization beyond the High Wall, Li Fan possessed a tremendous advantage that they could never hope to match. He knew a vast number of True Immortal seal script characters. He even possessed the Nameless True Immortal's leg bone, which could manifest an original seal script state that approached the Great Dao itself.

Dao Primal was merely a translated form of symbols. No matter how they were combined, transformed, or resonated with one another, their ultimate goal was to come into contact with and utilize the power of the Great Dao.

If one could not know the Great Dao itself and could only study and ponder Dao Primal, it was equivalent to walking blindfolded and being able to do nothing more than reach out and grope around.

But if one had already studied the Great Dao that served as the target of the translation in considerable depth, then with the distant destination serving as a reference, learning it would surely be twice as effective with half the effort, advancing a thousand miles in a single day.

"Even, if I know enough Dao Primal symbols, I should be able to decipher them directly." Li Fan faintly sensed that this was possible.

"Of course, the expert who created the Dao Primal symbols back then could not possibly have failed to anticipate this. There should be some restrictions within them that I do not yet know about."

...

Before formally exploring beyond the High Wall, Li Fan still had several other important matters to deal with.

The Five Elders Association, Carefree Paradise.

The Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

Dharma-Transmitter Jiang.

The secrets of the Xuanhuang Realm, including the Ancient Immortal Formation that Protects the Realm.

The Azure Firmament Pearl, which still existed within the ravine fissure of the Darkest Star Sea.

...

According to the degree of difficulty, Li Fan first went to meet Dharma-Transmitter Jiang.

Back then, this person's extraordinary characteristic, "All Things Are Like Me," had nearly caused Li Fan to suffer a serious setback.

Now, Li Fan was extremely curious whether this strange power could still affect him after he had attained the True Immortal realm.

Dharma-Transmitter Jiang had been cultivating in seclusion within the Returning Ruins World for many years and never left his true body.

Li Fan flashed into this small world and looked around with great interest.

Yet Dharma-Transmitter Jiang, who was cultivating with his eyes closed inside, did not notice him in the slightest.

"The Returning Ruins World can continuously undergo [Return to Origin]. In other words, no matter what happens, everything can be reset and returned to its most initial state..."

"In some respects, this is somewhat similar to my own ability. Of course, the level of existence is far inferior. It can only maintain this single small world. It cannot even reset time, only the state of matter."

Even so, Li Fan was rather curious about the principle behind [Return to Origin].

Countless dark golden lines, like his eyes and his tentacles, surged outward in all directions like water pouring from a broken dam, analyzing this small world for Li Fan.

"Hm... I see. It is not a limitless Return to Origin. The original essence of the small world has been engraved extremely deeply, like marks carved into stone with a file."

"All kinds of changes that occur within the small world can be erased directly by removing one layer. The engraving itself remains, and when it manifests again, it appears as though everything has returned to its starting point."

After a while, Li Fan understood.

He could not help but feel somewhat disappointed.

But that was only natural. After all, Dharma-Transmitter Jiang was merely at the Star Sea Supreme realm. Without the power of [Truth], it was fundamentally impossible to achieve a genuine [Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood].

Being able to devise such a method was already extraordinarily ingenious.

"Although it is somewhat shallow and crude, it can still provide me with some inspiration."

As Li Fan's eyes flickered, he took a single step and arrived before Dharma-Transmitter Jiang, who was quietly sitting cross-legged in the air.

He bent his head and lowered his gaze.

He carefully examined the other party.

"All Things Are Like Me." Li Fan savored the strange sensation rising within his heart.

There was no doubt that Dharma-Transmitter Jiang deserved to be the only existence whom the Immortal Ancestor treated with special regard.

Even though Li Fan had already reached the Half Immortal realm, he still could not prevent this feeling from arising within his heart.

The longer Li Fan stared at Dharma-Transmitter Jiang, the stronger the sense of familiarity and closeness became.

However, compared to when he was still an ordinary mortal, Li Fan could now clearly distinguish the difference between Dharma-Transmitter Jiang and himself.

"Alike."

"But ultimately, not the same."

"Without Return to [Truth], the false cannot become true."

Li Fan extended his right hand, curled his finger, and lightly flicked Dharma-Transmitter Jiang on the forehead.

Dharma-Transmitter Jiang, who had been cultivating in seclusion, suddenly opened his eyes.

After seeing a completely unfamiliar face, his expression was filled with shock.

He asked coldly, "Who are you?!"

Li Fan smiled.

"Me? I am you!"

A fantastical, hazy light emanated from Li Fan's eyes. In the meantime, he had quietly deployed an Immortal Formation, using the Final Dissociation Disc to deduce the formation and imitate this strange characteristic.

Then, he reversed it and applied it to Dharma-Transmitter Jiang.

After all, he was the original owner of this characteristic. At first, Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's will did not become confused so easily.

He stared at Li Fan with great vigilance.

But gradually, the appearance and image of Li Fan in his eyes began to change without him realizing it.

It became more and more like himself.

In the end, they truly appeared to be the same person.

It was as though he were looking into a mirror. Seeing himself standing in front of him, all vigilance disappeared from Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's heart.

"I have a feeling that my current strength is far from my limit."

"If the highest realm of All Things Are Like Me is reached, would that not mean that all the various Great Daos ultimately return to my body?"

Hearing his own voice speak these words from across the distance, Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's mind shook violently.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1425: The Heaven-Communing Grand Formation

It was as if a bolt of lightning had forcibly split open the long-sealed thoughts of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang. Naturally, he followed Li Fan's guidance and continued thinking along that line.

"Exactly! All things are like me, heaven and earth are like me, and even the Great Dao is like me..."

At this moment, Dharma-Transmitter Jiang was like a mayfly that had lived in a dazed state ever since its birth, suddenly seeing the azure sky for the first time. Yet rather than feeling inferiority, shock, or fear, this tiny mayfly inexplicably gave birth to an ambition to swallow the heavens.

"The Great Dao is like me, the Great Dao is like me..." Dharma-Transmitter Jiang muttered as if possessed. As his eyes gradually began to glow, different lights and shadows also started flickering throughout this Returning Ruins World.

For the convenience of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's comprehension, Li Fan had directly used his means to forcibly relocate a completed small world from within the Xuanhuang Realm to this place.

Under the influence of the Immortal Formation, Dharma-Transmitter Jiang lost himself and became completely immersed in comprehending the Dao. Indeed, with his supreme characteristic of All Things Are Like Me, he could continuously perceive and assimilate everything in the world, even the countless Great Daos of heaven and earth.

But Dharma-Transmitter Jiang was far too weak at present. The gap between him and the Great Dao of heaven and earth was even greater than that between a mayfly and the azure sky. For him to assimilate the Great Dao into himself was nothing more than a delusion. Even if he possessed endless longevity, it would still be impossible. Before he could accomplish this goal, the Dao Annihilation Calamity that would engulf the mortal world would already have arrived.

If Dharma-Transmitter Jiang had remained clear-minded, he would never have made such an unwise move. But once he entered Li Fan's Immortal Formation, he was no longer in control of himself.

Li Fan did not care whether Dharma-Transmitter Jiang could successfully prove the Dao.

What mattered to him was that Dharma-Transmitter Jiang was currently comprehending the Dao through his own body.

By utilizing the Immortal Formation's miraculous characteristic, which was similar to All Things Are Like Me, Li Fan could now faintly synchronize with Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's thoughts, feelings, and perceptions.

Under normal circumstances, such close resonance between two different living beings would certainly cause both sides to notice and even become wary of each other. But Li Fan had used the Immortal Formation to block Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's perception of him. Thus, he could achieve one-way sensing of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang.

Although there was still a certain gap between this and Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's true thoughts, to Li Fan, it was equivalent to having a clone that was constantly in a state of enlightenment, comprehending the Great Dao of the world on his behalf.

Moreover, Li Fan believed that as the Final Dissociation Disc continued to deduce and iterate upon the Illusory Formation, it should be able to further narrow the difference between their comprehensions in the future.

Li Fan's figure within the Returning Ruins World gradually became faint. Before leaving, he extended a hand forward.

The enormous Returning Ruins World grew smaller and smaller compared to Li Fan's body. In the end, it landed in his palm like a dazzling pearl.

Although the increase was extremely small, during the period Dharma-Transmitter Jiang spent comprehending the Dao, Li Fan could clearly sense that his own comprehension of the Dao was slowly but steadily increasing.

Li Fan was extremely satisfied with Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's wondrous innate characteristic. Thus, he intended to carry this Dao-comprehending machine with him for a long time.

"It can practically be regarded as a controllable, weakened version of the Immortal Celestial Pillar."

"Moreover, although its speed of comprehending the Great Dao is far inferior to that of the Nameless True Immortal, its accuracy may not be much weaker. It might even be superior!"

Li Fan compared the two and suddenly realized something.

"That is because Dharma-Transmitter Jiang is not relying on his own talent and understanding to comprehend the Dao. Rather, he relies on his innate characteristic, which, in a certain sense, can be regarded as the Great Dao itself, to carry out the process of comprehension."

"Using the Dao to comprehend the Dao. Truly marvelous!"

"The only regret is that his strength is indeed a little too weak. But if he were too strong, he would probably notice that something was wrong and break free from my methods. There is no such thing as perfection in this world. I can only wait until my strength advances further before finding a way to help him increase his strength."

After sealing the Returning Ruins World Pearl containing Dharma-Transmitter Jiang deep within his body, Li Fan once again carefully savored the Bewildering Illusion Formation deduced by the Final Dissociation Disc based on Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's characteristic.

"This characteristic absolutely originates from an extraordinary Immortal Artifact. Once this kind of characteristic is mastered, analyzing the Great Dao and deriving it from nothing will no longer be an unattainable matter."

"Unfortunately, too much time has passed, and it has already completely fused with Dharma-Transmitter Jiang, making it impossible to separate. But by imitating it with a formation, I can still reproduce eighty to ninety percent of its effects."

"As for this formation, it shall be named..."

After pondering for a while, Li Fan slowly said, "Heaven-Communing, the Great Dao Is Like Me!"

Li Fan keenly sensed that this Heaven-Communing Grand Formation was definitely not suitable only for Dharma-Transmitter Jiang. Rather, all things in the world could serve as tools to assist him in comprehending the Dao.

"However, they still need to be carefully selected."

"The Dao comprehended by Dharma-Transmitter Jiang is the closest to the origin of the Great Dao. But other people, no matter how extraordinarily talented they appear on the surface, are constrained by their own cultivation and strength. What they comprehend will inevitably differ from the origin of the Dao. These discrepancies will instead become blemishes that contaminate my understanding."

While he was pondering, the Final Dissociation Disc had already completed its first iteration of the Heaven-Communing Grand Formation, expanding its coverage to an entire world.

Although the Final Dissociation Disc possessed an inexhaustible supply of immortal spiritual qi, there was still an upper limit to how much could be drawn upon each time. So far, Li Fan had yet to find a way to increase this upper limit.

Moreover, arranging this Heaven-Communing Grand Formation would require roughly thirty days of preparation.

Li Fan personally took charge of arranging the formation.

At the same time, under his will, an announcement soon spread throughout the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

"The Heavenly Sovereign is compassionate, saving all living beings and teaching without discrimination.

"Anyone who believes they possess talent may go to the prefectural cities of the Immortal Alliance to undergo testing. Those who meet the requirements, regardless of their birth, regardless of their past, regardless of their age, and regardless of their gender, may enter the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and receive one opportunity to open the Heavenly Sovereign's Secret Treasury. They will also have the opportunity to personally listen to the Heavenly Sovereign expound the Dao."

Once the news was released, the entire world was shaken.

Everything described in this announcement by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was simply too good. It was so good that it hardly seemed real, as if some hidden scheme must be involved.

However, looking back at the past, although the Immortal Alliance occasionally committed absurd acts, it had never dared to act recklessly in the name of the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor. Therefore, the people of the Immortal Alliance initially remained skeptical.

But there were always people in this world who could not resist temptation, people who were willing to risk their lives for profit. And when these people truly entered the Heavenly Sovereign's Secret Treasury, returned with abundant gains, and appeared before the world once again, the facts spoke louder than words.

Greed and temptation completely overwhelmed reason.

Every day, large numbers of cultivators confident in their own talents flocked to the prefectural cities of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance to participate in the tests. The Immortal Alliance headquarters even made the number of talented individuals recommended each year one of the most important assessment criteria for every prefecture. The Heavenly City Lords of the various prefectures consequently exhausted every possible effort and employed every means at their disposal.

They searched their entire prefectures as if scouring every mountain and sea for hidden treasures, seeking out all kinds of talented individuals within their territories. Even mortals were not overlooked.

There were even quite a few Heavenly City Lords who fought fiercely over a promising talent.

When the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance revolved around a single consciousness, the efficiency with which things were handled was countless times greater than when Li Fan acted alone.

Batch after batch of heaven's favored geniuses were continuously sent into the [Gou Realm] created by Li Fan.

Unless something special happened, their true bodies would remain here forever from this point onward.

Meanwhile, their clones, assembled from simulated personalities and flesh, would reappear in the public eye, serving to inspire others and create confusion.

Li Fan floated in midair, looking at the [Gou Realm], which had gradually begun to take shape and momentum, and could not help but nod slightly.

True Immortal seal script characters floated throughout the heavens and earth. Corresponding to the True Immortal seal script characters were ninety-nine Dao Primal symbols.

Before the many heaven's favored geniuses, a phantom of the Immortal Ancestor sat cross-legged, imparting the Dao, teaching them, and answering their questions.

This phantom was naturally not the Immortal Ancestor himself, but something Li Fan had created.

To reap a harvest, one must first sow the seeds.

And as could be predicted, the harvest these heaven's favored geniuses within the [Gou Realm] would bring to Li Fan would far exceed what he invested. It could truly be called an investment with enormous returns.

"At present, the comprehension bonus brought to me by the living beings of the [Gou Realm] is not even as great as that of the sole Dharma-Transmitter Jiang. However, once their understanding continues to improve, this gap should quickly narrow."

Highly satisfied with the efficiency of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance under his command, Li Fan naturally had to reward them.

With Li Fan's current foundation, casually taking out a small portion of it would be enough for them to benefit endlessly. However, this time, Li Fan chose an improved version of the Taijia Divine Art that could communicate with the Star Sea.

He did not directly impart the Taijia Divine Art.

Instead, he created a formation and bestowed it upon the people of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, giving them the opportunity to enter the formation and connect their minds with the true intent of the Star Sea Origin.

Cultivators in the Xuanhuang Realm had never escaped the attraction of the Immortal Ruins, nor had they ever personally witnessed the true might of the Star Sea. Yet this Taijia Divine Art allowed them to remain within Xuanhuang while still comprehending the Dao of the Star Sea.

In this life, Li Fan had not promoted the ascension of the Xuanhuang Realm. On the surface, Xuanhuang was still merely an ordinary cultivation world within the Star Sea. The intricacies of the Star Sea laws were far beyond the laws of heaven and earth within the Xuanhuang Realm.

Even comprehending the Dao of the Star Sea for only a few breaths would bring immense benefits to the cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm.

And just as Li Fan had expected, everyone who experienced it was filled with awe and became deeply immersed in it.

After the brief experience ended, they all yearned for another opportunity.

Thus, they became even more determined to establish merit and obtain Li Fan's rewards.

Li Fan merely handed control of the formation to Emperor Sanmo and chose to become a hands-off manager.

He first went beyond the void and suppressed and sealed the returning Immortal Ancestor and Heavenly Doctor.

Li Fan then unsheathed the phantom of the wooden sword and entered the underground river within the depression beneath the High Wall.

Following his previous memories, Li Fan once again arrived before the quietly floating Azure Firmament Pearl.

After continuously releasing vitality for ten thousand years, the Azure Firmament Pearl was now on the verge of destruction.

It was merely maintaining a phantom image.

Although it no longer possessed the might of an Immortal Artifact, it still held tremendous significance for Li Fan.

He did not approach it, nor did he even use his divine sense to get close. Instead, he carefully observed it from afar.

"An artifact created by the [Divine Shaman Realm]..." Li Fan pondered inwardly.

"Originally, I intended to reverse the flow of vitality into the Azure Firmament Pearl, forcibly pulling this treasure back from the brink of destruction. Then, I would use the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood to gradually 'repair' it."

"Now that I look at it, that is still somewhat too difficult."

Li Fan had a premonition that as long as he made even the slightest disturbance, the extremely fragile Azure Firmament Pearl would instantly be reduced to ashes.

"The only method is to directly use the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood and superimpose it. First, I need to reverse its state of impending destruction."

However, to perform the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, one needed sufficient observational samples at the very least. One did not need to possess complete knowledge of the essence of that existence, but at the very least, there had to be a "target" upon which to perform the technique.

Just as Li Fan had previously recreated Ancient Cang Primal Essence.

The problem now was that the Azure Firmament Pearl was simply too fragile. Li Fan could not even observe the process at close range. He could only watch from afar.

After remaining at an impasse for a long time, a flash of inspiration suddenly passed through Li Fan's mind.

"I cannot interfere through external influences. I can only place my hopes on the changes within the Azure Firmament Pearl itself."

"In the past, the cultivation techniques of the twelve Dharma Kings of the Xuantian Sect all originated from this Azure Firmament Pearl."

"And within the Xuantian Sect, there was also a secret technique capable of stripping away and seizing all the cultivation brought about by the techniques cultivated by the sect members. It was called [Seize Heaven and Replace It]."

"I remember that in Zhao Ruoxi's dream, she once said that the Grand Dao Sect obtained this secret technique after exterminating the Xuantian Sect."

"......"

Li Fan narrowed his eyes.

"It seems that if I want to obtain the Azure Firmament Pearl, I may first have to make a trip to the Five Elders Association."

"That is fine. There is also another Eternal Remnant Will there. I can take it at the same time."

Having made his decision, Li Fan flew out of the depression in the Star Sea. He did not head to the Five Elders Association immediately.

Instead, he first went to Ten Thousand Immortal Island.

Beneath Ten Thousand Immortal Island were buried the remains of countless Dharma-Transmitters who had died in battle long ago, suppressing what was known as the Nether Passage.

The Nether Passage, which continuously emitted strange power outward, was merely an abnormal organ produced by the Xuanhuang Realm during the process of devouring and merging with other worlds, one that it had difficulty digesting. The power at the level of a cultivation world was now no longer worthy of Li Fan's attention.

Instead, Li Fan was much more interested in the countless remains of the Dharma-Transmitters.

"There is no aura of an Eternal Remnant Will here."

"But there seems to be an Eternal Remnant Will preserved within the Soul-Grinding Plate of the Five Elders Association."

"The Five Elders Association once even launched a surprise attack on Ten Thousand Immortal Island in order to seize the remains of the Dharma-Transmitters buried beneath it..."

Past memories slowly flowed through Li Fan's mind. With a single thought, Ten Thousand Immortal Island began to shake violently.

Then, under the horrified gazes of countless cultivators, the island slowly rose into the air.

"It seems the key still lies with that Soul-Grinding Plate."

After collecting the Dharma-Transmitters remains that emitted a sinister aura, Li Fan did not sense any other disturbance.

He looked toward where the Five Elders Association was located and sped away.

Without Li Fan's power supporting it, Ten Thousand Immortal Island once again fell toward the surface of the sea.

However, with its suppression gone, all kinds of strange powers had already begun emerging from the Nether Passage below.

As the cultivators of Ten Thousand Immortal Island fled in panic, they received Li Fan's orders, and light continuously flashed within the teleportation formation.

Teams of black-robed cultivators emerged from within, sealing off the area and handling the aftermath.

Li Fan did not use the Wooden Sword movement technique. Instead, he deliberately slowed his speed and made a circuit around the territories of all five Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association.

In the past, once he stepped into these places, he would become involuntarily affected.

But now, the principles of heaven and earth that they had defied could no longer affect Li Fan in the slightest.

As the saying went, once one had proven oneself a True Immortal, one had already transcended beyond heaven and earth.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1426: The Ink Death Dao Annihilation Aspect

When Li Fan stepped into the Land of Carefree, he was not immediately drawn into an illusory dream like ordinary people.

Instead, he saw the real Land of Carefree.

There were still some traces of human activity around the outskirts of the real paradise. But the deeper he went toward the central region, the fewer traces of human habitation there were.

After flying for some time, he arrived at the center of the Land of Carefree. What entered his sight was a completely pristine ancient natural landscape devoid of any human presence. Sunlight shone brightly, and the climate was humid. Within the endless rainforest, great rivers wound their way through the land.

The vitality here could be said to be the richest in the entire Xuanhuang Realm. There were many species that seemed to have evolved later, species that had never appeared in any other region of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Li Fan also keenly sensed that there seemed to be a force subtly maintaining the ecological balance of this natural paradise.

"Carefree..."

Li Fan's gaze flashed like lightning. He separated a strand of divine sense and sent it directly toward this force within the rainforest.

The instant that force was pierced through, faint, intermittent cries of an infant suddenly seemed to ring beside Li Fan's ears.

The surrounding rainforest scenery vanished in an instant.

In a daze, it seemed as though he had arrived at a small mountain village. The ceaseless cries were coming from one of the houses.

Li Fan looked around with great interest. He had actually seen the scenery of this mountain village before. It had come from a painting called The Record of the Demon Infant Who Devoured Father and Mother and Forgot Worries.

Following the source of the cries, Li Fan pushed open the door and entered.

A thick smell of blood assaulted his nose. The scene inside the house was also quite horrifying.

An infant lay wrapped in swaddling clothes upon the bed.

Beside the bed, a man and a woman had already been reduced to bloody, mangled flesh, no longer resembling human beings.

Yet there was not the slightest trace of pain on their faces. Instead, they continued to smile foolishly and blissfully.

As though they could not feel any pain at all, they reached into their own bodies, which already exposed ghastly white bones, and tore off pieces of flesh.

Then they fed them to the infant on the bed.

"Little one, eat more. Eat until you're full. Hehehe."

They muttered as if possessed while coughing up large amounts of blood.

The blood seemed to be something extremely precious to them. Their faces immediately filled with heartache, and they hurriedly gathered the blood that had spilled onto the ground bit by bit. They mixed it with pieces of broken flesh and tissue before stuffing it into the infant's mouth.

The infant was completely powerless to resist and could only cry weakly.

...

Even Li Fan could not help but frown slightly at this sinister scene.

There was no doubt that this was part of Carefree' past.

However, Li Fan, who had already become a master of formations, could see at a glance that this was very different from an ordinary illusion formation.

"At the very least, these two people, who should be Carefree' parents, truly exist?"

Li Fan's divine sense incarnation slowly walked over to them.

Under Li Fan's interference, their movements were forcibly halted.

It was as though they had awakened from a dream and returned to reality. The possessed and delighted expressions disappeared from the faces of the man and woman. Although their bodies could not move, after regaining consciousness, they seemed to realize their own situation. Their eyes filled with endless despair and madness.

"Ahhh..."

They wanted to say something, but they could not speak at all. They could only make meaningless sounds.

Apparently displeased by Li Fan's interference, that force once again forcibly seized control of the couple.

They began once more to dig out their flesh and strip away their bones, feeding the infant in an act resembling a nightmare.

"That is what they themselves insist on seeking. Why must Senior interfere?"

"Is it not good to help others achieve what they desire?"

A smiling voice suddenly appeared, echoing throughout the small house.

"Playing tricks and pretending to be mysterious."

Li Fan gave a cold snort and was just about to act.

But a figure had already appeared in an instant, gracefully bowing before him.

At the same time, she softly cried out, "Senior, spare my life!"

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and examined her.

She wore not a single piece of clothing. Her body was slender and frail, with hardly any curves. Her long hair seemed never to have been trimmed since the moment she was born. It covered the floor like a winding black river.

The naked Carefree showed not the slightest embarrassment under Li Fan's gaze.

It was as though this was simply the form she had been born with.

"Senior, do you know what happened afterward?" Carefree pointed toward the man and woman in the house, who were feeding the infant with their own flesh and blood.

Li Fan did not answer.

Carefree answered her own question instead.

"These two people somehow heard from an immortal master about a secret method capable of defying fate and changing one's destiny. By nourishing a newly born infant with the parents' essence blood, then swallowing the infant after eighty-one days, one could instantly become an immortal."

"Hehehe..."

The scene before them suddenly changed.

As the infant nourished by flesh and blood slowly grew at an incomprehensible rate, her cries also became increasingly loud.

Gradually, the cries spread from the small house to every corner of the mountain village.

The voice seemed to possess a kind of magic. Everyone who heard it lost their minds. Like walking corpses, they slowly approached and then "fed themselves to the demon."

In only a few days, everyone in the entire village had turned into the indistinct mass of flesh inside the room.

The infant's cries gradually spread throughout the entire mountain.

Birds flying through the mountains and beasts roaming the forests, just like the humans, entered the small house without fear of death.

An overwhelming aura of blood and fiendish energy sealed off the house, as though even sunlight could no longer penetrate it.

Within the darkness, accompanying the ceaseless cries of the infant was the heart chilling sound of chewing.

In the end, every living creature throughout the entire mountain range was devoured by the demonic infant.

Silence.

Only the infant's cries continued to echo.

...

Such a degree of gore was already insignificant to Li Fan.

He looked toward a certain corner of the illusion.

"As expected of Senior!"

Carefree' voice sounded at the same time.

Li Fan's gaze fell upon that place. The vague figure that had been almost imperceptible gradually became clear.

"Wonderful, wonderful! The Saint Infant has grown even faster than planned!"

"In just a few more days, this spirit pill will be completely refined. The parents' sincere love, the innate desire for survival, together with the innate instincts of countless beasts..."

"Old Ancestor will definitely be extremely satisfied with this Saint Infant."

"After wasting seven attempts, this one is finally going to succeed."

The figure muttered to himself in delight.

After calculating the time with his fingers, the cultivator flew down, filled with anticipation, and slowly entered the small house.

"Obtaining the Saint Infant is what he has dreamed of day and night. Let him remain in this beautiful dream forever."

"Is it not good to help others achieve what they desire?"

Li Fan, however, saw that the cultivator's expression changed drastically the instant he entered the dark little house.

Confusion and terror continuously alternated across his face.

He tried to break free from the state in which he had lost control of himself.

Yet step by step, irresistibly, he continued approaching the infant.

Then he completely sank into it, just like the countless living beings who had already been buried within the infant's belly, and began digging out his own flesh bit by bit.

"Is Senior satisfied now?"

The scene suddenly shattered.

Carefree' expression was extremely innocent as she asked expectantly.

Li Fan did not answer. He merely watched as countless fragments of light surged toward him from the shattered scene.

The countless points of light were as vast as the starry sky. Each one represented an illusory dream.

Some came from humans, while others came from birds and beasts.

It was as though an endless river of memories was washing over Li Fan's divine sense.

These were not merely records of scenes. They were also accompanied by all kinds of insights of varying depths.

There were the insights of the countless beings who had become lost within these illusory dreams, and there were also...

Carefree' own insights.

Her various thoughts and comprehensions regarding the principles of the Xuanhuang Realm, and how to reverse them.

A mere strand of divine sense was originally incapable of enduring the baptism of such an enormous amount of information.

It was about to be assimilated and transformed into another part of the beings lost within the illusory dreams.

Yet the final portion of this strand of divine sense remained intact, no matter what.

No matter how much Carefree' will tried to wash over it, she could not harm it in the slightest.

In Carefree's eyes, a trace of curiosity could not help but emerge.

Yet with her strength, she was actually unable to see through what had been left behind within this remnant divine sense for a time.

This instead aroused Carefree's curiosity even further.

After the starry sky, even more points of light lit up. Like a galaxy descending from the highest heavens, they crashed down like a waterfall upon the final remnant of divine sense, which was like a diamond.

"Are you certain you really want to see it?"

After her unrelenting attempts, just as she was about to succeed, Li Fan's voice suddenly sounded beside Carefree's ear like a ghost.

After hesitating for only a moment, Carefree continued what she was doing.

The dazzling silver divine sense suddenly exploded.

What appeared before Carefree was...

Floodwaters surged, fire rained down from the heavens, black clouds blotted out the sun, and the world was sealed in ice.

Countless scenes of the world's destruction erupted all at once.

And this was merely the appetizer.

The destruction of the world was only the beginning.

A monstrous tide of black qi descended upon the world, swallowing everything.

Within the rolling sea of black qi, entire stretches of star seas suddenly vanished.

It was like a house filled with light gradually sinking into complete darkness.

The very existence of the entire world collapsed in an instant.

Yet the terrifying calamity was far from over.

The scene lit up within the darkness, then expanded billions upon billions of times in an instant.

It was as if within an endless ocean, every single wave contained a complete world.

And now, the terrifying darkness from the depths of the sea was continuously devouring everything.

Every apocalyptic scene imaginable was unfolding within this vast ocean.

An overwhelming sense of sinking and despair.

As the darkness gradually enveloped her and slowly eroded her existence.

When this vast ocean had completely fallen into dead silence.

After a brief pause, what appeared before her eyes once again were countless parallel threads.

The endless ocean she had just witnessed was merely one insignificant point of light on a single thread.

And now, these brilliant threads began to vanish in great numbers.

Every time a ray of light disappeared, corresponding scenes of world destruction surged into Carefree's mind.

The Great Dao Annihilation Calamity, involving countless possibilities, was drawn from the scenes Li Fan had personally witnessed, as well as the memories of Evil Su Ba within the Eternal Remnant Will.

Such terrifying yet incomparably real scenes.

They were far beyond what Carefree could endure.

Even though, from a spiritual perspective, her strength was not much weaker than that of Immortal Ancestor or the Heavenly Doctor.

But now...

Clouds representing apocalypse, terror, and despair were descending into Carefree's Dream Paradise through Carefree herself.

Boom!

One dream world after another inexplicably plunged into the apocalypse.

The very foundation of the Dream Paradise was subjected to a violent impact and inevitably began to shake violently.

"Ah!" Carefree let out a scream and awoke from the nightmare.

Her body trembled uncontrollably, drenched in cold sweat.

She attempted to stop the terrifying calamity from spreading throughout the Dream Paradise.

But the calamity spread far too quickly. Even she herself could barely protect herself, let alone the countless living beings within the dream.

"Senior, spare me!"

Carefree left the illusionary dream and arrived in the real world.

She knelt before Li Fan with her body trembling.

Although she had already learned through various channels that his strength was extremely terrifying, even somewhat inconceivable.

But being able to experience such a terrifying great calamity and survive it...

This had still far exceeded Carefree's imagination.

All her plans were now abandoned. The only thought remaining in Carefree's mind was her instinctive desire to survive.

"Carefree, Carefree."

"The great calamity has arrived. How can one resolve their worries?"

Li Fan spoke meaningfully.

Within Carefree's Dream Paradise, the calamity that had been violently affecting countless dreams suddenly fell completely still.

Then, just as it had arrived.

It disappeared without a trace, silently and without leaving the slightest sign.

A black point radiating monstrous baleful qi appeared in Li Fan's palm.

Then, with a light wave of Li Fan's hand, it floated toward Carefree's forehead.

Carefree's body trembled slightly, instinctively wanting to evade it.

But the point of light radiating rolling black qi seemed to possess the weight of countless layers of heaven and earth combined.

She could not evade it at all and could only allow it to enter her soul.

It was as if a haze had descended from the top of her head.

In an instant, it then sank into the depths of her heart and disappeared without a trace.

Only after a long time did Carefree, still shaken from what had happened, gradually come to her senses.

Looking at the figure before her, she could only feel that the thoughts she had entertained not long ago about playing with and testing him had been utterly ridiculous and absurd.

"Take me to meet the other four," Li Fan stood with his hands behind his back and ordered calmly.

A layer of silver light seemed to illuminate the edges of his body, concealing his face.

It made others dare not look directly at him.

"Yes." Carefree lowered her head and accepted the order.

The surrounding scene changed once again.

This time, Li Fan could feel the power of the Dream Paradise lingering around him.

However, he had already implanted a means of countercontrol within Carefree's mind.

With a single thought from him, Carefree's dream would collapse. Thus, Li Fan simply allowed her to pull him into the illusionary dream.

"Integrating the Ink Death Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth into the aspect of the Dao Annihilation Calamity."

"The power is not bad."

Li Fan thought this to himself as he looked around.

Having been intimidated by his methods, Carefree was indeed extremely efficient.

One-Heart Heavenly Sovereign, Zhao Ruoxi, was the first to appear before Li Fan.

She was also completely naked, just like Carefree. It seemed that she had previously been lying on the beach, basking in the sunlight.

Awakened by the inexplicable great calamity, she abruptly rose to her feet and looked around in shock and uncertainty.

And now, the sudden arrival of Carefree and Li Fan startled her even more.

Li Fan had already seen the beautiful body before him countless times.

No matter how graceful her figure was, it could no longer cause the slightest ripple in Li Fan's emotions.

Through a brief exchange of glances with Carefree, Zhao Ruoxi also learned what had happened.

While inwardly shaken, she changed into a white robe.

"Greetings, Senior." She respectfully bowed to Li Fan.

Li Fan nodded slightly.

Just then, another fluctuation came from the surroundings.

Carefree's form rapidly changed, returning to the appearance of an infant wrapped in swaddling clothes.

The other three Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns arrived belatedly.

Next

Chapter 1427: Falling Into Unceasing Reincarnation

A man dressed in a Daoist robe, and a white-robed cultivator dressed like a scholar. Li Fan had already seen the illusory figures of these two before. They were respectively the Truth Heavenly Sovereign and the Human Dao Heavenly Sovereign. Within the Land of Truth, no lies could exist. As for the Human Dao Heavenly Land, anyone could overturn heaven and earth, regardless of their cultivation, so long as their heart and nature were utterly sincere.

As for the final one, he was a young man with an extraordinary and otherworldly bearing, appearing to be no more than twenty years old.

Although Li Fan had never met him in reality, he had encountered him once during the trial in the Fallen Immortal Illusion Realm.

Ji Wangxian!

Ten thousand years ago, he had single-handedly established the [World Heaven Chamber of Commerce], which spread throughout the Xuanhuang Realm, and used spirit stones to construct an incomparably luxurious heavenly city.

“I never expected that the Ji Family's Ancestor, once notorious for being unscrupulously greedy in business, would transform himself into the representative of the Contract.”

“However, back then, the entire Ji Family, including the World Heaven Chamber of Commerce, was supposedly merely a cultivation tool for Ji Wangxian. After the great transformation of heaven and earth, he still wanted to maintain his state of longevity, so it makes sense that he would switch to a new cultivation method.”

Li Fan also immediately understood the exact situation of the Wanfa Chamber of Commerce, headquartered in Nine Mountains Prefecture and situated between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association.

“The Wanfa Chamber of Commerce is a remnant of the former World Heaven Chamber of Commerce. After Ji Wangxian attained the Dao of Longevity, this merchant association became useless to him. However, since it was once the direct force of one of the Five Heavenly Sovereigns, as long as it remained peaceful, neither the Five Elders Association nor the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance would actively cause trouble for it. It could also serve as a middle ground for facilitating private transactions between the two sides.”

“But that is all it amounts to. Before true power, the so-called Wanfa Chamber of Commerce is no different from the other ants of the world. Back then, beneath a single palm strike from the Immortal Ancestor, it turned to ashes along with the countless living beings of Nine Mountains Prefecture.”

His thoughts raced as Li Fan's gaze swept across the three newly arrived Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns one by one.

Clearly, they had already learned from Carefree about the unfathomable depth of Li Fan's cultivation. Although they appeared calm and continued to maintain the bearing of the Xuanhuang Realm's supreme experts, Li Fan could faintly discern their restraint.

“Hehehe, do not be nervous.”

“The Immortal Ancestor has already been suppressed by me.”

Li Fan spoke these words lightly, yet the five Heavenly Sovereigns present immediately felt their hairs stand on end.

And when they saw the Immortal Ancestor himself, trapped within countless illusions in Li Fan's palm, seemingly asleep yet seemingly awake, their faces inevitably paled. One after another, they lowered their heads, not daring to look directly at Li Fan.

Carefree in particular could not stop trembling once again. She was filled with lingering fear over her previous rude and foolish attempt to test Li Fan.

Before this, although the Five Heavenly Sovereigns knew that this senior, who had suddenly descended and was suspected to be a True Immortal, possessed immense strength, they had never had any concrete understanding of exactly how powerful he was.

Now they knew, the Immortal Ancestor had suppressed the Xuanhuang Realm for an entire era, forcing even the five of them to submit when they joined forces. Yet this same existence had been casually suppressed by the other party with a mere flip of his hand.

How could such strength not fill the five of them with dread?

Li Fan, however, seemed to disregard their feelings entirely and continued calmly, “There is a separation between immortals and mortals. I will not interfere with you excessively. However, there are some matters that require a little assistance from you.”

“Senior, please give your orders!” Carefree was the first to respond.

Her eagerness was completely abnormal compared to her usual indifference toward worldly affairs, causing the other four Heavenly Sovereigns to glance sideways at her.

However, anyone who could attain the Dao of Longevity within the Xuanhuang Realm was naturally no fool. They all followed suit and expressed their willingness.

“I have come here for one thing. The Soul-Grinding Plate.”

As soon as Li Fan said this, Carefree and the others all turned their gazes toward the man dressed in the Daoist robe.

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign's face twitched slightly. He then cupped his hands, took out a stone object resembling a millstone, and handed it over.

Li Fan saw that the Soul-Grinding Plate still seemed to be rotating slightly without end. Beneath it, one could vaguely hear the miserable wails of countless souls being crushed beneath the enormous stone mill.

There was indeed a faint disturbance coming from [Truth] as well.

Within the Soul-Grinding Plate were fifty-six remnant souls and one hundred and three skeletal remains. Yet the aura emanating from these remnant souls and skeletal remains was actually stronger than that of countless wandering souls and ghosts.

After carefully examining it, Li Fan saw through the mystery.

The Soul-Grinding Plate was like an enormous stone mill, continuously grinding these remnant souls and skeletal remains without rest. In theory, no matter how extraordinary they were, they could not withstand such torment. At most, they would survive for another decade or so before being completely obliterated.

Yet they had endured from ancient times until now.

This was because, every moment, streams of pure energy emerged from the Soul-Grinding Plate, nourishing these remnant souls.

It maintained their vitality without allowing them to perish, only for them to once again endure the torment of being crushed beneath the enormous stone mill.

This cycle repeated endlessly, as though they were trapped within an infernal prison with no end.

Li Fan finally understood how these existences, which were suspected to be Eternal Remnant Wills, had come into being.

“Not a bad method.” Li Fan gave a faint evaluation.

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign lowered his head and did not dare respond.

Li Fan exerted a little force with his hand.

In an instant, the enormous stone mill vanished into thin air.

The remnant souls and skeletal remains were freed and absorbed by [Truth].

The small amount of pure energy remaining within the stone mill also gave Li Fan a faint sense of familiarity.

It was exactly the same as the pure cultivation base formed after erasing one's own techniques by practicing the Taishang Primordial True Scripture and dissolving one's own cultivation technique.

“Survivor of the Taishang Sect.”

Li Fan glanced at the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, his expression thoughtful.

Using the Taishang Primordial True Scripture to transform cultivation, not all of it is inherited. A small portion instead disappears into thin air, its whereabouts unknown. Now it seems that it had actually flowed into the hands of the Taishang Sect.

“The life and death of the cultivators under heaven have nothing to do with me. It truly fits Taishang's persona.”

Back when the great catastrophe descended upon the Xuanhuang Realm, some members of the Ten Great Immortal Sects had already fled in advance. People like One-Heart and Truth Heavenly Sovereign might prove extraordinarily useful in the process of exploring what lay beyond the High Wall.

Li Fan temporarily paid them no attention and instead focused wholeheartedly on sensing the changes in [Truth].

Compared to [Truth]'s previous greedy and pleasurable desire to devour, its reaction this time was somewhat strange.

It was as if it had eaten something extremely difficult to swallow and wanted to spit it out. Yet it continued swallowing it bite by bite, repeatedly forcing itself to endure.

Li Fan's expression became somewhat subtle.

He stood motionless, examining the notifications transmitted by [Truth].

Several Longevity Immortal cultivators from the Five Elders Association also stood respectfully at his sides, not daring to move.

Perhaps this Eternal Remnant Will had not been condensed from a single person, but had instead been formed from numerous fragmented souls.

Li Fan had not even experienced the trial of the true illusion scene before [Truth] directly succeeded in absorbing it.

“Unlocked the [Unceasing Reincarnation] function.”

There was not even any poetry this time. It merely perfunctorily gave this one sentence.

“Unceasing Reincarnation...”

Even so, these four words immediately aroused Li Fan's interest.

“Using a certain life that has been experienced in the past as a template, create a period of time with a beginning and an end.”

“Any living being that is incapable of resisting can be thrown into this period of time.”

“If an identity corresponding to the living being exists within this timeline, the living being will possess that identity. If no such identity exists, possession will be assigned randomly.”

“The identity possessed by the living being can be designated.”

“The living being thrown into the reincarnation will lose all memories unrelated to the reincarnation.”

“Throughout the entire period of Unceasing Reincarnation, only the memories of the living being thrown into it will be retained.”

“Time will continuously reincarnate from beginning to end. The events that occur according to the fixed template will serve as linear constraints, ensuring that the Unceasing Reincarnation does not deviate from its original trajectory.”

“Unceasing Reincarnation depends upon my existence. Every time [Truth] is activated and returns to the initial anchor point, the reincarnation that was created will disappear and must be recreated. It is also possible to pay a certain price to choose to preserve it.”

“The flow of time within Unceasing Reincarnation is one thousand times faster than the normal spacetime I have experienced. It can also be accelerated to a considerable degree according to my needs...”

The description of Unceasing Reincarnation was far more complicated than the previous [Embodiment] and [Dao Origin] functions.

But in reality, it was very easy to understand.

It was simply a constantly repeating period of history that Li Fan had once experienced.

One had to know that every time Li Fan forced [Truth], a great terror would descend. This also meant that within Unceasing Reincarnation, every few decades would bring about a catastrophic calamity.

Furthermore, because [Truth] itself imposed restrictions, the living beings undergoing reincarnation would be unable to make the same gradual progress that Li Fan did each time and ultimately defy heaven to change their fate.

“It can even continuously observe the condition of the living being trapped within the reincarnation...”

“Interesting. Truly interesting.”

As if he had found some novel toy, a wave of curiosity surged within Li Fan's heart.

“Qiao Sidao was able to escape after billions upon billions of reincarnations throughout an Era.”

“Then, is there anyone who can escape from the Unceasing Reincarnation created by [Truth]?”

According to [Truth]'s explanation, the Unceasing Reincarnation would only end when the final outcome of the reincarnation underwent a complete deviation.

Yet the living being trapped within it would still be unable to escape.

Only the reincarnation timeline itself would enter a state of nothingness between truth and illusion.

It would wait for Li Fan to issue his next command.

Whether to release the living being trapped within it, or create a new Unceasing Reincarnation.

“It truly lives up to the name Unceasing.”

Li Fan was almost moved to applaud. He suddenly turned around and looked toward Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

A meaningful smile appeared on his face, and then he lightly pointed toward him.

One of the few Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns in the Xuanhuang Realm disappeared silently before everyone's eyes.

His life and death were unknown.

The remaining Carefree and the others were horrified.

Yet they had absolutely no thought of avenging Truth Heavenly Sovereign. They could only secretly pray that this True Immortal senior before them would spare their lives.

The entire process of the True Immortal suppressing Truth Heavenly Sovereign produced not the slightest fluctuation of energy. In a manner completely beyond their understanding, he simply disappeared.

They could not even speak of resisting. They could not even comprehend what they were seeing.

The difference between them was simply too vast, to the point that they had no courage to resist at all.

At this moment, however, Li Fan's attention had already been focused on the Unceasing Reincarnation that had been opened.

The period of history he chose was the one in which he himself, as a Holy Embryo, rode the Medicine King True Cauldron and charged toward the Immortal Ruins, ultimately provoking the Hunger Immortal to destroy the world with a single glance.
