Next

Chapter 1428: Dream Formation Derived From The Heart

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign within the Unceasing Reincarnation lost his memories of ever seeing Li Fan and returned to the Anchored Year once again.

A trace of doubt flashed through his eyes.

But how could someone who was merely in the Longevity Immortal realm resist the might of [Truth]? Very soon, this thread of doubt vanished without a trace. Following the course of history he had once experienced, he continued living on.

...

Li Fan sensed the images within the Unceasing Reincarnation changing at tremendous speed.

The thousandfold increase in the flow of time placed a peculiar pressure upon him. Unlike the feeling of carrying an Immortal Domain Go piece on his back, where his body and soul seemed to bear some kind of heavy burden, this was different.

It was as if everything around him had been shrouded in a hazy mist. His thoughts, and even his every movement, felt as though he were inside a dream. Under this influence, Li Fan even faintly began to doubt whether his existence in the present world was truly real.

And it was not only Li Fan.

Even the four remaining Heavenly Sovereigns standing beside him began to have increasingly hazy gazes. Some recalled memories from the distant past, some suddenly saw scenes they had never experienced in their minds, while others suddenly remembered a fleeting thought they had long since forgotten.

None of them could resist this gradual contamination. They became deeply immersed in it and were unable to extricate themselves.

It was only after the Unceasing Reincarnation's time rapidly advanced, experiencing its first ending and entering its second cycle, that Li Fan gradually adapted.

His mind focused, and the surrounding mist became somewhat thinner. He could still feel a slight sense of abnormality, but at least it no longer affected his movements. Only then did Li Fan have the opportunity to observe what was happening within the Unceasing Reincarnation.

Without paying attention to the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, who was caught between surprise, joy, and fear after his "rebirth," the first thing Li Fan did was search for the existence of "himself" within the Xuanhuang Realm of the Unceasing Reincarnation.

What relieved him was that he did not see another version of himself.

The one replacing him and pushing the Unceasing Reincarnation toward its predetermined ending was a cultivator named "Zhang Fan," who was extremely similar to him.

"There naturally isn't another [Truth] either. What Zhang Fan possesses is merely the illusion that he can continually reincarnate. Once the Unceasing Reincarnation is activated, his memories will also be reset without him realizing it..."

"He is nothing more than a tool created for the purpose."

Li Fan shook his head slightly and stopped paying attention to him. He turned his gaze toward the Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign firmly believed that the destruction of the Xuanhuang Realm he had witnessed in his "previous life" was absolutely not an illusion. Yet he did not immediately tell others about his experience of reincarnation.

Instead, he put aside all other affairs and devoted himself to investigating the truth behind the destruction of the world.

As expected of a powerful expert capable of attaining Longevity, he soon discovered that the arrival of the calamity might be connected to the inexplicably appearing Heavenly Dao Bai.

Following the clues, he eventually traced them all the way to Medicine King Valley.

When the Chaos Character Art was detonated and the Medicine King True Cauldron containing the Holy Embryo soared into the sky, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, who had been hiding in the darkness, also keenly sensed its existence.

He was about to intervene.

But...

After Heavenly Dao Bai rescued the cultivators of the Medicine King Sect, it flashed directly in front of the Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

Although it was not a match for the Immortal Ancestor, it was ultimately the manifestation of the will of the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm and could mobilize the power of all the souls of heaven and earth.

For a time, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign was actually unable to break free.

He could only watch helplessly as the Medicine King True Cauldron flew farther and farther away.

In this life, the ending remained unchanged. The Xuanhuang Realm was still destroyed by a single glance from the Hunger Immortal.

...

"Is this the so-called linear constraint of [Truth]?"

Li Fan pondered thoughtfully, then accelerated the flow of time within the Unceasing Reincarnation. His mind became dizzy, and the pressure he endured also deepened considerably. He fast-forwarded to the Truth Heavenly Sovereign's next attempt to save the world.

Long before Heavenly Dao Bai descended, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign had already noticed Zhang Fan, who was secretly plotting his schemes. He decisively attacked, intending to capture him.

But the aura he revealed actually alarmed the restrictions within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

The Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign of the Five Elders Association infiltrated the territory, causing the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance to face the situation as though confronting a formidable enemy. The phantom of the Immortal Ancestor descended and, without saying a word, struck the Truth Heavenly Sovereign with a single palm, severely injuring him.

Amid the chaos, Zhang Fan had already disappeared without a trace.

Not long afterward, the world-ending calamity descended once again.

"Interesting. The Immortal Ancestor phantom had never actually appeared in this life. Activating the Immortal Ancestor phantom requires the sacrifice of several Immortal Chosens."

"But this is not the Unceasing Reincarnation arbitrarily fabricating a simulation. Rather..."

A hint of amusement flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

"It is almost as though it had already foreseen the actions of the Truth Heavenly Sovereign. At the beginning of the reincarnation's renewed cycle, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had already begun conducting a grand selection of Immortal Chosen. There was a certain deviation from what had happened in the previous cycle."

"But the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, focused entirely on tracking down the true culprit behind the world's destruction, inadvertently overlooked this point."

"It seems that the so-called constraint of the Unceasing Reincarnation only requires the beginning and the ending to remain consistent with the original. The specific details that occur in between do not matter."

The third cycle.

This time, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign simply stationed himself at the frontier of the Xuanhuang Realm, waiting for the Medicine King True Cauldron to appear. Yet for some unknown reason, the Medicine King True Cauldron failed to appear for a long time.

What surprised him even more was that on the day when the apocalypse he remembered finally arrived, the suction force transmitted from the Immortal Ruins above suddenly became much stronger.

Caught off guard, he was unable to control himself and began falling toward the Immortal Ruins.

Doing everything he could to survive, he saw a skeleton standing upon the land filled with ruins. Beside it were countless golden chains that had been torn apart.

The Hunger Immortal of the Immortal Ruins grabbed the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, who attempted to resist, and absorbed and refined him.

Then, its eyes filled with crimson light, it looked toward the Xuanhuang Realm below, still unsatisfied.

...

"Hm? This is a possibility I have never experienced before."

"Did the Hunger Immortal emerge from seclusion ahead of time?"

In the present world, Li Fan gazed from afar at the Hunger Immortal within the Unceasing Reincarnation. Even with her strength, who had already successfully comprehended the Dao and approached the Nameless realm, she still could not detect Li Fan's probing gaze.

It merely gave rise to an inexplicable insight, leaving it suspicious and uncertain.

...

The performance of Hunger Immortal after successfully comprehending the Dao made Li Fan's heart jolt, and he temporarily stopped the simulation function of the Unceasing Reincarnation.

It was not that he feared Hunger Immortal.

"Strictly speaking, this Unceasing Reincarnation is actually a new possibility that was created out of nothing by [Truth]."

"Then is it possible that there could be an unparalleled expert capable of traversing possibilities, discovering something strange about it, and leaping out of the Unceasing Reincarnation?"

"For example, if I use my experiences from the previous life as the template, would that mean that within this created Unceasing Reincarnation, there would be another darkened Heavenly Doctor?"

Just as Li Fan was filled with uncertainty, [Truth] seemed to sense his thoughts and promptly sent a reminder.

"The incomplete timeline that was created naturally will not include existences capable of transcending possibilities."

"The variables caused by such existences within the template experience will manifest in other ways, ensuring consistency between the origin and the ultimate..."

Li Fan silently sensed the fluctuations from [Truth]. Although he breathed a sigh of relief, he also felt somewhat regretful.

"As long as nothing goes wrong."

He glanced at the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, who was in the process of collapse within the Unceasing Reincarnation and had not moved for more than ten years. Li Fan shook his head slightly.

"I wonder when this Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign will be able to break free from the fate of reincarnation."

"I hope you do not disappoint me."

Of course, even if this Truth Heavenly Sovereign completely abandoned the idea of escaping, Li Fan would not easily let him out. Li Fan had already noticed that every time the Unceasing Reincarnation began to turn again, it was equivalent to [Truth] activating another Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood.

It was simply incomparable to the level and scale when Li Fan activated it himself.

But its advantage was that it continued without interruption and did not interfere with Li Fan's actions in reality.

"This is a powerful aid for me to comprehend the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood."

"It is just a pity that with my current strength, I seem to be unable to withstand the opening of a second Unceasing Reincarnation."

Li Fan thought with some regret.

His thoughts returned to reality.

Li Fan looked toward Carefree and the others.

The four Longevity Immortal cultivators had been completely subdued by Li Fan's methods.

"I can vaguely sense that there is a great terror beyond the High Wall."

"This is also the reason why I stopped despite being capable of crossing the High Wall."

Li Fan's words caused the expressions of Carefree and the others to change drastically.

"I know that the Carefree Dream Realm you created has already explored to the vicinity of the High Wall."

"With it working together with my actions in reality, we can investigate the truth beneath the High Wall from both sides."

Li Fan stared intently at Wuyou.

This frail Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign hurriedly assured him, "It is truly Carefree's fortune to be able to serve Senior."

"As for you..."

Li Fan looked toward the Human Dao Heavenly Sovereign, Zhao Ruoxi, and Ji Wangxian.

He flicked three fingers, sending three particles of light flying into the brows of the three.

They were miniature formations centered around the Ink Death Dao Annihilation Aspect, powered by the phantom of the Xuanhuang Realm obtained as feedback from the Sitting Heaven Art. They could be used for surveillance and countermeasures.

The moment they entered their bodies, countless formation patterns resembling roots emerged and burrowed deep into their divine souls.

What made Zhao Ruoxi and the other two feel even more horrified was that they had not felt the slightest pain from beginning to end.

Instead, an unparalleled sense of satisfaction and joy surged from the depths of their hearts. As the formation patterns gradually covered their entire divine souls, their feelings toward Li Fan also slowly changed from their previous fear into reverence and loyalty.

They clearly knew that the light cores rooted deep within their divine souls were constantly monitoring everything about them. Yet not only did they feel no desire to resist, they were instead almost impatient to completely expose their divine souls and offer themselves up to him.

The Human Dao Heavenly Sovereign's blood and qi surged as he developed an impulse to die for one who truly understood and valued him.

Ji Wangxian dropped to one knee, thoroughly subdued.

As for Zhao Ruoxi, her cheeks flushed red. Her body went limp, and she collapsed onto the ground. Her eyes were filled with a dazed fascination as she stared at Li Fan, unwilling to look away for even an instant.

Through his top-down observation of the Unceasing Reincarnation, Li Fan had gained an even deeper understanding of the several Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns before him.

Combined with the formation modifications that the Final Dissociation Disc had tailored specifically for them, this produced such an astonishing effect.

"It is even a hundred times more effective than the True Temptation Fruit back then."

Li Fan thought this to himself.

He turned his head toward Carefree, who had witnessed everything and whose face had already gone pale.

"The level of your dream realm is rather shallow. If you want to explore beyond the High Wall, I am afraid you lack the ability."

"Seven days from now, all of you are to come to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance."

"I will have someone assist you in making some modifications to your dream realm."

His voice was still echoing through the air when Li Fan's figure had already faded away, disappearing without a trace.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1429: Repairing The Azure Firmament Pearl

Through the formation patterns rooted deep within Zhao Ruoxi's soul, Li Fan went through all of her memories in an instant.

As expected of the One-Heart Heavenly Sovereign, who had gathered the myriad beings into a single person, the scenes she had experienced were truly astonishing.

But that was not the important part.

Li Fan quickly found what he was looking for.

"Seize Heaven and Replace It..."

This was one of the secret inheritances of the Grand Dao Sect, as well as one of the rich legacies left behind after the destruction of the Xuantian Sect. It was said that it could completely strip members of the Xuantian Sect of their cultivation techniques and cultivation. It could even transfer the realm and strength obtained through cultivating Xuantian Sect techniques onto the caster himself, like transplanting flowers and grafting trees.

Li Fan remained skeptical of the description of this secret technique.

After all, existences like the Twelve Dharma Kings should not have been so easily restrained by a technique as simple as this.

But regardless, the vast majority of the Xuantian Sect's cultivation techniques originated from the Azure Firmament Pearl. For the pearl to reclaim them was not necessarily beyond reason. What Li Fan needed to do now was verify whether the secret technique Seize Heaven and Replace It was connected to the Azure Firmament Pearl.

By feeding his cultivation base back into the Azure Firmament Pearl, he would pull it back from the brink of total collapse. Afterward, Li Fan could supplement this with the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, repairing it step by step.

As for the cultivation power infused into the Azure Firmament Pearl...

Zhao Ruoxi, who cultivated the Eternal Sole Self Art, was naturally the best source.

From Zhao Ruoxi's memories, Li Fan already knew that although she had attained the Dao through the principle of ‘Myriad Beings as One Heart,’ the core of her cultivation technique still followed the path of the Eternal Sole Self Art.

"Addicted to games, with no thought of making progress."

"She managed to succeed in reversing the principles only because she took advantage of the heaven-defying nature of this cultivation technique."

Li Fan snorted inwardly as he evaluated her. In the blink of an eye, he had already returned to the depression beneath the High Wall, where the Azure Firmament Pearl was located.

The Final Dissociation Disc had long since translated the Seize Heaven and Replace It secret technique into a formation.

Fine points of light carrying the formation's information silently entered Senior Sister Zhao's soul.

The points of light spread along the densely packed formation patterns. As they circulated, they slowly extracted Senior Sister Zhao's cultivation.

Meanwhile, within Li Fan's hand, drops of liquid glowing with a strange green light were slowly taking shape.

Zhao Ruoxi naturally sensed the sudden loss of her cultivation with great clarity.

For an existence in the Longevity Immortal realm like her, there was nothing more terrifying than becoming a mortal overnight.

Her face turned pale. She had no idea how she had offended that unpredictable True Immortal.

The speed at which Zhao Ruoxi's cultivation drained became faster and faster. Yet she had no means of resisting and could only watch helplessly as the essence she had painstakingly accumulated over thousands of years vanished before her eyes.

Soon, she realized something even more terrifying.

Ever since she had reversed the principles and become the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign of the Xuanhuang Realm, she had become a part of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Although she was still in the Longevity Immortal realm, as a massive amount of cultivation power drained away, her increasingly feeble body seemed to be unable to support the Xuanhuang Principles she had reversed.

As if crushed beneath an enormous weight, her soul and body both began to ache faintly.

"Ugh..." Zhao Ruoxi could not suppress a muffled groan. Countless tiny bloodstains appeared across her body.

And it was not merely this one body of hers.

Every single version of her stood on the very brink of collapse.

The pain continued to intensify.

The spiritual power within her body was almost completely drained, leaving her even weaker than an ordinary Qi Condensation cultivator. Zhao Ruoxi felt an unprecedented weakness. The sensation of her soul and body being crushed and torn apart inch by inch was amplified a hundredfold, even a thousandfold, as her strength declined.

Blood gushed from the countless cracks covering her body, soaking her into a bloodied figure and concealing the tears and mucus streaming down her face. The pain was so overwhelming that she could not utter a single cry, producing only faint, hoarse sounds from her throat.

The terror of possibly becoming a mortal at any moment, combined with the ever increasing and omnipresent pain in her soul and body, caused Zhao Ruoxi to drift into a trance, as if she had returned to Mount Daqing in her childhood.

Back then, bandits had burned, killed, and looted the village, with blood staining the sky.

Hiding outside the village, Zhao Ruoxi watched in panic as the vicious bandits who seemed to have discovered her came running toward her with sinister smiles.

Then she noticed a figure that had somehow been standing silently nearby for who knew how long.

As if grasping the only possible lifeline, Zhao Ruoxi cried out in desperation.

"Immortal Master, save me! Immortal Master, save me!"

The hoarse cries from her throat seemed to overlap with those from her memories of the past.

Only, the figure Zhao Ruoxi had once regarded as her savior was no longer Zhang Rong, the fourth generation disciple of the Moonlight lineage of the Grand Dao Sect, whom she had long since taken revenge upon and devoured.

It had become Li Fan.

The blurred appearance of the figure within Zhao Ruoxi's heart gradually became clear. As if responding to her plea, Li Fan smiled faintly.

From the center of the countless formation patterns within Zhao Ruoxi's soul, within the phantom image of the Xuanhuang Realm, streams of incomparably pure energy began to seep out.

At first, it was merely like a trickling stream. But in an instant, it transformed into great rivers surging and roaring without end.

In the blink of an eye, it filled Zhao Ruoxi's body.

The One-Heart Heavenly Sovereign, who had been on the verge of death only moments ago, instantly became vigorous and full of life once more.

Feeling the enormous power within her body, seemingly inexhaustible and originating from the phantom image of the Xuanhuang Realm, Zhao Ruoxi felt that she had become even stronger than before.

The bloodstains on her fair skin disappeared without a trace. Zhao Ruoxi respectfully bowed in gratitude toward the figure standing deep within her soul.

...

Li Fan paid no attention to the minor disturbances on Zhao Ruoxi's side. At this moment, all of his attention was focused on the extremely fragile Azure Firmament Pearl before him.

All the cultivation Zhao Ruoxi had accumulated throughout her life through cultivating the Eternal Sole Self Art had only transformed into four drops of green liquid.

Li Fan did not need to manipulate them much. Relying on the mysterious connection between them and the Azure Firmament Pearl, the moment the green liquid appeared, its energy slowly began to drift toward the Azure Firmament Pearl nearby.

Nearly ten thousand years of painstaking cultivation from a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign finally allowed this Immortal Artifact to successfully escape the brink of destruction.

Li Fan was delighted. His divine sense followed like a shadow, quickly enveloping the Azure Firmament Pearl completely.

On the surface of the Azure Firmament Pearl, layers of azure radiance resembling the vast blue heavens continuously flowed, attempting to prevent Li Fan from probing it.

This layer of protection might perhaps have been effective against ordinary mortals. Or perhaps, at the Azure Firmament Pearl's peak, it might even have been capable of stopping Li Fan.

But at present, it had only just escaped the state of imminent destruction and was barely clinging to life. How could it possibly stop Li Fan, whose body was already half immortal?

His divine sense became like a needle, piercing through the azure heavens. It advanced straight inward and stabbed into the interior of the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Boom!

The scene before Li Fan's eyes shook violently. In the next moment, he seemed to arrive in a strange realm.

Twelve solemn palaces stood high above the clouds, arranged in a circle and occupying the firmament of the world.

Within each palace, a statue was enshrined.

Cultivators continuously came from the earth. They knelt and prayed.

Yet from time to time, the faces on the statues actually manifested as living faces. They seemed to be communicating with the cultivators kneeling before them.

...

The Divine Shaman Realm!

After the images in his mind faded, Li Fan finally understood what he had just witnessed.

That strand of divine sense had already been forcibly expelled by the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Li Fan did not attempt to enter again. With the contact he had just made and relying on the unique aura of the Azure Firmament Pearl, he could use the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood to reproduce it within a short period of time.

But Li Fan did not leave to attempt it.

He already had a premonition that using the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood to create another Azure Firmament Pearl out of thin air would undoubtedly place a far greater burden on him than before. He needed to make some preparations in advance.

The Azure Firmament Pearl could not be moved for the time being, so Li Fan laid down layer upon layer of formations around it.

Points of light representing Immortal-grade formations continuously flickered around him. Only then did Li Fan feel somewhat at ease.

“Falsehood Becomes Truth!”

Li Fan silently intoned in his heart. However, the target of his divine ability was not the Azure Firmament Pearl, but himself!

His cultivation, divine soul, and various aspects of his power all surged, doubling in an instant.

Li Fan looked at the Azure Firmament Pearl, yet still felt that it was not enough.

After catching his breath for a while, he once again used the divine ability Falsehood Becomes Truth.

The threefold increase in his cultivation could not be maintained for long.

Li Fan merely waited until his body adapted to the burden it was carrying, then focused his mind and locked onto the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Using its brilliant azure color, like the boundless blue heavens, as a reference, Li Fan recalled the scene of the Divine Shaman Realm that he had glimpsed only briefly.

He visualized the existence of another Azure Firmament Pearl.

“Falsehood…”

“Becomes Truth!”

Li Fan felt that this was dozens of times more strenuous than when he had previously recreated the Ancient Cang Primal Essence.

This was probably because the Ancient Cang Primal Essence existed merely as a single form of energy, whereas the Azure Firmament Pearl before him was a convergence of countless laws.

Veins bulged across Li Fan’s forehead. In the very next moment, he felt as though his entire body had been drained empty.

An overwhelming weakness washed over him.

However, when he saw that the color of the Azure Firmament Pearl seemed to have become even more vivid and lifelike, Li Fan knew that his attempt had succeeded.

The accumulated cultivation from Falsehood Becomes Truth slowly disappeared. Exhaustion of both body and mind crashed over him like a collapsing mountain.

But Li Fan merely closed his eyes and rested for a moment before beginning to replicate the Azure Firmament Pearl once again.

With every use of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, his comprehension deepened slightly. Yet, just like those things created out of thin air, most of these insights would gradually dissipate as time passed. Only a very small portion would remain.

“The Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood is ethereal and elusive, yet it is the foundation upon which I stand.”

“Relying solely on the insights gained from each [Truth], as well as the auxiliary comprehension of the Unceasing Reincarnation, is far from enough.”

A trace of determination flashed through Li Fan’s eyes. While the insights from his previous use of the divine ability were still fresh, he endured his exhaustion of both body and mind and once again used Falsehood Becomes Truth.

Within the underground river of the depression, indescribably wondrous auras descended and vanished one after another.

Time slowly passed. Although Li Fan's appearance remained completely unchanged, his aura somehow became increasingly difficult to fathom.

He truly seemed like an ethereal True Immortal of the present age, transcending the mundane world!

After the time it takes for an incense stick to burn, Li Fan finally felt that his body and divine soul had reached their limits.

He let out a soft sigh as overwhelming fatigue surged over him like a tide.

Without even having the energy to care about the Azure Firmament Pearl beside him, which had been restored and was almost reborn, Li Fan first closed his eyes and sat in meditation, resting properly.

His mind became empty, devoid of thought or sensation, as if he had fallen into a deep sleep.

Yet faintly, it seemed that countless points of light were continuously lighting up, only to return to darkness shortly afterward.

Again and again.

When Li Fan regained his spirit, three days had already passed.

Through these short bursts of high intensity, near-limit uses of Falsehood Becomes Truth, Li Fan had obtained nearly as much comprehension of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood as he would normally gain from using [Truth] five times.

“This is also because this was my first time attempting such continuous, limit-pushing use.”

“It awakened the insights I had gained from using the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood in the past but had since forgotten. The effect will certainly not be this good the next time I attempt something like this.”

“But its significance remains immense.”

Through this attempt, Li Fan had already sensed that the insights into the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood obtained each time he activated [Truth] seemed to be different from those he gained by imitating and using it himself.

Without a doubt, the insights gained through [Truth] were purer and closer to the truth.

The insights Li Fan gained through his own attempts, although a level weaker, were much easier for him to control.

“There is still a stretch of road between comprehension and practice.”

“Each use of [Truth] deepens my comprehension, but the actual practice still has to be carried out by me personally.”

Li Fan seemed to have realized something.

At this moment, [Truth] actually sent out a faint fluctuation, as if saying that he was teachable after all.

Li Fan pondered for a while and continued trying to communicate with [Truth].

What he received, however, was silence.

Li Fan shook his head slightly and temporarily put the matter aside. He looked toward the Azure Firmament Pearl not far away.

He reached out and drew it toward him.

Perhaps because Li Fan had restored a small portion of it, he did not sense any resistance. The immortal artifact lightly fell into his hand.

“Although it has barely escaped its shattered state, its power has still declined severely.”

“After all, the life force contained within it was extracted and used to repair the Darkest Star Sea.”

Li Fan looked at the abundant life vitality filling the underground river of the depression and thought to himself.

However, what Li Fan valued was not the vitality of the Azure Firmament Pearl, but rather its legendary and miraculous functions.

“The Azure Firmament Pearl can devour other immortal artifacts and transfer their functions into itself.”

“It can also extract the cultivation techniques a cultivator practiced during their lifetime from their corpse.”

After carefully investigating it, Li Fan discovered that there was no longer any artifact spirit within the Azure Firmament Pearl.

If not for Li Fan repairing it, it would now be nothing more than a completely functionless empty shell.

But now…

“The function can still be used.”

Li Fan's heart stirred. Carrying the Azure Firmament Pearl, he returned to the Xuanhuang Realm.

To prevent the Hunger Immortal of the Immortal Ruins from noticing anything, the surrounding concealment Immortal formations had not been dispersed. Instead, they had transformed into a layer of white mist.

It made Li Fan appear especially ethereal, almost like an Immortal.

“Devour an immortal artifact…”

Li Fan looked toward the underground secret chamber of the Sun family, where the Boundless Mirror was located.

He reached forward with one hand, and the Boundless Mirror pierced through spacetime and appeared in Li Fan's palm.

“Who is it!”

Ignoring the cry of the Boundless Mirror spirit, Sun Luyao, Li Fan directly fed it to the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Although both had once been immortal artifacts and both were severely damaged, the Boundless Mirror was clearly not on the same level as the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Furthermore, with Li Fan suppressing it from the side, the Boundless Mirror did not have even the slightest ability to resist.

It disappeared into the vast azure radiance of the Azure Firmament Pearl.

The surface of the Azure Firmament Pearl seemed to be struck by a torrential downpour, sending waves of ripples across it.

Perhaps because it was too weak and had suddenly been forced to devour something, causing it to be unable to digest the nourishment properly, the Azure Firmament Pearl took almost half a day to complete this digestion.

When the torrential rain finally subsided, a peculiar aura was born from within the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Next

Chapter 1430: Taking Corpses As Teachers

Originally, the vast, uniform surface of the Azure Firmament Pearl was divided by countless lines into numerous different regions.

Most of these regions had boundary lines that flickered indistinctly, making them difficult to see clearly.

Only the region displaying a bright mirror seemed to be truly "alive." Moreover, it emitted fluctuations vastly different from those of the Azure Firmament Pearl itself.

From the aura rising from it, Li Fan could vaguely see what seemed to be a complete Boundless Mirror.

"These different regions are the different immortal artifacts that the Azure Firmament Pearl once devoured?"

"But after the Azure Firmament Pearl was shattered, all of those functions became unusable. Only the newly opened Boundless Mirror region can barely be used..." A realization surfaced in Li Fan's heart.

Compared to the Azure Firmament Pearl itself, this region that had been developed into the Boundless Mirror was actually much easier to break through.

With great patience, Li Fan used the Boundless Mirror region as his point of entry. He finally succeeded in sending another strand of divine sense into the interior of the Azure Firmament Pearl, and this time, he was not easily expelled.

Instead, within the Boundless Mirror region, his divine sense took root and established itself.

Before long, formations arose with his thoughts, growing without end.

Countless pale green formation patterns, almost identical in color to the Azure Firmament Pearl itself, took root and proliferated from within the Boundless Mirror region.

Li Fan attempted to completely refine and control this immortal treasure through the technique of "formation refinement."

With the Final Dissociation Disc taking action, the efficiency was naturally extraordinary.

In less than a day, an inexplicable connection was born between Li Fan and the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Li Fan let out a long breath and toyed with the Azure Firmament Pearl in his right hand.

There was no tangible sensation of physical contact. It was merely cold, smooth, soft, and extremely pleasant to the touch.

The Azure Firmament Pearl had been reborn from destruction, as though it had been reforged anew. All the shackles and restrictions that had once bound it seemed to have vanished without a trace.

At the very least, after Li Fan carefully inspected it from the inside out, he found no traces of any residual energy of unknown origin.

Even so, Li Fan was not completely at ease. The formation refinement continued without interruption.

Across the vast azure sky on the surface of the Azure Firmament Pearl, countless silver stars gradually appeared. They were traces revealed by the operation of the formations.

Li Fan first began studying the functions of the Boundless Mirror.

He had once infiltrated this damaged immortal artifact and remained inside it for some time, so he could be said to understand it extremely well.

"The many divine abilities of the Boundless Mirror are built upon the immortal-grade formation [Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form]. In theory, it can analyze all things in the world. Even things beyond its own level can be slowly analyzed through the perseverance of a fool moving a mountain."

"But the Boundless Mirror I encountered in the Xuanhuang Realm had suffered tremendous damage to its power. Let alone all things in the world, even the Tianxuan Mirror, which is also an immortal artifact, could only be secretly observed by taking advantage of fleeting opportunities."

"And now..."

Li Fan returned to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance with the Azure Firmament Pearl in hand.

Within the space at the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters, the Azure Firmament Pearl in Li Fan's hand rippled with emerald light. Suddenly, it transformed into the appearance of the Tianxuan Mirror.

A faint, dim radiance spread outward in a circle.

Li Fan did not receive any warning of danger from the simulated artifact spirit of the Tianxuan Mirror.

However, he keenly sensed that a new functional region was being opened within the Azure Firmament Pearl.

It was another small mirror, but this one was an ancient bronze-green color, almost identical to the main body of the Tianxuan Mirror.

"The Boundless Mirror it devoured was merely a damaged and weakened version. Yet the Azure Firmament Pearl can use its own extraordinary properties to complete the functions of the Boundless Mirror!"

"Moreover, judging from the current situation, after devouring the Boundless Mirror, it can actually reproduce the various functions of other immortal artifacts merely through [analysis]?"

Li Fan was both shocked and delighted.

However, this delight did not last long.

Perhaps because it had been generated through the analysis of the Boundless Mirror, the Tianxuan Mirror functional region within the Azure Firmament Pearl was adjacent to the Boundless Mirror functional region. Moreover, the boundary between them was not particularly distinct.

The Tianxuan Mirror functional region existed independently for only the time it took to drink half a cup of tea before it was actually swallowed by the Boundless Mirror functional region.

Moreover, Li Fan was unable to control this merging.

After the fusion occurred, the small mirror image faintly visible on the surface of the Azure Firmament Pearl also changed accordingly.

The bronze-green color faded, and its shape became circular.

Within the mirror surface, there seemed to be a pair of eyes hidden inside, secretly observing everything outside.

Li Fan's heart stirred slightly, and the Azure Firmament Pearl changed according to his thoughts.

That illusory mirror appeared realistically in his hand.

The analysis had not yet been completed, but it already possessed many of the functions of both the Boundless Mirror and the Tianxuan Mirror.

Apart from conventional functions such as opening an internal space, storing items, deduction, and analysis, what Li Fan valued even more was the Tianxuan Mirror's ability to "split and produce secondary forms."

Unfortunately, this seemed to be an ability that the Tianxuan Mirror had comprehended later in its existence rather than an innate ability. The Azure Firmament Pearl's analysis function could not directly copy it onto itself.

The Boundless Immortal Formation should already possess a sufficiently high level. Judging from the Tianxuan Mirror information analyzed within the Azure Firmament Pearl, it is already almost exactly the same as what Li Fan read from the simulated artifact spirit of the Tianxuan Mirror.

"The only thing missing is the existence of an artifact spirit."

A trace of hesitation flashed through Li Fan's heart. In the end, he did not rush to use the method of nurturing intelligence from living beings to give the Azure Firmament Pearl consciousness. He did not even apply the method of constructing a simulated personality to the Azure Firmament Pearl.

From that fleeting glimpse of the scene in the Divine Shaman Realm, Li Fan had seen twelve statues standing high in the heavens. They had given him a faint sense of unease.

"For now, it is better to simply use it as a pure artifact."

After the analysis and fusion of the Boundless Mirror and Tianxuan Mirror were complete, Li Fan next began testing the Azure Firmament Pearl's ability to manifest cultivation techniques.

After Emperor Sanmo brought several corpses, Li Fan used the Azure Firmament Pearl to devour them.

As the corpses disappeared into the vast azure void, several unusual auras also emerged within the treasure pearl.

They were composed of countless tiny characters, forming one cultivation technique after another as they floated across the azure sky.

They corresponded exactly to the cultivation techniques these death row prisoners had practiced during their lifetimes.

Even though their cultivation techniques had long since been forcibly crippled, the Azure Firmament Pearl was still able to reveal them.

After comparing them, there was not the slightest discrepancy.

Li Fan reached out and drew one of them toward himself.

"Nine Refinements Wandering Spirit Art..."

Li Fan absorbed these characters into his body.

It was not merely the cultivation technique itself.

Even the corpse's understanding of the technique during its lifetime, along with the insights it had gained while cultivating, surfaced within Li Fan's mind.

"With the experience of the 'previous life,' cultivation becomes far too fast!"

"The only pity is that there are insights, but no memories."

Li Fan felt somewhat regretful, but he also knew that being able to accomplish this much already made it unquestionably an unparalleled treasure.

"Taking corpses as teachers. Its efficiency is absolutely beyond what ordinary instruction can compare with."

"After all, a corpse cannot conceal anything."

However, in the current Darkest Star Sea, there were very few people who could make Li Fan want to learn from or imitate them.

"I wonder if these can still be of use."

With this thought, Li Fan tentatively threw all the large amount of ash and powder left behind after Qin Zhuang was silenced outside the High Wall into the Azure Firmament Pearl.

The powder was slowly devoured by the azure light. Even after Qin Zhuang's soul had been scattered and destroyed, he continued to contribute his remaining light and heat.

A strong wind also seemed to rise within the Azure Firmament Pearl.

Li Fan stared at it intently, afraid of missing even the slightest detail.

The Azure Firmament Pearl was, after all, a supreme treasure worthy of the Xuantian King’s regard. Ordinary methods of erasing traces and destroying evidence seemed unable to withstand the Azure Firmament Pearl’s probing.

After a period of brewing, a strand of aura with a peculiar color emerged within the boundless azure.

It was extremely conspicuous.

It continuously shifted in a gradual transformation from black to white, passing through what seemed like every color that existed throughout time.

Li Fan drew this strand of aura toward himself.

“[Thirty Lectures on the Fundamentals of Dao Primal]...”

Sensing this name, Li Fan’s expression became somewhat strange. However, he soon immersed himself in Qin Zhuang’s cultivation and understanding of the Dao Primal symbols.

It was an experience completely incomparable to forcibly probing memories with an Immortal Formation.

Soul searching and memory extraction, especially with numerous restrictions still remaining within Qin Zhuang’s soul, could not provide such an immersive experience.

He could only sense what Qin Zhuang had once learned and how much time he had spent on it.

But through the Azure Firmament Pearl, all of Qin Zhuang’s thoughts and insights concerning these Dao Primal symbols during his lifetime suddenly replayed themselves in Li Fan’s mind.

It was as though Li Fan had assumed Qin Zhuang’s identity and experienced his life all over again.

By the time Li Fan’s consciousness returned to clarity, he had already mastered [Thirty Lectures on the Fundamentals of Dao Primal].

In fact, combined with Li Fan’s own understanding, he was even somewhat more proficient than Qin Zhuang, a member of the Immortal Hunters Organization.

This feeling of obtaining something without any effort was truly intoxicating. Li Fan could not help but smack his lips.

“With such a supreme treasure in hand, I almost wish I could immediately go outside the High Wall and sweep everything clean. It would surely be equivalent to hundreds of years of painstaking cultivation.”

A dangerous glint appeared in Li Fan’s eyes.

But in the end, he firmly suppressed it.

Risk was one consideration.

More importantly, Li Fan could not yet determine whether obtaining insights through this heaven-defying method using the Azure Firmament Pearl had any side effects.

The changes within Li Fan’s soul could still, to a certain extent, undergo [Truth] and be cleansed.

If the changes were few, they could perhaps be ignored.

But if they accumulated, and even one or two uses of [Truth] were unable to wash them away, then the situation would become extremely dangerous and completely not worth the cost.

“First, I should use a large number of test subjects to conduct safety verification.”

“Moreover, theoretically speaking, if it can capture the thoughts of a corpse from before its death, then it certainly cannot be limited to cultivation techniques alone.”

“I wonder if there is some method capable of restoring all the memories and thoughts a corpse possessed during its lifetime?”

“If that were truly possible, then even if I created or replicated a cultivator out of thin air, it could resemble the original even more closely.”

Li Fan toyed with the Azure Firmament Pearl, which had returned to its original appearance, his thoughts churning endlessly.

...

The remaining four Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association arrived as summoned on time.

Without alerting anyone else, they were personally received by Emperor Sanmo and Qiao Sidao, who brought them into the Returning Ruins World where Dharma-Transmitter Jiang was located.

Because of the blockade of the Heaven-Communing Grand Formation, they naturally could not sense the presence of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang at the core of the formation.

However, Li Fan deliberately released a portion of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang’s insights and transmitted them to the four.

Thus, the moment they entered this small world, they became immersed in the wondrous state of enlightenment known as “The Great Dao Is Like Me.”

Especially Zhao Ruoxi.

“Eternal Sole Self” and “The Great Dao Is Like Me” were actually connected at their core.

However, “Eternal Sole Self” achieved ultimate unity with the Dao through continuous devouring.

“The Great Dao Is Like Me,” on the other hand, directly skipped the countless steps in between and reached the endpoint: I am the Dao, and the Dao is me.

Even though Li Fan had already thoroughly tempered Zhao Ruoxi, causing her mentality to undergo a transformation, she could not help but feel a sense of imbalance and jealousy at this moment.

The same was true of Zhao Ruoxi. The reactions of the other three Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns, as well as Emperor Sanmo, Qiao Sidao, and the others, were not much better.

“I originally thought that the [Taijia Divine Art] the senior used in the Immortal Alliance to inspire others, the one capable of communicating with the Star Sea, was already an art beyond imagination. I never expected that directly facing the Great Dao would actually be...” Qiao Sidao was so shocked that he became speechless, remaining silent for a long time.

With [Great Dao Is Like Me], one personally felt as though they had conversed with the Great Dao and comprehended many things. Yet once they left this state and returned to reality, it seemed as though nothing had happened at all. Not even the slightest trace of their comprehension of the Great Dao remained.

When Li Fan withdrew his merciful reward, the expressions on the faces of the several people immediately revealed their disappointment.

“How was it?”

At this moment, Li Fan appeared and asked.

The expressions of the several people became solemn. They first bowed respectfully, then one after another began praising it, their words overflowing with admiration.

“I intend to connect this comprehension of the Great Dao to your Dreamland Paradise.”

After everyone had finished speaking, Li Fan once again said calmly.

His words caused everyone's eyelids to twitch violently.

Especially Carefree.

After the initial, uncontrollable delight, what surged within Carefree’s heart was an overwhelming sense of fear.

Such an incredible stroke of luck falling from the heavens and landing on her...

Could this True Immortal have once again devised some new method of tormenting people?

Carefree had learned quite a lot from Zhao Ruoxi over the past few days.

Just as Carefree was at a loss for how to respond, Li Fan spoke again.

“Within dreams, there is neither life nor death.”

“Cultivators who are utterly unafraid of death are the most suitable vanguard to explore beyond the High Wall.”

“But there is also one drawback. Their strength is too weak, and their cultivation increases too slowly.”

Li Fan smiled.

“If they are too weak, then no matter how many ants there are, they will never amount to anything.”

“But if the ants become hyenas, that is different.”

“In Carefree’s dreams, strength is manifested solely on the level of consciousness. Through this method of directly facing the Great Dao, which is almost equivalent to an empowerment, ordinary mortal cultivators can be directly elevated to the Longevity Immortal realm within a short period of time.”

“Moreover, this can be quantitatively controlled.”

Li Fan spoke softly, but to the several people present, his words were no different from a thunderclap.

They still harbored faint doubts, but the various methods Li Fan had displayed recently made it impossible for them not to believe him.

“Take this divine soul as an example.”

Li Fan opened his palm. A point of light floated within it.

It was precisely a promising talent Li Fan had personally selected from the [Era].

With a thought, all the transformed insights of [Heaven-Communing, the Great Dao Is Like Me] were poured into this divine soul.

Although it remained incomparably weak, the divine soul’s theoretical understanding expanded at an unimaginably terrifying speed.

It was not merely the insights of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang himself.

There were also the thoughts and ideas Li Fan had accumulated over many years.

Everything except the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood had been recorded within the Heaven-Communing Grand Formation.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1431: Paradise Descends Upon Xuanhuang

Under normal circumstances, an ordinary cultivator's divine ability would be unable to withstand the enhancement brought about by comprehending the Dao at such an astonishing speed.

For them, "Hearing the Dao in the Morning, one may die without regret in the evening" was their destined fate.

But at this moment, the Returning Ruins Realm was under Li Fan's complete control.

Life and death were no longer decided by Heaven, but by Li Fan!

A mysterious and unfathomable power descended, carving and strengthening the divine soul in Li Fan's palm.

The point of light became increasingly crystalline and brilliant. The aura emanating from it also became more and more astonishing.

Unlike everyone else present, Li Fan could see a string of numbers floating above the divine soul light in his palm.

It was precisely one of the formations of the Primordial Mystic Spirit Grand Formation, [Astral Mechanism, All Spirits Return to Numbers], which was quantifying the degree of enhancement of the divine soul.

The number rapidly climbed from 23, and only after reaching 80 did its rate of growth begin to slow.

"Without the [Infinite] attribute of immortal spiritual power, even with the many phantoms of Xuanhuang Realm power within my body, it is not enough for me to use the [Desolate Fall, Flowers Bloom in an Empty Room] formation without restraint. But there is no problem using All Spirits Return to Numbers."

"Still, to be approaching the Longevity Immortal realm and only barely reaching a strength of 90..."

Li Fan could not help but glance at the top of his own head.

"9999."

A hundred times the strength of the Longevity Immortal realm.

Yet this was not Li Fan's actual combat strength. It was merely the maximum strength that the All Spirits Return to Numbers formation could quantify.

"If a True Immortal descended into the formation, I am afraid it could only temporarily be represented by a row of question marks."

The Heaven-Communing Grand Formation combined with the All Spirits Return to Numbers formation could use numerical values to measure the strength of cultivators within an illusion. It could even transmit comprehension of the Great Dao to cultivators in the form of "experience points."

This would make it possible for large numbers of Longevity Immortal realm combatants to be born within the Dreamland Paradise.

The difference between the Dreamland Paradise and an illusion was that an illusion could be freely fabricated and distorted at little cost. However, it was almost impossible for things within an illusion to descend into the real world.

The Dreamland Paradise, on the other hand, was built upon reality. As long as sufficient energy was provided, everything within the dream could rapidly manifest in the real world.

"Let us set aside the True Immortal level for now. If I forcibly connect it to the power of the Infinite Star Sea, then in a single night, countless combatants at the Longevity Immortal realm and below could be summoned into reality."

"It is already extremely close to the Fallen Immortal Realm."

Within the Fallen Immortal Realm existed countless worlds that were almost indistinguishable from reality, yet also seemed like illusions. Among them were many cultivators of the Longevity Immortal realm.

Since objects from the Fallen Immortal Realm could be brought into reality, then theoretically, cultivators from the Fallen Immortal Realm should be able to do so as well.

Yet throughout history, only the consciousnesses of cultivators from the Xuanhuang Realm had traveled to the Fallen Immortal Realm. There had never been any reports of cultivators from the Fallen Immortal Realm breaking through the barrier between reality and illusion and descending into the Xuanhuang Realm.

Li Fan believed this should be a restriction imposed by the True Immortal who had originally created the Fallen Immortal Realm.

"If someone were to achieve the True Immortal realm within the Fallen Immortal Realm, perhaps they could cross this restriction."

"Unfortunately, that is almost impossible."

Li Fan, who had gone through countless hardships just to prove the Half-Immortal Dao, naturally understood how difficult this was. At the very least, over the next several thousand years, such a figure would not appear within the Xuanhuang Fallen Immortal Realm.

The Fallen Immortal Realm formed from the lingering thoughts of a True Immortal, the Dark Sea formed from the corpse of a True Immortal, and the will to survive of a True Immortal sleeping deep within the Dark Sea all still held tremendous significance for Li Fan.

After all, perhaps the opportunity to undergo another transformation and achieve the True Immortal realm was hidden within them.

Although achieving the True Immortal realm might attract pursuit from forces beyond the High Wall, Li Fan could not shrink back and abandon his path forward because of this.

Moreover, he had the assistance of [Truth].

At worst, he could first prove himself a True Immortal and personally experience what the True Immortal realm was truly like. If the situation was indeed critical and the circumstances of True Immortals were perilous, he could simply return to his Half-Immortal anchor point and begin again.

As Li Fan pondered, the divine soul in his palm, judging purely by strength, was already no weaker than Zhao Ruoxi.

Such an astonishing method naturally left everyone present deeply convinced.

Carefree, however, was somewhat worried. She asked softly, "If so many powerful beings suddenly appear within the Dreamland Paradise, I am afraid I will be unable to maintain it..."

Before she could finish, Li Fan interrupted her.

"Do not worry. With my assistance, I will naturally ensure that the Dreamland Paradise remains safe and sound."

Carefree's heart sank.

She knew that from now on, the authority over the Dreamland Paradise would probably no longer belong to her.

But she also understood that this might likewise be an enormous opportunity for her.

Her strength was closely connected to the scale of the Dreamland Paradise. Even if control were forcibly transferred, the Paradise would inevitably continue to expand under the management of a True Immortal, and it would certainly return tremendous benefits to her.

As her thoughts stirred, Carefree could only silently thank Senior True Immortal for his grace.

...

The transformation of Carefree's Dreamland Paradise was absolutely a colossal undertaking.

Most importantly, the safety of the billions upon billions of living beings already residing within the Paradise had to be ensured.

Under Li Fan's orders, Emperor Sanmo and several other Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association entered different regions of the Dreamland Paradise respectively to oversee the integration of the formations.

As for Carefree, she opened her mind completely and allowed Li Fan to manipulate it as he pleased.

Following the Ink Death Dao Annihilation Aspect that had been implanted earlier and had already seeped silently into every corner of the Dreamland Paradise, Li Fan connected the power of the combined [Heaven Connecting, The Grand Dao Is Like Me] and [Astral Mechanism, All Spirits Return to Numbers] formation into the dream.

Using several Longevity Immortal cultivators as nodes, the formation's power soon took root completely within the Paradise.

The Dissociation Disc's immortal spiritual power slowly seeped through. Through the parasitic formation, it began transforming the Dreamland Paradise.

With every portion transformed, Li Fan's authority over the Dreamland Paradise increased.

Although Carefree Heavenly Sovereign was the original creator of the Paradise, she could now do nothing about the transfer of her creation.

She could even vaguely sense that she was still able to obtain benefits through her faint connection with the Paradise because Senior True Immortal had shown mercy and had not completely severed this connection.

How could Carefree know that the reason Li Fan had retained her connection with the Paradise was actually because he intended to use her as a scapegoat at a critical moment?

After all, exploring beyond the High Wall in this life might attract the attention of some peerless existence, who could follow the Dreamland Paradise and come looking for him.

With Carefree as the nominal owner on the surface, the brief buffer of time this provided would allow Li Fan to escape calmly instead of being forced into such a sorry state.

The process of taking over the Dreamland Paradise seemed to be proceeding extremely smoothly.

Through the Dissociation Disc's perception, Li Fan could see a layer of illusory light bands resembling a thin veil floating within the Darkest Star Sea.

It intertwined with reality without overlapping it.

From time to time, wisps of light were drawn from reality and flew toward the Dreamland Paradise. Within the light bands, a new thread would immediately emerge.

That was a new dream taking shape.

Seated within Xuanhuang, Li Fan's consciousness traveled rapidly with the aid of the Dreamland Paradise.

It was even several times faster than the escape technique of the Wooden Sword phantom.

In an instant, he arrived at the edge of the Star Sea, beneath the High Wall.

It was completely different from the formless and intangible scene visible through normal perception.

What Li Fan saw now was actually a dim, curved boundary line dividing the space inside and outside the High Wall.

The boundary line still gave him an insurmountable feeling.

But now that it had taken on a tangible form, crossing the High Wall no longer seemed impossible.

"So this is the High Wall's restriction. It is relatively weak against consciousness."

"No wonder the Five Elders Association members are so determined to use Carefree's Paradise to escape beyond the High Wall."

Li Fan observed it from close range. After a long while, a sudden sense of unease welled up within him.

Previously, whenever he probed the High Wall in reality, he would be subjected to the pressure of the High Wall. More importantly, comprehending the principles of the High Wall carried the characteristic of "once lost, lost forever."

Yet in the Dream Paradise, this characteristic did not exist.

Li Fan pondered deeply, remaining motionless for a long time.

As he gazed at the dim line not far away, an inexplicable thought suddenly surfaced in his mind.

"Perhaps it is because only thoughts exist within the Dreamland Paradise, and they are not complete."

"The High Wall is like a mirror, reflecting oneself."

"The state in which one observes the High Wall determines what one sees and perceives."

Li Fan then recalled how a mere High Wall could completely block the probing of existences such as the Darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai.

"The realm of the High Wall is also a one-way mirror."

As he gazed at it, Li Fan suppressed his growing impulse to cross the High Wall within the Dreamland Paradise.

Based on Mo Rubin and the Heavenly Doctor's previous comprehension of the High Wall in reality, Li Fan had already vaguely guessed that the High Wall might be like a Dragon Gate.

Although to the living beings within the wall, it was an insurmountable natural barrier, to those capable of crossing it, it likewise represented tremendous benefits.

With a thought, he crossed thousands of mountains and rivers in an instant and returned to the Xuanhuang Realm.

The transformation of the Dreamland Paradise had already been initially completed.

Next, it was time to open more access points.

Previously, because of Carefree's own limitations in strength, as well as the deterrence provided by the existence of the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor, only living beings residing in a small portion of the territories under the Five Elders Association could enter the Dreamland Paradise.

But now, under Li Fan's instructions, the illusory band of light floating above the Xuanhuang Realm radiated thousands upon thousands of tendrils of light.

They spread throughout the entire Xuanhuang Realm, as well as several affiliated small worlds and Cave Heavens.

The illusory light and shadows flickered in and out of existence. At this moment, countless living beings were drawn into dreams.

As they fell asleep, their physical bodies in reality were immediately swallowed by the Dreamland Paradise and temporarily stored there.

What moved about within the Paradise were entirely new bodies of consciousness, fabricated and created according to their souls.

There were quite a few cultivators among them, but compared with the enormous number of mortals, their numbers were insignificant.

Compared with before, the greatest change in the updated Dreamland Paradise was that every region now had a gigantic city floating in the sky. It was an enhanced version of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance Heavenly City. Training, resting, trading, and many other functions were all available. One could even actively choose a dream region.

New arrivals found themselves dazzled by everything before their eyes.

For the old players of the Dreamland Paradise, the greatest surprise of this update was that there was no longer any penalty for death. No matter how many times they died within the dream world, they would respawn in the "main city."

Moreover, compared with the cultivation method of the past, which was the same as in reality, the dream world now contained "Dao Comprehension Points."

By completing dream quests and using various other methods, one could obtain Dao Comprehension Points. Using these points, one could directly increase one's cultivation and realm.

The speed at which cultivators could now grow was completely incomparable to the past.

Furthermore, according to the announcement, a batch of entirely new dream worlds would soon be opened. Those who obtained gains first could even receive additional rewards.

...

With the injection of so many new elements, the Dreamland Paradise presented a flourishing scene.

In reality, however, Carefree Heavenly Sovereign was not doing nearly as well.

Even with Li Fan's transformation of the dream world, which expanded its capacity by nearly a hundredfold in an instant, it was still an enormous burden that Carefree could hardly endure.

If not for the protection of the Xuanhuang Realm's phantom bestowed upon her by Li Fan, Carefree would probably have been crushed beneath the weight of her own creations in an instant.

Now, in order to keep her mind from scattering, Carefree had already reverted to an infant state and fallen into deep slumber.

Only her tightly furrowed brows and the occasional sound of her cries revealed her suffering.

Everything has its advantages and disadvantages.

If Carefree bore the vast majority of the hardship of opening up the dream world, then Li Fan enjoyed almost all of its benefits.

As the true master of the Dreamland Paradise, Li Fan was able to observe such a vast number of living beings from close range.

Their every action, their joys and sorrows, and every fluctuation of their emotions.

Li Fan could perceive all of it with extraordinary clarity.

He gradually developed a deeper understanding of living beings known as "humans."

If, previously, because of the [Truth] reincarnations carried out with a single thought, all living beings in the world had gradually become meaningless symbols and designations in Li Fan's eyes.

Then now, through his close observation within the Dreamland Paradise, these "symbols" in Li Fan's eyes had once again become vivid and lifelike.

They were still symbols, but they had become much more distinct.

Li Fan realized that this more concrete and practical understanding was of tremendous help to his comprehension of the [Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood].

Ultimately, if one wished to turn the false into the true, or transform the true into the false, one first needed to possess a complete understanding of the essence of things.

Although Li Fan could rely on the great power of [Truth] to successfully perform the Transformation Between Truth and Falsey without satisfying this prerequisite.

Yet if Li Fan himself could comprehend the essence of things, perhaps the effort "saved" through [Truth] could help him accomplish even more.

With this thought in mind, Li Fan divided his consciousness into countless thoughts and immersed his mind within the Dreamland Paradise.

Exploring beyond the High Wall was not something that could be accomplished in a day or two. Given the Xuanhuang Realm's current strength, it still needed to accumulate strength for some time.

The countdown to the arrival of the World Annihilation Calamity steadily ticked downward.

In the blink of an eye, ten years passed.

The existence of these two things, the absence of death and Dao Comprehension Points, allowed the cultivators within the Dreamland Paradise to increase their strength at an extremely terrifying rate.

In just ten short years, the Paradise had approached collapse more than thirty times.

Li Fan personally intervened several times before barely managing to maintain the stability of the dream world.

After dozens of iterations, the Dreamland Paradise of today had completely filled the space within the High Wall.

Some of its vanguard tendrils had even already broken through the High Wall.

Although this was only on the level of consciousness, the stark difference between inside and outside the High Wall still greatly surprised Li Fan.

Simply put, within the dream world outside the High Wall, the "Dao Comprehension Points" that the Heaven-Communing Grand Formation could draw upon were generally more than five or six times those within the High Wall!
