Next

Chapter 1432: The Great Dao Manifests Its Form

The so called Dao Comprehension Point in the Dreamland Paradise was actually the Dao insights obtained by Dharma-Transmitter Jiang through the characteristic of [The Great Dao Is Like Me].

There was merely a wall separating them.

The efficiency of Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's Dao comprehension had not changed. What had changed was the environment itself. Simply put, cultivation outside the High Wall was generally many times easier than cultivation within the High Wall.

Just as Li Fan had previously speculated, as long as one could embark on the path of cultivation through one's own ability, even an ordinary cultivator could be considered a heavenly prodigy outside the High Wall!

And this was only the beginning.

Li Fan keenly noticed that after coming into contact with the environment outside the High Wall, Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's Dao comprehension efficiency within the Heaven-Communing Grand Formation was slowly increasing.

His long term comprehension of the Great Dao had finally begun to nourish him in return.

Li Fan synchronized with the other's sensations. The Heaven-Communing Great Dao that he looked up at from below seemed to gradually change from gray and white into something vibrant and colorful.

"Is this the difference between the land where the Great Dao is annihilated and a normal environment?" Even Li Fan was momentarily stunned by this magnificent sight.

"Outside the High Wall is so beautiful that it makes one's heart tremble. Yet, paradoxically, it makes one afraid to approach."

A voice sounded beside Li Fan's ear, interrupting his thoughts.

Li Fan turned his head and looked at the blue figure beside him.

It was Chen Tianhai of the Taiyan Sect!

"I can feel that the influence of the demonic voice on me seems to have intensified again. My past memories are becoming clearer. Even though I have personally witnessed the appearance of Dharma-transmitting Immortal Ancestor being suppressed. Even though I already know that those memories were merely altered."

"Yet that glimpse from back then is still like a nightmare, haunting me relentlessly." Chen Tianhai's figure trembled uncontrollably.

Li Fan did not respond. Instead, he tried silently reciting the Scripture Chanting Demonic Sound in his heart.

It was as if an invisible force pierced through the Dreamland Paradise, establishing a faint connection between him and the world beyond the High Wall.

The mysterious melodies became much clearer.

Li Fan's heart tightened. As a counterbalance, he immediately began silently reciting the Immortal Scripture of Protection originating from the Grand Mage.

"The Dao is born, passed down since primordial antiquity. Through sorrow and hardship, the Dao is extinguished."

...

The two voices resonated with each other, and the influence of the Scripture Chanting Demonic Sound was immediately diluted considerably. But compared with simply being within the High Wall, the harmonizing effect of the Immortal Scripture of Protection was clearly much weaker.

It was gradually becoming impossible to compare with that mysterious and unfathomable demonic voice.

"Xuanyuan Hong..." Li Fan's expression became inscrutable.

Back then, Immortal Ancestor had sought survival in the midst of death. He had relied precisely on the power of that whispering voice, which seemed to be innate to the Xuanyuan Clan, to influence and alter the memories of Chen Tianhai of the Taiyan Sect, who had merely met him once.

Relying on the deductions of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, he found a method to kill many Longevity Immortal cultivators of the Ten Great Immortal Sects.

Although Immortal Ancestor had been suppressed by Li Fan, the mark of that whispering voice left within Chen Tianhai had not disappeared.

When Chen Tianhai also came outside the High Wall with the power of the Dreamland Paradise, its influence erupted once again. Even though Chen Tianhai had already become informationalized and had become part of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, he still could not escape it.

"Part of this is because the Great Dao has become more distinct. The profound sounds that depict the Great Dao through musical tones have naturally been strengthened as well. If one were to write True Immortal seal script, its might would presumably also be amplified several times over."

"But that is not the only reason."

"The extent of its enhancement is far too great. Both are paths of musical tones, yet the Immortal Scripture of Protection has never been strengthened to such an exaggerated degree..."

The faint whispering beside his ear appeared and disappeared intermittently.

After thinking it over again and again, Li Fan could only attribute it to that missing Xuantian King.

Five years ago, when Li Fan went to the Taiyan Sect and met Chen Tianhai.

With a single sentence, he had exposed the truth behind the demonic voice corroding him.

At first, Chen Tianhai had refused to believe that his memories had always been altered by someone else. It was not until Li Fan imparted the Immortal Scripture of Protection and used it to counteract the force of the alteration.

After recovering his true memories, Chen Tianhai had been struck as if by lightning and remained silent for a long time.

"It is not that you were inadequate. How could a mortal possibly be immune to the methods of immortals?"

Only Li Fan's words had allowed Chen Tianhai to truly free himself from the suffering of his past.

Naturally, the reason Li Fan had gone to the Taiyan Sect was for that Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

After helping Chen Tianhai break free from the nightmare that had tormented him for ten thousand years, Li Fan had used a combination of intimidation and temptation to make the Taiyan Sect submit.

Now, this Dao Deviation Stone Mother was within Li Fan's sea of consciousness.

However, after studying it for some time, Li Fan did not choose to directly fuse it with the supreme-grade Dao Deviation Stone in his mind.

Back then, after the entire Taiyan Sect had chosen to become informationalized and merge into the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, countless thoughts and consciousnesses had fused with the Stone Mother and become extremely difficult to separate. They had become inherent characteristics of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother itself.

Li Fan had no confidence that he could erase these thoughts without damaging the Stone Mother.

If he were to devour it directly...

The strength of the Stone Mother was countless times greater than that of the supreme-grade Dao Deviation Stone.

The thoughts of the Taiyan Sect's members within the Stone Mother were even more likely to contaminate the Dao Deviation Stone possessed by Li Fan.

Although Li Fan could forcibly remove these thoughts afterward.

Deep within the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, however, there was still a rather peculiar consciousness hidden and dormant, one that even Li Fan, as a Half Immortal, found quite troublesome.

Moreover, now that Li Fan had mastered the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, he no longer needed to crudely devour it in order to increase his strength.

Around the Dao Deviation Stone Mother in his sea of consciousness.

Countless threads flew in from the void, touching, analyzing, and comprehending the essence of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

And deep within the sea of consciousness, in a place isolated from the other regions.

Another crystal, exactly identical in appearance to the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, was silently floating there. However, it seemed to exist in an ethereal, mist like state, flickering indistinctly.

It was precisely the Dao Deviation Stone Mother that Li Fan had "copied" through the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood.

Having already successfully recreated Ancient Cang Primal Essence, his own cultivation, and even the Azure Firmament Pearl, Li Fan had originally thought that recreating this Dao Deviation Stone Mother would also be effortless.

But what he had not expected was that recreating this crystal was even dozens of times more difficult than recreating the immortal artifact Azure Firmament Pearl.

When recreating the Azure Firmament Pearl, he only needed to have three times his cultivation added to himself. Although it was somewhat strenuous, he could ultimately succeed.

But when facing the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, brute force seemed to have lost its effectiveness.

After several failures, Li Fan came to understand that it was not because his own strength was insufficient. Rather, his understanding of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother had not yet reached the minimum requirement needed to recreate it.

This was a first for Li Fan.

In the past, when he used the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, he had never needed to meet any requirements regarding his understanding of the essence of an object.

However, after carefully comprehending it and communicating with [Truth] through considerable difficulty, Li Fan ultimately understood why.

To put it simply, all things that Li Fan could see completely at a glance could be temporarily recreated through the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood.

This principle still held true for the Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

The reason his attempts to recreate it had failed was that Li Fan had not been able to see the Stone Mother "in its entirety."

This was also the reason Li Fan had sealed the Stone Mother within his sea of consciousness, observing it day and night without pause.

He had opened up a separate region, so the Stone Mother could not spy on his memories.

At the same time, the people of the Taiyan Sect could leave Li Fan's sea of consciousness from this region at any time and enter the real world.

Now, Li Fan was already capable of reproducing the Dao Deviation Stone Mother. By continuously applying the stacking method and strengthening the Dao Deviation Stone within his sea of consciousness, he had naturally also managed to see the Stone Mother in its entirety.

Through his communication with the Stone Mother during this period, Li Fan also understood why, despite being a Half Immortal, he had been unable to "see" the Stone Mother's complete form at a single glance.

"Dao Deviation Stone Mother, Dao Deviation Stone Mother."

With a thought from Li Fan, the illusory Stone Mother he had reproduced transformed into a mass of radiance and attached itself to the Dao Deviation Stone.

He circulated the improved Dao Transformation Divine Unity Secret Technique, rapidly absorbing the incomparably pure energy within it.

The illusory Stone Mother reproduced by Li Fan contained none of the Taiyan Sect's consciousness. It did not even contain the mysterious consciousness hidden within the true Stone Mother. It was merely the Stone Mother itself in its purest form. Therefore, it could be absorbed without restraint.

Although each Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood left behind only a tiny amount of residue, the Dao Deviation Stone Mother was simply too miraculous. Every time he used it, the quality of the Dao Deviation Stone within Li Fan's sea of consciousness could increase severalfold.

Within ten years, Li Fan had already completed nearly a hundred cycles of reproduction and stacking.

At this moment, the Dao Deviation Stone in his mind was already not much weaker than the real Stone Mother.

Thus, he had also developed a vague conjecture regarding the essence of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

This extraordinary treasure from beyond the heavens might actually be a direct manifestation of the Great Dao itself!

It was the same principle as the Primeval Transformation of the demon beast race.

However, Primeval Transformation represented only a single Great Dao. As for the Dao Deviation Stone Mother...

Li Fan guessed that it might even be a collection of all complete Great Daos!

It was just that the possibility from which the Dao Deviation Stone Mother originated was far weaker than the one Li Fan was familiar with.

Furthermore, having endured the Dao Annihilation Calamity and weathered the baptism of crossing through different possibilities.

The Stone Mother had therefore been further weakened.

Thus, even though it was a manifestation of complete Great Daos, in terms of might, it could not even compare to an ordinary Nameless True Immortal.

Of course, all of the above were merely Li Fan's speculations based on his own insights while reproducing the Stone Mother.

The only memories remaining within the Dao Deviation Stone Mother consisted of seemingly endless, desperate flight to evade some kind of calamity, before it eventually arrived in the Xuanhuang Realm by a stroke of fate. It was subsequently discovered by the Taiyan Sect.

Li Fan speculated that the reason the Dao Deviation Stone Mother had severed itself completely from its past was precisely to evade the pursuit of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Although the Stone Mother had lost its memories of the past, from the terrifying calamity it sensed, Li Fan could still detect traces of the ominous aura of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Li Fan had considerable confidence in his deduction. After all, within the entire mortal world, what could possibly force a crystallized body of a complete Great Dao to flee in such a panic?

And the Dao Deviation Stone Mother had arrived in the Xuanhuang Realm by "coincidence."

This reminded Li Fan once again of the scene he had seen in the memories of a True Immortal.

"This Xuanhuang Realm must still possess some extraordinary mysteries that I have yet to discover."

"It is capable of attracting numerous strange existences to cross through spacetime, and even traverse different possibilities, to arrive here."

"Even my own transmigration..."

The discovery that the Dao Deviation Stone Mother had come from another possibility also caused Li Fan to develop some doubts regarding his own transmigration.

Although he had no particular nostalgia for the days before his transmigration, nor did he have any strong desire to return.

What Li Fan cared about was the crucial question of whether the awakening of [Truth] had occurred within the body of this possibility, meaning that he had merely occupied another person's body.

Or whether it had occurred within himself.

As he continued to develop and utilize the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, Li Fan's horizons and state of mind had undergone an extremely subtle change compared with before.

In the past, whenever Li Fan encountered rare treasures of the mortal world, he would inevitably develop the desire to make them his own. Sometimes it was greed, and sometimes it was simply to increase his own ability to preserve his life.

But now, this desire to possess things had gradually faded.

What he thought of was merely analyzing and understanding.

After all, with the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood in his possession, as long as he wished, he could transform any treasure into countless copies at any time.

Now, the only thing Li Fan truly wanted to possess.

Was [Truth] itself.

...

His myriad thoughts came to an end along with the fusion of yet another illusory Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

Li Fan only felt that his thoughts were now operating much faster.

His attention once again returned to the mysterious and unfathomable demonic voice beside his ears.

After Li Fan's reminder, based on the Immortal Ancestor's memories from the past, the Stone Mother had repeatedly reviewed the memories countless times and reached a conclusion.

The lingering whispering voice should have been the work of the Xuantian King.

But it was not a contingency left behind in an attempt to resurrect after death.

Rather, it was a natural change produced in his bloodline descendants after the source of their bloodline had entered an inconceivable state.

One piece of evidence was that the influence of the demonic voice on the Xuanyuan Clan had grown increasingly stronger.

It reached its peak with Xuanyuan Sheng.

Xuanyuan Sheng simply could not withstand it any longer and wanted to sever this influence completely.

Yet he still could not prevent the demonic voice from appearing within Xuanyuan Hong.

And after arriving beyond the High Wall, the demonic voice became even stronger.

The Dao Deviation Stone Mother's deduction seemed to have received confirmation from another angle.

"After the Xuantian King mysteriously disappeared back then, he not only did not die, but instead advanced even further?"

This conjecture caused Li Fan's thoughts to run wild.

After seeing the Darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai, there had always been a thought in Li Fan's mind that seemed ready to emerge.

Yet he had never been able to understand what it was.

Only after the Dao Deviation Stone Mother's conjecture appeared did this thought finally take root and sprout, ultimately surfacing in his mind.

The Darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai both came from an extremely special possibility.

Then could the possibility Li Fan currently inhabited have likewise given birth to an unknowable powerhouse capable of crossing between possibilities?

If such a possibility truly existed.

Then, among the history Li Fan currently knew, the person who most resembled someone holding the protagonist's script might be that Xuantian King.

After all, he was the final ascender before the destruction of the Immortal Realm.

His legend was simply too extraordinary.

"Perhaps beyond the High Wall, there are other figures even more heaven defying."

"But regardless, someone whose methods can cross the High Wall and traverse the past and present cannot be left without precautions."

"The stronger they are, the more likely they are to know of the existence of [Truth]."

When Li Fan comes into contact with such people, he must be even more careful to conceal himself.

"I wonder whether the Xuantian King still has any other bloodline descendants within the Xuanhuang Realm.”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1433: Original Possibility

“Times have changed. The Xuanyuan Village of those years has long since ceased to exist.”

“Even the [Reveal] Character seal script failed to obtain any clues from the Immortal Ancestor…”

“Perhaps, if I want to investigate the Xuantian King’s current situation, I still need to start with his childhood sweetheart.”

Within Li Fan’s sea of consciousness, countless points of light suddenly flashed.

Within every point of light, countless images rapidly flickered. Like a star sea, the countless points of light simulated different possible events of the future.

Yet this immense amount of deduction did not hinder the operation of Li Fan’s thoughts in the slightest.

This was one of the benefits brought about by the upgrade of the Dao Deviation Stone within his own sea of consciousness.

Its computational power seemed practically infinite. Even if he directed everything he had learned in the past toward deducing Immortal techniques, while simultaneously deducing the future course of this life, synchronizing and analyzing everything that happened within the Dreamland Paradise, and conducting an overall analysis...

Even with all of this combined, Li Fan still felt completely at ease.

He even developed a feeling of frustration, as though he possessed immense divine power but had nowhere to use it.

He had once used the Taiyan Sect’s [Inverse Calamity], a small game based on reversing chaos into order and disorder into order, as a test for himself.

Compared to the 49,999 sheets of paper left by Yi Yin in his tomb, the number of possibilities was ten thousand times greater.

The difficulty was worlds apart.

Yet after only a single breath of time, Li Fan had solved it without the slightest pressure.

With the computational power of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, problems involving permutations and combinations could no longer obstruct Li Fan, no matter how enormous their fundamental scale was.

...

As he contemplated various matters, Li Fan said to Chen Tianhai, “Hasn’t the Taiyan Sect always wanted to see what lies beyond the High Wall? I can create independent bodies for anyone who has such an intention, allowing them to explore beyond the High Wall.”

Chen Tianhai was clearly tempted upon hearing this.

“Coming into contact with more variables beyond the High Wall will also help stimulate the hidden power of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother. Perhaps it may even awaken that consciousness deep within the Stone Mother,” Li Fan added.

Chen Tianhai quickly made up his mind.

The consciousness hidden within the Dao Deviation Stone Mother had not even been detected by the Taiyan Sect in the beginning. It was only after Li Fan pointed it out that the members of the Taiyan Sect, having merged with the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, were able to “see clearly” that hidden existence.

Relying on their mysterious connection with the Stone Mother, the people of the Taiyan Sect all believed that this consciousness could only benefit the Dao Deviation Stone Mother and would not harm it. It had merely fallen into a deep sleep for some unknown reason. If they could find a way to communicate with it, they might be able to uncover the mystery of the Stone Mother’s origin. It might even promote the Stone Mother’s further transformation.

Li Fan’s proposal was something the people of the Taiyan Sect simply could not refuse.

Chen Tianhai looked at the scenes from the Dreamland Paradise depicting the world beyond the High Wall.

In order to obtain as much authentic information about the world beyond the High Wall as possible, Li Fan had changed the way the dreams beyond the High Wall were generated. Everything was now generated based on the real environment, with fabricated and false elements eliminated.

After dozens of iterations, the Dreamland Paradise was no longer merely the Carefree Realm, or even a creation of Li Fan. It had become a collective derivative of the thoughts of countless living beings within the dreams.

Now, this enormous existence had already crossed the High Wall and was slowly exploring the world beyond it.

The Darkest Star Sea within the High Wall was merely a small portion of the Annihilation Land.

It was just that throughout most of the remaining regions, under the baptism of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, even the concept of “existence” had already disappeared. The tentacles of the Dreamland Paradise extended outward along the narrow passage beyond the High Wall.

Everywhere they passed was completely barren.

Yet when they looked back toward the location of the Darkest Star Sea, they could discover nothing at all. Even though a portion of the tentacles was still connected to the interior of the High Wall, they simply could not sense it.

This bizarre situation was as if a part of one’s own body had inexplicably vanished.

Li Fan controlled a portion of the dream tentacles to continue exploring toward the outskirts of the Annihilation Land, while allowing the rest to attempt to cross the High Wall and return to the Darkest Star Sea.

Because they could not sense the existence of the High Wall, they could only follow the traces of their own existence. In this way, they successfully groped their way toward the vicinity of the High Wall.

However, every other point was unable to enter or even discover the High Wall. Only by passing through the point where the dream itself had pierced through the High Wall, while remaining connected to both the interior and exterior, could they return inside the wall.

“The hidden and mysterious nature of the outer side of the High Wall truly far exceeds what we can see from within.”

Li Fan did not give up on exploring the High Wall.

Countless dream tentacles began spreading toward the outside world along the passage that had initially been opened.

Like creeping ivy, they densely occupied the area where the High Wall was located.

Although there was currently no way to completely lock onto the existence of the High Wall, the Dreamland Paradise contained the collective understanding of countless living beings, while the Dao Deviation Stone Mother provided assistance with the research. Li Fan believed that with gradual progress, they would eventually be able to completely unravel the secrets of the High Wall.

With the help of the Dreamland Paradise, Li Fan also sent a strand of his divine sense beyond the High Wall.

Since he was uncertain what he might encounter, the entire process was carried out with extreme caution.

Fortunately, no unexpected incident occurred. Li Fan’s Half-Immortal divine sense gazed toward the High Wall, seeing something different from what the beings within the dreams could perceive.

Where the dream tentacles could see nothing at all, Li Fan vaguely glimpsed a dark gray, sharp protrusion.

It seemed like a long, narrow cone, yet also seemed like nothing more than a line segment.

It quietly stretched across the void, piercing through space.

It seemed to be supporting something, yet also seemed to be connecting something.

“Is this the High Wall?”

Li Fan silently observed it, and a difficult-to-describe feeling suddenly arose within his heart.

He looked around.

The small region near the High Wall was the surviving land beneath the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Beyond this surviving land, existence itself had already disappeared.

In a daze, Li Fan seemed to see the terrifying scene from when the calamity had descended and the Great Dao had crushed everything beneath it.

Across the vast and boundless Star Sea, enormous regions of light successively went out without warning.

Darkness devoured the world.

The apocalypse descended.

Only the High Wall remained as a support, allowing the surrounding region to barely survive beneath the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

And even after ten thousand years, the High Wall still stood unmoving, maintaining its “existence.”

“If the High Wall were to collapse...”

According to Li Fan’s deductions, even if the Dao Annihilation Calamity never descended again, the possibilities of this region would gradually dissipate.

In the end, it would follow in the footsteps of the surrounding regions, becoming annihilated and disappearing.

Observing the ruins left behind by the destruction caused by the Dao Annihilation Calamity, Li Fan gained an even clearer understanding of this terrifying disaster that could annihilate the Immortal Realm and even encompass and destroy all possibilities of the mortal world.

“It does not descend continuously.”

“Instead, it attacks intermittently, like wave after wave.”

“The Immortal Realm stood at a higher level, so when the Dao Annihilation Calamity descended, it was the first to be affected.”

“Perhaps, when the Dao Annihilation Calamity first erupted back then, the Immortal Realm was still barely able to withstand it. But eventually, one of the most violent waves suddenly arrived and completely swallowed the entire Immortal Realm before it could react.”

“The Immortal Realm collapsed, and the disaster began spreading toward the lower realm.”

“After the great wave, it seemed to begin accumulating strength. The Dao Annihilation Calamity entered a low tide, and thus the lower realm star sea, whose strength was far inferior to that of the Immortal Realm, suffered only the destruction of a portion of its regions. A vast amount of life still remained.”

...

With sufficient computational power supporting him, Li Fan’s ability to “fill in the gaps” had also undergone a qualitative improvement.

By combining the clues he had learned in the past, he could reconstruct various possible truths from nothing more than the broken walls and ruins scattered throughout the Annihilation Land.

Li Fan had vaguely glimpsed the pattern of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

If this speculation is close to the truth, then perhaps the day when the possibility Li Fan currently inhabits is completely destroyed is not far away.

“For the Dao Annihilation Calamity, ten thousand years is by no means a long time, but it is certainly not a short time either.”

“Perhaps the black qi stirred up by falling from the highest point of the trend is a sign that the Dao Annihilation Calamity is approaching.”

“Just like ripples spreading across the surface of water. They only appear after something falls into the water.”

“The manifestation of the black qi means that the tide has begun to rise, and the water is spreading over.”

Li Fan pondered this, and a sudden sense of unease arose in his heart.

That invisible pressure seemed to have affected even True Immortals who had returned to the true state.

Not to mention the true Dao Annihilation Calamity, even the black qi of Dao Annihilation was something Li Fan was currently incapable of surviving within.

“Perhaps the power of the Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, can help mediate it to some extent.”

Li Fan did not know what had happened to this experimental ground in the Xuanhuang Realm back then. It was almost as if, overnight, everyone involved had either disappeared or died.

All that remained was a field of ruins.

“Perhaps the existence of the High Wall was an emergency protective measure created at the same time as this experimental ground was designed.”

“Only, as time passed, when the Dao Annihilation Calamity truly descended, the measure was indeed activated. Yet not a single one of the people involved remained.”

“All that was left were us experimental subjects, our minds filled with doubts and bewilderment.”

Li Fan gazed at the High Wall once more with a profound look. Moments later, his divine sense returned to his body.

With Li Fan's guidance, the dream tentacles now had a clear path to follow. They began spreading and growing along the High Wall without restraint.

Although he had now truly seen the High Wall and deduced its possible origin, Li Fan still knew very little about its principles and how to break through it.

He could only spend the time ahead slowly deciphering and analyzing it.

“An experimental ground of the Immortal Realm.”

“Pieces on the Immortal Domain Go Board.”

Images that Li Fan had once seen within the Immortal Domain Go pieces surfaced in his mind.

Black and white pieces stood side by side on the Go board.

The victor remained, while the defeated was removed from the game.

The Immortal Domain Go piece left behind by Sun Piaomiao was merely one of those that had failed and been discarded.

Yet even within such a failed piece, there existed an entity like the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar.

It was truly difficult to imagine what kind of foundation the Go pieces that ultimately remained on the board possessed.

“More importantly…”

“How could the Immortal Realm have only one experimental ground like the Xuanhuang Realm?”

This question suddenly emerged in Li Fan's mind.

From the glimpse of the Immortal Realm he had once caught, it appeared boundless.

It could genuinely be described as infinite.

As for the so called Immortal Domains, no one knew how many there were.

Even an unofficial force of the Immortal Realm like Sun Piaomiao had to leave behind numerous reserve Go pieces. It was difficult to imagine that if the vast Immortal Realm truly wanted to scheme toward some objective, it would rely on only a single operation.

“In the past, my horizons were simply too narrow.”

“Even after advancing to the Half Immortal realm, I only possessed the cultivation realm and status of one. Without undergoing the baptism of immortal spiritual qi, my level of thought was inevitably still inferior to that of a True Immortal.”

“But with the Dao Deviation Stone Mother as a supplement, it was like having enlightenment poured directly into my mind. Only then could my thoughts truly expand in all directions.”

“There may not be only one experimental ground in the lower realm star sea.”

“Thinking about it this way, the abandonment of the Xuanhuang Realm also makes perfect sense.”

Because the Dao Annihilation Calamity was raging, most of the domains had already been annihilated. Only a few ruins and broken remnants remained, supporting themselves within the Lands Of Annihilation.

They were like shallow sandbars floating upon a river.

“Even that flourishing region beyond the High Wall is probably nothing more than a somewhat larger island.”

“The starry sky is vast and boundless, obscured by countless lands of annihilation, making it impossible to see its original appearance. Thus, believing that the place one inhabits is the only place in existence is only natural.”

“But with the Dao Deviation Stone Mother in my possession, I can glimpse a corner and see the whole world…”

The complete panoramic view of the possibility in which Li Fan currently existed was finally being slowly filled in within his mind.

The scene depicted by this panorama was truly vast and boundless. Even the vast Darkest Star Sea was merely an insignificant point within it.

However, most regions within this panoramic view were constantly flickering between appearing and disappearing. This was because there was too little data at present to determine whether they truly existed.

Only the Darkest Star Sea within the High Wall, where Li Fan currently resided, remained brightly illuminated without ever extinguishing, regardless of how the light and shadows changed.

“The starry skies of all possibilities are able to shine because of my existence.”

“Let this possibility in which I reside be called the Yuanchu.”

Li Fan quietly thought this to himself.

Sooner or later, Li Fan's strength would become sufficient to leap into other possibilities.

There was no harm in establishing the identity of his own possibility in advance.

However, the most important task at present was still to make contact with the civilizations within the Lands of Annihilation.

Even with the living beings of the Xuanhuang Realm as his foundation, Li Fan still felt that the expansion of the dream tentacles was far too slow.

As early as ten years ago, Li Fan had already promulgated laws encouraging childbirth.

But after ten years, the increase in population was still extremely limited.

“In the short term, I cannot count on that. It seems I will have to find a way myself.”

Feeling the enormous amount of idle computing power within his sea of consciousness, a thought suddenly emerged in Li Fan's mind.

Back when he visited the [Era] apparatus in the Museum of Divine Treasures, Li Fan had already conceived an idea.

The countless worlds deduced by the Dao Deviation Stone were almost indistinguishable from reality.

If the Dao Deviation Stone itself continued to grow stronger, then sooner or later, each of these deduced worlds would become no different from real worlds.

“Perhaps it is precisely because the Stone Mother is the manifestation of all the Great Daos within a single possibility that it caused me to have such a thought.”

“In theory, every world that has ever appeared within the Stone Mother is completely reasonable and real.”

“It is like having already forged the molds. As long as they are filled, physical objects can be produced.”

“If my comprehension of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood advances another level, I would not even need to fabricate worlds myself. I could simply use the possibilities previously deduced by the Stone Mother to transform them from false into true. It would also save me a great deal of effort.”

Li Fan's eyes gleamed.

“But all I need are materials to build up the Dreamland Paradise. It does not matter if they do not exist in the real world.”

With this thought, within the Dao Deviation Stone in Li Fan's sea of consciousness, another Xuanhuang Realm began to evolve.

[T/N:- 原初 (Yuánchū), The core meaning is the original beginning or the primordial state at the origin.]

Chapter 1434: Immortal Hunters Hunt True Immortals

The Dao Deviation Stone in Li Fan's sea of consciousness finally began to feel the pressure.

Creating a world out of nothing, even with an actual existing world in reality as a template, was far more difficult than Li Fan had estimated.

The key to creation lay in living beings.

Creating countless individuals was not difficult.

What was difficult was creating individuals with their own "thoughts."

At first, Li Fan's thoughts had entered a wrong path.

He wanted to directly simulate and create within his own world a true consciousness belonging to living beings.

As a result, nearly half of the Dao Transformation Stone's computational power was consumed, yet it had only managed to give birth to three individuals.

Three thoughts possessing subjective agency drifted through the skies of the virtual Xuanhuang Realm.

These three individuals had completely separated themselves from the Dao Deviation Stone. Their thoughts could circulate without relying on the Dao Deviation Stone. However, the computational power they had consumed was very real.

The Dao Deviation Stone's performance had plummeted by half. If not for Li Fan's ability to transform the false into the true, allowing him to ignore such consumption, he might have abandoned the experiment on the spot because of the cost.

"The capabilities of these three thoughts alone already surpass mine in some respects. However, they are still in the earliest stage of their birth, in a state of ignorance and unconsciousness. Most of their computational power has yet to be activated..."

"If they were not all derived from the Dao Deviation Stone, I could erase their existence with a single thought. Keeping three such colossal beings in my sea of consciousness is indeed somewhat frightening."

Li Fan quietly gazed at the three sleeping thoughts that resembled infants.

Then his thoughts returned to the main issue.

"Under normal circumstances, creating living beings with self-awareness certainly would not require such a massive expenditure. I have directly achieved the Dao through sheer force. Although I have not comprehended the method of creation, I have used immense computational power to break through and ignore that unseen restriction."

"I have become attached to appearances. To develop the Dreamland Paradise, it is not necessary to create true living consciousness. I only need to add the command [Dreaming] to those simulated consciousnesses..."

After a brief pause in his thoughts, Li Fan began connecting the Dreamland Paradise to the simulated Xuanhuang Realm.

The Dao Deviation Stone shimmered, and streams of radiance representing thoughts rapidly flowed into the dreamscape.

At the same time, large numbers of living beings descended into the dreamscape.

Their behavior was no different from that of real people.

They communicated and interacted, bought and sold goods, and ventured through the dreamscape together. Everything ordinary cultivators could do, they could handle perfectly.

Moreover, with the data from the first batch of samples, the simulated thoughts of the living beings created by the Dao Deviation Stone began to iterate and evolve at an astonishing speed.

At first, they would occasionally appear somewhat wooden during conversations. Later, even Li Fan could barely distinguish between reality and illusion.

The operation of the simulated living beings did not require much computational power from the Dao Deviation Stone.

That was because all these living thoughts could be reduced to ninety-nine types of behavior.

The Dao Deviation Stone only needed to maintain these ninety-nine original templates, and it could use them like a lever to drive countless, boundless living beings.

"They look like countless different individuals, but at their core, they are merely ninety-nine people."

Having found the correct method, the simulated living thoughts increased exponentially, making even Li Fan feel slightly uneasy. Fortunately, after confirming once again that, with the Dao Deviation Stone in his mind, he possessed absolute control over them, the unease in his heart gradually faded.

The living beings within the virtual Xuanhuang Realm did not need to live in the real world. They were all connected to the Carefree Dreamland.

With these "Ghost Soldiers" joining the battle, the Dream tentacles' rate of exploration outward increased dramatically.

Li Fan then repeated the process.

While continuing to restore the Dao Deviation Stone through the accumulated power of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood, he also "copied" and created more simulated Xuanhuang Realms.

After creating twelve more virtual Xuanhuang Realms in succession, bringing the total to thirteen, Li Fan finally stopped.

"Thirteen worlds of Ghost Soldiers are enough."

Li Fan looked at the three thoughts in the first virtual Xuanhuang Realm, which remained in a hazy, ignorant state. After hesitating for a moment, he ultimately sent them into the Dreamland Paradise as well.

"Let me see what their potential is in the dreamscape first."

"With such immense computational power as their foundation, no matter which direction they choose, they can almost certainly reach the pinnacle."

The three thoughts, respectively named [Qi], [Shi], and [Zhong] by Li Fan, finally manifested physical forms within the dreamscape.

Compared with the simulated thoughts, they appeared especially clumsy. They gazed at everything around them with immense curiosity.

But Li Fan knew that their rate of growth would be faster than that of any other living being in the dreamscape.

Li Fan did not interfere with the growth of Qi, Shi, and Zhong.

He merely recorded and preserved the method by which the Dao Deviation Stone had originally given birth to them, ensuring that even after destroying them, he could quickly reproduce them again.

Afterward, Li Fan's attention returned to the exploration of the dream tentacles.

Crossing vast stretches of annihilated land and traversing the boundless, silent star sea, the dream tentacles finally reached the thriving starry sky where forces such as the Sword Domain and Sacred Shang were located.

The exploration of the tentacles became increasingly cautious.

An invisible veil slowly enveloped this region of the starry sky.

Yet what Li Fan had not expected was that among the countless living beings in this starry sky, not a single one had been captured by the power of the dreamscape.

It was as if a natural barrier existed between reality and illusion, resisting the erosion of living beings in reality by the dreamscape.

As the master of the dreamscape, Li Fan sensed that if he poured the full power of the Dreamland Paradise into it, he might be able to force open a gap in this barrier. But doing so would inevitably attract the attention of the various forces.

Li Fan chose to wait patiently for an opportunity.

Hard work eventually paid off. After the dream tentacles had endured in the thriving starry sky for an entire year, a turning point finally appeared.

A tremendous upheaval in the star sea had even affected the ethereal Dreamland Paradise. Naturally, this drew Li Fan's attention as well.

Seven figures suddenly appeared in the starry sky, jointly blocking another dim and distorted figure.

"Hmm? Not on the list of the [Immortal Capture Record]?"

"I did not expect that while lying in wait for [Longevity Immortal], we would get an unexpected harvest."

"Unfortunately, there does not seem to be any sign that he has attained the Dao of the Nameless."

"Do not be careless... I can sense a faint sense of danger from him."

The seven figures spoke one after another. This was precisely the unique method of communication used by the Immortal Hunt Organization from Qin Zhuang's memories.

Their conversation did not hinder them from attacking at the same time.

It was also the first time Li Fan had truly witnessed the might of Dao Primal.

If the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth was like a vast net, crisscrossing and stretching between heaven and earth, then these seven Immortal Hunters possessed the strength to pull that enormous net with every movement and capture their prey.

Li Fan sensed countless mysterious and unfathomable techniques.

For example, countless swords transformed from illusion into reality and emerged from thin air. Behind each sword, there seemed to stand a faint, blurred figure. Thousands upon thousands of sword Dao divine abilities erupted simultaneously across the starry sky, causing the Sword Dao to resonate as if receiving additional amplification. The power contained in every single sword technique was enormous, seemingly enough to split apart the Xuanhuang Realm.

For another example, the dim starry sky seemed to be illuminated by something. It was as though countless pairs of eyes were staring intently at everyone engaged in battle from beyond the curtain. The auras of the seven Immortal Hunters suddenly surged, while the True Immortal being besieged repeatedly lost control of his movements. His figure even trembled violently to the point of splitting into another version of himself.

And for yet another example, it seemed that there was a brush secretly recording and altering the course of the battle. Li Fan had clearly seen earlier that the True Immortal's long concealed charged strike had struck one of the Immortal Hunters. His body had trembled violently, and he was clearly heavily injured. Yet after a blur of light and shadow, it was as if this scene had never happened. The injury had vanished completely. Instead, the Immortal Hunter launched a counterattack that struck the True Immortal directly.

The True Immortal was utterly bewildered and soon fell into a disadvantageous position, able only to struggle desperately to defend himself. In the blink of an eye, his situation became extremely perilous.

...

Seven Immortal Hunters each displayed their own divine abilities.

Li Fan, however, keenly observed that besides these seven Immortal Hunters, there seemed to be another Immortal Hunter hidden in the shadows.

This Immortal Hunter did not participate in the hunt for the True Immortal.

Instead, they silently repaired the starry sky that had been affected by the great battle.

In a trance, Li Fan saw the fragile Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, which had been bent and strained by the invocation of Dao Primal power, gradually becoming firm and stable again under the maintenance of this power of order.

Although the battle involved a chaotic clash of True Immortal level powers, it did not cause too much damage to the starry sky.

The Great Dao of Heaven and Earth even seemed to record the memory of this battle.

If a similar battle occurred in the future, the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth would become more resilient and better able to withstand it.

"Besides the Immortal Hunters, there is also a [Heaven Mender]?"

"Interesting."

As the saying went, destruction was easy, while creation was difficult.

Li Fan lurked in the shadows and silently watched the battle. However, most of his attention remained focused on the Heaven Mender.

Li Fan keenly sensed that this [Heaven Mender] might actually be the strongest person present.

After all, it was obvious that no matter what kind of damage was caused by the various Dao Primal powers, this person could always repair it with ease. Meanwhile, from beginning to end, the seven Immortal Hunters only displayed divine abilities related to the same type of Great Dao.

The seven Immortal Hunters only wounded and did not kill. It seemed they intended to capture the True Immortal alive.

As the True Immortal gradually fell into a desperate situation, countless True Immortal characters suddenly emerged from his body, densely covering his entire body.

His aura suddenly expanded several hundred times, and the web of the Great Dao shook violently as if struck by a raging storm.

Even the divine abilities used by the seven Immortal Hunters were affected.

The True Immortal was making a desperate final stand.

At this very moment, a golden ring suddenly lit up, truly resembling a prison drawn upon the ground. It completely sealed off the area where everyone was fighting.

Everything inside and outside the golden ring was completely severed from one another.

Even the tentacles of the Dreamland Paradise were directly cut off.

The scene suddenly disappeared.

Li Fan could only watch from afar, outside the golden ring.

The True Immortal's desperate final attack had just been completely blocked by the golden ring before his eyes.

No matter how violently the wind and rain battered it, not the slightest trace could be sensed from outside.

Li Fan stared at the golden line before him, which radiated a sacred aura. A strange sense of familiarity suddenly arose in his heart.

"This..."

"Is this the Immortal Celestial Pillar?"

In his mind, the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar, forged from the leg bone of a True Immortal, began to overlap with the scene of the golden ring before him.

Li Fan was astonished. Following a sudden flash of inspiration, he drastically retracted the tentacles of the Dreamland Paradise.

He merely observed from an extremely distant location.

A figure dressed in white quietly appeared outside the golden ring.

They seemed to be solemnly observing the battle inside the golden ring.

From time to time, they raised their head toward the sky, seemingly recording something.

Li Fan also sensed that it was precisely because of the support provided by this golden ring that the pillars of the Great Dao web, which had become somewhat fragile during the great battle, were able to avoid the descent of a calamity.

The white-robed figure occasionally released streaks of radiance, filling in the golden ring and making it increasingly solid.

"This should be that [Heaven Mender]."

"Fortunately, their attention was completely focused on the battle, and the power of the Dreamland Paradise is special enough that they failed to discover my observation."

Li Fan secretly counted himself lucky, but he still did not dare approach too closely.

He continued observing from afar.

Before long, the battle finally seemed to subside. The radiance of the golden ring gradually faded, and the seven Immortal Hunters once again revealed their figures.

But the True Immortal had somehow disappeared without a trace.

Seeing the various remarkable expressions on the faces of the seven Immortal Hunters, the white-robed figure seemed somewhat surprised and finally spoke.

"He escaped?"

One of the Immortal Hunters solemnly explained, his tone heavy. "He was already at the end of his strength. But at the final moment, it seems another force intervened and rescued him."

"My Heaven-Reversing Brush was also powerless to alter this fact. Clearly, an Nameless True Immortal intervened." Another Immortal Hunter spoke.

"And it was an Nameless True Immortal who has never been recorded."

As soon as these words were spoken, the expressions of all the Immortal Hunters present became somewhat strange. There was excitement mixed with fear.

The white-robed figure nodded slightly.

"Although we failed to capture the True Immortal, we unexpectedly learned of another Nameless True Immortal. So there was both loss and gain. The rest of you return first and report the traces of the Nameless True Immortal. Brother Chu, stay behind and repair the Dao Marks with me."

In an instant, six figures disappeared.

Only the two of them remained standing in the starry sky after the great battle.

Without seeing how they repaired it, Li Fan, who was watching from afar, could clearly sense that this area of the starry sky had once again become much more "solid."

"Heaven-Reversing Brush. Brother Chu."

Li Fan recalled the voice of that distant Immortal Hunter.

"Divine Brush Sect, Chu Youhong?"

After a moment, Li Fan had more or less determined the other party's identity.

Chu Youhong, the former Sect Master of the Divine Brush Sect.

He was the cultivator who had once said, "Even if all of you die, it does not matter. As long as I am alive, the Divine Brush Sect will still exist. Before my master died, he entrusted the Divine Brush Sect to me, so I absolutely cannot allow it to be destroyed in my hands."

He was a cultivator who was utterly devoted to private interests while remaining completely selfless toward the sect.

After leaving a profound mark on the history of the Xuanhuang Realm, he had disappeared without a trace.

Li Fan had never expected that today, in the vibrant starry sky beyond the High Wall, he would encounter this person once again.

Li Fan recalled the divine abilities Chu Youhong had displayed just now.

"He can use a brush to overturn established facts that have already occurred. He can even rewrite them to a certain extent..."

"Although he is not a True Immortal, I am afraid his actual combat strength is absolutely no weaker than a True Immortal's."

Li Fan was secretly shocked.

He had finally gained a somewhat clearer understanding of the power of [Dao Primal] beyond the High Wall.

While Li Fan was pondering, Chu Youhong and the white-robed figure had already finished repairing the so-called "Dao Marks."

"Brother Chu, what do you think of that inexplicable Nameless True Immortal who appeared at the end?"

"If they could conceal themselves from everyone else, I imagine they should not have been able to escape your observation."

"After all, Brother Chu is said to be someone capable of imitating and reproducing the Great Dao."

The white-robed figure's words once again left Li Fan deeply astonished.

Chapter 1435: Dao Primal Weaves The Dao Net

Imitating and replicating the Great Dao?

It should be known that the rumored figure had already become an Inscription Dao Immortal of the Nameless Realm. Yet even he could do nothing more than use True Immortal seal script to describe one Great Dao after another. And this Chu Youhong could actually accomplish something to this extent?

Upon hearing this, Li Fan's first reaction was disbelief.

Chu Youhong's performance also seemed to confirm his judgment.

Chu Youhong gave a bitter smile. "I was merely fortunate enough to preserve a trace of the Great Dao's imprint. And even that was only possible amid the phenomenon of a Nameless True Immortal's death, when his Dao returned to heaven and earth..."

"Moreover, without this Heaven-Painting Brush in my hand, I absolutely could not have done it. Brother Lu, there is no need to praise me so highly."

"On the other hand, your divine ability of mending the rift in the heavens with a single hand, is truly known by everyone throughout the Shuo Star Sea."

The white-robed cultivator surnamed Lu did not deny Chu Youhong's praise. He merely smiled faintly.

However, Chu Youhong suddenly returned to the main subject. "That Nameless True Immortal who secretly intervened just now is indeed somewhat suspicious. Although I cannot completely depict the scene of the Great Dao, I am still capable of clearly sensing the traces left behind by the circulation of the Great Dao. However..."

Chu Youhong's expression became somewhat solemn. "There was no sound and no sign. It was completely unlike the intervention of a True Immortal of the Nameless Realm. Even the traces left behind were not as substantial as those of an ordinary True Immortal."

The cultivator surnamed Lu nodded in agreement. "That is precisely why I sought you out to discuss this privately. I believe that either the one who intervened this time was not a Nameless True Immortal, or that Nameless True Immortal has already found a way to conceal the traces of their existence within the [Dao Net]."

The two exchanged a glance.

"Whichever possibility it is, neither is good news for us."

"If it was not a Nameless True Immortal, yet they could calmly escape our complete encirclement, then their actual combat strength is no weaker than that of the Nameless Realm. The power of a new system is what we have always been most worried about. After all, the many methods we have summarized over the years are all aimed at [Immortals]. Once the [Immortals] break free from their former shackles, even if it means they must start over, we will likewise have to face entirely new and unknown enemies."

"As for concealing traces within the [Dao Net]..." A trace of worry flashed between Chu Youhong's brows.

The white-robed cultivator surnamed Lu looked back at the flourishing Starry Sea. "Our last attempt to surround and kill the Nameless Realm failed, and our losses were severe. Some people have already begun to stir restlessly. It is difficult to say whether this is some people's pledge of allegiance. If those True Immortals learn how to lurk within and manipulate the Dao Net, then we will be in serious trouble!"

"The patterns of the Dao Net must also be modified. Besides that..."

The cultivator surnamed Lu looked at Chu Youhong again. "I will have to trouble Brother Chu to secretly notify Senior Wan Shiyong."

"After all, this senior has a strange temperament. Since you come from the same world as him, you should at least be able to speak with him."

Chu Youhong looked somewhat troubled, but ultimately nodded in agreement. "The Starry Sea is currently without a leader and must also face the covetous eyes of other places. We indeed need a senior capable of commanding everyone's respect to step forward and take charge of the overall situation."

"Although I was severely beaten when I disturbed Senior Wan previously, considering that we are both cultivators from the Xuanhuang Realm, he should not really do anything to me." As Chu Youhong muttered to himself, he and the white-robed cultivator flew toward the Starry Sea.

In the blink of an eye, their figures disappeared.

While pondering their conversation, Li Fan controlled the dream tentacles and arrived at the place where everyone had fought earlier.

Generally speaking, after experts fought, there would always be traces, fluctuations, lingering auras, or other remnants. Yet after carefully observing the battle he had witnessed between the Hunters of the True Immortal, Li Fan could not find even the slightest trace left behind.

It was as if the battle had never happened.

"Apart from that white-robed cultivator [Heaven Mender], who consumed all those remnants to reinforce the Great Dao here, it seems that, in some unseen manner, something else simultaneously absorbed the remaining power as well."

After investigating for a long while, Li Fan finally arrived at a vague conclusion.

He could not help thinking of the [Dao Net] mentioned by Chu Youhong and the white-robed cultivator.

"The term Dao Net did not appear in Qin Zhuang's memories. Perhaps it was something that only came into existence in the Starry Sea after Qin Zhuang went to the Xuanhuang Realm. But judging by its name, it is clearly inseparable from the interwoven network of the Great Dao of heaven and earth."

"A Nameless True Immortal is one with a Great Dao. Every action they take will inevitably leave traces within the Dao Net. That is only natural. Yet Chu Youhong said that they can modify the [Dao Net]?"

Li Fan's eyes revealed a thoughtful expression.

Although the conversation between Chu Youhong and the white-robed cultivator had only lasted a few brief exchanges, it contained quite a lot of information.

First, the attempt to encircle the True Immortal had resulted in heavy losses. This showed that the battle between the Starry Sea and the True Immortals was not one-sided. The Starry Sea was without a leader, and it seemed that all its higher-ups were occupied with some important matter.

Second, apart from the Starry Sea, there were other forces. Perhaps another region of starry sky surrounded by the Land of Annihilation. They were watching the Starry Sea covetously. The situation in the Starry Sea did not seem optimistic.

Third, judging from the white-robed cultivator's words, he clearly knew that Chu Youhong came from the Xuanhuang Realm. And the Wan Shiyong he mentioned, who likewise came from the Xuanhuang Realm...

Li Fan's brows furrowed tightly.

"Wan Shiyong, the founding patriarch of the Beast Taming Sect. An old acquaintance of Emperor Sanmo, and an extraordinary figure who comprehended the True Art of Beast Taming from the Beast Taming Reincarnation Wheel."

"Everyone whom Emperor Sanmo chose to submit to was an exceptionally talented and brilliant figure. But this Wan Shiyong, as far as I remember, had already died, had he not?"

Confusion filled Li Fan's heart. He immediately summoned Emperor Sanmo and asked him about it.

"Hm?" Upon hearing Li Fan suddenly mention this name, the wise beast was also somewhat dazed for a moment. After all, this had happened tens of thousands of years ago.

"He indeed died. I personally buried his body. No matter how extraordinary his talents were, he ultimately could not escape the limitations of a mortal lifespan." After recalling for a while, Emperor Sanmo answered with absolute certainty.

"Then why does another Wan Shiyong exist in the Starry Sea beyond the High Wall?" Li Fan immediately gave Emperor Sanmo a brief account of the scene he had witnessed through the dream tentacles.

Emperor Sanmo pondered for a while, then seemed to think of something.

"His temperament was somewhat strange..."

"Could it be Wan Shiyong's companion beast from before his death?" he said uncertainly.

Li Fan was somewhat surprised. "Wan Shiyong's beast tamer companion was not you?"

Emperor Sanmo showed an awkward expression. He then stroked his beard and said, "My relationship with Wan Shiyong was merely one of cooperation. He was only a mortal, so naturally he had no qualifications to command me. Wan Shiyong's actual beast companion was a [Withered Shadow Beast]. Its appearance was like a shadow, and its form could change in countless ways."

"Normally, it would conceal itself within Wan Shiyong's shadow. At a critical moment, it could leap into the enemy's shadow and transform or replicate itself."

“The shadow resembles its owner. It can even achieve complete likeness. Even the one being copied would find it almost impossible to distinguish the difference. After Wan Shiyong died, that child also disappeared without a trace. If he had continued living under Wan Shiyong’s identity, then given how familiar he was with Wan Shiyong when the latter was alive, others might not necessarily have noticed the difference.” Emperor Sanmo slowly analyzed.

“Since he is a member of the demon beast race, would the methods we once used to restrain him still be effective? Or, failing that, can we at least sense him or establish contact with him?” Li Fan asked.

Emperor Sanmo hesitated for a while. Surprisingly, he did not give an answer.

“Under normal circumstances, it should be possible. But according to what Senior just said, if even Chu Youhong, who could reproduce Great Dao imprints, was no match for Wan Shiyong…”

“I am afraid the other party has already surpassed our ability to control him.”

Fearing that Li Fan might misunderstand and think he was unwilling to help, Emperor Sanmo paused, then hurriedly explained, “Since Senior already knows, the current demon beast race was all evolved from Emperor Yi.”

“And Emperor Yi himself originated from the fusion of countless members of our original race. The key point is that the original origin of our race…”

Emperor Sanmo sent out a flash of light. The appearances of thirteen strange ancient beasts appeared before Li Fan.

“These are the Thirteen Primeval Dao Forms of our race. Although they were once the strongest beings within our race and natural incarnations of the Great Dao, they were not the source of our race. In truth, there is no consensus regarding the origin of our race. Even the memories of the Primeval Dao Forms contain no answer. However, there was one thing that we once agreed upon.”

There was a trace of reminiscence in Emperor Sanmo’s expression as he looked at the Primeval Dao Forms before him.

Only then did he slowly continue, “In our timeline, our race was favored by the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. Compared with the myriad Great Daos of Heaven and Earth, the Thirteen Primeval Dao Forms were merely an insignificant portion. All the other members of our race also possessed the possibility of attaining the Dao after birth. If compared to human cultivators, it would be equivalent to cultivating to the realm of an Nameless True Immortal.”

“To completely fuse and evolve all the Great Daos of Heaven and Earth into one Primeval Dao Form after another. This was the [Golden Age] our race dreamed of…”

“It is a pity that before any new Primeval Dao Forms could emerge, fierce Nameless True Immortals crossed over from another possibility and slaughtered your entire race.” Li Fan interrupted Emperor Sanmo’s reverie at an inappropriate moment.

Emperor Sanmo showed no embarrassment. He merely sighed softly. “Our race was indeed favored by the Great Dao in terms of our own possibilities. But there are differences in strength even among possibilities. The strong prey upon the weak. Having lost to others, we have no complaints.”

Emperor Sanmo’s topic once again returned to Wan Shiyong.

“What I am trying to say is that although we all originated from Emperor Yi, every member of our race possesses the potential to surpass Emperor Yi. Even if, theoretically speaking, this possibility is extremely small, almost to the point of impossibility, it is ultimately not zero.”

“As long as our history of reproduction is sufficiently long, we can turn that possibility into certainty. This was also something the Primeval Dao Forms had considered when they entered the enormous furnace back then. The only thing they failed to anticipate was that it was not merely the possibility we inhabited that had fallen into crisis. All possibilities had encountered a world-ending calamity. Our repeated escapes left our race with no time to recuperate and multiply. The power of the Primeval Dao Forms was completely exhausted…” Emperor Sanmo let out a long sigh.

After remaining silent for a moment, Emperor Sanmo suddenly volunteered, “I wish to go and speak with this Wan Shiyong.”

“Even though his strength now far surpasses mine, I am confident that he definitely will not harm me.”

Li Fan pondered for a moment before finally agreeing. “I will use the power of dreams to evolve a body for you.”

“After you establish contact with him first, if there are no problems, I will then help send your physical body over.”

“Remember, the dream body is essentially the manifestation of the thoughts of Carefree and the living beings of Xuanhuang. Aside from containing a trace of your consciousness, it actually has little connection with your true self. As for whether you can gain Wan Shiyong’s trust, you will have to find a way yourself.” Li Fan gave another warning.

Emperor Sanmo nodded. “Senior, rest assured. Our race has its own methods of communication and identification.”

“The Shuo Star Sea’s situation is unclear. Even if the first attempt fails, it does not matter.”

As Li Fan spoke, the tentacles of the dream slowly evolved a body outside the Shuo Star Sea.

Its entire body was suffused with yellow light, and its appearance was exactly the same as Emperor Sanmo’s real body. At first, it seemed to exist between reality and illusion.

But as specks of crimson light and an extraordinarily mystical power were infused into it, this illusory body actually transformed from falsehood into reality.

It suddenly burst into existence from the void.

Emperor Sanmo sensed his new body for a moment, astonishment appearing in his eyes. “Senior’s methods are truly beyond imagination. I cannot sense any difference between this body and the one I have in reality!”

Li Fan’s voice sounded beside Emperor Sanmo’s ear.

“Not every dream avatar can reach such a state of seeming completely false yet being almost indistinguishable from reality.”

“I have prepared some energy for you within this body. It should be enough for you to travel. Proceed carefully. With a dream avatar, consciousness and body are fused into one. As long as the energy remains, it can continuously regenerate. But once the energy is completely exhausted, the body will be utterly destroyed, and the consciousness will cease to exist.”

“If your consciousness does not return to the Dreamland Paradise before the body is destroyed, that portion of your memories will be completely lost. Therefore, I suggest that you return once every so often and report the information you have gathered.”

“Of course, this must be done while ensuring that you do not expose yourself. Although the power of dreams is mysterious and difficult to detect, the Shuo Star Sea is by no means weak. If you manifest too frequently, you may inevitably reveal traces of yourself…”

Li Fan continued giving his instructions.

Emperor Sanmo narrowed his eyes and, relying on that faint connection in the depths of his consciousness, rapidly fled toward the target location while concealing himself.

At first, Li Fan could still use the power of dreams to sense the scenes on Emperor Sanmo’s side.

But after Emperor Sanmo approached a place where light had gathered, even the connection through the dream was severed.

“Sword Domain, Sacred Shang, and the various faction strongholds within the Shuo Star Sea indeed all possess some form of protective force.”

“The Dao Net…”

Li Fan watched as Emperor Sanmo’s dream avatar departed into the distance, sensing the so-called power of the Dao Net.

According to the deductions of the Dao Deviation Stone in his sea of consciousness, Li Fan had already become certain that this Dao Net should have been established upon the Dao Primal system.

He recalled how the Tianxuan Mirror had once replaced Heaven with itself and woven an Mysterious Net across the skies of the Xuanhuang Realm.

“If the Dao Net can be modified, that means it is not impossible to break into or invade.”

“It is merely necessary to find the corresponding method.”
