Next

Chapter 1436: Secretly Infiltrating The Shuo Star Sea

The ideal was a good one.

But after observing it for several days, Li Fan still could not find the slightest trace of the so called Dao Net. It seemed even more elusive and unfathomable than the Heavenly and Earthly Great Dao itself.

However, Li Fan was not discouraged. From what he could see, the Dao Net was very likely the trump card of this Shuo Star Sea in its struggle against the True Immortal forces. It could even contend with the Nameless True Immortal. How could it possibly be so easily deciphered by Li Fan, a newcomer who had only just arrived?

"Even the most impregnable fortress can be breached from within."

"Since I cannot forcibly infiltrate the Dao Net for the time being, I might as well lie low first and infiltrate the Shuo Star Sea. I cannot place all my hopes on Di Sanmo's side..."

Li Fan shook his head slightly. First, he carefully used the power of dreams to make a "rubbing" of the residual traces of fluctuations in this place. Only then did he continue cautiously probing toward the Shuo Star Sea.

Although on the surface, all traces of the great battle had already been completely erased by the white robed cultivator's method of Heaven Mending, Li Fan knew very well that nothing in this world was absolute. As long as something had happened, it would inevitably leave traces behind. The reason they could not be detected now was simply that his abilities were insufficient. This did not prevent Li Fan from recording them first and studying them repeatedly in the future.

The distant Shuo Star Sea was not filled with life everywhere. Instead, life had gathered in numerous places like islands. The strength of the aura emanating from these gatherings was not determined by their size. Each had its own distinct characteristics.

"Those who have managed to survive in this starry sky until now are probably no ordinary figures."

Li Fan observed from afar, identifying several familiar auras among them.

He soon discovered the Sword Domain, which was suspected to be the gathering place of the Immortal Dao's Ten Great Sects from the former Xuanhuang Realm. However, he still could not find the Sacred Shang from Qin Zhuang's memories.

"The Xuan Immortal Ark can still sense the existence of the Shang Immortal Ark beyond the High Wall. The Xuanshang should not have been completely destroyed."

Recalling the strange actions of the Sacred Shang in Qin Zhuang's memories, such as cutting off communication with the outside world and even beginning to withdraw treasures that had previously been put up for sale, the Dao Deviation Stone in his sea of consciousness flashed. After a round of deductions, the most likely possibility immediately surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

"There is a very high possibility that the Sacred Shang discovered something. Either it went into seclusion and refused to come out, or it simply fled."

The Sacred Shang was merely an entry point. Li Fan was not obsessed with finding the former Shang Immortal Ark.

He began probing the forces in the Shuo Star Sea one by one, looking for those with weaker defenses.

Just as he had sensed, the strength of these forces had little connection with the amount of life gathered within them.

There was one place where only two life auras existed, one large and one small. They seemed to have been ostracized and lived on the outskirts of the Shuo Star Sea. Yet Li Fan had not even approached them. Merely observing from afar caused an intense sense of danger to surge within his heart. Even his dream tentacles were affected, with part of them becoming distorted and mutated. Li Fan could only keep his distance.

There was also a huge gathering place containing the auras of tens of millions of living beings. Yet above it hung a massive dark cloud woven from countless negative emotions, such as slaughter, hatred, and greed, all emanating an appalling stench. Although numerous protective measures existed outside the gathering place, preventing Li Fan's dream power from directly eroding it, he could already see a breakthrough point for dream infiltration when he observed that cloud of emotions from afar.

However, Li Fan did not rush to act.

This weakness could also be a trap deliberately created by the forces of the Shuo Star Sea to lure in True Immortals.

After investigating all the other gathering places, Li Fan discovered that only this place offered an opportunity to connect to the Dreamland Paradise. He proceeded with extreme caution. First, he accumulated a massive amount of dream power and formed an additional instance of the Dreamland Paradise. He then sent one strand of his divine sense into this instance and severed its connection with the main world of the Dreamland Paradise.

Only after ensuring that nothing could go wrong did Li Fan personally control his divine sense and initiate the infiltration of this gathering place.

This instance of the Dreamland Paradise was completely empty. Since it had been separated into an independent instance, its dream power would only decrease with each use. If it could not receive effective replenishment before its energy was exhausted, this instance world would perish.

Although Li Fan had only divided off a single strand of divine sense, it carried nine projections of the Xuanhuang Realm, along with computational power equivalent to a supreme grade Dao Deviation Stone.

"If even these preparations cannot open a breach, then I can only find another way."

The progress was not smooth.

That invisible barrier seemed to completely sever contact between the dream power and the thoughts of living beings. Even though the suppressed negative emotions of those living beings had already become almost tangible, stretching across the sky, there was still a membrane separating them. They simply could not be touched.

"If I disregard the consequences and consume all the power of this instance in one go, I might be able to forcibly open a breach. But that would inevitably alert all the forces of the Shuo Star Sea."

"The gains would not be worth the losses."

After studying it for a while, Li Fan ultimately chose not to alert the enemy. Instead, he patiently waited for an opportunity.

Cultivators would occasionally leave their gathering places and travel through the starry sky in the Shuo Star Sea. Li Fan did not believe that every one of these "passersby" possessed strength so high that he could not approach them.

As it turned out, Li Fan's judgment was correct.

Although the target he had selected did not travel outside as frequently as those from other forces, it did happen from time to time.

Li Fan kept watch for half a year. Finally, a streak of light flew out from the gathering place.

Its destination seemed to be in the direction of the Sword Domain.

As the streak of light shot out, it carried a large mass of emotional dark clouds with it. His emotional fluctuations were so obvious that Li Fan did not even need to deliberately eavesdrop to clearly sense his thoughts.

"They're making this old man run errands again!"

"Even so, I, Ma Tiande, passed the entrance examination and received certification as a Master Dao Controller from the Shuo Star Sea. Although I have only just begun to understand it, I can at least coordinate and manipulate the Dao Net patterns within a designated area!"

"They make me deal with all these trivial matters every single day. They are simply wasting my life, Ma Tiande's life..."

This cultivator named Ma Tiande raced through the starry sky with overwhelming resentment.

Li Fan never made a move. He merely watched as Ma Tiande entered a gathering place adjacent to the Sword Domain. After staying there for three days, he finally returned at a leisurely pace.

Perhaps having vented his frustrations, all his resentment had disappeared. He happily hummed a tune as he slowly followed the same route back.

Seeing that Ma Tiande was about to enter the protective formation, Li Fan finally made his move.

Ordinary methods of possessing a body or infiltrating had a high chance of being discovered by the protective layer surrounding the gathering place. Even any power related to immortality could potentially trigger an alarm.

Therefore, Li Fan employed the Xuan Immortal Ark's [Dao Pill] technique. Ma Tiande, who was flying through the void, merely paused slightly.

Yet he had already been refined by Li Fan into a [Human Pill] that could be swallowed at any time. Li Fan's divine sense silently swallowed the pill, and in an instant, he transformed into Ma Tiande.

Temporarily severing his connection with the Dreamland Paradise instance, Li Fan organized the information in Ma Tiande's mind while flying straight toward the gathering place ahead.

"Blissful City?"

"Interesting."

In theory, this body was still Ma Tiande. It was merely that another strand of thought had been added to his mind.

Any living being would inevitably experience fluctuations of thought like this as long as they remained alive.

Moreover, the Dao Pill technique was unique to the Xuan Immortal Ark. It had never appeared in this starry sky before.

Although the protective layer outside Blissful City was powerful, it was still difficult for it to guard against such an unfathomable method.

Without encountering any complications, 'Ma Tiande' smoothly flew into Blissful City.

Unlike the foul-smelling emotional clouds that surged into the heavens from what could be seen from outside, what Li Fan saw upon entering was a scene of singing, dancing, and peace.

Colorful lights stretched all the way to the horizon. Wild and unrestrained laughter, lewd and obscene moans, unrestrained curses and furious shouts...

They filled every corner of the city.

"Blissful City. Three years of day, three years of night, one cycle every six years."

"During the night, indulge in pleasure, free from all worries."

"During the day... killing is unpunished?"

As Li Fan sorted through the information in Ma Tiande's mind, he first went to report back.

Ma Tiande had gone to the [Saber Island] this time precisely to deliver an invitation on behalf of the Lord of Blissful City.

Thirty days later, the transition between day and night in Blissful City would take place.

Everyone on Saber Island was invited to witness the grand occasion.

...

With the information flashing through his mind, Li Fan calmly made his way to the center of Blissful City.

Compared to the prosperous and bustling surroundings, the manor at the center was so cold and gloomy that it created an intense contrast. Anyone who set foot here could not help but feel a trace of horror rising from the depths of their heart.

Even though Ma Tiande had come here more than once, his body still instinctively felt fear.

Li Fan did not suppress this feeling. Instead, he appropriately allowed the corresponding changes to appear on his face.

Step by step, he arrived before the Lord of Blissful City and reported the situation.

"Scholar Zhu, I delivered the invitation. But as for how many people from Saber Island will actually come, that is difficult to say."

Li Fan put on a troubled expression and recounted everything honestly.

"As soon as I entered, the people on the island invited me to spar with them. They even threatened that if I refused, they would tear up the invitation. I really had no choice but to agree."

"They called it a spar, but in reality, I was the only one getting beaten..."

Following Ma Tiande's usual manner of conduct in secret, Li Fan complained bitterly to the Lord of Blissful City.

The Lord of Blissful City, whom Ma Tiande called Scholar Zhu, appeared extremely old.

His hunched and shriveled body was curled within a spacious robe, making him look rather comical. Yet the overwhelming sinister aura emanating from Scholar Zhu made Li Fan dare not underestimate him.

Li Fan even vaguely sensed that the enormous mass of hidden emotions accumulated above Blissful City was actually anchored to this Scholar Zhu, with nearly nine tenths of it being maintained by him.

Faced with Ma Tiande's incessant complaints, Scholar Zhu did not interrupt him.

He merely sat there quietly, his withered fingers tapping lightly against the tabletop from time to time. Occasionally, his fingernails scraped across the long table, producing an unbearably shrill sound.

Li Fan could see clearly that the long table before Scholar Zhu was actually made from human skulls that had been piled together and polished.

Even the lingering remnants of consciousness within those skulls had not completely dissipated. As Scholar Zhu's fingers gently passed over them, faint cries of pain and anguish could still be heard.

Cold sweat trickled down Ma Tiande's forehead, yet he did not stop speaking. He completed his report from beginning to end.

Afterward, he respectfully stood beneath the long table, waiting for the city lord's instructions.

The manor fell silent for quite some time.

Occasional noises and disturbances from outside drifted into the room.

Scholar Zhu, curled within his spacious robe, pressed his fingers against the long table.

Each time he pressed down, one of the sounds outside abruptly ceased.

Only after the surroundings had become completely silent, with not even the slightest trace of outside noise remaining, did Scholar Zhu finally speak.

His voice was not that of an elderly man.

Instead, it was the innocent and lively voice of a child.

"You have worked hard running around outside."

"Stay and have dinner before you go."

As his words fell, a ghostly figure floated out from the darkness and arrived before Li Fan.

After a rustling sound, a bowl of steaming red soup appeared before Li Fan.

Ma Tiande's body trembled slightly.

It was partly an instinctive reaction from this body, but to a certain extent, it also represented the fluctuation of Li Fan's emotions.

The red soup in the bowl was clearly made from countless pieces of minced flesh mixed with blood.

Floating in the soup were eyeballs, one after another.

They seemed to possess wills of their own, all staring at Li Fan from within the bowl.

Although Li Fan had destroyed worlds several times and regarded all living beings as insignificant, even he had never done anything quite so "disgusting."

"Mr. Ma, drink it quickly. Why have you not drunk it yet?"

Seeing Li Fan hesitate, Scholar Zhu's voice immediately became somewhat anxious.

It was like a mischievous child who had suddenly lost his beloved toy. A strange aura began spreading from Scholar Zhu.

"Are you not hungry? Or are you dissatisfied with this bowl of soup?"

"It does not matter. I will have my servants prepare something better right away."

Information regarding the Lord of Blissful City flashed through Li Fan's mind.

"He can manifest many different personalities."

"An old man, a child, an alluring woman, a brutal butcher..."

"Among them, the child is the most emotionally unstable."

Having made his decision, Li Fan said, "There is no need to trouble yourself, Scholar Zhu. This bowl of soup looks exceptionally delicious."

As he spoke, Li Fan picked up the bowl and drank the red soup in one gulp.

To his surprise, it was nothing like its appearance suggested.

There was not even the slightest trace of blood or flesh in it.

Instead, it was indescribably delicious.

With Li Fan's level of experience, after a hundred reincarnations in the Xuanhuang Realm and having nearly excavated all its secrets, even he could hardly find anything he had tasted that could compare with it.

After finishing the bowl of red soup, Li Fan looked at the empty bowl and unexpectedly felt a sense of reluctance.

"What? Would you like another bowl?"

Scholar Zhu had also noticed the change in Li Fan's emotions. As if he had finally found someone who understood him, he became somewhat excited.

With tremendous willpower, Li Fan shook his head and refused.

"Such a rare delicacy must be savored properly."

"More is not necessarily better. It is better to eat less."

He spoke with a solemn expression.

Upon hearing this, Scholar Zhu immediately let out a deep, muffled laugh.

His voice quietly changed from that of a child back into that of an old man.

"What you said does make some sense."

"In that case, you may leave for now."

Li Fan bowed and took his leave.

Just as he was about to walk out of the manor, Scholar Zhu's light and airy voice suddenly drifted into his ears.

"For this Day and Night Ceremony, I have also prepared a seat for you."

Li Fan's face lit up with joy, and he hurriedly bowed again.

"Thank you, Lord Zhu!"

The light and shadows before his eyes shifted for a moment, and the city lord's manor disappeared.

In its place was a red-light district where lewd sounds continuously rang out.

Li Fan licked his lips.

The information from Ma Tiande's memories surfaced once again.

"Scholar Zhu, one of the members of the Shuo Star Sea Administrative Council."

"He was once a True Immortal, but later descended into mortality!"

Previous

Next

Chapter 1437: Mysterious Sounds Enshroud The Ghost City

"Moreover, he was likely not just an ordinary True Immortal."

"He may very well have once been an Nameless True Immortal!"

Li Fan lowered his head, concealing the thoughts in his eyes. He quickened his pace and headed toward his residence.

"Honored Guest, come and have some fun!"

"Brother Ma, long time no see. Come over and have a drink!"

"You brat, I finally caught you again! Don't run!"

...

As he strolled through Blissful City, different cries continuously reached his ears. Along with these sounds came fragments of memories associated with their voices.

The owners of these voices were either old acquaintances he had befriended while spending his days listening to songs in pleasure quarters, sworn brothers with whom he had once shared life and death, or enemies who had brought trouble upon him.

Scene after scene rapidly surfaced, so vivid that they seemed to be experiences he had personally lived through.

Yet Li Fan merely ignored them, lowering his head and proceeding toward his destination at an unhurried pace.

It had not been long since he had possessed Ma Tiande. Li Fan was certain that none of these memories had existed within Ma Tiande's previous memories. They had simply been imprinted into his mind along with the voices he heard.

"This is not merely some simple illusion technique."

"It is more as if these events had actually happened within Blissful City."

Even as Li Fan moved farther away and the voices gradually faded, the memories that had already been engraved into his mind did not disappear. Instead, they became increasingly vivid.

After walking this entire distance, Ma Tiande had been forcibly endowed with countless experiences.

Each experience represented many years.

Wrinkles quietly crept onto the corners of his eyes. Ma Tiande's face gradually took on a weathered appearance.

Naturally, Li Fan did not overlook these changes in his body. Deliberately controlling himself, he slowed his steps, making it seem as though every step had become extremely difficult.

After crossing an entire district of Blissful City, Ma Tiande finally returned to his residence.

The instant the black gate closed, all the noisy sounds from outside abruptly vanished, disappearing into the darkness.

Following Ma Tiande's habits from the past, he dealt with various affairs.

Afterward, he sat cross-legged, closed his eyes, and meditated, seemingly beginning to study the [Dao Net Patterns].

In reality, Li Fan was still pondering the changes that had occurred in this body along the way.

"Allowing a false past to influence the true present."

"Is this the Scripture Chanting Demonic Sound of the Xuantian King?"

The supreme-grade Dao Deviation Stone within his divine sense began to operate, assisting Li Fan in his analysis.

"If Ma Tiande had been unable to resist the temptation and had been drawn into any one of those voices, participating in what was happening..."

"Then the 'past events' mentioned during their conversations would all be realized upon his body."

Within the scenes deduced by Li Fan, he vaguely saw Ma Tiande at a banquet aging at an incredibly terrifying speed. In the blink of an eye, he transformed into a withered skeleton, yet continued laughing and conversing with those around him.

Only when the revelry finally ended did the decayed bones turn to ashes. Ma Tiande's consciousness, meanwhile, remained forever within this Blissful City, exactly like the countless other consciousnesses within the city.

"Brat, once you go to Blissful City, aside from what Scholar Zhu instructed you to do, do not do anything else. Stay there honestly for three full years. You may still have a chance to return."

The scene of a senior from the Shuo Star Sea Administration Council earnestly giving Ma Tiande this advice before he came to Blissful City suddenly surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

"The Shuo Star Sea's ability to hunt immortals with mortals cannot do without the assistance of existences like Scholar Zhu, who have transformed from immortals into mortals."

"Although they have fallen into the mortal realm, the strength these former True Immortals possess is still not to be underestimated."

"Whether to appease them or to give vent to their twisted minds, the Administration Council prepared special 'playgrounds' for them. This Blissful City is the one specifically belonging to Scholar Zhu."

"The countless living beings seen within the city have all been dead for a long time. It is a genuine ghost city. They can still retain physical forms only because some strange energy is sustaining them. Even after three years of alternating day and night, and endless mutual slaughter, no one truly dies. They merely wait for the next cycle to begin before being reborn."

"However, the ultimate victor of the slaughter..."

Li Fan smacked his lips, savoring the taste of the bowl of red soup he had just drunk.

They were all ghostly remnants of consciousness, so naturally there was no real taste of flesh and blood.

What Li Fan had consumed at Scholar Zhu's residence was the taste of the consciousnesses of countless living beings, tempered through countless refinements and carefully cooked!

"It is just like an Eternal Remnant Will!"

"No wonder it is so delicious."

After swallowing one bowl of red soup, there was no need to experience everything the beings within the bowl had experienced. He could directly obtain the gains produced by those experiences.

And the entire Blissful City could be regarded as a much larger bowl of red soup.

The only difference was that the method used to cook the beings within Blissful City was even more exquisite.

Li Fan's thoughts raced as the computational power of the supreme-grade Dao Deviation Stone operated at full capacity.

He peered through the prosperous yet horrifying scenes he had witnessed and sought to uncover the truth beneath them.

"Under the influence of the omnipresent Scripture Chanting Demonic Sound, the experiences of the remnants of consciousness within the city are increasing almost every moment. The life experiences of any single resident of the city are probably hundreds or even thousands of times richer than those of ordinary living beings!"

"First, consciousnesses that can continue to exist even after being forcibly stuffed with such complicated experiences have already undergone a transformation in quality. They are no longer comparable to ordinary consciousnesses."

"Second, even when boiling medicinal herbs, there is naturally a tremendous difference between using a hundred-year-old ginseng and a ten-thousand-year-old ginseng."

"Under the mysterious sounds that enshroud it, the efficiency with which Blissful City cooks them has increased by an unknown amount."

Although he did not know whether what the Lord of Blissful City ultimately sought was truly an Eternal Remnant Will, what Li Fan had witnessed within Blissful City made him, who had once entertained the same idea, deeply aware of the crudity of his own methods.

"I originally thought that cultivating Eternal Remnants Wills within an artificially created dream paradise was already the pinnacle of perfection."

"Yet this former True Immortal has still taught me a lesson."

"Truly worthy of someone who survived the great catastrophe that destroyed the Immortal Realm. His methods of tormenting people are truly unheard of. They are worth learning from."

"Three years to blossom, three years to bear fruit. Repeating this cycle again and again..."

"The so-called [Grand Ceremony] is naturally the moment when these fruits are enjoyed."

These living beings indulged themselves without restraint within Blissful City, enjoying themselves for three years. Their desires continuously surged and climbed higher.

Until the moment they reached the peak, when, like oil boiling beneath raging flames, everything suddenly exploded.

"The slaughter during the daytime certainly is not as simple as it appears on the surface."

"If the demonic sounds still enshroud Blissful City, then what the survivors have to endure may be far greater than imagined. And under such circumstances, those who can survive until the very end..."

Even Li Fan found himself somewhat tempted by the vitality of these "fruits" for a moment.

"In thirty days, perhaps I will be able to witness it with my own eyes."

"It can be imagined that these fruits of Remnant Wills must be enormously beneficial to cultivators. Yet Scholar Zhu invited only the [Saber Island] faction to the banquet?"

Although Ma Tiande had only just arrived, he had previously heard about Blissful City hosting banquets for cultivators from all directions.

“This time, I wonder what happened.”

Within the Shuo Star Sea, Saber Island and the Sword Domain were two enormous forces that served as the pillars of the region. The Sword Domain was centered around the former Heavenly Sword Sect of the Xuanhuang Realm and had gathered countless sword cultivators. Its influence was immense, with a million members.

As for Saber Island, from beginning to end, it had only ever had eighteen cultivators. Anyone who wished to ascend the island had to first challenge the cultivators residing there. The loser died, while the victor joined Saber Island. It was said that this was the rule established by the cultivator who founded Saber Island and was also the first person to set foot upon it. The number eighteen was actually an increase from the original. In the beginning, only five peerless saber cultivators lived on Saber Island.

In the past, when the Shuo Star Sea was teeming with life and saber cultivators could be found everywhere, cultivators who went to Saber Island to issue challenges came one after another. Although none of them ever returned, those who came afterward continued to arrive without end.

The reason was that the [Saber Dao] stood upon Saber Island.

It was not some wondrous phenomenon. It was truly the [Saber Dao] itself, manifested between heaven and earth.

To glimpse it was to directly face the Great Dao. If one attained enlightenment in a single moment, one could die without regret.

The Great Dao was formless, yet it had manifested upon Saber Island. There was naturally a reason for this.

However, there had never been a definitive explanation. Countless theories circulated, and even the Shuo Star Sea Council had never given a direct response.

Of all the related rumors, the most convincing was that the leader of Saber Island had once been an Nameless True Immortal. Moreover, the Great Dao he had integrated with was precisely the [Saber Dao].

After he transformed from mortal to immortal, the Great Dao returned to heaven and earth. Yet somehow, he had managed to solidify and manifest it, allowing the formless and profound Great Dao to be seen by mortal eyes.

“The one on Saber Island may perhaps have been an old acquaintance of Scholar Zhu from Blissful City.”

In the past, Nameless True Immortals had existed only in ancient legends within the Darkest Star Sea. Yet upon arriving in the Shuo Star Sea, Li Fan had immediately encountered two individuals who might have been Nameless True Immortals.

“Rather than calling this a matter of luck, it would be more accurate to say that as the Immortal Realm catastrophic destruction has developed to this point, even the immortals of the past can only struggle to survive beneath the same starry sky as mortals.”

Li Fan sneered inwardly.

He had no interest in the mentality of these True Immortals who had transformed back into mortals.

What truly interested him was exactly how an Nameless True Immortal could transform into a mortal while still preserving their life.

Li Fan had already witnessed True Immortals transforming into mortals in the Xuanhuang Realm, so there was nothing particularly strange about it.

But those in the Nameless realm were united with the Dao in body and being.

They had transformed into one of the Great Daos and returned to heaven and earth. How could they possibly escape so easily?

Recalling Scholar Zhu's neither human nor ghostly appearance, Li Fan was certain that for an Nameless True Immortal to transform into a mortal, they must pay an extremely tragic price.

“To become Nameless, one must surely be an unparalleled genius throughout the ages. Yet even such beings have to abandon their own paths one after another…”

“Was it an unwilling act of desperation to preserve their lives during the Dao Annihilation Calamity, or was it a lofty ideal of returning the Dao to heaven and saving the world?”

Li Fan revealed an expression of considerable interest.

It was much like the Xuanhuang Realm after the birth of the Immortal Ancestor's New Law Method.

Cultivators devoured the Great Dao of heaven and earth to cultivate. After a cultivator died, everything within their body returned to heaven and earth. The Nameless were united with the Dao in body and being.

If an Nameless being died while the Great Dao itself did not perish, then naturally, the Dao would return to heaven and earth.

However, a contradiction naturally arose.

Since an Nameless True Immortal had already become one with the Dao in body and being, how could a situation arise in which the Nameless being perished while the Great Dao remained?

“Perhaps this can likewise be compared to the situation in the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“Those Dao Integration cultivators, although they are united with the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, can still be forcibly separated from it using my current methods and my understanding of the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm after I capture them…”

“It is not difficult!”

“Although the strength of an Nameless True Immortal is far beyond that of a Dao Integration cultivator in the Xuanhuang Realm, the underlying principle should be the same.”

“Perhaps the answer lies within the [Dao Net].”

Li Fan's eyes flickered.

As the ultimate means by which the Shuo Star Sea resisted and hunted True Immortals, the Dao Net naturally had to extend to the vicinity of former True Immortals like Scholar Zhu.

And Ma Tiande was one of the people responsible for maintaining and connecting to the Dao Net.

In truth, whether Ma Tiande lived or died was not important.

Even if he angered Scholar Zhu and was transformed into an eternal, fallen remnant within Blissful City's Infinite Reincarnation Purgatory, the Council would simply dispatch new personnel the following day.

In Ma Tiande's eyes, he was a high-end combat asset of the Shuo Star Sea. Running errands for a True Immortal who had transformed into a mortal like Scholar Zhu was an insult to his abilities, a complete waste of talent.

But as an outsider, Li Fan saw the situation clearly.

He was nothing more than expendable material.

And expendable material could never be exhausted.

Unperturbed by honor or disgrace, Li Fan began studying the information related to the Dao Net within Ma Tiande's mind.

“Just as I expected. The key to utilizing the Dao Net is precisely the Dao Primal Symbols!”

“I have already mastered the earliest Dao Primal Symbols. Using Ma Tiande's body to connect to the Dao Net should not be a problem…”

But Li Fan soon realized that he had been completely mistaken.

Compared with the time of Qin Zhuang, the complexity of the Dao Primal had increased by more than tens of thousands of times!

Every command required the coordinated operation of hundreds of thousands of Dao Primal Symbols. The slightest mistake would be recorded and transmitted to the Council.

“Everyone who passes the examination has already become thoroughly proficient in utilizing the Primal Origin.”

“The Council allows one or two mistakes, but if there are too many, they will inevitably arouse suspicion.”

The Dao Pill's possession technique could only observe and inherit memories. As for the application of Dao Primal Symbols, such complex and intricate operations were much more difficult to perfectly reproduce.

Especially since Li Fan had not come here with his complete consciousness as his true self, but only with a split consciousness. His ability was further limited.

“If the Azure Firmament Pearl were here right now, that would be perfect.”

“It can inherit Ma Tiande's cognition without any loss.”

Li Fan shook his head slightly and suppressed this unrealistic fantasy.

Now that he had infiltrated the Shuo Star Sea alone, it was impossible for him to obtain the assistance of the Azure Firmament Pearl.

“In thirty days, the grand ceremony will be held, and it will require the cooperation of the Dao Net.”

“Looks like learning it myself is the best course of action.”

“Fortunately, I have the supreme-grade Dao Deviation Stone to assist me. Whatever Ma Tiande can accomplish, reproducing it should be as easy as turning over my hand.”

Li Fan began studying the knowledge of Dao Primal Symbols within Ma Tiande's mind from the very beginning.

The [Thirty Lectures on the Fundamentals of Dao Primal] that he had previously learned from Qin Zhuang's memories had already become completely outdated. The meanings of quite a few symbols had even changed entirely.

Li Fan had no choice but to abandon his original understanding and start over from the beginning.

However, mastering Dao Primal Symbols was not as easy as Li Fan had imagined.

The greatest problem was that the process through which Ma Tiande had learned Dao Primal Symbols had already disappeared from his memories.

Ma Tiande had absolutely no recollection of how he had mastered the Dao Primal. He only knew that after going through an arduous period of study, he had ultimately mastered this skill.

There was only the result, but no cause.

Li Fan could only work backward from the result.

Without the Dao Deviation Stone, he would not even be able to take this first step.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1438: One Hand Covers The Great Dao

However, even with the help of the Dao Deviation Stone, it was by no means easy for Li Fan to analyze the method of connecting to the Dao Net within a short period.

Unable to reproduce the process Ma Tiande had once undergone, Li Fan could only proceed through constant trial and error, starting from the most basic Dao Net pattern commands.

“Every pattern command will react with the Dao Net and be monitored by the Administration Council. There are only one or two chances to make a mistake.”

“But my trial and error does not need to connect to the Dao Net. I only need to verify whether the commands formed from Dao Primal can actually be established.”

According to Ma Tiande’s memories, once the writing of a Dao Primal command succeeded, a strange and chilling energy would be sensed within the body. This energy connected with heaven and earth like a key, opening the patterns of the Dao Net.

What Li Fan needed to do now was continuously write Dao Primal commands, verify the possible arrangements and combinations of Dao Primal, and then disperse them before any of the commands could establish a connection with the Dao Net.

The difficulty lay in blocking the transmission between the Dao Primal commands and the Dao Net.

However, Li Fan soon thought of a brilliant idea.

“The Dao Net of heaven and earth is ultimately still the Great Dao of heaven and earth. The Great Dao is formless, flowing through all things. Every person and object in this world inevitably exists beneath the shadow of the Great Dao.”

“Yet there is one thing that even the Great Dao of heaven and earth has no choice but to avoid!”

A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan’s eyes. In an instant, a vague figure emerged from the Xuanhuang phantom enveloped by his divine sense.

It was the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death.

At first, Ink Death’s appearance remained identical to its original state in the Xuanhuang Realm. But under the stimulation of Li Fan’s divine sense, it rapidly began to transform.

It was as though the true Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, had descended here!

In truth, ever since Li Fan had entered the Dao through the Celestial Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, his divine sense alone had been enough to evolve the Ink Death Celestial Spirit. However, this strand of divine sense that had infiltrated the Shuo Star Sea was far too weak. Even if he pushed it with all his strength, it would likely be consumed very quickly in its confrontation with the Dao Net.

To fight a prolonged battle, he still needed to draw upon the energy within the Xuanhuang phantom.

“The Ink Death Celestial Spirit is not the Dao Annihilation Calamity itself. Rather, it is the product of the fusion between the black qi of Dao Annihilation and the Heavenly Dao and Great Dao. It is both a part of the Great Dao and possesses certain characteristics of Dao Annihilation.”

“As long as the disturbance is not especially violent, it should be able to block the Dao Net’s perception.”

In the blink of an eye, the Ink Death Celestial Spirit transformed into vast expanses of black shadow, completely sealing off the area around Li Fan.

Li Fan sat cross legged within the black void and began attempting to combine Dao Primal symbols.

In the Shuo Star Sea, Dao Primal symbols were used to resonate with the power of the Great Dao of heaven and earth. According to Qin Zhuang’s memories, the combination of several hundred Dao Primal symbols could unleash might comparable to True Immortal seal script.

But now, through his firsthand experiments in the Blissful City, Li Fan discovered that all of the original combinations had already become ineffective.

Or rather, they seemed to have undergone some kind of upgrade.

After trying all possible combinations at the scale of hundreds of Dao Primal symbols, not the slightest fluctuation of energy could be produced. Li Fan gradually increased the scale of his experiments.

Thousands, tens of thousands, hundreds of thousands...

It was not until the synchronized dance of a million Dao Primal symbols that they finally seemed to form a key and insert it into the correct keyhole.

The instant the combination was completed, Li Fan indeed sensed a strand of energy that spread outward with lightning speed.

Even with his current reaction speed, he had no time to stop it.

Yet when this strand of energy approached the shadows formed from the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, it seemed to have encountered some extremely terrifying predator, and suddenly stopped moving forward.

After trying several times, it continued to hover there, unable to advance!

Li Fan’s plan had indeed worked.

The birth and transmission of the Dao Primal command were astonishingly fast, even far exceeding Li Fan’s perception speed.

But ultimately, they were no match for Dao Annihilation.

Unable to break through the Ink Death Celestial Spirit’s blockade, it was firmly confined within the black spherical shadow surrounding Li Fan.

Li Fan watched the strange strand of energy writhing like a frightened snake that had been disturbed, but he did not immediately try to capture it. Instead, he observed it from a distance.

“Dao Net pattern, basic level command...”

This strand of energy seemed almost like a living creature, possessing its own lifespan. Trapped and unable to advance, it gradually went from flourishing to weakening, slowly declining before finally dissipating completely within the shadows.

Li Fan recorded this successful result.

“According to Ma Tiande’s memories, this is still one of the simplest ones. It belongs to the most basic introductory stage.”

“The practical commands required to connect to and manipulate the Dao Net are a hundred times more complicated than this. Some are even stacked combinations of multiple commands...”

However, now that he had found a way to conduct the experiments, no matter how complicated they were, they could no longer stump Li Fan.

Within the shadow sphere, Dao Primal symbols rapidly gathered and dispersed, evolving into countless different forms.

From time to time, electric like energy signals emerged from within, only to be intercepted by the Ink Death Celestial Spirit.

Before each one vanished, Li Fan recorded it.

Gradually, Li Fan finally gained a preliminary grasp of the command method used by the so called Dao Net patterns.

Just as he was preparing to attempt a higher level command, Li Fan inexplicably sensed a trace of danger in his heart.

He abruptly stopped the movement he was about to make.

“The Ink Death blockade should not have any problems.”

“Could it be that those energy signals that seemed to have dissipated had not truly disappeared, but continued to exist within this space?”

Li Fan quickly found a possible cause.

His gaze was like a torch as he stared closely, sweeping over the area again and again.

Yet he still failed to detect anything unusual.

After pondering for a while, Li Fan changed his approach.

“The one who tied the bell must be the one to untie it. It should be related to the Dao Net’s power system. Then I will use the corresponding method to resolve it.”

With a thought, countless Dao Primal symbols were generated.

Under Li Fan’s deliberate control, they did not form any meaningful pattern commands.

They simply floated and drifted continuously, as if attracting something.

They circled around for a while, yet nothing came of it.

The sense of danger in Li Fan’s heart, however, did not diminish at all. Thus, he changed to another arrangement.

After patiently experimenting, he finally obtained a result.

Within the space sealed by the Ink Death shadows, a dim fluctuation suddenly appeared. The Dao Primal symbols generated by Li Fan acted like a magnet, pulling out the command hidden in the darkness!

Li Fan narrowed his eyes. The Ink Death shadows transformed into chains and rapidly wrapped around and locked down the command.

“So, every time a previous command naturally dies out, part of it remains behind. If too many of these remnants accumulate at the same time, they will spontaneously combine into a completely new warning command. And...”

This warning message composed of remnants of Dao Primal commands was unlike the other commands. It was not suppressed by the Ink Death Celestial Spirit.

Even under the suppression of the shadows, it continued to violently surge and resist. The chains shook violently, as if the warning message could break free at any moment.

“This pattern command does not exist in Ma Tiande’s memories. It should circulate only within the Dao Net itself. It is used to...”

“To warn of the Dao Annihilation Calamity!”

In an instant, Li Fan analyzed the seemingly somewhat “ferocious” combination of Dao Primal symbols before him.

This command was somewhat different from the ones Li Fan had previously reproduced.

It did not dissipate as time passed. Instead, it seemed that some invisible force was maintaining its existence.

Moreover, Li Fan faintly sensed that this command would continuously copy and strengthen itself according to the length of time it existed.

Around the warning command, light and shadows began to fluctuate.

Under the summons of some force, a new warning command spontaneously assembled.

“Looks like leaving it here will not work either.”

“One warning cannot break through Ink Death’s blockade. But if there are too many...”

Li Fan’s heart tightened, and the Ink Death shadows began charging toward the warning signal bound at the center.

Under the devouring of countless shadows, the warning signal’s vitality proved extraordinarily tenacious.

Li Fan spent nearly three days and three nights before finally swallowing it completely.

Had it not been for an energy supply equivalent to that of an entire Ascendant State Xuanhuang Realm, he might not even have been able to outlast the other party!

“This is probably the underlying logic of the Dao Net. It is also one of the fundamental reasons why the civilizations beyond the High Wall are able to barely survive the Dao Annihilation Calamity!” Li Fan was deeply shaken.

The power of Ink Death was him, and he was Ink Death.

As he devoured the warning signal, Li Fan also vaguely sensed the other party’s true nature.

If Ink Death was an attempt to side with the Dao Annihilation and ‘learn from the barbarians to control the barbarians,’ then this underlying warning signal had pushed the concept of resistance to its absolute limit.

Li Fan seemed to see a figure that remained resolute and unyielding even amid wave after wave of the surging Dao Annihilation Calamity.

He seemed to see an indomitable will that remained standing even as the Great Dao crushed everything and all things collapsed, supporting the heavens that were on the verge of falling.

Almost instantly, the image of the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar that he had once seen appeared in Li Fan’s eyes. And from the Dao Net warning signal just now, he had truly sensed an aura that was extremely similar!

“It is not an illusion!”

Li Fan repeatedly savored the sensation. After confirming it, he was filled with astonishment and doubt.

“The reason the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar could become a Celestial Pillar was because of the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal within the stone tablet. Heaven and earth are like a net, while the Great Dao is like a pillar. Although the Nameless True Immortal had merged with the Dao and escaped from it, after his death, he could still return to heaven and earth and once again transform into something that supported them.”

“And this Dao Net can do the same…”

His wariness faded.

Carrying his conjecture, Li Fan continued testing the Dao Net’s pattern commands.

All Dao Net commands were built upon the most basic operations.

After mastering the fundamental commands, with Ma Tiande’s memories as an aid, Li Fan’s progress accelerated like a snowball gathering momentum.

Three days before the opening of the Blissful City Grand Ceremony, Li Fan’s Dao Net operating skills had finally caught up with, and even surpassed, Ma Tiande’s.

However, he had never formally connected to the Dao Net before. His judgment was based solely on the speed and proficiency with which he could generate Dao Net commands.

“What exactly does the Dao Net look like? Let me have a glimpse of it.”

Around him, the shadows of Ink Death quietly withdrew, lifting the blockade.

From this moment onward, every command Li Fan formed would connect to the Dao Net. Li Fan had also already prepared himself for the possibility that his possession would be exposed, ready to sever his connection and abandon this body at any moment to preserve himself.

With a solemn expression, as his thoughts turned, a pattern formed before Li Fan.

This was something that had never appeared while the area was under blockade.

Li Fan sensed an indescribable force quietly descending from heaven and earth. It even ignored the blockade imposed by Blissful City Lord Zhu Xueshi and enveloped his body.

His field of vision instantly rose infinitely higher. Blissful City rapidly shrank, becoming little more than a black dot before disappearing from his perception.

A seven-colored great net interwoven with countless hues stood across heaven and earth.

However, this net did not fill every corner of his vision. Instead, it was actually somewhat tattered and incomplete!

Not to mention the countless enormous holes scattered throughout it.

Just the several vast regions that were completely missing were enough to make one's heart tremble.

Underneath this seven-colored net, Li Fan could faintly glimpse the true Great Dao of heaven and earth.

The [Dao Net] was like a sheet of white paper, imprinted and laid across the interwoven Great Dao of heaven and earth.

Through this connection, it became something that mortal cultivators could control.

Li Fan also saw countless seven-colored halos gathering within the damaged regions of the Dao Net. They seemed to be attempting to repair the gaps.

And beyond the large blank regions of the Dao Net, there faintly existed an invisible giant hand. It continued pressing the sheet of white paper that was the [Dao Net] toward the location of the Great Dao of heaven and earth.

This caused those existences that had not yet been “imprinted” to gradually emerge upon the Dao Net.

Even though they appeared extremely ugly and crude at first glance.

Li Fan was still deeply captivated by the magnificent sight before him.

“This is the scene of man prevailing over heaven.”

“The myriad Great Daos are formless and shadowless, ethereal and beyond reach. Yet through such an inconceivable method, they can be transformed into constructs that humans can manipulate…”

While Li Fan marveled inwardly, he did not forget the operation that had to be performed when connecting to the Dao Net for the first time.

With the Blissful City Grand Ceremony about to begin, according to convention, an inspection of the Dao Net connection and coverage in the area where Blissful City was located was required.

Naturally, Ma Tiande did not need to handle the maintenance work himself.

He only needed to send out a notification signal and then cooperate with the relevant cultivators.

Li Fan saw seven-colored streams of light fly over from the depths of the Dao Net. Following the signal he had transmitted, they first traveled toward the several small points corresponding to the location of Blissful City.

After making a circuit, they quickly returned.

Next, another stream of light suddenly appeared. This one flew in from another direction of the Dao Net.

It continued probing the area where Blissful City was located.

This cycle repeated again and again.

Li Fan silently watched everything, and a strange feeling suddenly arose in his heart, a sense that it was both advanced and backward.

“It is actually called the Dao Net, yet it still requires human assistance…”

“Hmm?”

Li Fan’s gaze suddenly sharpened as he looked toward the Dao Net.

Perhaps it was merely an illusion, but during the Dao Net’s operation and inspection of Blissful City, this entire sheet of “white paper” covering the Great Dao of heaven and earth.

It actually seemed to rise and fall continuously, as though being blown by a gentle breeze.

And even…

It seemed as though it could be peeled away at any moment!

Yet outside the [Dao Net], the shadow resembling a palm remained firmly pressing it down.

Preventing it from truly separating from the Great Dao of heaven and earth.

“So unstable…”

“This Dao Net does not seem to be in a stable state either.”

Li Fan pondered thoughtfully.

Apart from that invisible giant hand, Li Fan also sensed that there were other forces within the Dao Net maintaining its stability.

Li Fan observed carefully, but ultimately found nothing.

The inspection of Blissful City was about to conclude.

Just before disconnecting from the Dao Net, inspiration suddenly struck Li Fan.

“Nameless True Immortal?”

He abruptly turned his gaze toward several important nodes within the Dao Net.

Next

Chapter 1439: Consciousness Soul Transformation

Li Fan faintly sensed an aura similar to that of the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar from several crucial nodes of the Dao Net.

Before he had time to think about it further, the magnificent [Dao Net] before his eyes suddenly faded and vanished from his sight.

Within Blissful City, the bustling and noisy sounds seemed to disappear collectively for an instant. Then, they immediately returned to normal. Perhaps only Li Fan, the cultivator responsible for maintaining and overseeing the Dao Net, or the Blissful City Lord, could sense the changes that had occurred above Blissful City.

If the Dao Net was equivalent to a sheet of white paper imprinted upon the surface of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, then maintaining the Dao Net was equivalent to splashing ink onto that sheet of white paper and then pressing down upon it with force.

"The traces of the Dao Net are becoming increasingly clear. Everything within the net is tightly bound and unable to escape."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes, seemingly seeing that sinister face of Scholar Zhu. Yet he did not stop the Dao Net from continuing to erode Blissful City. He merely watched in silence, allowing his own 'toy' to sink deeper and deeper into this net.

After the rumbling of the Dao Net disappeared, Scholar Zhu cast one final glance toward where Li Fan was.

Li Fan slightly cupped his hands, deliberately putting on a nervous expression.

Only after a full three breaths did Scholar Zhu withdraw his gaze. His figure disappeared into the city.

Li Fan paid no attention to the other's threat. He instead temporarily cast aside all distracting thoughts and recalled every detail of the [Dao Net] he had witnessed moments ago.

As one of the Dao Net's grassroots operators, Ma Tiande was not merely a disposable tool. In truth, being able to connect to and observe the Dao Net from close range was itself an unparalleled opportunity dreamed of by every cultivator.

The Dao had no form and could not be seen.

Cultivators sought it from above and below precisely because they wished to approach the Dao, the origin of all things.

Although Ma Tiande was supposedly a [Dao Controller], his actual cultivation was only equivalent to the Nascent Soul realm. At such a level, let alone seeing the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, even comprehending one among the countless Great Daos was far beyond his reach.

Yet precisely because of his special identity as a [Dao Controller], he could frequently glimpse the Dao Net, which was also a projection of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth.

"Ma Tiande's aptitude is simply too ordinary. If he were in the Darkest Star Sea, he probably would not even be able to step into the Qi Refining stage. Moreover, the Shuo Star Sea Council deliberately never taught him any concepts or knowledge related to cultivation. So even after coming this close to the Great Dao countless times, his cultivation remains mediocre."

"He possesses a supreme treasure without even knowing it."

Li Fan sighed before slowly digesting the profound insights lingering within his mind.

"The True Immortal seal script is a retelling by the Inscription Dao Immortal. By studying the True Immortal seal script, people can glimpse the Great Dao of the world through the eyes of the Inscription Dao Immortal."

"But the Dao Net is directly the shadow of the Great Dao. Or rather, it is a replica."

"It is even closer to the [Dao] itself than the True Immortal seal script."

"Remarkable!"

Even Li Fan, who had seen and experienced countless things, could not help but praise it sincerely.

Li Fan's comprehension was naturally incomparable to Ma Tiande's.

His inspiration surged more violently than ever upon this first glimpse. Li Fan compared and verified the scattered fragments of the Great Dao he had seen through various channels in the past against the genuine Dao Net.

His comprehension of the [Dao] rose steadily, like waves upon an enraged sea, each wave higher than the last.

In a sense, this was the accumulation of countless years finally erupting. It was also the inevitable result of everything falling naturally into place.

Although it was merely a strand of divine sense, once it reached the point of merging with the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, it could undergo a transformation capable of turning decay into something miraculous.

That strand of divine sense was like a thin membrane. Countless blurry lines, interwoven vertically and horizontally, emerged upon its surface.

They were astonishingly similar to the Dao Net Li Fan had seen, resembling it by seventy to eighty percent.

It was clearly Li Fan using his divine sense as paper and making another imprint upon the Dao Net, this replica of the Great Dao itself.

Although this was a replica of a replica, its original source was ultimately the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth.

Even though the membrane formed by Li Fan's divine sense had barely managed to reproduce the most basic shape, it was already sufficient to manifest genuine power belonging to the Great Dao.

Light flickered across the membrane, seemingly resonating with the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth from afar.

As if containing infinite vitality and driving force, the flickering light and shadows gradually filled out Li Fan's ‘weak’ divine sense.

Slowly, this strand of divine sense actually began to hatch into a complete image of Li Fan.

Only the details remained somewhat blurry.

"This is... the Consciousness Soul Transformation?"

At the instant the Consciousness Soul Transformation was born, Li Fan's true body, far away in the Xuanhuang Realm, also underwent a drastic change in expression. He sensed everything that had occurred within Blissful City in the Shuo Star Sea.

Clearly, he had already severed all connections with his divine sense. There was no longer any connection through the power of the Dreamland Paradise, and dozens of Darkest Star Seas separated them. An invisible High Wall even stood between them.

Yet Li Fan was still able to establish a synchronized connection with this phantom manifestation of his divine sense.

Li Fan's expression gradually became more interesting.

"It is not a direct connection between the two. Instead, the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth is serving as an intermediary."

"Everything in the world, from top to bottom, is a projection of the Great Dao. The position of the part belonging to me within the projection of the Great Dao is unique."

"Although they are separated by an extremely great distance, with all kinds of methods obstructing the space between them, as long as both exist beneath this same Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, nothing can affect this synchronization."

Li Fan quickly understood why this seemingly unbelievable result was possible.

Relying on his profound understanding of the Great Dao, he had actually used the Dao Net as a template to construct another miniature Dao Net within himself.

At the same time as it connected with the manifestation of his divine sense in the Shuo Star Sea, the divine sense of Li Fan's true body also underwent the corresponding transformation.

The difference between the true body and the separated divine sense did not cause a clearer pattern to manifest upon that thin membrane. Everything depended upon one's understanding of the Great Dao.

Within his sea of consciousness, after the Consciousness Soul Transformation was born, Li Fan's phantom figure stood there.

The Dao Deviation Stone suddenly erupted with dazzling blue light. It then transformed into countless rays of light that surged into the phantom figure.

They further completed the pattern of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth upon it.

Some of the rays from the Dao Deviation Stone perfectly merged into the phantom of the Consciousness Soul Transformation. Others circled around it but failed to find a suitable place to reside. Unable to usurp another's position, they wandered about aimlessly several times before emerging from the phantom once again.

They then reformed into a Dao Deviation Stone.

However, because part of the Great Dao composing it was now missing, the quality of this Dao Deviation Stone had already fallen from the Stone Mother level to middle grade.

But this was not what Li Fan cared about.

As long as he was given some time, he could repeatedly stack the Transformations Between Truth and Falsehood and restore the quality of the Dao Deviation Stone with ease.

"The phenomenon displayed by the Dao Deviation Stone further confirms my previous judgment."

"The Dao Deviation Stone Mother truly is the manifestation of an entire possible Great Dao. It is just that the Great Dao of that possibility is different from the [Yuanchu] where I reside. It is even considerably weaker. Therefore, only a portion of the Great Dao within the Dao Deviation Stone emerged and filled out the image of the Great Dao within my Consciousness Soul Transformation."

"Even so, with the supplementation of the Dao Deviation Stone, this miniature Dao Net I constructed went from being barely recognizable to taking shape in an instant."

Li Fan's mind wandered within the Great Dao Net contained in his divine sense, completely unable to extricate itself.

Because it had received a great deal of external assistance, Li Fan was temporarily unable to achieve the feeling of controlling it as effortlessly as his own limbs. This was both an advantage and a disadvantage.

However, since it had ultimately been constructed through Li Fan's divine sense, he still possessed absolute control over it.

After being immersed in it for a long time, Li Fan finally returned to his senses and let out a long breath.

"This is already very close to the theoretical Consciousness Soul Transformation."

"Using one's own mind to bear the shadow of the Great Dao."

"By some fortuitous coincidence, I actually skipped the several steps preceding the New Cultivation Method and directly arrived at this stage."

"Although it still has some flaws..."

If it were the theoretically perfect state of the New New Method, then what appeared upon Li Fan's divine sense projection at this moment would not merely be a deformed and distorted imitation painting. Instead, his divine sense would be like a mirror, reflecting and manifesting within itself the entirety of the Heavenly Dao.

"From the lowest level, my divine sense traces upward, all the way back to the origin of the Great Dao. Every thought and every constant dwelling carries the ability to draw upon the power of the Great Dao."

"Unfortunately, what I am reflecting is merely an incomplete and fragmented replica."

Li Fan felt slightly regretful.

However, he also knew that even at his current Half-Immortal realm, attempting to gaze into and probe the true Great Dao of this world was absolutely nothing more than wishful thinking.

"With a counterfeit manifested with the assistance of an unparalleled expert, it is still a suitable substitute for the time being."

After manifesting a miniature Dao Net through his thoughts, the benefits were certainly not limited to being able to communicate in real time regardless of distance.

At the very least, Li Fan now possessed an ability like that of the Immortal Hunter cultivators from the Shuo Star Sea, allowing him to directly draw upon the power of the Great Dao to fight his enemies.

"However, my miniature Dao Net is worlds apart in power from that [Dao Net] constructed by the Shuo Star Sea."

"But in terms of efficiency, it is still more than a hundred times higher than directly using True Immortal seal script."

Now that Li Fan possessed a Dao Net of his own, he naturally understood the difference in operation between it and conventional methods.

"Perhaps only now, after the destruction of the Immortal Realm and with the various immortals no longer appearing, can this method be used without risk."

"If this were ancient times, when several Nameless True Immortals had merged with the Great Dao, then if I used the Dao Net to draw upon the power of the Great Dao, those existences dwelling within the Great Dao would inevitably sense it."

"I would become the common enemy of all Nameless True Immortals. At the very beginning of proving the Dao, I would probably already invite their united pursuit. I would truly die without even knowing how."

After integrating the Great Dao contained within the Dao Deviation Stone, even though the quality of his own Dao Deviation Stone had declined, the speed of Li Fan's thoughts was not affected in the slightest.

In an instant, countless thoughts flashed through his mind.

"For now, who knows how many Nameless True Immortals are still left. Moreover, there is another, even larger Dao Net serving as concealment and drawing their fire."

"It is fundamentally impossible to attract the attention of the Nameless True Immortals."

"Furthermore..."

An inexplicable confidence suddenly surged within Li Fan's heart.

After his Consciousness Soul Transformation and his own Dao Net was reproduced, even if he faced a Nameless True Immortal, he would no longer be completely powerless to fight back.

"A Nameless True Immortal has completely mastered a single Great Dao."

"Although my Consciousness Soul Transformation is merely a replica of an incomplete imprint of the Great Dao, and the amount of Great Dao power I can draw upon is extremely limited..."

"It has the advantage of quantity. Moreover, it is ultimately still the power of the Great Dao. Theoretically speaking, the power used by me and the Nameless True Immortals is of the same nature. The only difference is that my strength is much smaller."

Li Fan gradually understood.

This was by no means the result of becoming arrogant after a lucky breakthrough.

Rather, it was a reasonable deduction built upon his understanding of the Great Dao.

"This is still only an incomplete Consciousness Soul Transformation."

"If the New New Method truly reached its ideal state, perhaps Consciousness Soul Transformation could genuinely contend with the Nameless True Immortals."

"If the Soul Transformation realm is already like this, then what about Star Sea Dao Integration and Dao Resonance Longevity?"

"Could those be the realms occupied by Darkened Heaven Doctor and Evil Su Bai?"

Li Fan could hardly suppress his imagination.

Only after a long while did the thoughts surging through his mind finally recede like a tide.

"The reason I was able to achieve Consciousness Soul Transformation was purely due to a fortuitous combination of circumstances."

"Over the course of my past hundred reincarnations, I not only witnessed numerous True Immortal seal script characters, but also glimpsed the complete Immortal Domain, the leg bone of a True Immortal, and even the descent of the black qi and the annihilation of the Great Dao. Witnessing the birth and destruction of the Dao allowed my comprehension of the Great Dao to accumulate to a level beyond what ordinary people could imagine."

"Even all of this alone would still have been far from enough. With the [Dao Net] of the Shuo Star Sea serving as a stepping stone, I was finally able to transform all my accumulated knowledge from the past into reality. A replica of a replica is naturally even farther removed from the original, but it is the best method for allowing a 'beginner' like me to carry out an imitation."

"Beyond that, the most crucial factor is..."

Li Fan's divine sense projection examined every part of himself.

For the first time, he truly faintly sensed the existence of [Truth].

It was as unfathomable as the Heavenly Dao itself. In Li Fan's perception, it was merely a vague figure that was difficult to describe. Yet Li Fan, who had accompanied [Truth] for so long, was absolutely certain.

That was [Truth].

"The Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood contained within [Truth] actually seems to encompass all the Great Daos of Heaven and Earth."

"As my comprehension of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood continues to deepen, in a certain sense, I am also continuously deepening my perception of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth."

"Now, I can already temporarily maintain the existence of objects under [Falsehood into Truth]. Perhaps this is precisely the fundamental reason why I can use my divine sense projection to accept a Great Dao whose possibility is perhaps complete."

"If it were someone else..."

Li Fan pondered for a moment before slowly shaking his head.

"Only a peerless genius, with the right time, place, and people all coming together, might be able to accomplish this."

"I originally thought I had truly opened up the path of the New New Method."

"I never expected that I was still relying on [Truth] to cheat, directly skipping the preceding stages. This is merely an isolated case and cannot be replicated."

However, Li Fan had never cared whether other people could cultivate this method.

As long as it worked on him, that was enough.

"Although this is only an incomplete Consciousness Soul Transformation, it has inadvertently pointed out the direction for my next step."

"Come into contact with the [Dao Net] as much as possible, allowing my imitation to become more complete."

"The Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood and my comprehension of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth must advance hand in hand. By reflecting the Dao Net, I can gradually approach the source of the Great Dao..."

Li Fan's heart was filled with indescribable joy.

Although the Immortal Path had been severed, under the inspiration of the civilization of the Shuo Star Sea beyond the High Wall, he had unexpectedly found a new possible path in such a short period of time.

"This is not merely something that can be accomplished by a mortal through the efforts of a single lifetime."

"Setting aside the involvement of True Immortals for now, there is also the difference in the flow of time between within and beyond the High Wall..."

Li Fan slightly furrowed his brows as he began calculating.
