Next

Chapter 1440: The Death Of The Nameless Immortal

The flow of time within the Darkest Star Sea was inherently inconsistent due to the existence of the Immortal Ruins. Thus, Li Fan only compared the Xuanhuang Realm with the Blissful City of the Shuo Star Sea.

Under normal circumstances, the difference was astonishingly as much as one hundred and twenty times!

In other words, more than one hundred and twenty years could pass in the Shuo Star Sea while barely a single year passed in the Xuanhuang Realm.

"With a time flow more than a hundred times faster, it is no wonder the difference between the inside and outside of the High Wall is so enormous."

"Even the current state of the Shuo Star Sea is already vastly different from when Qin Zhuang left. Truly, times have changed."

After initially constructing a projection of the Great Dao and a miniature Dao Net within himself, Li Fan's understanding of the Dao had already reached an entirely new stage.

Thus, when faced with this bizarre phenomenon of differing time flows, Li Fan naturally began to harbor doubts.

"In theory, when the Hunger character art is comprehended to its utmost limit, it can indeed produce such an effect. But..."

"That should be a manifestation that only an Immortal who proved the Nameless Dao through Hunger would possess."

"Judging from the manifestations of the Hunger Immortal I have witnessed, it is obvious that she has not yet reached such a realm."

Various scenes of the Hunger Immortal he had witnessed in the past flashed through Li Fan's mind.

When Cat Treasure appeared in the world, she had been captured in a sorry state. When the black tide of qi that heralded the Dao Annihilation Calamity appeared, rather than the calamity itself descending, she had already been terrified at the sight of it. It seemed that she could only rely on the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar to survive the calamity. Even when Evil Su Bai approached her extremely closely and stepped on her face, she remained completely unaware.

"She may be only a single step away from becoming Nameless. But the difference contained within that single step manifests externally as a difference between heaven and earth!"

"The abnormal flow of time in the Darkest Star Sea should not have been caused by the Hunger Immortal. Or rather, only a small part of it may be related to her comprehension of the Dao."

"The greater reason seems to originate from the Immortal Ruins themselves."

Li Fan recalled the scenes he had witnessed within the Immortal Ruins.

Aside from the vast stretches of ruins and remnants, the most striking feature was the enormous character, [Hunger], engraved upon the earth.

Li Fan had previously speculated that this character, Hunger, might have been personally inscribed by the Inscription Dao Immortal. Now, it seemed that this character might contain another profound secret.

"The Hunger Immortal can break free from the shackles on her body at any time. Those golden chains were clearly restraints she placed upon herself. The Immortal Ruins were once the location of a passage leading to the Immortal Realm. When the Dao Annihilation Calamity struck the Immortal Realm, the Great Dao collapsed upon itself and all things disintegrated. The surviving immortals fled desperately from the Immortal Realm into the lower realm through the Immortal ascension passage."

Li Fan reconstructed and deduced the scenes from that time.

"The Dao Annihilation Calamity was powerful enough to destroy even the Immortal Realm. At that time, who could guarantee that this terrifying calamity would not spread downward through the ascension passage? In theory, the farther away one fled, the better. Yet the Hunger Immortal stopped in such a dangerous place. She even disregarded the danger and began comprehending the Dao right there..."

"In the past, I overlooked the aspects of this that did not make sense."

Li Fan's gaze involuntarily shifted once again toward the direction of the Immortal Ruins above his head.

"If the Hunger Immortal truly needed to rely on the True Immortal seal script engraved upon the earth to comprehend the Dao, she could simply have carried away the entire piece of land along with the character. She could have secluded herself in a safe place and comprehended the Dao afterward."

"Yet she did not do so."

"The Dao Annihilation Calamity suddenly came to a halt. It did not spread into the lower realm. The phenomenon caused by the True Immortal seal script on the land of the Immortal Ruins had even reached the level of the peak of Dao comprehension, the realm of the Nameless Immortal. And the leg bone of the Nameless Immortal that served as the Immortal Celestial Pillar..."

These numerous clues passed through Li Fan's mind one by one. Eventually, they became completely connected, pointing toward the possibility closest to the truth.

"The Hunger Immortal did not move that character, [Hunger], not because she was unwilling. She simply could not. Because beneath that character, or perhaps the character itself, was transformed from the remains of a Nameless True Immortal!"

"This Nameless Immortal, whether willingly or by force, in any case, after falling, returned to the Great Dao and transformed their body into a Celestial Pillar. They temporarily held back the collapsing heavens of the Immortal Realm!"

"And precisely because the Nameless Immortal who proved the Dao through Hunger died, the one within the Immortal Ruins had the intention of comprehending the Hunger character and proving the Nameless Dao anew!"

Li Fan's heart trembled as his thoughts once again spread outward.

Given the boundlessness of the Immortal Realm and the lower realm star sea, there was a very high probability that the so called ascension passages were not limited to a single location.

Judging from the current state of the lower realm star sea, although the vast majority of regions had already been destroyed by the Dao Annihilation Calamity, quite a few surviving regions still remained.

"Perhaps this is the protection of other Nameless True Immortals!"

"They used themselves as pillars to withstand the overwhelming calamity descending from above."

Li Fan let out a long breath.

"If the High Wall is the fallen ruins and broken remnants of what once stood, then the Nameless Immortals are the pillars above it."

"With the protection of Nameless True Immortals, the Darkest Star Sea has already become this desolate..."

"Perhaps the lower realm star sea is able to continue sustaining life precisely because of the sacrifices made by the Nameless True Immortals."

Nameless True Immortals had entered the Dao with their very bodies. Compared with ordinary beings, or even True Immortals, they could be said to have become an entirely different species.

But this was merely Li Fan's own view.

Perhaps among these Nameless True Immortals were people naturally noble like Su Bai, willing to sacrifice themselves to save the living beings of heaven and earth, even if only temporarily.

Or perhaps the immortals who had fled downward had already foreseen that the Dao Annihilation Calamity would pursue them. For the sake of their own survival, they needed some people to step forward and "sacrifice themselves", buying everyone enough time to escape.

In any case, some Nameless True Immortals had transformed into pillars standing between heaven and earth, temporarily holding back the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

However, this method could only preserve life for a time. It could not provide protection for eternity.

The Dao Annihilation Calamity could only be delayed. It could never disappear.

"Behind the Dao Net, there are faint traces of these immortals' handiwork."

"Some of the Dao Net nodes I have glimpsed even carry the aura of the remains of Nameless True Immortals."

Yet Li Fan was not surprised by this.

The miniature Dao Net within Li Fan's divine sense was merely a projection. He deeply understood just how difficult it was to make a real, tangible creation conform to the ethereal and intangible Great Dao.

"When the Dao Net operates, it is constantly shifting and unstable."

"The enormous hand I glimpsed outside the Dao Net, which was used to stabilize it, can only serve as supplementary support. That hand clearly does not manifest constantly. The things truly anchoring the Dao Net..."

Li Fan seemed to see that Dao Net stretching across heaven and earth once again.

All the other insignificant lines disappeared.

Only several of the most crucial nodes remained.

They belonged to the Dao Net, yet at the same time, they existed within the Great Dao of heaven and earth!

It was precisely because of the peculiar nature of these nodes that the Dao Net could remain firmly affixed to the invisible and formless Great Dao of heaven and earth!

"Nameless True Immortals!"

"There is even a very high possibility that they are living Nameless True Immortals!"

After his divine sense had transformed into a spirit, Li Fan once again peered into the true secrets of the Dao Net.

In his field of vision, the few remaining Dao Net nodes unexpectedly began to faintly manifest the indistinct forms of human figures.

Then, as if sensing Li Fan's gaze, they suddenly opened their eyes without warning!

One Nameless True Immortal after another seemed to reveal their true forms.

All of them turned their gazes toward Li Fan!

An inexplicable sense of terror surged through Li Fan's heart. Startled, he immediately broke free from the illusion.

The human figures were gone. The nodes seemed to be nothing more than ordinary nodes.

Yet Li Fan's heart remained far from calm.

Was it merely an illusion?

Li Fan did not think so.

Having cultivated to his current realm, even a genuine Immortal-grade illusion formation would already find it extremely difficult to affect him.

Nor would hallucinations inexplicably appear before his eyes.

“The most likely explanation is…”

“The nodes of the Nameless True Immortals simultaneously exist within both the Dao Net and the Great Dao.”

“The projections of the Great Dao within my Consciousness Soul Transformation have likewise reflected these nodes into existence.”

“So, in a certain sense, I truly did see them!”

With a turn of his thoughts, Li Fan reached this conclusion.

Soon, another question surfaced in his mind.

Since Li Fan could see them, could these Nameless True Immortals see Li Fan as well?

Inevitably, a wave of unease surged through Li Fan's heart.

He considered himself capable of contending with the Nameless True Immortals. But that did not mean he could remain completely composed under the gaze of several Nameless True Immortals.

Although his spiritual intuition, which had always been extremely accurate, was not giving him any warning of danger at this moment, and although he had already been nailed into the Dao Net and become a “living anchor” node, the Nameless True Immortal existences Li Fan sensed still made him feel as though he were sitting on pins and needles, with thorns pricking his back.

But for Li Fan to give up the power of Consciousness Soul Transformation that he had already obtained simply because this danger had yet to arrive was absolutely impossible!

Li Fan's true body rapidly turned over various thoughts in his mind as he considered how to respond.

Meanwhile, in Blissful City of the Shuo Star Sea, Li Fan's divine sense incarnated as Ma Tiande had already begun preparing for the Blissful City Grand Ceremony.

The all-range communication ability of Consciousness Soul Transformation allowed his divine sense to disregard the restrictions of spacetime and instantly know everything his true body had deduced.

But those troubles could be left to his true body to bear.

What his divine sense needed to do now was continue lying low within Blissful City and further investigate the Dao Net.

But first, he had to get through the Blissful City Grand Ceremony right before him.

Scholar Zhu was clearly extremely resistant to the Dao Net's erosion. However, for some reason, he would not directly turn hostile. Instead, he would vent his anger upon Ma Tiande, who appeared before him and was directly responsible for carrying out tasks.

A cultivator like Ma Tiande, although wearing the identity of a Dao Net operator, was still nothing more than an ant in Scholar Zhu's eyes.

Li Fan sat quietly within Ma Tiande's residence, pondering how to escape.

“This time, the Saber Island side did not show Scholar Zhu any respect. Not a single person came. The Sword Domain, on the other hand, sent three cultivators to participate.”

“The identities of these three people are still unknown, but as representatives of the top power in the Shuo Star Sea, their lives should not be as precarious as mine.” Li Fan's divine sense mocked himself inwardly.

“Throughout the entire Shuo Star Sea, only the Sword Domain is participating. Although it is called a grand ceremony, in reality, it is merely Scholar Zhu amusing himself. And we are his toys.”

Within Ma Tiande's memories were scenes from grand ceremonies of the past.

After spending three years indulging themselves in pleasure within Blissful City, the living beings would begin slaughtering one another without restraint. Only when just a few survivors remained would the slaughter finally stop.

These survivors might even rejoice at having escaped from an endless hell. But they would never know that they were merely enticing fruits on Scholar Zhu's plate.

“Everyone invited to participate in the grand ceremony can devour these fruits of slaughter.”

“They truly are extraordinarily precious divine medicines. Even though my true body has already reached the realm of Consciousness Soul Transformation, they are still greatly beneficial. But for the weak, they are nothing short of deadly poison!”

Li Fan understood Scholar Zhu's intentions perfectly.

Yet he was not so petty as to insist on putting Ma Tiande to death.

It was merely a fruit that could absolutely be considered a heavenly treasure. Who could blame him if he could not withstand it?

“With my divine sense now strengthened, consuming the fruit of slaughter should not be a problem.”

“The difficult part is how to swallow the fruit safely without arousing suspicion from Scholar Zhu or the Sword Domain cultivators at the grand ceremony. After all, judging from the strength Ma Tiande has displayed, there is absolutely no reason for him to survive the fruit of slaughter.”

Although abandoning Ma Tiande's identity would not cause any significant loss, Li Fan's divine sense was unwilling to disappear in such a humiliating manner.

“Perhaps the opportunity lies within the Dao Net of Blissful City.”

'Ma Tiande' raised his head and looked at the invisible dark clouds shrouding the skies above Blissful City, a strange expression appearing on his face.

Not long afterward, three streaks of sword light descended from beyond the heavens.

“Junior Ji Xingdao!”

“Junior Situ Tiantian!”

“Junior Zhao Xing!”

“Greetings, Scholar Zhu!”

Three sonorous voices resounded across the heavens. Two men and one woman.

Their voices had barely faded into the sky when the three had already flown into Blissful City.

The City Lord's Residence suddenly materialized from nothingness.

It drew the three people from the Sword Domain inside.

At the same time, Scholar Zhu's somewhat coquettish voice reached Li Fan's ears.

“Come quickly and help me receive our guests.”

A wave of disgust immediately surged through Li Fan's heart.

Following Ma Tiande's past habits and mannerisms, he did not conceal it, allowing the disgust to appear directly on his face.

He knew that this was Scholar Zhu's most psychologically twisted and unfathomable state.

Li Fan proceeded toward the City Lord's Residence with extreme caution.

By the time he entered the residence, the three people from the Sword Domain were already seated.

Li Fan glanced at them. All three looked extremely young. They could even be described as somewhat childish. Their eyes were clear and foolish, with almost no signs of cunning or worldly experience.

Clearly, the Sword Domain intended to use the fruits of slaughter refined in Blissful City to give these three youngsters some tempering.

“Even with such a method of rapid cultivation, even if they can withstand it, their temperaments will probably become somewhat twisted, perhaps even insane.”

“Three excellent seedlings. Even in our Xuanhuang Realm, they could be considered prodigies. Is the Sword Domain really willing to waste them like this?”

Without revealing anything, Li Fan first bowed to Scholar Zhu.

Then, with an arrogant expression, he merely nodded slightly toward the three people from the Sword Domain.

In theory, the Dao Net operators of the Administration Council were not inferior in status to the three people from the Sword Domain.

But in reality…

Consumable materials could be replaced without limit. Direct disciples, however, required painstaking effort to nurture.

The two were clearly not beings of equal status.

The three disciples of the Sword Domain seemed to understand this as well.

They did not become angry at Ma Tiande's attitude. Instead, they exchanged a smile, completely unconcerned.

They first happily drank the three bowls of red broth before them.

Compared to what Li Fan had tasted before, it looked even more horrifying. It contained not only eyeballs, but also the severed flesh and blood of various monsters that did not belong to humanity.

Yet the three from the Sword Domain remained completely unfazed. Their expressions did not change in the slightest as they savored it carefully.

Next

Chapter 1441: The Fruit Of All Beings’ Wills

After drinking a bowl of red soup, the expressions on the faces of the three Sword Domain cultivators began to change violently.

Clearly, not everyone could enjoy the wonders of the desires of all beings as calmly as Li Fan had upon his first taste.

Ji Xingdao's eyes were wide open, and the aura around him surged and fluctuated like turbulent waves.

Situ Tiantian's brows were tightly furrowed, her face as cold as frost. Countless flakes of frost and snow seemed to dance around her. Upon closer inspection, those white crystals were actually shaped like flying swords.

Zhao Xing was the one who performed the best among the three. He merely looked as though he had drunk a bowl of exceptionally strong liquor. A strange expression flashed across his face before quickly returning to normal.

Regardless of how the three Sword Domain cultivators were faring, Scholar Zhu rose from the main seat and slowly descended, his long robe trailing behind him.

His hunched figure, concealed beneath the robe, emitted a strange creaking and growing sound. Before long, it gradually became slender.

A plaintive, sorrowful female voice slowly began singing throughout the hall.

"The Rainbow Skirt and Feathered Garment Bliss Pavilion, the golden goblets and jade wine fill the endless feast. Drums and music shake the heavens as graceful dances whirl, while the lanterns dim and the night remains far from over..."

Scholar Zhu softly sang as he danced.

Li Fan stared intently at that graceful figure whose face could not be seen.

Accompanying Scholar Zhu's singing, the doors of the City Lord's Residence seemed to suddenly swing open. The place, which had originally been utterly silent, instantly plunged into a boiling cauldron.

The voices of countless living beings from the City of Blissful came from all directions, gathering within the residence.

In a daze, Li Fan saw everything taking place within the Blissful City.

Laughter, indulgence, fighting, lust, pleasure, delight, drunkenness, joy.

Countless scenes of living beings indulging themselves to their hearts' content exploded before Li Fan's eyes at the same time.

"The Blissful City. Blissful."

Yet everything suddenly changed along with Scholar Zhu's melody.

"When joy reaches its height, sorrow follows. Heaven and earth are drenched in blood. Blades flash as blood becomes a crimson mire. Evil spirits lurk beneath the shadows of night, while myriad ghosts are born. Heads become packed earth, bones become beams..."

His tone turned cold and sinister, his killing intent soaring to the heavens.

"Clang!"

It was like the sound of a sharp sword falling to the ground, awakening the countless living beings within the Blissful City and pulling them from their blissful dreams.

All the magnificent lanterns went out, and the Blissful City instantly fell into darkness.

Yet the darkness did not last long.

A blood red sun slowly rose from the horizon.

Its crimson light poured into the city.

The slaughter had begun.

Those who had been loving companions at each other's sides throughout the long night of bliss now suddenly drew their blades against one another, fighting until one side was dead.

Friendship, familial affection, love.

All of them became meaningless during the long night that followed.

Only survival remained, the sole eternal concern.

...

"Die, die, die, die!"

"Kill!"

"Ah!"

Screams and frenzied cries of slaughter rang out like a grand symphony within the City Lord's Residence.

Scholar Zhu had already returned from the appearance of a dancing maiden to that of a hunched old man. His face was filled with pleasure as he watched the slaughter taking place throughout the city.

Meanwhile, the three young cultivators of the Sword Domain, who had gradually recovered from their hallucinations induced by the red soup, began to turn pale and tremble as they faced the terrifying scenes in the Blissful City, where every aspect of human evil was laid bare.

Li Fan also put on an expression of extreme discomfort.

"What a pity. For such a spectacle, there should have been more people here to enjoy it."

"Yet only you juniors came."

Scholar Zhu's voice carried a hint of loneliness and desolation.

But the slaughter within the Blissful City did not stop because of this.

"Then it would be even more boring, and that would make me lose face."

Scholar Zhu's voice echoed in everyone's ears like that of a devil.

Another variable manifested within the Blissful City.

The living beings who had already been killed actually came back to life.

However, they no longer returned as living creatures. Instead, they became formless ghost shadows.

At this point, they had two choices.

The first was to directly take revenge as vengeful ghosts. This allowed them to kill their enemies outright, but at the same time, their own ghost forms would vanish completely.

The second was to possess survivors and use their bodies to carry out a complete revenge. The power of the possessed person was like the combined strength of two people, greatly increasing the chances of taking revenge.

However, those who were killed would likewise become ghost shadows.

In ghost form, they could attack one another, but the only possible outcome was mutual destruction. Thus, most would choose instead to seek out other survivors and possess them.

As the slaughter continued, the Blissful City gradually became populated only by survivors possessed by ghosts.

If a survivor possessed by a ghost shadow was defeated, no new ghost shadow would be born, and the ghost possessing them would also vanish completely.

The slaughter gradually approached its conclusion.

When only one hundred survivors remained, those who were possessed would be able to perceive the existence of the ghost shadow possessing them.

They could choose coexistence, or they could launch an all out mental attack in which neither side would stop until one was dead.

Those who defeated their ghost shadows could completely absorb the other side's power and make it their own.

"Tell me, do you think there will be more humans or more ghosts among the final victors?"

Scholar Zhu suddenly asked everyone present.

"There should ultimately be ten victors in this grand ceremony. Each of you four will receive one fruit. Whoever guesses correctly will receive an additional one as a reward."

The three Sword Domain cultivators immediately showed delighted expressions.

Their fear and reluctance when watching the slaughter in the Blissful City were instantly thrown to the back of their minds.

"I guess the ghosts will definitely outnumber the humans among the survivors," Situ Tiantian immediately declared loudly, filled with confidence.

The other two Sword Domain cultivators also gave their own judgments.

One guessed that the ghosts would win.

The other guessed that the humans would.

When it was Li Fan's turn, he pondered for a moment before answering seriously.

"Among the ten victors, I think the humans and ghosts will each account for half."

The three Sword Domain cultivators all looked at him in surprise.

Scholar Zhu gave Li Fan a meaningful glance but said nothing.

The sun climbed to the center of the sky, and the slaughter in the Blissful City continued.

Those who had survived the initial massacre had already accumulated a certain amount of survival experience.

Hiding, lying low, launching attacks from the shadows.

As a result, the pace of the slaughter gradually slowed.

However, the Blissful City itself was shrinking, forcing them to gather closer together and accelerating the game once again.

Repeated viewing brought about aesthetic fatigue. After seeing it enough times, no matter how bloody or bizarre the scenes were, they gradually seemed ordinary.

The three Sword Domain cultivators had already become numb.

No unusual expressions appeared on their faces anymore. They had even begun to feel somewhat impatient.

Only Li Fan continued to seriously observe the different reactions and choices made by every living being in the Blissful City when faced with life and death.

As promised, the Blissful City underwent one cycle every three years.

This slaughter truly lasted for three whole years.

It seemed that, behind the scenes, some mysterious force was secretly controlling the progress of this game.

During those three years, the three Sword Domain cultivators drank bowl after bowl of red soup.

By now, drinking it was no different from drinking boiled water. They felt nothing at all.

Li Fan did not receive such treatment.

During the first year, he was rewarded with a total of six bowls.

After that, Scholar Zhu deliberately ignored him.

Li Fan knew that Scholar Zhu was gradually increasing the three Sword Domain cultivators' ability to withstand it, so that they would not be unable to endure the infinite killing intent contained within the fruit when they finally consumed it and end up becoming idiots.

After all, they were people of the Sword Domain, and Scholar Zhu had to give them at least some consideration.

As for Ma Tiande...

His life or death simply depended on fate.

Three years had now passed.

The blood red sun finally disappeared beyond the horizon.

Only a final small section of the Blissful City remained, and it once again fell into darkness.

Yet at this moment, it seemed to enter a state of suspended time. All the survivors remained motionless as though frozen in ice.

The light and shadows contracted and flew directly into the City Lord's Residence.

The survivors radiated faint light as they emerged from the phantom image of the Blissful City.

The ghost shadows and the humans each separated.

"Five Ghosts, five people? You actually guessed it, kid!" Situ Tiantian looked at Li Fan in shock.

The three people from the Sword Domain were somewhat dejected when the extra meal they had been looking forward to slipped away.

Li Fan, however, knew that this was Scholar Zhu saving their lives.

With the strength of these young people, they might barely withstand swallowing a single fruit. If they swallowed two in succession, there was absolutely no chance of survival.

"So this Scholar Zhu is truly petty to the extreme."

Li Fan sneered inwardly.

Judging from how precisely the duration of the slaughter in Blissful City could be controlled, the entire process was merely going through the motions.

The final outcome was entirely determined by a single thought from Scholar Zhu.

Because Li Fan had guessed that the final result would be an equal number of humans and ghosts, the final survivors would necessarily consist of equal numbers of humans and ghosts.

"Very good. These two fruits will be given to you!"

The survivors, glowing with a faint white light, looked as though they had returned to their mother's womb. Their bodies curled up, taking the form of fruits.

Scholar Zhu selected the two most robust ones and personally handed them to Li Fan.

Li Fan bowed and expressed his thanks.

The three people from the Sword Domain were visibly displeased, but they did not dare to act recklessly. They could only swallow their respective fruit in one gulp, their dissatisfaction written plainly on their faces.

Li Fan sensed that Scholar Zhu's gaze remained fixed on him.

He followed suit and swallowed one of the fruits first.

There was no sensation of tasting it. The Fruit of All Beings melted instantly upon entering his mouth.

Countless thoughts, memories, and emotions overlapped and then exploded violently within Li Fan's mind.

It was as though a terrifying storm capable of destroying everything had suddenly arisen from a completely calm plain.

Li Fan's original consciousness was swept up by the violent winds, helpless and precariously close to being overwhelmed.

"This is what is meant by the will of all beings, which can refine all gods."

"If it were my divine sense, tempered through a hundred reincarnations and reflecting the Great Dao, then no matter how powerful this storm became, it could not shake me in the slightest. But Ma Tiande simply does not possess such ability..."

"Unless he uses the Dao Net."

Hidden within the storm of the wills of all beings, Li Fan secretly observed Scholar Zhu from the corner of his eye, sneering inwardly.

He had already thought of a way to deal with this.

"Within the Dao Net, there are Nameless True Immortal anchors."

"From one of those nodes, I vaguely sensed an aura extremely similar to Scholar Zhu's..."

Li Fan's thoughts raced.

In reality, Ma Tiande was already weeping uncontrollably, his face twisted in agony.

A single face was somehow displaying several completely different expressions at once. His hands and feet also moved and flailed uncontrollably, like a drowning man desperately reaching for any possible lifeline.

Scholar Zhu watched Ma Tiande struggle with an expression of enjoyment.

Yet from the corner of his eye, he could not help looking toward the heavens beyond.

When a Dao Net operator dies, a signal will be generated and transmitted through the Dao Net to the Administration Council.

Within the Shuo Star Sea, there were surviving forces from various cultivation worlds, and even from different star seas. There were even beings who had once been True Immortals. Strange and secret methods emerged one after another. Only the Dao Net could disregard all of these obstacles and transmit the Dao Net operator's condition to the Administration Council in real time.

This was the means by which the Shuo Star Sea maintained its own stability.

Yet it had become the very phenomenon Scholar Zhu had been hoping and waiting for.

This was also the true reason he had always wanted Ma Tiande to die.

However, the situation Scholar Zhu had expected did not occur.

The Dao Net operator who had already fallen into a state of mortal struggle seemed to have genuinely found something he could hold onto.

Although his expression was horribly distorted, Scholar Zhu could tell that the self-consciousness deep within him had never dissipated.

"Hm?"

This immediately piqued Scholar Zhu's interest.

"This is..."

As he narrowed his eyes and observed carefully, Scholar Zhu's expression suddenly changed.

He saw what was happening within the opponent's sea of consciousness.

Faced with a threat to his life and on the verge of death at any moment, Ma Tiande had instinctively written a large number of meaningless Dao Primal symbols.

These Dao Primal symbols could not form a Dao Net command, so they did not cause any disturbance within the Dao Net.

Yet, somehow, they formed a long dam that blocked the storm generated by the Fruit of All Beings from the outside.

"By a twist of fate, he managed to successfully invoke the power of the Dao Net without establishing an official Dao Net connection?"

Scholar Zhu possessed a profound understanding of the Dao Net as well.

He had already seen the fundamental reason Ma Tiande was able to survive.

The Dao Net had already become a genuinely existing entity.

Although it had fallen under the manipulation of certain individuals, the Dao Net's controllers could only regulate its behavior and commands.

They could not control all of its instinctive reactions.

After all, what the Dao Net imitated was every single Great Dao between heaven and earth.

"The Dao Primal command triggered the Dao Net."

"In theory, this should have caused a disturbance within the Dao Net. However, the Administration Council blocked the disturbance on their end."

"Amid an infinite number of possible combinations, it just happened to form one capable of saving his life."

"Was it a coincidence?"

Scholar Zhu remained silent.

He did not interfere, allowing Li Fan to swallow and digest the first fruit.

The storm of wills gradually subsided.

Li Fan awoke from his confusion and pain.

Before he had time to comprehend the changes brought about by swallowing the fruit, Scholar Zhu once again spoke coldly.

"Continue. There is still another one."

Li Fan was slightly stunned, seemingly unable to understand the reason for Scholar Zhu's coldness.

Nevertheless, he gritted his teeth and swallowed the second fruit.

The storm rose again within his sea of consciousness.

This time, it seemed even more violent than before.

Once again, it devoured Li Fan's consciousness.

"I want to see whether you can get lucky a second time!"

This time, Scholar Zhu had been staring at Ma Tiande's sea of consciousness from the very beginning.

Sure enough, once he was no longer able to struggle and gradually began losing consciousness, Ma Tiande's dying instinct once again summoned countless Dao Primal symbols.

The command they formed this time was different from the previous one.

"Looks like good fortune will not descend upon you a second time." Scholar Zhu was somewhat satisfied.

He waited for the storm of the wills of all beings to completely devour Ma Tiande.

But soon, his expression became somewhat strange.

The Dao Primal symbols this time had not formed another dam or wall.

Instead, they transformed into another storm.

The power of the two storms canceled each other out.

And once again, Ma Tiande emerged completely unharmed!

Previous

Next

Chapter 1442: Immortal Skin Covers New Life

Once could still be explained as an accidental stroke of luck.

But for the second time, the same thing had happened...

At this moment, countless thoughts flashed through Scholar Zhu's mind. He even had the impulse to feed the third Fruit of All Beings Wills to Ma Tiande.

However, Scholar Zhu ultimately did not act. Instead, he recalled the two Dao Primal Commands that had surfaced in Ma Tiande's sea of consciousness.

Clearly, among the many dancing Dao Primal symbols, only a small portion had actually taken effect. This made it impossible for an observer to accurately reproduce them.

Scholar Zhu himself had no interest in the two Dao Primal Commands. Even after transforming from an Immortal into a mortal, the various methods at his disposal were by no means something these two commands could compare with.

What Scholar Zhu truly cared about was the possibility represented by Ma Tiande's performance.

Those ‘hidden commands’ that existed outside the current control system of the Dao Net, yet could still invoke the power of the Dao Net.

The gloom on his face gradually faded. In the blink of an eye, the hunched old man had transformed into a spirited Grand Scholar.

Scholar Zhu wore a benevolent expression as he waited for the four people seated before him to awaken from their absorption of the Fruit of All Beings Wills.

With Scholar Zhu's special care, although the three people from the Sword Domain had suffered some torment, they ultimately emerged unharmed. The childishness on their faces had long since faded after absorbing the memories of countless living beings and personally experiencing boundless slaughter.

They first expressed their thanks to Scholar Zhu, then all looked toward Ma Tiande sitting on the other side. There was no longer any contempt in their eyes.

No one understood the terror of the storm of thoughts of all living beings better than those who had personally experienced it.

Regardless of one's origins or whatever method one had used, as long as one could swallow a single fruit and remain safe and sound, one absolutely could not be underestimated.

And if one could swallow two fruits in succession while still maintaining clarity of mind...

"Such a person must not be missed!"

The three from the Sword Domain had already reached an agreement through their earlier exchange of glances. If they could bring such a talent back to the Sword Domain, the merit would be even greater than completing this trial.

Now, all that remained was to see whether this Ma Tiande could truly maintain his sanity rather than descend into madness.

Under the gazes of everyone present, the twisted and twitching expression on Li Fan's face slowly calmed. After keeping his eyes closed for a long while, he suddenly opened them and met the gazes of everyone present.

His eyes were bright like stars, revealing his current state.

Scholar Zhu gently applauded and seemed delighted. "It seems you gained quite a lot this time!"

"I must thank you, Grand Scholar, for granting me this opportunity," Li Fan replied neither servile nor overbearing.

Having swallowed two Fruit of All Beings Wills, his words and actions no longer needed to follow Ma Tiande's past mannerisms. Any changes could be attributed to the countless thoughts of living beings contained within the fruits.

"Senior Brother Ma, would you be interested in coming to our Sword Domain? I, Situ Tiantian, can guarantee that you will receive the highest grade of cultivation resources. If you nod your head, there is no need to apply to the Administration Council. Come back to the Sword Domain with us first. Once we arrive, we can complete the formalities afterward..." Situ Tiantian secretly glanced at Scholar Zhu. There was only a moment's hesitation in his eyes, but he quickly made his decision and directly extended an invitation to Li Fan in front of Scholar Zhu.

Ji Xingdao and Zhao Xing also repeated Situ Tiantian's invitation jokingly, even promising all kinds of wonderful conditions.

"Although I have long heard of the Sword Domain's great reputation, my aspirations do not lie in the Sword Dao."

Before Scholar Zhu had time to reveal any change in his expression, Li Fan had already directly refused.

"Do not reject it so quickly. Think it over again..." Scholar Zhu smiled faintly.

"The ambition of my life lies only in the Immortal Hunters! Previously, my strength was too weak, making it completely impossible to achieve. Thus, I buried this ambition deep within my heart and never mentioned it to anyone. But today, thanks to Grand Scholar Zhu's blessing, after swallowing two fruits and defying fate itself, anything may be possible from now on!"

Li Fan's expression was incomparably solemn as he spoke with booming confidence.

The smile on Scholar Zhu's face instantly froze.

The three people from the Sword Domain were also filled with astonishment.

"Immortal Hunter..."

The weight of these words in the current Star Sea was simply too great.

The Immortal Hunters was directly subordinate to the Administration Council of the Star Sea. Its members were selected from various forces throughout the Star Sea. Once a cultivator joined the Immortal Hunters, regardless of their previous identity or status within their original faction, all of it had to be cast aside.

Everything was to be centered around the Immortal Hunting.

In return for the price they paid, they received unparalleled authority.

Under the name of the Immortal Hunters, they could not only freely enter and leave the major forces throughout the Star Sea, but under special circumstances, they could even directly execute punishments and killings. More importantly, becoming a member of the Immortal Hunters allowed one to move even closer to the Dao Net.

Accompanying the Dao Net day and night, one's progress would be astonishingly rapid.

The moment Li Fan spoke these words, the entire hall fell into an eerie silence.

Scholar Zhu's expression darkened as he scrutinized Li Fan, seemingly unable to determine what he was thinking.

It was still Situ Tiantian who forced a somewhat awkward smile and tried to ease the atmosphere. "Members of the Immortal Hunters are all selected through countless rounds of screening. Senior Brother Ma is certainly extraordinary, but joining them is not an easy matter."

Li Fan, however, remained completely unconcerned and spoke with absolute confidence. "I am certain to join the Immortal Hunters. If I remember correctly, the next recruitment is in five years. At that time, I hope Grand Scholar Zhu will recommend me."

"I definitely will not disappoint you, Grand Scholar!"

Seeing that Li Fan's intentions were already firmly decided, the three from the Sword Domain no longer tried to persuade him.

After exchanging pleasantries for a while at the banquet and drinking several bowls of red broth, they took their leave. Before departing, they did not lose the dignity of the Sword Domain, stating that the Sword Domain would certainly return the favor before long.

Inside the City Lord's Residence, only Li Fan and Scholar Zhu remained.

Outside the estate, the noise of Blissful City once again rang out. Neither of them spoke, silently watching the prosperous night outside.

"Crunch, crunch..."

A bone chilling chewing sound came from Scholar Zhu's mouth. He had actually stuffed the remaining five Fruit of All Beings Wills into his mouth all at once and swallowed them whole.

Unlike the fruits melting instantly upon entering the mouths of Li Fan and the others, the five fruits inside Scholar Zhu's mouth actually emitted countless desperate shrieks from living beings on the verge of death.

Voices from hell soared into the heavens, temporarily even drowning out the sounds of revelry in Blissful City.

Scholar Zhu was like an old man whose life was nearing its end, chewing with agonizing slowness. As he listened to the continuous, fluctuating wails, an expression of enjoyment appeared on his face.

Li Fan did not even frown. His expression remained completely calm as he silently watched.

After a while, Scholar Zhu also seemed to find it boring. He swallowed the fruits directly in two or three gulps.

The cries of despair and grief abruptly vanished.

"Do you know why I built a Blissful City here in the Star Sea?"

"Was it simply to torment them?"

"These Fruit of All Beings Wills may possess the power to defy fate for you. But to me, they are completely tasteless."

"What I have experienced, the despair and cruelty, was a hundred or a thousand times worse than anything in this Blissful City."

Scholar Zhu gave a sinister cackle, and before anyone noticed, he had once again changed from the Grand Scholar into the hunched old man.

"The Great Dao Net covers heaven and earth. All things in the world exist within it. Ordinary living beings may be weak and insignificant, but when the thoughts of countless living beings are condensed, they may perhaps resonate with some Great Dao hidden in the darkness."

Li Fan pondered for a moment before answering.

"Hehehe..." Scholar Zhu let out another deep, sinister laugh.

"But you are mistaken."

"The primary purpose of my building Blissful City was simply to torment these ants whenever I grew bored."

Li Fan was slightly taken aback upon hearing this.

"Of course, if some unexpected delight is born in the process, that would be even better."

"But if nothing comes of it, there is no need to force it."

Scholar Zhu's skull-like head slowly emerged from his loose robe and gazed toward Blissful City outside the estate. His expression was hazy, as though he had fallen into his memories.

"Back then, the Immortal Realm was even more joyful than this city. Everything was flourishing, and the future seemed to contain infinite possibilities. Yet who could have expected that one day the great edifice would collapse..."

In his dazed state, Scholar Zhu spoke these words.

Li Fan remained silent, and Scholar Zhu then suddenly asked, "You said you want me to recommend you for the Immortal Hunters?"

"I ask that Grand Scholar grant my request," Li Fan replied with a nod.

"Very well. But I have one condition."

Scholar Zhu agreed exceedingly readily.

"No problem," Li Fan answered even more readily, without even hearing what the so called condition was.

Scholar Zhu burst into laughter, and his outward form changed once again.

The hunched old man within turned into a pool of blood, merging into the robe he wore.

The robe squirmed violently and actually transformed into the shape of human skin!

It drifted toward Li Fan and said sternly, “One day, no matter what method you use, bring my body back to me!”

There were no facial features on the human skin.

Only five sinister hollow cavities silently stared at Li Fan.

Li Fan remained silent for a moment before nodding in agreement.

“Good.”

The human skin seemed as though it had finally fulfilled some long-held wish. Having lost the force that kept it floating, it fell directly to the ground.

It then began to squirm once more, crawling across the ground like a human being. Eventually, it returned to the main seat.

It transformed back into a robe.

Scholar Zhu’s aged voice came from within.

“As far as I know, there are several ways to help me attain release.”

“First, successfully capture an Nameless True Immortal and exchange one body for another.”

“Second…”

Li Fan felt Scholar Zhu’s gaze repeatedly sweep across his face.

“Completely tear apart the Dao Net. I will naturally regain my freedom.”

“Third, illuminate the unknown regions of the Great Dao and redeem my freedom through merit.”

“…”

Scholar Zhu paused, then asked again, “I wonder which method you intend to use.”

Li Fan carefully considered his words. After a while, he slowly said in a low voice, “Perhaps all three can be pursued simultaneously.”

Scholar Zhu remained silent.

Only after a long while did he say, “Whatever you need, just tell me.”

“You have five years. Before the Immortal Hunter assessment, I will first test your true ability.”

“If you disappoint me…”

“Hehehe…”

Scholar Zhu’s indifferent tone concealed boundless killing intent.

Li Fan, however, remained completely unfazed. “Lord Grand Scholar, just wait and see.”

…

After the grand ceremony ended, Li Fan returned to Ma Tiande’s residence.

He also brought with him a gift from Scholar Zhu.

“This is…”

Li Fan examined the object in his hand with great interest.

It was a piece of skin extremely similar to Scholar Zhu’s human skin form.

It looked as though it had only just been peeled from a human body. Li Fan could even smell the strong scent of blood lingering upon it. Being intimately familiar with the structure of the human body, Li Fan unfolded the piece of skin and carefully felt it from top to bottom.

He confirmed that it was indeed human skin.

However, unlike Scholar Zhu’s, it did not possess any consciousness of its own.

After conducting a thorough examination, Li Fan tried various methods, but none could stimulate anything unusual within the skin. It seemed to be nothing more than ordinary human skin.

Yet when Scholar Zhu had handed this piece of skin to Li Fan earlier, Li Fan had clearly seen a complicated mixture of emotions in his eyes.

There had been remembrance, reluctance, and even some hatred.

After turning the piece of skin over and examining it repeatedly, Li Fan once again recalled the scene when Scholar Zhu had revealed his true appearance.

After pondering briefly, he had already made his decision.

He sat upright, took a deep breath, and then put the human skin onto his body like a piece of clothing.

Its original size and shape did not match “Ma Tiande” at all.

Yet under Li Fan’s touch, the human skin automatically shrank until it fit him perfectly. It was as though this very skin had been peeled from Ma Tiande’s body!

As Li Fan felt the gradually tightening force around him, along with the changes taking place in his body shape and facial features, a sudden premonition arose in his heart.

He seemed to be transforming into an entirely different person.

An unstoppable fear of the unknown surfaced within his heart. But when he sensed the miniature Dao Net reflected within his divine sense, all fear vanished.

His heart became as calm as a mirror. He simply experienced firsthand the changes brought about by wearing this layer of skin.

Once the outer skin had perfectly fused with his body.

Li Fan actually seemed to have truly become another person.

A vast number of images and memories inexplicably surged into his mind.

Even…

Li Fan looked at his own hands.

“This realm…”

“The realm of a True Immortal?”

It was not merely an illusion.

Li Fan pointed a finger toward the space before him.

A brilliant radiance, as though the star sea had just been born, blossomed from his fingertip.

Within that radiance seemed to be contained the infinite totality of existence.

“Immortal spiritual qi.”

“So this is immortal spiritual qi?”

Li Fan had once sensed the existence of immortal spiritual qi within the Final Dissociation Disc.

Now, this strange and magnificent power was surging forth from within his own body. Naturally, he immediately recognized its essence.

Li Fan’s gaze remained fixed upon that tiny point of light at his fingertip, not daring to move even slightly.

In a daze, he seemed to pass through that point and arrive within an endless ocean.

Within this vast sea, countless things and phenomena, all living beings and spirits, appeared together.

It was an indescribably strange sight, beyond imagination.

With Li Fan’s understanding, he actually became so captivated that he nearly lost himself within it.

Fortunately, as the radiance at his fingertip gradually faded, the infinite ocean concealed within the light also disappeared.

Li Fan blinked and emerged from the illusion, returning to reality.

The seemingly boundless immortal spiritual power had actually been exhausted like a candle that had burned itself away.

It could no longer support Li Fan in peering into that infinite ocean.

“Hmm?”

Li Fan frowned slightly.

He had not felt the immortal spiritual power disappearing from within his body.

With a thought, a faint light once again illuminated his fingertip.

This time, however, Li Fan did not allow his mind to sink into that infinite ocean.

He merely observed the light at his fingertip in silence.

Next

Chapter 1443: The Infinite Power Of The Immortal

Clearly, within that radiance lay an infinite ocean containing every possibility in existence. Yet at this moment, the radiance of immortal spiritual power at Li Fan's fingertip was as fragile as a dying candle flickering in the wind.

It flickered erratically, wavering without end.

What shocked Li Fan even more was that after ninety-nine breaths, the flame of immortal spiritual power at his fingertip suddenly went out without warning. It was as though some unknown, invisible existence had aimed at it and then gently blown upon it.

"Only the external manifestation of the immortal spiritual power has inexplicably disappeared. This body... or rather, the immortal skin, still contains immortal spiritual power." After sensing his body carefully, Li Fan reached this conclusion.

He tried again.

This time, Li Fan was no longer satisfied with merely transforming the immortal spiritual power into a single point.

Instead, he attempted to construct an immortal formation with the immortal spiritual power.

Of course, to prevent the Shuo Star Sea from noticing, Li Fan had no intention of causing too much disturbance. He merely used the [Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form] formation, which was capable of observing and analyzing everything.

It was no different from the formation deployed by the Final Dissociation Disc. In fact, Li Fan experienced an even stronger sensation of having the formation completely at his command. There was no need for the Final Dissociation Disc to process the information first. All kinds of information touched and analyzed by the Primal Chaos Reveals in Form formation directly surfaced within Li Fan's mind.

It was as though Li Fan had figured everything out himself.

Li Fan looked toward the city outside the residence. Through his eyes, there was no prosperous Blissful City at all. It was merely a gigantic vortex spinning at tremendous speed.

Countless points of consciousness floated, whirled, and settled within the vortex.

Occasionally, a few of them suddenly emitted a peculiar blue radiance as they circulated. Then, completely ignoring the power of the vortex itself, they slowly sank from the upper edge all the way to the very bottom.

Li Fan even vaguely saw a gigantic shadow resembling a predator lurking there. When the blue points of light above drew near, it suddenly swallowed them into its belly.

As if sensing Li Fan's probing gaze, the predator-like shadow slowly turned its head toward him. The shadow rippled, seemingly revealing a meaningful smile.

While maintaining the Primal Chaos Reveals in Form formation, Li Fan did not forget to silently calculate the passage of time. Sure enough, the moment ninety-nine breaths passed, the formation constructed from immortal spiritual power melted away like snow and disappeared in an instant.

The connection between Li Fan's body and the immortal formation also seemed to have been blocked. It could no longer provide support.

He could only allow the immortal formation to dissipate.

The vortex scene before Li Fan's eyes vanished along with it.

"That feeding shadow..."

"It really is Scholar Zhu."

"It seems that the selection of suitable targets among the consciousnesses of all living beings takes place simultaneously during the operation of the Blissful City."

"The Fruits of All Beings Wills from the Great Bliss Ceremony held once every three years are more like a smokescreen." Li Fan gradually came to an understanding.

Although he had already reached a certain cooperative agreement with this former Nameless True Immortal, there was absolutely no trust between them.

Scholar Zhu had merely made a desperate investment after Li Fan demonstrated a certain potential. He did not care about Li Fan's identity or background, nor did he care that Li Fan had quietly infiltrated the Blissful City, secretly replaced Ma Tiande, and taken his place. He did not care about Li Fan's purpose either.

As long as Li Fan could help him escape from the Dao Net, Scholar Zhu was willing to provide support.

"Perhaps he does not genuinely expect me to help him escape at all. As long as I can infiltrate the Immortal Hunters and cause some trouble and uncertainty for the Administrative Council and the Dao Net, thereby increasing his chances of escape, then this transaction will already be worthwhile..."

Just as Li Fan was pondering this, a sudden feeling of rejection emerged from within this body.

"Get out! Get out of me!"

"Get out, get out, get out, get out!"

"You insignificant ant! How dare you profane me!"

...

The feeling of rejection grew increasingly intense, while the voice echoing in Li Fan's ears became louder and louder. It was like thunder crashing incessantly, almost unbearable to endure.

And what was even stranger was that it was Li Fan's own voice.

Hearing himself roar at himself made the situation all the more bizarre.

In the end, Li Fan was forcibly expelled from that immortal skin.

Returning to his original body, Li Fan carefully examined the supreme treasure in his hand.

It could allow an ordinary mortal to instantly wield power comparable to a True Immortal. Calling this layer of human skin a supreme treasure was by no means an exaggeration.

"Based on the scenes that surfaced in my mind after putting on the immortal skin, the original cultivation of the owner of this skin should already have been approaching the Nameless realm."

"However, before he could merge with the Great Dao, the Dao Annihilation Calamity that would destroy everything had already descended."

"The Immortal Realm was destroyed. True Immortals and even Nameless True Immortals died one after another in the calamity. As the Dao manifested and was born, then perished and vanished, this scene in which the creation of heaven and earth coexisted with the apocalypse actually allowed this person to suddenly gain enlightenment. He directly took the final step!"

"It was a pity that his breakthrough came at the worst possible time. That Immortal Domain could originally have continued to endure for a little longer. But the moment he proved the Dao, it was equivalent to directly pulling away a load-bearing pillar that was crucial at the most critical moment..."

"In an instant, heaven collapsed, earth sank, and the Great Dao crushed everything."

...

The memories that followed became blurred and even somewhat deranged.

Memories from different periods, and even contradictory memories from before and after, appeared simultaneously. Li Fan could only barely deduce that although this Nameless True Immortal had escaped the calamity at the time, he was eventually captured by someone and nailed into the Dao Net, becoming a crucial node connecting the Great Dao.

"Unlike Scholar Zhu."

"This Nameless one had truly perished."

"And the day he died was the day the Dao Net was completed."

"The Great Dao he had merged with had no time to return to heaven and earth. Instead, it was captured and utilized by some mysterious power, becoming one of the most stable nodes of the Dao Net..."

Li Fan tried to reconstruct the scene from those fragmented memories.

The opening of the Dao Net. What an extraordinary event that must have been.

Yet it was accompanied by the resentful curse of the Nameless True Immortal.

"Nameless True Immortals like the owner of this immortal skin, who were used as living sacrifices to construct the Dao Net..."

"There were three others!"

"Four great nodes in total, together with that invisible giant hand. Only then was the Dao Net initially stabilized."

Li Fan took a deep breath.

Even after death, a mere layer of skin left behind by this existence could allow an ordinary mortal to instantly possess the power of a True Immortal. There were even consciousness and memories remaining within the immortal skin. This alone was enough to demonstrate how powerful its owner had been.

Yet such a powerful existence had been cruelly reduced to raw material for establishing the Dao Net, becoming a living foundation. After death, he had not even left behind his name, life story, or any other trace of his existence.

"The Nameless ant dies."

"Before a great catastrophe, those who cannot seize the initiative and control their own fate are hardly any different from mortals." Li Fan sighed inwardly.

After undergoing one round of possession, the immortal skin had visibly become thinner. It had also taken on a strange pallid color. This was also why it had forcibly expelled Li Fan.

However, after conducting some research, Li Fan discovered that the immortal skin had not actually been consumed by this.

It quickly became rosy and full again, almost at a speed visible to the naked eye.

However, Li Fan was still unable to put it on again.

Only after waiting for the time it took an incense stick to burn did Li Fan finally sense that he could continue using this immortal skin.

"It is not a property inherent to the immortal skin itself."

"Rather, another existence has imposed restrictions upon it. Unless my strength surpasses theirs, I must abide by these rules." Li Fan seemed to understand.

"It should be a restriction established by those powerful beings in the Shuo Star Sea who originally constructed the Dao Net. After all, the skin of a Nameless True Immortal can arbitrarily allow any living being to instantly possess power comparable to a True Immortal. It is absolutely a devastating weapon."

Li Fan once again donned the immortal skin.

This time, he wanted to verify whether he could extend the effective duration of wearing the immortal skin through his own adaptability.

As the power of the immortal spiritual qi once again draped over his body, Li Fan's consciousness submerged into it, as if beholding that infinite ocean once more.

"They say that the Immortal Realm is vast and boundless."

"This place is even a thousand or ten thousand times vaster than the Immortal Realm."

Even though this was his second time seeing it, Li Fan still found himself momentarily dazed.

"The power of the Immortal spiritual power originates from here. No wonder it possesses the property of [Infinity]."

As he wandered within it, Li Fan once again thought of the Star Sea.

Although the power of the Star Sea was inferior to the Immortal spiritual power in terms of energy level, it likewise possessed the property of [Infinity].

"Does this mean that the energy source of the Star Sea also originates from here?"

Li Fan suddenly thought of a crucial question.

Had the infinite nature of the Star Sea existed since time immemorial?

Or was it only born after the destruction of the Immortal Realm?

Li Fan gazed across this Infinite Ocean and remained silent for a long time.

Recalling the appearance of the Immortal Realm that he had once seen within a True Immortal's memories, Li Fan vaguely arrived at an answer.

"[Infinity] was probably originally a property unique to the Immortal Realm."

"It was only because of the destruction of the Immortal Realm that this property was passed down to the lower realm Star Sea."

“Scholar Zhu once said that the bliss of the Immortal Realm surpassed even the Blissful City. Presumably, this was all because of the power of Infinity. There was no longer any need to worry about energy consumption, leaving nothing but pure enjoyment. Had the Dao Annihilation Calamity not descended so suddenly, perhaps the Immortal Realm could have continued its ultimate bliss forever.

After achieving the Nascent Soul realm through the Five Elements Great Grotto Heaven, Li Fan had deeply understood the various benefits brought about by having no need to worry about consumption. The Five Elements Grotto Heaven was an Xuanhuang phantom. Although its energy was several times greater than that of ordinary cultivators, it was obviously still incomparable to the property of [Infinity].

"This is also one of the most important distinctions between immortals and mortals."

"With an endless supply of energy, there is no longer any need to consider consumption. Any spell or divine ability can have its destructive power amplified without limit..."

Even if a mortal threw an ordinary punch at the Xuanhuang earth, they would be unable to cause the world any harm. But as long as that action were repeated enough times, even a mortal could eventually smash the Xuanhuang Realm into dust.

"Ultimately, no matter how weak a force is, once multiplied by infinity, its might will become unimaginable."

Li Fan's thoughts were temporarily interrupted as the immortal spiritual power disappeared.

The difference between immortal and mortal power caused a faint sense of disappointment to rise within Li Fan's heart.

While waiting for the restriction to disappear, Li Fan suddenly thought of another question.

"Infinity is indeed wonderful. But what is the price?"

The existence of infinite power itself was somewhat contrary to Li Fan's original understanding.

He could understand the tremendous strength of immortal spiritual qi. But the infinity immortal spiritual power was the one thing he could never make sense of, no matter how he tried.

"What exactly is that Infinite Ocean?"

"It seems like some beautiful thing that could only exist within fantasy..."

"Could it really have no negative effects?"

The worry that had naturally arisen within him vanished without a trace after he came into contact with the infinite immortal spiritual power once again.

Nothing could possibly be worse than knowing the predetermined time of the entire mortal world's inevitable destruction.

Li Fan cast aside all other distracting thoughts and immersed himself in researching the immortal Skin and the immortal spiritual power.

Time passed quickly.

This time, the duration for which he could wear the immortal skin was exactly the same as the first time.

Not even the slightest difference.

However, Li Fan did not reach a conclusion just yet.

Once the cooldown period ended, he continued to don the immortal skin.

He repeated the process again and again.

Three years passed in the blink of an eye.

Li Fan finally confirmed that the duration for which the immortal skin could be used each time was indeed fixed. It would not increase with an increase in "proficiency."

However, three years of research had not been without results.

The immortal spiritual power once again lit up at his fingertip like a flame.

This time, as Li Fan looked at the immortal spiritual power within "his" body, he unexpectedly felt an inexplicable sense of familiarity.

It was as if it were a part of his own body.

The body referred to here was not the immortal skin. Nor was it Ma Tiande.

Rather, it was the core that governed all thoughts and actions, the divine sense that reflected the Great Dao.

"The reason I have remained stuck in the Half-Immortal realm is because the Immortal Realm was destroyed, leaving me unable to receive an infusion of immortal spiritual qi."

"And the immortal spiritual qi remaining in the world seems to possess specific ownership marks. It cannot be stolen and used."

"Only through the process of ascending to immortality via the Xuanhuang Realm can these marks be washed away to a certain extent. The infinite immortal spiritual qi within the Final Dissociation Disc came from this process."

"But what an immortal artifact can use cannot be applied to a True Immortal."

With a thought from Li Fan, the immortal spiritual power surged throughout his entire body like a tide.

It seemed to form a tiny, miniature Infinite Ocean.

The owner of this ocean had once been that Nameless True Immortal whose origins were unknown and who had already been transformed into a Dao Net foundation node.

But now...

This miniature Infinite Ocean had gradually become imbued with Li Fan's imprint.

"A complete change of ownership is still a long way off."

"For now, I have merely added a little of my own imprint."

"But I have only been in contact with it and familiarized myself with it for three years. If I were given a little more time..."

A strange light flickered in Li Fan's eyes. The power of the immortal spiritual power spread throughout his body, only to disappear instantly because of the restriction of the immortal skin.

"Could the immortal spiritual power also be inherited?"

The immortal spiritual power originated from the unknowable Infinite Ocean.

And that Infinite Ocean was absolutely not something an individual could come into contact with and then draw upon.

Even a True Immortal could not do so.

"The process of Immortal Ascension Platform infusing the body with immortal qi in the past should have been the key to allowing an individual to connect to the Infinite Ocean."

"If every True Immortal needs a unique mark to draw upon the power of Infinity, then as long as these unique marks can be mastered, perhaps the Immortal spiritual power can be controlled."

Li Fan suddenly realized that, to some extent, the boundless vast ocean and the Dao Net were actually somewhat similar.

Just like the miniature Infinite Ocean currently manifesting within Li Fan, it resembled the miniature Dao Net reflected within his divine sense.
