Next

Chapter 1444: Faking A Connection To The Infinite Ocean

“The Great Dao of Heaven and Earth can be reflected with a single thought. However, a projection is still only a projection. To actually wield the might of the Great Dao with every gesture in the real world is no easy matter.”

“Connecting to the Infinite Ocean, with its limitless supply of energy, makes it possible.”

The projection of the Great Dao provided rehearsal and theoretical support, while the Infinite Ocean provided the actual foundation.

“Obtaining either one of these is enough to display strength far beyond the ordinary. If both are used together…”

Li Fan, clad in the skin of a True Immortal and reflecting the Great Dao with his divine sense, looked at this Blissful City and actually felt that with a casual wave of his hand, he could turn it into ashes.

It was not an illusion.

“I always have the feeling that my understanding and development of the infinite nature of immortal spiritual power is still far too shallow.”

“The Infinite Ocean is an existence that can be placed on equal footing with the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth…”

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and once again summoned a strand of immortal spiritual power at his fingertips. His mind flew into it and sank into that incomparably mysterious Infinite Ocean.

Unfortunately, the many fragmented memory traces contained within the immortal skin were basically all experiences from the life of its former owner. There were no memories concerning Immortal Arts or divine abilities.

“I wonder, if I use the Azure Firmament Pearl to absorb this immortal skin, would I be able to analyze the divine abilities of its owner from before their death?”

“After all, it was once an existence that had proven the Dao and attained the Nameless…”

Although this thought only flashed through Li Fan's mind, he firmly remembered it and would wait for an opportunity to give it a try if necessary.

In the blink of an eye, another year passed.

Li Fan became increasingly familiar with the immortal spiritual power within the immortal skin.

But afterward, he encountered a bottleneck.

No matter how many times he put on the immortal skin, in the “True Immortal” state, he seemed to truly transform the immortal spiritual power within the skin into energy belonging exclusively to himself.

Yet the moment he took off the immortal skin, his familiarity with the immortal spiritual power immediately plummeted back to the previous bottleneck.

He could no longer advance even the slightest bit.

“It seems I was still being too idealistic. If there were no means of restriction involved, how could Scholar Zhu have lent this immortal skin to me so easily?”

Li Fan smiled wryly.

Yet he had no intention of giving up on obtaining immortal spiritual power.

“As the saying goes, if Heaven does not grant it, I shall go and take it myself…”

A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

In the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan's main body was caressing a stone slab with both hands.

It was precisely the key means once hidden within the former Immortal Realm's Ascension Platform, the method by which cultivators could ascend and become Immortals.

Although it was merely a replica left behind by the Xuantian King, boundless might still seemed to linger around it.

“If I am not mistaken, there must be a method hidden within this stone slab that allows newly ascended cultivators to connect to the Infinite Ocean, thereby obtaining the infinite nature of immortal spiritual power.”

“However, it can only be achieved with authorization from the official authority of the Immortal Realm.”

“In the past, even if the Xuantian King had been fortunate enough to ascend to the Immortal Realm, the person presiding over the Ascension Platform had already perished. Without guidance from the Immortal Realm, even someone like the Xuantian King could not obtain immortal spiritual power from this stone slab…”

Li Fan called this stone slab the [Key of Dao]. Only by activating it with the corresponding True Immortal seal script could its true function be brought forth.

“Besides the True Immortal seal script, there must probably be another method needed to open the path to connecting with the Infinite Ocean.”

Li Fan fell into contemplation.

The divine sense clone in the Shuo Star Sea put on the immortal skin. A strand of immortal spiritual power passed through the miniature Dao Net and arrived at Li Fan's main body in an instant.

However, this strand of immortal spiritual power that had traveled across the star sea seemed to have been filtered layer by layer. Its original faint starlight color had become even more diluted, taking on a milky white hue.

Moreover, Li Fan's main body did not have the enhancement of the immortal skin. He was not the owner of this immortal spiritual power.

The moment this immortal spiritual power appeared in this foreign spacetime, it was like a wild horse that had broken free of its reins, surging about recklessly and trying to escape from Li Fan's grasp.

Li Fan's main body focused all his attention on it. Using the familiarity he had developed with this immortal spiritual power over four years as the reins, he could barely control it.

Within the surrounding Immortal formation, the immortal spiritual qi released by the Final Dissociation Disc also assisted from the side.

It helped Li Fan subdue this strand of immortal spiritual power.

After more than half a day of arduous effort, this violent immortal spiritual power finally became barely controllable.

“Next, I need to use this strand of immortal spiritual power to disguise my identity.”

“Then, through the Key of Dao stone slab, enter that Infinite Ocean.”

Although the target was clear, actually carrying it out was by no means easy.

After all, even something the Xuantian King had been unable to accomplish in the past was not so simple.

The first problem he had to face was how to activate the stone slab.

Li Fan used the Immortal formation as a blade, splitting the strand of immortal spiritual power transmitted by the divine sense clone into tens of millions of portions for experimentation.

If he merely used the immortal spiritual power to touch the stone slab, only a faint halo would appear on its surface, after which there was no further reaction. It seemed to be waiting for Li Fan to perform the next step.

Li Fan recalled the process by which he had previously used this Key of Dao stone slab to help the Xuanhuang Realm ascend into the Immortal Realm. He knew that it had to be activated through the corresponding True Immortal seal script.

“Could it still be that Immortal and Mortal Transformation character?”

With a thought, Li Fan controlled one portion of the immortal spiritual power and used it to write upon the stone slab.

Indeed, a powerful energy fluctuation suddenly erupted from the stone slab. Yet after searching all around and failing to find a suitable candidate, the fluctuation actually dissipated on its own after a long while.

Having eliminated this possibility, Li Fan began other experiments.

He tested each of the True Immortal seal script characters he knew, one by one.

It did not take long.

Unfortunately, not a single one matched.

“It should be an unknown True Immortal seal script character.”

Li Fan's main body frowned slightly.

…

After his attempt to connect to the Infinite Ocean was obstructed, Li Fan once again set his sights on Scholar Zhu.

Directly asking him was obviously impossible. Not only would it expose his own secrets, it might also attract the attention of the Shuo Star Sea Administration Council.

Li Fan's divine sense clone continued familiarizing himself with the power of the immortal spiritual power while waiting for an opportunity.

Very soon, only seven days remained before the Immortal Hunters recruited new members.

This time, the Administration Council issued an announcement stating that the number of cultivators to be recruited was actually twice as many as before. For a time, the Shuo Star Sea was filled with discussion, and people were both delighted and worried.

What delighted them was that their chances of joining the Immortal Hunters had increased considerably.

What worried and frightened them was that, according to rumors, the Immortal Hunters was recruiting more members this time because it had suffered heavy losses during the previous operation to hunt True Immortals and had been forced to do so.

Clearly, although joining the Immortal Hunters could grant one overwhelming authority and power, the risks one had to face were also greater than imagined.

But regardless, becoming a member of the Immortal Hunters was the lifelong pursuit of the young elites of the various great forces in the Shuo Star Sea. Thus, there were still countless applicants.

In Blissful City, Li Fan received a transmission from Scholar Zhu.

“Seven days from now, as promised, I will recommend you to participate in the selection.”

“However, as we agreed beforehand, I need to test you first!”

Li Fan straightened his robes and slowly walked into the City Lord's Residence.

When he pushed open the door, he did not encounter Scholar Zhu.

Instead, his foot suddenly stepped into empty space, and it seemed as though he had fallen into an endless abyss.

He plummeted downward at extreme speed, surrounded by endless darkness.

From time to time, specks of light flashed through the darkness. They seemed to be embedded within the cliff walls of the abyss.

Scholar Zhu's booming voice rang out beside Li Fan's ears.

“The Immortal Hunters examination places the greatest emphasis on one's comprehension of the Dao Net.”

“The points of light you see are Dao Net commands that may be involved in the examination. Try to reproduce them.”

“This is the first trial. If you cannot even pass this…”

“Then never think about leaving!”

Scholar Zhu's sinister laughter echoed through the abyss.

Li Fan paid him no attention. He merely carefully observed the points of light representing the surrounding Dao Net commands.

During this endless rapid descent, techniques such as flight and levitation had inexplicably become ineffective.

Every Dao Net command flashed past in an instant. Trying to observe and reproduce these Dao Net commands within such a brief span of time was exceedingly difficult.

Fortunately, within Li Fan's divine sense was a projection of the miniature Dao Net.

His divine sense spread out like a curtain, reflecting all the points of light flashing rapidly around him into itself.

If there was a corresponding command within his own Dao Net projection, it would automatically flicker and respond.

If it was still within a blind spot of Li Fan's understanding, it was simply skipped over.

Li Fan did not need to use his own consciousness to identify and reproduce them. The miniature Dao Net did it in his place.

Whether in reaction speed or reproduction accuracy, it was more than a level faster.

Li Fan sensed that with every successful recreation, it was as though a rope had suddenly tugged him upward, slowing his descent by a little.

And as each point of light was successfully recreated, it remained permanently illuminated above the endless abyss.

At last, Li Fan stopped falling.

The darkness was gradually dispelled. After leaving the endless abyss, Li Fan arrived above a desolate wilderness.

A figure stood suspended in the air before him.

Although Li Fan did not recognize the face, the aura it gave off felt faintly familiar.

It was indeed Scholar Zhu!

Sure enough, Scholar Zhu's voice came from the figure.

"The second trial can be martial."

"Immortal Hunter, Immortal Hunters. Its purpose is to hunt True Immortals."

"Can you do it?"

Before his voice had even faded, Scholar Zhu pointed toward Li Fan from afar.

"A True Immortal takes the great Dao of Heaven and Earth as its blade and commands infinite power."

The boundless wilderness actually began to curl inward like a sphere under Scholar Zhu's single gesture.

Li Fan seemed to have been trapped inside an urn, watching the earth around him rise up, leaving him with nowhere to escape.

It was not merely the ground. Every law between Heaven and Earth had transformed into a force of oppression. Trapped within this prison, it was as though he were facing the entirety of Heaven and Earth itself.

Li Fan's expression remained unchanged. He did not put on the immortal skin.

Instead, millions upon millions of arms suddenly manifested behind him.

Each arm seemed to be rapidly writing something.

Every time a complete inscription was written, a point of light appeared before Li Fan.

Gradually, these points of light began to weave themselves into a net.

Li Fan gently pressed both hands against the net, as though playing a zither, continuously plucking its strings.

With every movement, a large section of the surrounding wilderness and Heaven and Earth rolling toward him inexplicably vanished.

The plucking continued without pause, and in the blink of an eye, the wilderness had been eaten away until it was riddled with holes.

By the time it reached Li Fan, it no longer posed any threat.

Once again, they returned to the dark void.

Li Fan and Scholar Zhu stood facing each other from afar.

Scholar Zhu stared at the miniature Dao net woven before Li Fan, his eyes filled with contemplation.

"With the strength of your current body, joining the Immortal Hunters would be more than sufficient."

"However, I still have to test your true capabilities."

Scholar Zhu let out a cold laugh.

Within the darkness, vast clouds surged forth, accompanied by thunderous roars.

In an instant, they completely surrounded Li Fan.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes. Within the black clouds, he seemed to see countless imposing figures standing tall.

"These were all my former servants."

"Let me see just what you can do."

Scholar Zhu's figure suddenly disappeared.

One terrifying creation after another, their faces hideously distorted, surged forth from the black clouds like a tide.

They charged toward Li Fan.

Yet Li Fan seemed to have been frightened senseless. He simply stood rooted to the spot, showing no reaction for a long time.

Those Immortal creations, however, had no intention of holding back.

Just as they were about to reach Li Fan.

"Disperse!"

Li Fan uttered a soft command.

At the same time, countless creations froze in place as though they had been sealed in ice.

Then...

Together with the vast black clouds filling the sky, they vanished like smoke and dispersed like mist.

Scholar Zhu, who had been hiding behind the black clouds, revealed his true form.

He stared at Li Fan, his expression filled with uncertainty and astonishment.

"I too once had countless servants. Scholar Zhu, what do you think?"

Li Fan smiled faintly.

As his words fell, countless gloomy black clouds once again swept across the void, pressing down from above.

Figures darted about within the clouds.

They were none other than the countless Immortal servants that Li Fan had dispersed with a single word.

Only now, they had changed masters.

They surrounded Scholar Zhu and launched an attack.

"Presumptuous!"

Scholar Zhu flew into a rage.

It was as though Heaven and Earth had been struck by an invisible colossal object, causing a section of space to collapse without warning.

All living beings within the collapsed region were annihilated along with it.

Bang, bang, bang.

Strange sounds rang out without pause. The countless living beings were crushed at will, like flies being swatted.

Before long, every creation attacking Scholar Zhu had been completely wiped out.

Compared with Li Fan's effortless destruction of the entire region with a single word, however, his display was clearly inferior.

Li Fan did not manifest another horde of living creations. He merely smiled and looked at his opponent.

"Interesting. Interesting."

"I believe you will be able to give those fellows a few surprises."

"Go."

Only after staring at each other for a long while did Scholar Zhu finally speak.

The scene surrounding Li Fan vanished in an instant.

He was once again outside the City Lord's Residence.

Before him was that door which had never been pushed open.

Li Fan cupped his hands toward the front, then turned and left.

"After being trapped in the Blissful City for so long, how could he possibly still have these Immortal creations accompanying him?"

"If he had not insisted on summoning these things, I would not have been able to see through the truth so quickly."

"They were merely scenes similar to the Eternal Remnant Will, illusions so vivid that they were almost indistinguishable from reality. Under such circumstances, even a powerhouse like Evil Su Bai would not have been my opponent, let alone a mere Nameless True Immortal whose cultivation had been crippled."

Li Fan let out a cold laugh and had already returned to his own residence.

Next, all he had to do was infiltrate the Immortal Hunters and seize the most central secrets of the Shuo Star Sea.

"The essence of that test just now was a clash of Dao discourse and wills."

"Although I used the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood to forcibly suppress Scholar Zhu, his method of crushing everything like an invisible colossal force was by no means something to underestimate."

Next

Chapter 1445: Ants Surround And Slay An Immortal

Li Fan narrowed his eyes, recalling the divine abilities and methods that Scholar Su had displayed in the illusion just now.

The miniature Dao Net within his divine sense flickered with uncertain light as it searched for the corresponding Dao laws. Scholar Zhu had entered the Dao Net with his own body, so the Dao he had comprehended had already become one of the foundations of the Dao Net.

The replica within Li Fan's divine sense likewise contained a reflection of it.

The instant he found the corresponding one, that strand of the Dao Net emitted a uniquely profound light.

Li Fan's mind stirred slightly.

"This is... the character (磨), [Grind]!"

In an instant, it seemed as though a square millstone appeared before his eyes, repeatedly pounding and grinding down its target between heaven and earth. An endless stream of radiance flowed from this strand of the miniature Dao Net into him. In a short period of time, Li Fan's comprehension of this Dao advanced by leaps and bounds.

Yet after only a few breaths, Li Fan voluntarily interrupted his comprehension of this Dao.

"Although Scholar Zhu has already been driven into the Dao Net and made into a living formation stake, he is still alive. This Dao of 'Grind' is still something that has an owner."

"The deeper I study it, the easier it will be for its owner to sense me."

Although there were countless Great Daos between heaven and earth, Li Fan only needed to focus on the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood. As for the principles and laws of other Dao paths, he could merely use them as convenient weapons on his journey toward the very peak.

His deliberate revelation of the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood before Scholar Zhu this time was not an act of showing off his abilities before the Nameless True Immortal of old. Rather, it was a form of probing.

"Now it seems that the Transformation of Truth and Falsehood contains countless mysteries. Although there were people in the Immortal Realm who secretly coveted it in the past, not everyone knew of its existence. Even though Scholar Zhu had already become a Nameless True Immortal, when I used it right in front of him, he still could not recognize it. He was merely astonished by this inexplicable method of mine."

Indeed, this probing carried some risk. However, Scholar Zhu himself was already a prisoner within the Dao Net. Li Fan's true body was far away within the High Wall, in the Darkest Star Sea, with the Carefree Dreamscape concealing him. Even if something unexpected happened, he could calmly use [Truth].

In fact, even when the darkened Heavenly Doctor crossed possibilities to arrive, Li Fan had still successfully activated [Truth]. In the current possibility of the [Yuanchu], there were truly very few existences capable of threatening Li Fan.

Seven days passed in the blink of an eye.

As agreed, Scholar Zhu indeed recommended Ma Tiande to the Administration Council.

Ma Tiande had originally been sent to the Blissful City by the Administration Council to control the Dao Net and conduct surveillance. Now, the Administration Council merely assumed that Scholar Zhu and Ma Tiande did not get along very well and that Scholar Zhu wanted to find an excuse to have Ma Tiande transferred away.

After verifying through the Dao Net that Ma Tiande's identity was correct and consulting Ma Tiande himself, the Administration Council agreed to the recommendation.

The selection for the Immortal Hunters was concentrated within half a day.

When the selection officially began, the ethereal and intangible Dao Net seemed to reveal its true form. An actual entity emerged from the illusory realm.

A vast, faintly visible net enveloped the entire Shuo Star Sea.

Every faction within the Star Sea was encompassed within it.

The shadow of the Dao Net stretched across heaven and earth, extending beyond the boundaries of the world.

Such a magnificent and miraculous sight caused everyone who witnessed it to instinctively feel the urge to bow and worship from the depths of their hearts.

Ordinary mortals knelt and worshipped one after another.

Even cultivators lowered their heads in submission.

When this Dao Net stretched across the heavens, Li Fan did not make himself conspicuous. He merely lowered his head and silently observed.

Because of the miniature Dao Net within his divine sense, what he saw was different from what everyone else saw.

Four figures radiating an aura of holiness and majesty stood concealed behind the Dao Net, like four great pillars. Besides them, numerous other overlapping phantoms also seemed to stand within the Dao Net, their presence cold and imposing.

As for the invisible giant hand pressing down upon the Dao Net, it was nowhere to be seen.

Every time the Nameless True Immortal whose living formation stake was visible in reality swept his gaze across the Dao Net, Li Fan felt his heart pounding with alarm. Yet the projection within his divine sense became somewhat clearer precisely because of his close and more detailed observation.

Thus, Li Fan did not stop watching.

A ray of light traveled through the Dao Net.

Wherever it passed, everyone participating in this Immortal Hunters selection was illuminated by the light.

Their bodies floated upward uncontrollably for no apparent reason.

Li Fan was no exception.

Observing the nearly thousand cultivators throughout the Shuo Star Sea who were rising into the air just like him, Li Fan remained expressionless. While allowing himself to be seized, he carefully observed how the Dao Net operated in reality.

"Spanning the entire Shuo Star Sea, precisely locking onto a thousand targets at the same time, and lifting them into the air... In terms of the breadth and precision of its power alone, what the Dao Net has accomplished here is no inferior to a True Immortal."

Moreover, Li Fan vaguely sensed that those participating in the Immortal Hunters were not merely being lifted into the air.

Rather, it was as if they had collectively entered a spacetime parallel to the real Shuo Star Sea.

The living beings below could raise their heads and see them in the sky.

Yet they themselves gradually lost sight of everything below, seemingly entering a completely isolated region.

"This should be to ensure fairness and impartiality in the Immortal Hunters selection. The test is being conducted openly before every living being in the Shuo Star Sea."

Li Fan understood.

The surrounding scenery gradually darkened, as if he had been placed within an endless void. Nearly a thousand cultivators floated within it, quietly awaiting the beginning of the selection.

Not long afterward, a booming voice resounded throughout the area.

There was not a single word of unnecessary explanation. It went straight to the point.

"First Trial, Dao."

The Dao Net stretching across heaven and earth seemed to have arrived within this selection space.

It descended abruptly, then continuously pressed downward.

What had originally existed only in the distant heavens now drew closer and closer.

Until it was directly before their eyes.

The thousand participants were truly no more than ants within a net before the magnificent Dao Net.

The pressure originating from the Great Dao caused one third of the thousand cultivators to tremble uncontrollably on the spot.

Light flashed.

Those who trembled were instantly eliminated and transmitted out of the trial space.

The remaining cultivators all had their hearts tighten as they concentrated their minds and faced the Dao Net that was now almost directly before them.

The Dao Net continued to descend, as if it intended to crush them completely beneath it.

Faced with the crisis of being ground into fragments, not everyone could remain calm and expressionless. Even though they had made countless preparations before coming here, the Dao Net they had imagined beforehand was worlds apart from the Dao Net they were now actually facing. During this process, anyone whose expression changed even slightly was eliminated.

The Dao Net pressed down upon their bodies.

Their bodies and souls seemed to be mercilessly ground over once, before subsequently merging into the Dao Net.

When Li Fan could see again, he discovered that he had already arrived in an area resembling a maze.

The true form of the Dao Net was nowhere to be seen.

Buzz, buzz, buzz...

A strange sound came from nearby.

Li Fan turned his head and was astonished to see the silver-white paths of the maze inexplicably evaporating and disappearing from the edges.

Moreover, this disappearance was rapidly spreading toward where he stood.

"The Dao Annihilation Calamity?"

Naturally, these four words surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

Of course, this was not the true Dao Annihilation Calamity. It was merely a simulated disaster scenario created for the test based on the essence of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

"So what I need to do now is escape from this Dao Net maze."

Li Fan seemed to understand.

He tried to take a step forward and head deeper into the maze.

Yet when he instinctively exerted force, his body did not move at all.

Looking down carefully, Li Fan discovered that his feet had strangely fused with the Dao Net maze.

Yet the path ahead, which appeared smooth at first glance, was actually covered with uneven bumps and hollows. Under ordinary circumstances, every step would have been extremely difficult.

After observing for a moment, Li Fan already understood the purpose of this first test.

Through the tentacle-like sensation extending from the soles of his feet, Li Fan sensed the protruding sections of the Dao Net ahead.

“It seems I need to issue a Dao Net command to fill in this gap.”

Li Fan did not do it himself. Instead, he used the same method as before, just as he had during Scholar Zhu’s trial, directly responding with the miniature Dao Net within his divine sense.

In an instant, a dim light emerged from Li Fan’s body and traveled through his feet to the ground.

The gap was filled.

The road ahead was no longer uneven, and Li Fan smoothly took a step forward.

“This method of testing is rather interesting.”

The miniature Dao Net quickly resonated with the various gaps along the road ahead.

Li Fan walked slowly and steadily, neither rushing nor lagging behind.

With no pressure weighing on his mind, Li Fan began observing his surroundings. He deliberately kept his speed slightly behind the leading group.

He was not among the most outstanding or conspicuous, but he was still firmly within the first echelon.

Behind him, the Dao Net continued evaporating, relentlessly closing in.

Another four hundred or so cultivators were eliminated in this round.

Only after slightly more than three hundred cultivators remained did the first test finally end.

The Dao Net maze silently disappeared, and the enormous announcing voice rang out once again.

As before, it went straight to the point.

“Second Trial, Martial.”

The voice was still echoing through the space when an indescribable aura suddenly descended upon this place.

Li Fan’s pupils contracted sharply.

“A True Immortal?”

Standing opposite the three hundred cultivators was a tall figure clad in golden armor.

However, shackles bound both his hands and feet. His armor was also severely damaged and covered in wounds.

His hair and beard seemed to have gone untrimmed for many years, growing into a tangled mess that even obscured his face.

Yet Li Fan could still see the faint, ferocious grin that suddenly appeared on the figure’s face.

“Rain!”

The slightly hoarse voice, carrying a faint killing intent, resounded in everyone’s ears.

Suddenly, raindrops began falling from the heavens.

At first, only a few sparse drops fell. Then the rain grew heavier and heavier, until in the blink of an eye, torrential rain poured down.

Every drop of rain seemed to possess the weight of mountains.

When they crashed down upon them, many cultivators were caught off guard and let out waves of agonized screams.

Their flesh and blood were smashed into a bloody pulp by the rain.

Lights flashed, and those on the verge of death were transported out of the trial space.

However, a small number of unfortunate cultivators died in the torrential rain before they could be transported away.

“So this is really serious.”

Li Fan watched with his own eyes as one of the participants beside him died.

Yet he showed no trace of panic. He had far too many methods capable of resisting this immortal rain.

“But since this is the selection for the Immortal Hunters, I naturally have to make the greatest possible use of the power of the Dao Net.”

The moment this True Immortal appeared, Li Fan had already noticed that he seemed to be slightly closer to the Dao Net than before. It was seemingly intended to make it easier for the cultivators participating in the Immortal Hunters selection to draw upon the Dao Net’s power.

“Rain…”

“Umbrella!”

With a thought from Li Fan, an umbrella immediately appeared in his hand.

Its round canopy spread above his head, completely blocking the torrential rain.

However, this trial was clearly not something that could be passed so easily.

While protecting himself, Li Fan looked around and discovered that quite a few cultivators from Shuo Star Sea had adapted almost immediately.

They each displayed their own abilities. Some, like Li Fan, used umbrellas and protective equipment to shield their bodies. Others used invisible forces to alter the direction in which the raindrops fell, preventing them from striking them. Some even directly swallowed the torrential rain.

Once they were able to stand firmly beneath the rain, they all began attacking the Golden-armored True Immortal whose limbs were restrained by shackles.

All kinds of divine abilities and spells were unleashed.

The most eye-catching were a gust of wind and three swords.

The fierce wind howled, blowing the torrential rain back into the sky. What had originally been a divine ability used by the True Immortal to slaughter the trial cultivators was now turned back against him.

The three swords belonged to the same person. They floated behind a female cultivator in purple robes.

The female cultivator seemed to be blind, her eyes remaining tightly closed throughout. Yet even within the torrential rain that obscured her vision, she could firmly lock onto the direction of the golden-armored True Immortal.

The three swords behind her were held by a severed arm, a head, and a circular vortex.

Sword light crisscrossed through the air, cleaving apart the wind and rain as they mercilessly hacked toward the golden-armored True Immortal.

...

Amid the violent wind and torrential rain, energy surged like a raging tide, rising and falling without end.

Li Fan hid in the shadows, quietly observing without making a rash move. At the same time, he used the miniature Dao Net within his divine sense to record all the fluctuations of the various spells and divine abilities around him.

It was obvious that the opponent in this trial was a genuine immortal.

Although the Administration Council had restrained him, preventing him from displaying his full strength, his defensive power remained intact.

The divine abilities of the numerous cultivators continuously bombarded the golden armor he wore.

Yet they merely caused slight tremors. They could not even leave a faint white scar upon it.

The golden-armored True Immortal looked at the attacks of these ants before him and laughed wildly.

“Rain! Rain! Rain!”

Under the True Immortal’s chant, the wind and rain grew even more violent.

The shackles on the hands and feet of the golden-armored True Immortal clanged loudly. It seemed that under the True Immortal’s struggle, they were about to break free and snap apart.

Meanwhile, the rain assaulting the cultivators began displaying its true divine might.

Not only was every drop as heavy as a mountain, it also began corroding their souls.

It even began eroding the Great Dao of this world.

Seeing that the cultivators were gradually falling into a disadvantage under the True Immortal’s counterattack, Li Fan, who had been watching the battle from the sidelines, finally spoke.

“Disarm!”

Li Fan hid behind all the other cultivators and pointed casually toward the arrogant golden-armored True Immortal from afar.

His voice was soft, yet it pierced through the wind and rain and entered the ears of everyone present.

The wind and rain suddenly paused.

It was as if some incomparably mysterious power had descended.

The armor on the golden-armored True Immortal’s body began to tremble slightly, unable to resist.

“No!”

The golden-armored True Immortal, whose face had previously been filled with ferocity, was now overwhelmed with panic.

He cried out loudly, seemingly attempting to stop the armor from falling off his body.

But the power of the Dao Net crudely stripped away the protective armor covering his body.

In moments, the former True Immortal appeared naked before everyone.

Although his tangled body hair provided some concealment, it was still an unbearable and immense humiliation.

The cultivators participating in the trial finally reacted.

They once again surrounded and attacked the True Immortal.

The True Immortal, however, merely roared as he allowed their attacks to grind away at his flesh and blood.

His pair of crimson eyes pierced through the crowd and locked firmly onto Li Fan!

Previous

Next

Chapter 1446: Hunting A True Immortal For The First Time

The True Immortal was enraged to the extreme, causing the rain to grow even more violent.

Water droplets pierced through the curtain of rain like layer upon layer of veils, dividing the battlefield into isolated sections.

Amid the torrential downpour, Li Fan saw through the gaps that among the cultivators who had seized this critical opportunity to besiege the True Immortal, more than a dozen could not withstand the immense pressure of the rain and were instantly crushed into dust.

Then, as the powder of their remains mixed with the rainwater, it suddenly began to squirm and wriggle, unexpectedly transforming into [Rain Men]!

Taking advantage of the omnipresent rain covering the battlefield, they instantly surrounded and attacked Li Fan from all directions.

Li Fan pretended to be greatly alarmed. Holding an umbrella, his figure transformed into a streak of light and rapidly escaped from the encirclement. His speed was actually even greater than the falling rain!

Truly, the torrential rain passed through his body, yet not a single drop touched him.

After fleeing into the distance and reaching a temporarily safe location, Li Fan looked toward the True Immortal, who was still staring fixedly at him, and gave a cold laugh before pointing with one finger again.

"Shave Hair!"

His voice seemed to possess a strange kind of magic. The bodies of everyone present stiffened slightly, causing the movements of those besieging the True Immortal to halt for a moment.

As for the True Immortal, his eyes widened furiously, and he once again let out a heaven-shaking roar.

"Kill you! Kill you! Kill you..."

Resentment surged into the heavens, while rage boiled over.

The heavy rain that had originally been falling evenly across every part of the battlefield now seemed to be drawn toward a certain force. Like countless water dragons, it all howled as it surged toward Li Fan.

But even the True Immortal's attempt at retaliation could not stop the Dao Net command that Li Fan had already issued.

The invisible fluctuations of the Dao Net instantly swept across the battlefield and once again descended upon the True Immortal.

It was as though countless pairs of scissors were trimming the True Immortal's hair and beard simultaneously. As the shattered coverings were slowly removed, this True Immortal finally revealed his true appearance.

Although his face was somewhat gaunt, it was still possible to see that he was handsome and extraordinary. Yet his body was covered in enormous wounds that ran deep enough to expose the bone and could not heal.

His flesh continuously wriggled, but there was always some mysterious force preventing it from regenerating.

The remaining cultivators surrounding him seemed to understand one another without words. Their techniques and divine abilities all struck toward these wounds.

The True Immortal immediately let out a miserable scream.

In the distance, merely holding an umbrella was no longer enough for Li Fan to deal with the countless water dragons filling the sky.

Heavy rain descended from all directions, eroding Li Fan's body and consciousness. The voices reaching his ears were drowned out by the sound of rain, while his vision was completely covered by the dense curtain of falling water.

Even though he had put on a tightly protective raincoat, Li Fan gradually began to feel as though he were being severed from the world around him.

It was like the dust of the world being completely washed away by this torrential rain.

"Is this the Dao of Rain..."

"If I do not do something soon, I am afraid I will completely disappear from this world. All traces of my existence will be like mud stains washed away by the rain, never to remain again."

"Even while bound by the Dao Net, this True Immortal can still unleash such divine abilities. It seems that at his peak, he was by no means a weak existence!"

Standing within the rain, Li Fan closely experienced the divine ability of the Great Dao and gained considerable insight.

At the same time, as various flashes of inspiration emerged in his mind, another rain began to form around him.

The direction in which these raindrops fell was completely opposite to the rain controlled by the True Immortal.

As the raindrops collided with one another, the wind and rain around Li Fan immediately weakened.

Although it could not completely cancel out the True Immortal's divine ability, with the protection of the raincoat, it was enough to ensure his safety.

Li Fan continued sensing the Dao of Rain surrounding him. As he watched the disheveled True Immortal being attacked by the cultivators in the distance, a sudden sense of vigilance arose within his heart.

"It is merely a matter of removing his armor and clothing, leaving him naked."

"Compared with being captured alive by mortals, this level of humiliation is completely insignificant. The performance of this Golden Armored True Immortal is far too pathetic. He is shouting and roaring, almost as though he has completely lost his rationality..."

"He does not look like someone capable of comprehending such a divine ability."

"Something is wrong!"

Li Fan's heart tightened, and he immediately reacted.

Looking toward the battlefield, he discovered that the golden armor, beard, and hair he had previously removed with the art of 'Remove Armor, Shave Hair' had somehow disappeared without a trace!

"His loss of composure was merely bait. He was only trying to draw everyone's attention so that he could take advantage of their negligence and escape."

Li Fan's thoughts raced. He was about to connect to the Dao Net here and activate the divine ability [Restrain].

But then he reconsidered.

This was not truly a battlefield for fighting a True Immortal to the death. It was merely an examination organized by the Shuo Star Sea Administration Council to recruit Immortal Hunters.

With so many people watching, could they really allow a mere True Immortal to escape?

"It must simply be an additional trial. If I expose this fact, I will inevitably attract extra attention from the Administration Council. But if I pretend not to know..."

Li Fan hesitated for only a moment before making the unusual decision to reveal himself.

He suddenly shouted into the air.

"Everyone, be careful! Do not let him sneak away!"

He then swiftly swept his gaze across the surroundings while chanting in a solemn and dignified voice.

"Restrain!"

The Dao Net revealed across the battlefield suddenly seemed to be stirred by a gust of wind, rustling incessantly.

The Dao Strings, interwoven vertically and horizontally, consequently became even clearer.

As they trembled slightly, they radiated dazzling silver light.

The golden armor, beard, and hair that had already scattered and fled toward the edges of the battlefield were all locked down and restrained at the same time!

Buzz!

The Dao Strings shook violently, as though the True Immortal were struggling desperately.

Only then did the cultivators on the battlefield come to their senses as if awakening from a dream.

Some continued attacking the True Immortal's body, while others moved to surround the golden armor, beard, and hair.

The battle situation had already become clear. The True Immortal had completely lost any chance of turning things around and was about to die at the hands of this group of ants...

A flash of light passed.

Yet the dying True Immortal suddenly disappeared before everyone's eyes.

It was not that he had managed to escape again. Rather, the second trial had already ended.

Li Fan could sense the strange glances that the surrounding cultivators occasionally directed at him, but he merely responded with a smile.

"The third trial..."

"Life!"

Before the cold voice had even finished, the Dao Net that had previously served as nothing more than a backdrop suddenly descended from above.

It was as though the heavens themselves had collapsed. Dao Strings stretched across the sky like enormous shattered cliffs and boulders.

Caught completely off guard by this calamity, quite a few cultivators were killed on the spot.

Judging from their appearance, they had not been teleported away just before death. They had truly died!

The Dao Net that they had previously relied upon now instead transformed into a weapon of slaughter.

The cultivators participating in the trial could only rely on their own abilities to evade this inexplicable disaster.

But...

The cultivators of the Shuo Star Sea, especially those who had come to participate in the Immortal Hunters selection, relied almost entirely on Dao Primal Symbols and Dao Net commands for their cultivation and combat strength.

Without the augmentation of the Dao Net, the methods they could employ were pitifully few.

How could they possibly survive beneath such a catastrophe that seemed capable of bringing the heavens crashing down?

Aside from the outstanding elites of the various major factions, everyone else was killed violently in a matter of moments.

The remaining cultivators could do nothing more than struggle desperately before the overwhelming calamity brought about by the crushing Dao Net.

As the collapse of the Dao Net grew increasingly severe, the places where they could evade it became fewer and fewer.

Before long, everyone had gradually been driven into a dead end.

Relying on his Light Transformation Escape Technique, Li Fan was still able to move with relative ease.

However, looking at the brutal state of the selection before him, with only sixteen people remaining, including himself, he could not help but feel a trace of doubt.

"The previous Immortal Hunters trials were never this severe. Could it be that some great upheaval has occurred in the Shuo Star Sea recently?"

The brutality of this examination had clearly exceeded everyone's expectations as well.

The calmness they had shown before was nowhere to be seen on their faces. Instead, they were filled with uncontrollable panic.

They unleashed every means at their disposal, struggling desperately for survival amid this apocalyptic scene.

"Ah!"

Another person was crushed to death by a falling Dao String.

It happened right before Li Fan's eyes.

His soul and spirit were completely destroyed, turning into points of light that merged into the Dao Net.

Yet this brief glance allowed Li Fan to discover something unusual.

Li Fan had witnessed the annihilation of countless living beings. Yet the "death" occurring at this moment seemed different from the true fall of a cultivator.

His heart stirred, and Li Fan immediately understood.

"Of course. This trial appears to be testing the candidates, but in truth, it is testing their deaths. Without borrowing the power of the Dao Net, anyone capable of surviving a calamity of this magnitude, with the Dao Net collapsing around them, must possess cultivation and attainments that have reached a miraculous level. Cultivators of the Shuo Star Sea have been learning through the Dao Net since childhood. There is practically no possibility of them passing this trial..."

"If someone can persist until the very end, they are probably a villain secretly lurking from an external domain!"

"To die is actually to pass the trial. Anyone who survives until the end will probably be captured and subjected to severe interrogation."

As Li Fan pondered, he quickly understood the ingenuity behind this trial.

"Of course, dying too quickly probably will not work either. The first group of people should have genuinely died. However, having no ability to react at all when the calamity descended means their deaths were well deserved. Only those who die at neither the earliest nor the latest moment can be selected."

Li Fan considered the profound meaning of the trial from the perspective of its examiner rather than its examinee. Naturally, he quickly grasped the logic behind it.

Thus, he gradually pretended that he was running out of strength, allowing himself to fall into an increasingly perilous situation.

It had to be said that even if one could guess the truth behind this trial, when faced with the strands of the Dao Net collapsing downward, an ordinary person would still find it extremely difficult to give up resisting and calmly accept death.

There was great terror between life and death.

Moreover, the sight of the Dao Net crushing everything beneath it could instinctively provoke the desire to resist and survive within anyone's heart.

Even someone like Li Fan, who had spent countless years walking along the edge of life and death and had narrowly escaped death countless times, could barely suppress the urge to break free and flee when he sensed the silver-white strands of the Dao Net about to crush him.

Fortunately, he forcibly restrained himself.

He allowed the Dao strands to crush his body into pieces.

In an instant, endless silver radiance filled his vision.

Li Fan's divine sense seemed to lose consciousness.

In the midst of that state of neither awareness nor sensation, Li Fan vaguely sensed an enormous will sweeping across his mind.

Under his instinctive vigilance, Li Fan completely stilled his mind, returning it to infinite tranquility. In an instant, he relied upon the miniature Dao Net hidden within his divine sense and casually placed Ma Tiande's memories on its surface.

The miniature Dao Net and the Dao Net were, in essence, both imprints of the Great Dao. There was fundamentally no difference between them. Having perfectly merged into it at this moment, even that enormous will, after briefly examining him, failed to notice anything unusual.

The oppressive sensation of being watched by a terrifying monster hidden in the shadows disappeared.

Li Fan "came back to life," recovering both his body and consciousness.

He was no longer in the previous trial space. Instead, he had arrived on a circular floating landmass suspended within the void.

Outside the floating landmass stood numerous figures. Their features could not be seen clearly, but they were all gazing at the remaining participants, pointing at them and chatting and laughing among themselves. There was no malice in their expressions.

Li Fan glanced around.

He discovered that the other survivors seemed to have yet to recover from what had happened and were still somewhat dazed.

From time to time, figures flickered into existence and appeared on the circular floating landmass.

Only after the time it took to drink half a cup of tea had passed, when everyone had arrived, did that announcing voice sound once again.

This time, however, it no longer carried the coldness and solemnity from before.

Instead, it contained a hint of warmth.

"Final trial, battle!"

Li Fan understood.

"I am afraid that reaching this point means the trial has already been completed. The so-called battle is probably nothing more than a way to determine the rankings."

There was no doubt that those who had survived to this round were absolute prodigies of the Shuo Star Sea.

Although they had yet to fully recover, upon hearing the command, they instinctively launched surprise attacks against the "enemies" beside them.

Li Fan naturally came under attack as well.

His body flashed like lightning, effortlessly avoiding every attack.

At the same time, he shouted angrily, "Disarm!"

All the cultivators who had targeted Li Fan stiffened slightly. Almost in unison, they retreated rapidly.

Everyone present was intelligent. They all understood that this final trial was merely a formality. Losing during the battle was one thing.

Those watching from the surroundings were clearly fellow members of the Immortal Hunters. If someone were stripped naked in front of everyone, how could they face each other in the future?

Thus, under the deterrence of the "Disarm" technique, everyone tacitly chose not to take Li Fan as their opponent. They merely launched occasional sneak attacks from the shadows, all of which Li Fan easily evaded.

This battle was strictly limited to the point of defeat.

Before long, the outcome was clear.

A light screen descended above the circular floating landmass.

The names of everyone present were listed upon it.

There were twenty-eight names in total, arranged from first to last.

Their overall rankings were determined according to their performances in the four trials.

Ma Tiande's name was ranked fifth.

"It was probably his performance during the second trial, the operation to surround and capture the True Immortal, that raised his score."

Li Fan looked toward the person ranked first on the list.

The gazes of nearly everyone present were gathered upon him.

It was precisely the cultivator who had previously displayed a wind divine ability and blown the True Immortal's rain back against him.

His surname was Dao, and his given name was Yuan.

He had a slender figure and deep-set eyes. Facing the attention of everyone around him, he showed no trace of nervousness. Instead, he smiled and cupped his hands in greeting.

Recalling the information he had previously read, Li Fan remembered this person's background.

He came from the Shuo Star Sea, from the Myriad Dao Palace.

Since the Myriad Dao Palace dared to use the 'Dao' in its name, it naturally possessed the confidence to back it up.

It was originally a surviving faction from another shattered star sea. Despite being coveted by numerous forces, it had managed to establish a firm foothold.

It was said that it had also made an extremely outstanding contribution to the establishment of the Dao Net. It had now become an existence that could stand alongside the Sword Domain and Saber Island.

"Since all of you have already joined the Immortal Hunters, from this day onward, you must obey orders."

"Those who disobey orders will be executed without mercy."

"Next is the first mission you will have to face."

Everyone present wore somewhat peculiar expressions upon hearing this.

They had not expected to be sent on a mission immediately after joining the Immortal Hunters, without receiving any training whatsoever.

"Half a day ago, a True Immortal was attracted by the [Immortal Bait] we had set up. He crossed the Land of Annihilation and entered the area covered by the Dao Net."

"He is already not far from the Shuo Star Sea."

"This True Immortal has suffered severe injuries. The Dao he cultivates is the [Yi]. He is particularly suitable to serve as your first hunting target, allowing you to practice your skills..."

A figure stepped out from among the spectators outside the circular floating landmass.

Although he was short in stature, his body was covered in densely packed, well-tempered muscles. He wore a blood-red short shirt and carried a black stone pillar on his shoulder. His appearance was exceedingly strange.

"I hope your first hunt will be a complete success!" The man in blood-red clothes laughed sinisterly as he patted the stone pillar on his shoulder.

[TL Note: Xi (希), Yi (夷) and Wei (微) are Daoist terms derived from the Tao Te Ching. Laozi describes three properties of the formless Dao: Yi (夷), that which cannot be seen; Xi (希), that which cannot be heard; and Wei (微), that which cannot be grasped.]
