Next

Chapter 1447: Riding The Long River Against The Current

Before the blood-robed man had even finished speaking, everyone suddenly felt a strange suction force appear above the circular floating landmass. It was as though a vortex had suddenly formed, pulling all of them into it.

Before being swallowed by the vortex, Li Fan inadvertently glanced once more at the stone pillar carried on the blood-robed man's shoulder.

At the instant the man had lightly patted the stone pillar just now, Li Fan had sensed it. A faint tremor had simultaneously occurred within the Dao Net.

"A Pillar of the Great Dao? Or perhaps, it is itself one of the Great Daos?"

"To be able to carry the Great Dao on his shoulders and appear so completely at ease. This person's strength..."

"I'm afraid he is no weaker than Nameless!"

Li Fan's heart tightened.

The invisible vortex swallowed all twenty-eight newly inducted Immortal Hunters.

By the time they recovered their senses, they were already in an unfamiliar starry sky.

Relevant information about this operation came through the Dao Net.

"The True Immortal is expected to arrive here in half an hour."

"Use the remaining time to discuss how we will conduct the hunt."

As everyone absorbed the information, they all looked toward the so-called "Immortal Bait" floating in the void and emitting strange fluctuations.

It was a quietly floating bronze coffin, surrounded by carvings of nine dragons and nine phoenixes.

What caused an inexplicable chill to rise in their hearts was that one corner of the coffin lid had already been opened by someone, though no one knew who. Intermittent whispers could be heard coming from within.

Although the sound originated from the bronze coffin, it seemed as though the whispers were appearing directly behind their ears.

Demonic whispers filled their ears. Although they did not seem to have any effect on everyone for the moment, since this coffin could actually attract True Immortals to cross the Star Sea without regard for the hardships involved, it clearly possessed something extraordinary.

Everyone silently observed it for a while. In the end, Dao Yuan, who had ranked first in the test, was the first to speak.

"Fortunately, I happen to know a little about the origins of this Immortal Bait. Allow me to offer what little knowledge I have as an introduction. If anything I say is inappropriate, I hope everyone will forgive me."

Dao Yuan's attitude was humble, and the surrounding people nodded one after another.

"As everyone knows, among the surviving True Immortals in the Star Sea today, there are roughly two things they seek. The first is comprehension of the Great Dao, in order to prove the Nameless Realm. The second..."

"Naturally, it is a method of avoiding calamities and escaping disaster. Back then, the Immortal Realm suddenly perished, and countless True Immortals died like ants. Those immortals who were fortunate enough to survive consequently became obsessed with finding ways to escape and survive. It is said that after the destruction of the Immortal Realm, the Nameless True Immortals gathered together and researched three miraculous methods of avoiding disaster."

Although Dao Yuan had said that these matters were common knowledge, judging from the expressions of the newly inducted Immortal Hunters, it was obvious that such secrets were far beyond what ordinary people could know. Most of them were deeply captivated and listened attentively.

However, some people were unimpressed by Dao Yuan's display of knowledge and merely sneered coldly.

"It is said that these three methods of avoiding disaster can protect living beings and allow them to survive every calamity in the world. Even a disaster as great as the destruction of the Immortal Realm could be survived safely."

"The bronze coffin before us is one of the three methods of avoiding disaster, the [Mystic Coffin of World Traversal]. This Mystic Coffin seems to be connected to other worlds. Once someone lies inside it, they will completely disappear from this world..."

At this point, someone could not help interrupting Dao Yuan.

"Completely disappear? If that is the case, how can we determine whether the person has actually perished or truly escaped to another world to avoid disaster?"

This was clearly the question in everyone else's hearts as well.

Dao Yuan smiled faintly and pointed at the carvings of nine dragons and nine phoenixes on the bronze coffin.

"Have you noticed these dragon and phoenix carvings? Once someone lies inside the coffin and disappears, the dragons and phoenixes will cry out together and ultimately reflect the scene of the world on the other side. It is said that without exception, those who appear there are overjoyed and clasp their hands in thanks. Presumably, even while sending the person inside the coffin to another world, some form of communication can still be maintained."

"As for whether these scenes are genuine... The countless True Immortals, and even Nameless True Immortals, who have eagerly sought after the [Mystic Coffin of World Traversal] already tell us enough."

Everyone sucked in a cold breath upon hearing this.

"Then the one before us..."

Dao Yuan smiled.

"Naturally, it is an imitation."

Although this was the answer they had expected, everyone still could not help sighing.

"From the moment the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal was created, its purpose was to help immortals avoid calamities and disasters. Thus, back then, several great beings with divine abilities joined forces and announced its existence to all surviving immortals throughout the world. They also passed down the coffin's aura fluctuations and the method of activating it."

"Those who were fated could enter the coffin and avoid the calamity."

Dao Yuan continued explaining in an unhurried manner.

Only then did someone among the newly inducted Immortal Hunters realize something.

"If that is the case, does that not mean that the powerful beings who originally forged the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal already used it to escape the world and flee to other worlds?"

Dao Yuan nodded.

"Exactly!"

"The Mystic Coffin was also the legacy they left behind. It is said that the true Mystic Coffins float through the Star Sea according to a special pattern, continuously emitting their proclamation fluctuations in all directions. Although we of the Shuo Star Sea have never seen a genuine Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, we have already been able to imitate the coffin's fluctuations..."

Suddenly, a cold laugh interrupted him.

"Brother Dao Yuan, how do you know that we of the Shuo Star Sea have never seen a genuine Mystic Coffin? For such a divine object, if you had never witnessed it with your own eyes, how could you imitate it so perfectly?"

Dao Yuan's expression, as well as those of the surrounding Immortal Hunters, changed instantly.

However, after uttering that mocking remark, the owner of the voice said nothing more. It was impossible to determine who had spoken.

Everyone secretly scrutinized one another, but they seemed unable to find anyone suspicious.

Seeing that the atmosphere had momentarily fallen into an awkward stalemate, someone stepped forward to smooth things over.

"Brother Dao Yuan, tell us about the other two methods of avoiding the world. We have only just heard such a secret, and we are truly curious!"

The others quickly echoed his words. Dao Yuan's expression also relaxed, and he spoke again.

"As for the other two methods of avoiding the world, I only know one of them."

"It is the [Reverse Flowing Boat]."

"The Reverse Flowing Boat?"

Everyone quietly savored the meaning of those three words.

Dao Yuan's expression became solemn.

"The long river of time surges endlessly, always flowing forward since ancient times. This Reverse Flowing Boat is said to be capable of carrying people upstream along the river of time, traveling back to a point before any of the calamities had occurred..."

"Ah!"

The shock brought by the Reverse Flowing Boat was clearly far greater than that of the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal. After all, reversing the flow of time was a dream desired by every living being.

Everyone's enthusiasm for discussion was immediately ignited, and they began talking among themselves.

"Brother Dao Yuan, was this Reverse Flowing Boat truly created by those True Immortals?"

"I say that is absolutely impossible. If they truly returned to the past, why has the fact that the Immortal Realm was destroyed not changed in the slightest?"

"Ah, that is because you do not understand. If one returns to the past and various details of history change, it would be like a river changing its course. Naturally, a different river would form downstream. We, however, would still be living within the original long river, so naturally we would be unable to perceive it."

"That is nonsense! According to what you are saying, should we not disappear as the long river of time changes its course? Why do we and everything in the world still exist?"

"This..."

"The principles of time have already surpassed the scope of what we can understand or imagine. There is no need for the two of you to argue so seriously."

"However, since Brother Dao Yuan speaks of it with such certainty... I'm afraid the existence of the Reverse Flowing Boat may not be false after all."

...

Everyone spoke over one another, each offering their own words.

In the end, Dao Yuan smiled. “Whether the Reverse Flowing Boat truly exists or not, I really cannot say for certain. Let alone me, even those seniors in the Shuo Star Sea may not truly know. It is merely a vague and ethereal rumor.”

Someone else asked, “The second method of avoiding disaster can already reverse the flow of the river of time. Then, as for the third method of avoiding the disaster, exactly what…”

The discussion about the Reverse Flowing Boat among the newly appointed Immortal Hunters came to an abrupt end. Everyone stared intently at Dao Yuan, waiting for his explanation.

Dao Yuan smiled bitterly. “As for this third method of avoiding worldly disaster, even among the immortals of that era, there were not many who knew its true details. However, everyone who knew its true nature firmly believed that it was the most perfect method of avoidance. If one could master that method, all the calamities and disasters in the world would be nothing more than fleeting clouds. That is true great freedom, great ease, and great liberation!”

As Dao Yuan spoke, a deep yearning inevitably appeared in his eyes.

Everyone present was the same.

Yet there was one person whose heart was shaken far more violently than the others upon hearing this.

It was Li Fan.

Because he had already learned of the existence of the annihilation calamity that would sweep across the entire mortal world from the countdown of [Truth], Li Fan had initially been filled with disdain when he heard about these so-called three great methods of avoiding the worldly disaster.

However, that disdain had already vanished without a trace after he heard about the first method, the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal.

By lying inside the Mystic Coffin, one could travel to another world. Clearly, this world was not as simple as another lower realm star sea. Almost instantly, Li Fan thought of the [Ancient Cang Primal Essence].

The Yao beast race could use it to cross possibilities. The True Immortals who had survived the Dao Annihilation Calamity might likewise possess the power to cross possibilities.

“If it merely allows random jumps between possibilities like those of the Yao beast race, then it probably would not deserve to be called ‘World-Traversal.’”

“If what Dao Yuan said is true, and every survivor who jumped across was overjoyed and expressed their gratitude…”

“Could it be that this Mystic Coffin can survey in advance, or even select, the possibilities it can reach?”

A thought stirred within Li Fan's mind.

The entire mortal world still had seven million three hundred and sixty thousand years of lifespan remaining.

In other words, there must still be possibilities that could survive for more than seven million years.

Although this number did not seem particularly long, compared with the [Yuanchu] possibility where Li Fan currently resided, which was already on the verge of death and could suffer the disaster of the Dao Annihilation Calamity at any moment, it could already be considered a long life.

If one could find such a paradise within the ruins of the destroyed Immortal Realm, it would be perfectly normal to be overjoyed.

…

If the existence of the Mystic Coffin of World-Traversal had merely caused Li Fan some surprise, then the existence of the Reverse Flowing Boat had shaken him to his core.

Of everyone present, Li Fan was the one most convinced that the [Reverse Flowing Boat] truly existed.

Putting aside the fact that Li Fan himself possessed such a strange treasure as [Truth], merely the [Kun-Qian Bone Hand] he had seen in the Xuanhuang Realm was enough to prove that the ancient immortals had already mastered certain divine abilities related to [Time].

“However, although one can reverse time, the descent of the Dao Annihilation Calamity is the momentum of the greater order. It is like the earth beneath the world. If the west is high and the east is low, then rivers will generally flow from west to east and pour into the sea. Even if one can repeatedly return to the source of a river and begin again, one cannot change the final outcome of the river flowing eastward into the sea.”

“Applied to reality, if one is ultimately unable to discover the true cause behind the eruption of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, then even if time is reversed again and again, it will all be futile. Moreover, traveling backward along the river of time must inevitably require an extremely painful price. Perhaps it can be done once or twice, but not three times.”

After this contemplation, Li Fan also understood the fundamental difference between his [Truth] and treasures capable of reversing time.

“When the Truth is made False, the Falsehood also becomes Truth.”

“With a single thought, one can create something from nothingness.”

“Even the concept of time exists because existence itself exists.”

“Let us use the eastward-flowing river as an analogy. My [Truth] is like being able to freely alter the terrain of the earth, and even the condition of the heavens.”

“Not only can I make all the rivers flow backward, I can even make the waters of the earth surge upward from below and rush into the heavens. There is nothing impossible about it…”

“Is this not precisely the great freedom, great ease, and great liberation that was spoken of?”

Although Li Fan already knew that the end of the mortal world existed, he was not particularly frightened.

Precisely because he believed that possessing [Truth] meant possessing the power to rewrite everything.

After witnessing the scene of the countdown to the apocalypse and its numbers leaping forward by tens of millions of times, Li Fan became even more certain of his judgment.

If there truly existed some great method of avoiding the world that could grant true great freedom, then perhaps the only one was [Truth].

Dao Yuan's words, however, awakened a hidden concern that Li Fan had long kept buried deep within his heart.

The scene he had once witnessed in the Darkest Star Sea, where True Immortals silently waited within the Xuanhuang Realm.

It seemed to indicate that the immortals of the past had already known of the existence of [Truth].

Just like the Immortal Hunters of today, they had already laid a trap within the Xuanhuang Realm.

It was only for some unknown reason that [Truth] arrived far later than expected.

This was what ultimately allowed Li Fan to escape that calamity.

Although this was merely one of Li Fan's conjectures, today, after hearing the rumors surrounding the [Reverse Flowing Boat] and the third method of avoiding the world, Li Fan seemed to have thought of a certain possibility, causing his mind to become incomparably solemn.

“The mortal world is probably a gigantic Xuanhuang Realm.”

“Just as everything that once happened in the Xuanhuang Realm. When disaster arrives, some people choose to flee at the first sign of danger, while others choose to stand forth and resolutely search for a way to save the world.”

“Still others simply give up, lying down and wasting away the rest of their lives.”

“Indeed, there is nothing new under the sun. The secrets I thought were secrets may not have been truly secret at all.”

“It is merely that the group of people who happened to know them are already gone.”

Li Fan had never underestimated the power of immortals.

Especially those True Immortals capable of crossing possibilities.

“If one day I leave the [Yuanchu] possibility and travel to other worlds, I must be even more cautious.”

Dao Yuan's words had stirred Li Fan's thoughts into turmoil, making it difficult for him to calm himself.

Fortunately, the reactions of the others present were no better. Thus, he did not reveal any flaws.

“All right, let us return to the main matter. We have just received news that our target, that True Immortal, entered the Dao through [Yi].”

“What is [Yi]?”

“To look upon it and see nothing. We must be careful and guard against it.”

As Dao Yuan spoke, his gaze swept toward the distant horizon.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1448: Xi And Yi Enters The Mystic Coffin

“Perhaps that True Immortal has already stealthily lurked nearby...” The moment Dao Yuan said this, everyone became somewhat tense, instantly awakening from the vague legend of secluded existence they had been discussing earlier.

Unlike the previous trials, this was the first time they were truly facing a True Immortal.

People could die.

Under the pressure of a life and death crisis, the newly inducted Immortal Hunters quickly took action, cooperating with one another and making preparations. Although this was their first time, and they had only received instructions through information transmission without anyone providing guidance on-site, they were all outstanding cultivators from the Shuo Star Sea, so their movements remained orderly.

For Immortal Hunters to hunt and replenish against a True Immortal, the first thing they needed to do was strengthen the power of the Dao Net here, and then use the Dao Net to conceal themselves.

If the entire Dao Net was like an invisible giant hand pinning it onto the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth through the combined support of four great forces, then in the frontier regions of the Shuo Star Sea, where the Dao Net was weak, the Immortal Hunters needed to use themselves as nails to further stabilize the Dao Net. This would allow the Dao Net to mobilize more power during the battle that followed.

It was said that the veteran Immortal Hunters of the organization worked together with perfect coordination. In an instant, they could reinforce and seal off the Dao Net within an entire region. Compared to them, this group of newcomers appeared somewhat flustered.

There was especially some dispute over who should serve as the temporary 'nails.'

Under normal circumstances, for the sake of efficiency, they would naturally choose those with the deepest mastery of the Dao Net. However, they had only just met one another and did not know each other well. The rankings announced earlier were merely the final rankings based on comprehensive performance in Dao, Martial, Life, and various other trials. They did not specifically measure one's understanding of the Dao Net.

Moreover, if they relied on the combined efforts of everyone to personally enter the Dao Net, it was akin to bathing and immersing oneself within the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, bringing endless benefits. Thus, anyone with ambition was unwilling to yield.

Even Dao Yuan, who ranked first, could occupy one position himself, but if he wanted to decide all the other candidates as well, that was absolutely impossible.

However, the newly inducted Immortal Hunters did not have much time to prepare. With every passing moment, the tension in everyone's hearts grew stronger.

In the end, someone chose to yield.

Ma Tiande cupped his fists, his expression calm and composed.

“Everyone, as the saying goes, a wise man knows others, while a truly enlightened man knows himself. The reason I was able to rank fifth was largely because of my performance in the Martial Trial. I will not compete with everyone for it!”

With that, he calmly gave up his position as one of the nails.

Everyone looked toward the high-minded Li Fan, and their expressions subtly changed. Influenced by Li Fan, several cultivators in succession also took the initiative to declare that they would yield.

Very soon, the eight candidates who would serve as the Dao Nails were decided.

What surprised Li Fan somewhat was that the purple-robed female cultivator who ranked second among the newly inducted Immortal Hunters, the one who wielded three long swords against her enemies, had also voluntarily given up this opportunity to become a Dao Nail.

The purple-robed female cultivator was named Jin Changyue. Her eyes remained tightly closed as she said calmly, “What I care about more is using these three swords of mine to personally hack a True Immortal to death.”

The three long swords rang with repeated sword cries as Jin Changyue spoke.

With the personnel decided, everyone soon became solemn and, according to what they had learned, joined forces to reinforce the Dao Net here.

Eight figures faced one another and formed a circle.

The other Immortal Hunters surrounded them and then issued one Dao Net command after another.

Every command seemed to tighten the strings of the Dao.

The eight figures gradually became dim.

It seemed as though they were merging into this world.

Yet everyone could sense the strange changes quietly taking place in this world. There was now an additional layer of powerful and obscure shadows.

Although they were extremely well concealed, they secretly resonated with the Dao Net commands being issued by everyone.

The entire process of reinforcing and connecting the Dao Net lasted ninety-nine breaths.

Strictly speaking, it barely met the passing standard for a beginner's first practical attempt.

The eight cultivators who had served as Dao Nails also returned to their normal forms.

However, all of them looked somewhat dazed, with traces of surprise mixed within their expressions. It seemed they were still immersed in the experience of having personally entered the Dao Net.

Li Fan looked at the eight people and noticed that they had already become clearly different from before.

“Within this small world, the Dao Net power that each of these eight people can mobilize is almost equivalent to that of a True Immortal. Eight True Immortals joining forces...”

“It seems that the future Yi Immortal is in dire straits.”

Li Fan secretly assessed the Dao Nails before him.

Although they possessed power comparable to that of True Immortals, in terms of the pressure they gave off, they were absolutely not as oppressive as actual True Immortals.

“In essence, they are still mortals. Their ‘potential’ has not changed at all. It is merely that a corner of the Great Dao that originally floated in the heavens has descended and been placed within their grasp.”

The eight Dao Nails would serve as the main force in hunting the True Immortal. The others would serve as support and reinforcement.

Now that the battlefield had been prepared, the next step was to construct the barrier and ensure that the True Immortal could enter but could not leave.

With the cooperation of the Dao Net, this was extremely simple.

The seemingly ordinary void was actually in a state where the existence of its boundaries could disappear at any moment. It was much like the High Wall that surrounded the Darkest Star Sea. Once the concept of 'ahead' disappeared, naturally there would be no path forward.

“This is one of the most important trump cards of the Immortal Hunters organization. Its repeated success in hunting True Immortals owes much to this method. It is known as the [Immortal Mending Cage].”

“The direct disappearance of existence and concepts may have been inspired by the Dao Annihilation Calamity...”

As Li Fan worked seamlessly with everyone, issuing one command after another and generating the Immortal Mending Cage, he secretly recorded the subtle fluctuations produced by the Dao Net.

To others, the fluctuations of the Dao Net were both nearly imperceptible and incomparably complex.

Even if one personally participated as a caster, it was absolutely impossible to record them all in a single attempt.

Strictly speaking, the so-called 'fluctuations' were merely subtle changes resembling ripples produced as the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth operated on its own. They simply originated from the Dao Net. Only someone with an extremely profound understanding of the Dao could sense their existence at such close range.

Moreover, the Dao Net commands themselves were composed of countless Dao Primal symbols. The Immortal Hunters present coordinated with one another, issuing an unknown number of commands with every breath.

With such an enormous volume of data, even an Nameless True Immortal might not necessarily be able to comprehend it all at a glance.

But Li Fan did not need to identify or memorize them.

The miniature Dao Net within his divine sense was like a mirror, completely reflecting the fluctuations within the Dao Net at this very moment.

Although Li Fan could not yet completely analyze the principles behind these fluctuations, that did not prevent him from first [copying] and [pasting] them.

“This is the power of Consciousness Soul Transformation, reflecting the Great Dao.”

“It can accomplish what even the Nameless cannot.”

Having secretly stolen a divine ability, Li Fan felt rather pleased with himself. He became even more expectant of the upcoming infiltration into the Immortal Hunters organization.

A great hunting net had already been silently spread.

The newly inducted Immortal Hunters had completed all the necessary preparations. They concealed themselves around the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, waiting only for the Yi Immortal to walk willingly into their trap.

Everyone's minds were connected to the Dao Net as they communicated with one another.

“Everyone, look! What is that sign?”

In the real void, there was not the slightest movement.

Yet the Dao Net that had been reinforced throughout this stretch of starry sky suddenly twisted for no apparent reason.

And this distortion was rapidly charging toward the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal!

“Although it cannot be seen, it inevitably leaves traces within the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth! It is like an invisible monster walking across snow. Though you cannot see the monster, it will still leave footprints behind. Brothers, a True Immortal has taken the bait!”

Everyone was greatly encouraged, though they could not help feeling nervous.

“Do we act now?”

“Not yet. Wait a little longer. Let him fully reveal himself before we make our move. In any case, he definitely cannot drag away this Mystic Coffin. He can only obediently lie inside it according to the rumored method of entering the coffin to evade calamity.” Dao Yuan spoke with the bearing of a great general, his voice deep and composed.

Everyone nodded in agreement.

The distorted force traveling through the Dao Net soon arrived near the Mystic Coffin. After circling around it and observing it for a while, it seemed to be attempting to move the Mystic Coffin away.

However, although the Mystic Coffin was forged by Shuo Star Sea as a counterfeit, it still possessed some of the true Mystic Coffin's wondrous characteristics. It was as if it did not exist in this world at all. Any force exerted upon it simply came to nothing, like carrying water in a bamboo basket.

The Mystic Coffin remained completely motionless.

Although the attempt of the distorted force ended in failure, it instead seemed delighted.

It stood motionless in place as an even greater distortion approached rapidly from afar.

“Something is wrong! This force does not belong to one True Immortal. There are two of them?”

Through their movements within the Dao Net and the degree to which the bent Dao strings were distorted, the newly initiated Immortal Hunters quickly determined that besides the Yi Immortal who had entered the Net this time, there was actually another True Immortal!

The two accompanied each other and were almost completely fused into one.

By ordinary means, even if they were standing right in front of them, they might not necessarily be detected. Only through the traces they left within the Dao Net could their existence be distinguished.

“Everyone, do not panic. This is actually a great thing! Capturing one is a capture, and capturing two is also a capture. I never expected that we would be able to establish such a great achievement right after becoming Immortal Hunters!” The speaker was Jiang Yuming, ranked third among them.

With the power of the Dao Net supporting him, this person was filled with confidence. Even when faced with the unexpected appearance of two True Immortals, he remained completely fearless.

Dao Yuan also spoke up. “However, we must not be careless. Although this place was previously a region where the power of the Dao Net was weak, the fact that they were able to deceive the Dao Net's perception is enough to show how extraordinary these two are.”

“Unable to see them, unable to hear them. I think the other one has mastered the Dao of [Xi]. Only when Xi and Yi complement each other can they deceive the Dao Net.”

Although an unexpected variable had appeared, everyone remained calm despite their surprise. After secretly communicating and making some tactical adjustments, they simply waited for the two True Immortals to crawl into the coffin.

Under the attention of countless eyes, the lid of the Mystic Coffin suddenly moved.

Like an ancient bronze gate sealed away for countless years, it emitted a heavy and decayed sound.

The bent Dao strings instantly returned to normal.

At the same time, the newly initiated Immortal Hunters received confirmation that two people had indeed entered the Mystic Coffin being used as bait.

“Attack!”

Just as Dao Yuan issued the order, another voice suddenly rang out and interrupted him.

“Wait!”

The speaker was none other than Ma Tiande'!

Seeing someone object, Dao Yuan could not help slightly furrowing his brows.

Li Fan, however, quickly explained with a solemn expression, “Brother Dao said earlier that the Mystic Coffin requires a specific method to activate. Why don't we wait a little longer?”

“Perhaps they are also using a bait. That is not impossible.”

As soon as Li Fan said this, everyone indeed sensed that something was wrong. They all looked toward the Mystic Coffin once more.

After the fluctuations entered the Mystic Coffin, there was no further movement.

It seemed that they too were waiting for something.

“Sure enough, there is something strange about this.”

“If the two Yi and Xi Immortals themselves had truly come here to evade calamity, they should have immediately and impatiently begun verifying it the moment they entered the Mystic Coffin. How could there still be no movement even now?” Jin Changyue said solemnly.

“There will not be some flaw inside the Mystic Coffin, will there? If the two Yi and Xi Immortals realize that it is fake, would that not cause them to flee?”

“Rest assured. Although the Mystic Coffin is fake, it is also real. Other than being unable to cross the world, it is virtually indistinguishable from the true Mystic Coffin.”

The speaker was a cultivator who had never spoken before. His name was Nangong Hongzhao, but the others did not know much about his background.

Since he spoke with such certainty, he presumably had his reasons.

Now was not the time to investigate further.

Everyone held their breath and waited.

At this moment, Nangong Hongzhao spoke again. “It is said that every time the Mystic Coffin activates its world traversal, its location will change. This is also why the two Yi and Xi Immortals are merely testing it now and have yet to truly activate it.”

“It can be confirmed that the ones inside the coffin now are merely bait. They would never be willing to waste such a perfect opportunity.”

As if to prove Nangong Hongzhao's words, another mass of distorted fluctuations appeared within the Dao Net in the distance.

Its degree of distortion upon the Dao strings was exactly the same as before.

As though the scene were repeating itself, they once again crawled into the Mystic Coffin.

The moment they entered the Mystic Coffin, an incomparably profound sound came from within.

It sounded like a distant, ethereal song, yet also like some solemn and sacred chant.

“Attack!” Dao Yuan shouted again.

The lid of the Mystic Coffin suddenly slammed shut.

At the same time, the Immortal Mending Cage silently descended and completely sealed off this region of the star sea.

Inside the Mystic Coffin, there seemed to be no awareness of the changes outside. They apparently believed that the closing of the coffin lid was merely a normal part of its operation.

The chanting continued without end.

Li Fan listened carefully, committing every change in the syllables to memory. His heart suddenly stirred.

“This rhythm seems somewhat similar to the Immortal Scripture for Evading Calamity that the Grand Mage passed down.”

“Could it be that the shadow beside the Grand Mage has some connection with those Nameless True Immortals who forged the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal back then?”

Li Fan suddenly thought of something.

Back then, the obscure Xuanhuang cultivator had listened beside the shadow, and the later Ten Great Immortal Sects were born from that encounter. The Grand Mage had already reached the Half-Immortal realm, yet he had always remained seated beside the shadow, unwilling to leave even half a step. Even his words and actions clearly showed signs of imitating that shadow's method of transmitting the Dao.

“Could it be that this shadow was left behind by one of the Nameless True Immortals who departed back then?”

“Perhaps it originally possessed other forms of spirituality and divine abilities. But after the Nameless True Immortal used the Mystic Coffin to travel toward another possibility, all that remains now is an empty shell of light and shadow.”

Just as Li Fan's thoughts churned, another extremely strange change occurred within the scene.

The two Yi and Xi Immortals remained hidden inside the Mystic Coffin and refused to come out, leaving the others unable to break into the coffin. They could only wait for the two to discover that the Mystic Coffin was ineffective and emerge, at which point they would surround and attack them.

What they could never have anticipated was...

As the chanting inside the Mystic Coffin grew increasingly loud, the Mystic Coffin itself suddenly began emitting waves of hazy, multicolored radiance.

The radiance drifted unpredictably. Yet once it appeared, it showed no sign of fading.

As the Immortal Hunters gradually became suspicious and uncertain, the multicolored radiance suddenly erupted.

It completely engulfed the Mystic Coffin.

When everyone looked again...

The counterfeit Mystic Coffin had vanished into thin air together with the two Yi and Xi Immortals!

Next

Chapter 1449: The Case Of The Sacred Shang That Remains Unresolved

At the Immortal Bait site, the atmosphere suddenly fell into an eerie silence.

Everyone looked at one another, yet no one dared to speak first.

After a long while, someone finally asked in a timid voice, “Brother Dao, according to the information we received, that Mystic Coffin of World Traversal... is fake, right?”

Hearing this, everyone looked somewhat helplessly toward Dao Yuan.

Dao Yuan, who had always appeared extremely composed, also showed a trace of panic in his eyes at this moment. Nevertheless, he cleared his throat lightly and said solemnly, “Do not lose your composure! Perhaps the Mystic Coffin was merely concealed by the Two Immortals Xi and Yi through some unfathomable means, and is still in its original location. We...”

Before he could finish, a panicked voice interrupted him.

“How is that possible? It was clearly a fake Mystic Coffin, yet it actually managed to cross the world?!”

As he spoke, the person recklessly flew toward the place where the Mystic Coffin had originally been.

It was none other than Nangong Hongzhao, who had previously been so certain about the authenticity of the Mystic Coffin!

“Damn it!”

All the newly inducted Immortal Hunters changed expressions.

If Dao Yuan's judgment was correct, then Nangong Hongzhao's action could very well result in him directly facing the two True Immortals and losing his life on the spot. Worse still, it could expose everyone else who was lying in ambush.

Everyone wanted to stop Nangong Hongzhao's reckless action.

Unfortunately, his figure was elusive and his speed astonishingly fast. In the blink of an eye, he had already arrived at his destination.

Everyone immediately held their breath.

Fortunately, the tragic scene they had anticipated did not occur.

However, this did not make their expressions any more relaxed. Instead, they became even more bewildered.

“The Mystic Coffin of World Traversal is really gone.”

“Along with it, the two Xi and Yi True Immortals have also disappeared.”

The seriousness of the matter had clearly exceeded what these newly inducted Immortal Hunters could handle. Dao Yuan therefore decisively chose to report it through the Dao Net.

The reaction from the other end of the Dao Net also somewhat exceeded his expectations.

“What? The Immortal Bait is gone? Impossible!”

“According to the Dao Net's observations, it is clearly still in its original location!”

“Where is Yan Xuantai? Why wasn't it him who contacted me?!”

The furious voice traveled through the ripples of the Dao Net from the distant center of the Shuo Star Sea, reaching everyone's ears.

By the time the final word was spoken, the owner of the voice had already truly descended here.

It was a diminutive old man no more than three feet tall.

His silver-white hair and beard were even longer than his body, coiling beneath his feet like flowing clouds.

The old man's expression was frighteningly gloomy as his gaze swept across everyone.

Without exception, the newly inducted Immortal Hunters instinctively lowered their heads, not daring to meet his eyes.

“Who is this...”

Li Fan also sensed the extraordinary power of the old man before him.

With every breath the old man took, the invisible Dao Strings between heaven and earth produced corresponding ripples. Even the Dao Net in this place became clearer and more stable because of his arrival.

In Ma Tiande's memories, he had once seen this old man from afar.

The gathering where he had originally obtained the position of Dao Net operator had been presided over by this old man.

“One of the governing elders of the Shuo Star Sea Administration Council, Nangong Fusheng.”

Nangong Fusheng instantly appeared at the place where the Immortal Bait Mystic Coffin had originally been.

Looking at Nangong Hongzhao, who was standing there and muttering incoherently, Nangong Fusheng angrily shouted, “Useless!”

With a single slap, he sent Nangong Hongzhao flying.

Leaving him helplessly drifting through the star sea, Nangong Fusheng's fury gradually subsided. He carefully examined his surroundings.

Wave after wave of ripples spread outward with Nangong Fusheng at the center.

The Immortal Hunters watching from afar felt as if the entire world had transformed into a sea of violent storms. It churned and roared. The howling waves seemed capable of swallowing them completely at any moment.

They could only desperately rely on the power of the Dao Net to keep their bodies stable.

The ripples of the Dao Net continued without end.

In the end, Nangong Fusheng reached his right hand into the void and pulled out a black long spike covered in brilliant seven-colored flowing light.

After the long spike was removed, Nangong Fusheng did not become angry. Instead, he laughed.

“Yan Xuantai, what a method! You actually managed to deceive the Dao Net and conceal the truth from heaven itself!”

The newly inducted Immortal Hunters also stared fixedly at the black long spike radiating seven-colored light in Nangong Fusheng's hand.

Their gazes flickered.

In their previous understanding, the Dao Net was equivalent to the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. Its heavenly might was unmatched by anything in the world, and one could only find ways to control and harness it.

Yet now, during the first mission they had carried out after officially joining the Immortal Hunters organization, they had encountered an existence capable of deceiving the Dao Net.

Moreover, right before their eyes, it had staged a complete escape by means of the golden cicada shedding its shell.

“Just who exactly is this Yan Xuantai?”

A question inevitably arose in everyone's hearts.

The fake Mystic Coffin had actually managed to take the two True Immortals, who had already been regarded as prey, away with it.

Moreover, there seemed to be faint traces of a conspiracy involving members of the Shuo Star Sea Immortal Hunters organization.

This was absolutely no trivial matter.

Soon, apart from Nangong Fusheng, three other cultivators arrived one after another.

Even though they concealed their figures and restrained their imposing auras, their arrival still seemed to stir enormous waves within the Dao Net of this place.

The newly inducted Immortal Hunters could only tremble in fear. When questioned, they did not dare conceal anything and truthfully recounted everything that had happened.

“Go back first.”

“I believe this matter is not that simple.”

Nangong Fusheng spoke solemnly to the three figures. He showed no intention of concealing his words from the others.

His silver hair and beard continued to grow without any wind.

They enveloped all the Immortal Hunters within.

Just as they were about to leave, Li Fan vaguely heard Nangong Hongzhao's anxious cry from outside the silver-haired enclosure.

“Third Granduncle, wait for me!”

The silver hair enveloped them, then dispersed.

In the blink of an eye, everyone had been brought back to the center of the Shuo Star Sea.

They were separately taken into countless pitch-black compartments for a second round of questioning.

Since they were genuinely newcomers who knew nothing about the matter, the organization did not make things too difficult for them. After several routine interrogations, the questioning ended.

However, they were not released.

Instead, they were still confined within countless pitch-black compartments resembling death row cells.

Although their freedom of movement was not restricted, it was limited to this area.

They could not leave.

The endless darkness gradually devoured everyone's will.

At first, the newly inducted Immortal Hunters still held onto hope, believing that their confinement would soon come to an end.

But as the days passed, it seemed as though the organization had completely forgotten about them. After arranging for them to stay here, there had not been the slightest word from outside.

Gradually, everyone's state of mind began to change in different ways.

"I understand now. Hunting True Immortals and the disappearance of the Mystic Coffin are all still part of the Immortal Hunters assessment. We are still undergoing the assessment! I must pull myself together and not become dispirited!"

"I had such a promising future, yet I actually encountered something like this. I'm afraid my entire life is ruined."

"The Mystic Coffin disappeared with two True Immortals. Such a major incident must have someone held responsible. Could it be that they intend to use us as scapegoats?"

...

Most people lived in constant fear and anxiety.

Under such circumstances, those who could still remain calm were the true prodigies.

Feeling the three sword intents beside him that had never ceased, Li Fan nodded slightly.

"Ever since Jin Changyue was imprisoned here, she has continuously been nurturing this sword intent. Being imprisoned for no reason and suffering an unjust accusation has naturally generated resentment within her. Yet this is precisely the best time to nourish the sword with her intent."

"However, for this sword intent to reach great accomplishment, someone will have to be sacrificed to the sword."

Aside from Jin Changyue, Dao Yuan was also quite capable of remaining calm. Apart from some fluctuations in his aura at the beginning, he had spent the rest of his time like a hidden dragon lying dormant in the depths, quietly biding his time and waiting for an opportunity.

As for Li Fan, naturally, he felt no anxiety whatsoever. Even so, he continued to conceal his true state, repeatedly pretending to show signs of anxiety and despair.

In reality, he was constantly recalling the scene of the two Xi and Yi Immortals activating the Mystic Coffin World Traversal and departing.

As time passed, the mysterious sound recited by the two Xi and Yi Immortals gradually faded from Li Fan's memory. Even the recording within the Dao Deviation Stone was slowly becoming blurred.

Li Fan could only repeat it again and again, deepening his impression.

But the overall momentum that had ultimately faded away completely was impossible to change.

Li Fan had also tried directly using the miniature Dao Net within his divine sense to reflect the fluctuations from that time.

However, the tremors of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth caused by the mysterious sound had all occurred within the Mystic Coffin.

Li Fan could only observe it from afar through the Dao Net and was unable to record it accurately.

"That Mystic Coffin is said to be fake, yet it is actually real."

"Apart from being unable to traverse the world, it is almost indistinguishable from the genuine Mystic Coffin..."

Li Fan recalled what Nangong Hongzhao had said previously, and his mind stirred.

Although Nangong Hongzhao had not been taken back by Nangong Fusheng earlier, several days later, he was also thrown into this Black Prison.

However, he did not display any anxiety or worry. Instead, he looked utterly dejected, as if he had botched some important task.

These days, he had simply been lying lazily on the ground.

His eyes closed as he pretended to sleep.

Li Fan quietly approached him.

"Brother Nangong, there is something I have been holding in for a long time."

"It feels like a bone stuck in my throat. I will not be satisfied until I ask."

Perhaps because Ma Tiande's previous performance had left a relatively favorable impression in Nangong Hongzhao's mind, Nangong Hongzhao suddenly opened his eyes and glanced indifferently at Li Fan.

Then, without saying anything, he closed them again.

Li Fan knew that since he had not explicitly refused, it meant he agreed.

He immediately transmitted his voice again.

"That fake Mystic Coffin suddenly became real. What exactly happened? Brother Nangong, do you know anything about the circumstances behind it?"

Nangong Hongzhao said irritably, "Didn't you see me get swept away like trash for eighteen thousand kilometers? How could I know anything about what happened?"

Li Fan hurriedly consoled him. "Brother Nangong, don't put it that way. That was Elder Nangong of the Administration Council. How many people would love to be swept away like that, yet not even have the qualifications?"

Upon hearing this, Nangong Hongzhao opened his eyes again.

He narrowed them slightly and examined Li Fan once more. Then, as if realizing something, he said, "I almost forgot. You were originally a Dao Net operator, so you could also be considered someone who came from the Administration Council. No wonder you know how formidable my Third Granduncle is."

Li Fan nodded repeatedly. "Indeed, indeed."

Seeing that Nangong Hongzhao's attitude was gradually softening, Li Fan soon got straight to the point.

"These days, I have had nothing to do but think about it. There are several possibilities regarding this fake coffin."

"First, that fake coffin was constructed according to the genuine coffin. Apart from certain necessary conditions, it was no different from the real Mystic Coffin of World Traversal. And the two Xi and Yi Immortals who came all the way from the distant star sea happened to possess those necessary conditions. Thus, they naturally used the Mystic Coffin to escape to another world."

"But there is one point that does not make sense with this theory. It was merely bait used to lure the True Immortals into a trap. As long as it looked convincing, there was no need to make it this realistic. So I have another theory. Perhaps Yan Xuantai secretly tampered with it, or used a substitution trick, replacing the fake coffin with another one that was much closer to the genuine coffin. He then hid inside it, waiting for the two Xi and Yi Immortals to enter. Afterward, he revealed the truth to them and escaped together with them."

"But..." Li Fan paused.

"This Yan Xuantai, who exactly is he? He was sent to secretly monitor and protect the first hunt of newly inducted Immortal Hunters. This was neither a particularly important nor insignificant assignment, so his identity and status should not have been very high. Yet he actually possessed such a supreme treasure?"

"And then there was that black seven-colored nail capable of deceiving the Dao Netw. No matter how I think about it, I cannot figure it out!" Li Fan let out a long sigh.

"Seeing as we may be imprisoned here forever with no hope of ever getting out, could Brother Nangong enlighten me? Don't worry, I absolutely will not tell anyone."

After a long silence, Nangong Hongzhao's faint voice finally sounded.

"Your thoughts are quite meticulous. Some of your deductions coincide with mine. However, I happen to know some inside information that you do not."

"It is not exactly a secret, so there is no harm in telling you. That Yan Xuantai originally joined the Immortal Hunters as a member of the [Sacred Shang]. A hundred years ago, the Sacred Shang suddenly disappeared from the Shuo Star Sea, from top to bottom, leaving behind only some surviving remnants and abandoned pawns."

"I originally thought he had already severed his ties with the Sacred Shang. Yet who would have thought..." Nangong Hongzhao let out a cold snort.

"Speaking of which, the Sacred Shang was one of the founders of the Immortal Hunters back then. They also voluntarily provided many of the techniques for capturing immortals. Furthermore, the members of the Sacred Shang had little interest in fighting or power. Their sole objective was hunting immortals. Thus, during the early days of the Immortal Hunters organization, they held a transcendent status. After the Sacred Shang suddenly left without saying goodbye and disappeared without a trace, Yan Xuantai, as an abandoned member of the Sacred Shang, was not excessively targeted either. They even arranged an important position for him..."

Li Fan's heart stirred slightly when he heard the words "Sacred Shang."

He continued asking, "According to what Brother Nangong said, the Sacred Shang disappeared a hundred years ago. In those hundred years, who knows how much our Dao Net techniques have advanced. Even if Yan Xuantai was a member of the Sacred Shang, how could he possess a method capable of deceiving the Dao Net? Could it be that the Sacred Shang is really that far ahead of us? Or perhaps..."

Li Fan seemed to realize something and suddenly lowered his voice.

"Could it be that Yan Xuantai secretly reestablished contact with the Sacred Shang recently, and only then found a way to escape?"

"But that does not make sense either. Unless the fake coffin was not escaping to another world at all, but to another Star Sea, where the Sacred Shang is located?"

Nangong Hongzhao shook his head slightly.

"That is beyond what I can know. This matter is extremely complicated. Yan Xuantai is naturally solitary and usually acts alone. Investigating him will be somewhat difficult."

"However, you can rest assured. We definitely will not be imprisoned here until we die. Once there is a general conclusion to the matter, we will be released."

Nangong Hongzhao kindly offered his consolation.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1450: The Great Sun Flame Body Refinement

Li Fan was stunned at first when he heard this, but then he was overjoyed.

“If someone else had told me this, I would have assumed they had been locked up in the Black Prison for too long and had become fond of spouting crazy nonsense. But since Brother Nangong is the one saying it…”

“Then I have nothing to fear!”

Faced with Li Fan’s flattery, Nangong Hongzhao did not say anything on the surface. However, the faint smile that inadvertently appeared on his face had already betrayed his inner thoughts.

Taking this conversation as an opportunity, Li Fan and Nangong Hongzhao gradually grew closer.

Li Fan quietly learned some information about the Nangong family. It could not be considered absolutely secret, but it was something that cultivators in the Shuo Star Sea would never have the qualifications to know unless they reached a certain level.

For example, the reason Nangong Fusheng was given the name 'Fusheng - (Restorer)' was because, before Nangong Fusheng, the Nangong family had indeed produced an existence who could be called a 'Sage.' This person was named Nangong 'Fusheng - (Blessed).' One of his achievements had been the initial establishment of the Dao Net and its protection of all living beings. However, Fusheng had not appeared in the mortal world for a long time, leaving only the Fusheng (Restorer) to act in his stead.

Another example was that the Nangong family possessed an supreme treasure. It was said to have been obtained when they once surrounded and hunted the Nameless True Immortal. It was the clothing worn by the Nameless True Immortal.

Setting aside how precious the material of these clothes was, their miraculous effect alone was astonishing. In truth, they had been transformed from the Great Dao that the Nameless True Immortal had fused with. After the Nameless True Immortal was captured, the clothes were forcibly stripped from his body.

If a cultivator was fortunate enough to wear these clothes and could withstand them without dying, it would be as though they had fallen into a sea of the Great Dao, with the very bodies of the Great Dao surrounding them. Their efficiency in comprehending the Great Dao would be even greater than when cultivating within the Dao Net.

The only drawback was that such extraordinary efficiency only manifested along the Dao corresponding to the Nameless True Immortal.

...

Nangong Hongzhao gradually discovered that Ma Tiande, this former Dao Net operator, possessed an understanding and perception of the Dao Net that was even faintly superior to his own. With only a few words, Li Fan would often leave Nangong Hongzhao benefiting immensely.

It had to be known that among these newly appointed Immortal Hunters, most had studied under famous masters. None of them was willing to submit to another. Someone like Ma Tiande, who ranked near the top in the examination yet remained exceptionally humble, was extremely rare.

“This person is worth befriending.”

Nangong Hongzhao thought this to himself.

Time passed day by day amid the two of them exchanging ideas.

As if confirming Nangong Hongzhao’s words, after being imprisoned in the Black Prison for a full ninety days, they were finally released.

“Every word and action of yours in the Black Prison has been recorded.”

“This is the final stage of your induction assessment.”

“The previous rankings will be adjusted slightly.”

“The official appointments of the Immortal Hunters will be based on the new list.”

“Go rest for now. Wait for further notice.”

Upon being released, the newly appointed Immortal Hunters heard these words.

Some were overjoyed and congratulated themselves for having remained restrained throughout. Others had expressions that changed repeatedly, regretting their earlier actions.

Of course, true prodigies such as Nangong Hongzhao, Dao Yuan, and Jin Changyue clearly would not be deceived by such rhetoric.

Jin Changyue let out a cold snort. The three long swords behind her simultaneously released shrill sword cries. It took considerable effort before she managed to suppress the killing intent that had leaked out.

Nangong Hongzhao exchanged a glance with Li Fan, then silently left with him.

The Nangong family's resting place was a separate courtyard opened within the Immortal Hunter organization. The Immortal Hunters organization itself was located within the Dao Net and did not exist in the real world.

This separate courtyard was also transformed from the power of the Dao Net. As the owner's level of authority increased, its area would continuously expand. It could also freely change its appearance according to the owner's thoughts.

Because it was formed from the power of the Dao Net, residing within it allowed one to constantly comprehend the power of the Dao Net. Compared to cultivating in the external Star Sea, the efficiency was countless times greater.

Under Nangong Hongzhao's control, the scenery of the courtyard instantly transformed into a winding karst cave. Following Nangong Hongzhao, Li Fan gradually arrived deep within the cave.

Beneath the enormous cavern was blazing lava.

“Congratulations, Brother. Your ranking has improved once again, putting you in the top three. It seems you should be entrusted with an important position.”

Nangong Hongzhao said this teasingly with a smile.

As he spoke, he suddenly leaped down and plunged into the lava.

Immersed in the extremely high temperature lava, Nangong Hongzhao actually let out a series of groans.

“I never expected Brother Nangong to have such a peculiar hobby.”

Li Fan stared down below without following him.

Unlike the various illusions manifested within the Tianxuan Mirror's dwelling space, everything within this Dao Net courtyard was real. Just like all things in heaven and earth, they were manifestations of the Great Dao.

Cultivators of the Shuo Star Sea focused solely on comprehending the Dao Net, while neglecting their own cultivation.

'Ma Tiande' clearly did not possess the ability to survive in such scorching lava.

“Hehe, you wouldn't understand. This is Great Sun Flame Essence. In theory, only after the great suns of ninety-nine cultivation worlds are collectively destroyed, followed by refining them with Yin power, can a single drop like this be formed.”

“And now, through the unfathomable power of the Dao Net, countless complicated steps in between can actually be omitted, creating something from nothing! Such treasures, which in the past could only be enjoyed by True Immortals, can now be enjoyed to our hearts' content by ordinary mortals like us!”

As Nangong Hongzhao spoke, he raised a drop of Great Sun Flame Essence in his hand.

Li Fan saw that the viscous scarlet liquid was continuously and slowly seeping into Nangong Hongzhao's body through the pores all over his body.

His body gradually turned red, as if it had been ignited.

Yet Nangong Hongzhao acted as though nothing had happened. He even happily took a sip.

Li Fan's eyes immediately narrowed.

Even from a distance, he could tell how terrifying the destructive power of this so-called Great Sun Flame Essence was. The bathwater beneath Nangong Hongzhao alone was probably enough to turn the entire Xuanhuang Realm into scorched earth.

“Even if this were my true body, without using spiritual power to protect myself, plunging into this Flame Essence would probably cause me to turn to ash in an instant. Yet Nangong Hongzhao can actually achieve this relying solely on his physical body…”

Li Fan suddenly recalled the scene when Nangong Hongzhao had been sent flying with a single slap by the enraged Nangong Fusheng.

When Nangong Fusheng descended, the surrounding starry sky seemed to instantly plunge into a violent tsunami, with waves surging wildly.

And that slap had clearly contained no restraint. With such tremendous force, even a True Immortal would probably have suffered severe injuries after taking it.

Yet Nangong Hongzhao had been completely unharmed and had even been able to follow him and return at extreme speed.

“Body Refinement Technique.”

Li Fan's heart was filled with understanding.

Nangong Hongzhao's following words also confirmed Li Fan's guess.

“In the Shuo Star Sea, the power of the Dao Net is supreme.”

“This is such a magnificent and extraordinary creation. It allows mortals to manipulate the laws of the Great Dao just like the immortals of old. However, compared to immortals, we mortals are still far too insignificant. It is like a child holding a sharp blade. They may fail to injure their enemy and instead wound themselves.”

“And the methods of the Immortals have been explicitly prohibited by the Council. Therefore…”

Nangong Hongzhao took a deep breath.

All the pores throughout his body opened simultaneously, like enormous bloody mouths, swallowing the entire pool of Great Sun Flame Essence into his body.

“Refreshing!”

At this moment, Nangong Hongzhao's face had been completely dyed red.

He grunted and slowly exhaled a long breath of scorching air.

There was not the slightest fluctuation of spiritual energy from his body. He merely relied on trembling at an extreme speed to digest the Great Sun Flame Essence.

Nangong Hongzhao’s expression slowly returned to normal. He leaped up and came to Li Fan’s side before continuing, “Dao Yuan, Jin Changyue, and the others, every one of them has a unique Body Refinement method just like I do. Do not be fooled by their seemingly frail appearances!”

“This is also the greatest difference between the famous families of the Shuo Star Sea and people like you, brother, who were born naturally.”

Nangong Hongzhao patted Li Fan on the shoulder and spoke meaningfully.

“Use the body as a nail and incorporate it into the Dao Net. The stronger the body is tempered, the more Dao Net strings it can withstand. Dao Yuan and the others received special cultivation from childhood and have long since been tempered through countless trials. But someone like Brother Ma...”

“Even though you can now draw upon the power of the Dao Net to a certain extent, figuring out by yourself what materials to use and what methods to employ for Body Refinement, while ensuring that you do not injure yourself in the process, how could that possibly compare with our inheritance passed down through countless generations?”

Having spoken this far, how could Li Fan not understand what Nangong Hongzhao meant?

Li Fan bowed deeply, his expression solemn. “I ask Brother Nangong to teach me. Whether I succeed in mastering it or not, I will certainly repay this great favor in the future. Should you ever have need of me, I will not hesitate even if it means certain death!”

Nangong Hongzhao helped Li Fan up. “You and I are exceptionally compatible, brother. There is no need to speak like this. However, I am not merely putting on an act. You should know that every Body Refinement cultivation method nowadays has already been deduced to its limits and tested through the lives of countless people. They are indeed extremely precious.”

Li Fan nodded. “I naturally understand this principle.”

“You spent a long time talking with us in the Black Prison, and I have also come to understand you quite well. You have already passed the best age for Body Refinement. With ordinary Body Refinement methods, even if you put in several times the effort, the results would still be greatly diminished. However, your advantage lies in your understanding of the Dao Net, which far surpasses that of ordinary people. Therefore...”

Nangong Hongzhao once again looked Li Fan up and down before saying softly, “This [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art] could not be more suitable for you!”

Before Nangong Hongzhao could finish speaking, Li Fan raised his hand and stopped him.

Li Fan’s expression became somewhat solemn as he quietly asked, “I suddenly thought of something. This Body Refinement method is so precious. If Brother Nangong privately passes it on to me...”

“Senior Fusheng will not punish you, will he?”

“If so, then I absolutely cannot accept it.”

Seeing how sincere Li Fan looked, completely unlike someone making a polite refusal, Nangong Hongzhao froze on the spot.

But he quickly recovered and waved his hand. “It is fine. Although the cultivation method is precious, its confidentiality level is not particularly high. I am only passing it on to you. Third Granduncle will certainly not punish me.”

“Listen carefully...”

The cavern formed within the Dao Net Courtyard quietly sealed itself off. Inside, Nangong Hongzhao transmitted the complete [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art] to Li Fan.

With Li Fan’s experience, he quickly understood the origins of this cultivation method.

Without a doubt, this technique bore traces of immortal cultivation arts.

It involved visualizing the Great Dao of the world as a net to suppress oneself, using the weight of the Heavenly Dao and Earthly Dao to temper the body.

During the Body Refinement process, besides increasing physical strength, it could also deepen one’s affinity with and comprehension of the Great Dao.

Just as Nangong Hongzhao had said, it was an extraordinarily profound and exceptionally precious Body Refinement art.

However, it also had its shortcomings.

Its difficulty of cultivation was simply too high.

To reach the point of visualizing the Great Dao descending upon the body, one’s comprehension of the Heavenly Dao and Earthly Dao had clearly already reached an extremely high level, perhaps even surpassing that of countless immortals.

With such profound familiarity with the Great Dao, one could simply merge with the Great Dao and ascend into Nameless.

Why would there be any need to slowly temper the physical body? Would that not be taking the long way around?

“Perhaps only the Shuo Star Sea, under the coverage of the Dao Net, could give birth to such a deformed and peculiar cultivation art.”

“First, it uses the tangible Dao Net to replace the intangible Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, lowering the threshold for cultivation by a considerable amount.”

“Second, the Shuo Star Sea is a surviving gathering place of the Immortal Realm and the lower realm Star Seas beneath the Dao Annihilation Calamity. True Immortals, and even Nameless True Immortals, have gathered here. Only under such circumstances could this Great Dao Embodiment Body Art have been deduced...”

Li Fan’s mind surged with countless thoughts, but this did not delay him from beginning to cultivate the Body Refinement art.

Since he was already within the Dao Net, he naturally did not need to go through the complicated process of visualizing the Dao strings descending upon his body.

Suddenly, it was as if layer upon layer of mountains had crashed down upon his body.

Li Fan immediately fell to the ground in a sorry state. In an instant, large amounts of blood sprayed from the pores all over his body.

It nearly splashed all over Nangong Hongzhao, who was watching from the side, but fortunately, he stopped it in time.

The corner of Nangong Hongzhao’s mouth twitched.

Li Fan struggled to get up from the ground and tidied his disheveled appearance. Somewhat weakly, he cupped his hands toward Nangong Hongzhao and said, “I have made a fool of myself before Brother Nangong. I did not expect this Body Refinement method to be so difficult to cultivate.”

Nangong Hongzhao comforted him, “Brother Ma, remember to advance gradually and do not become greedy. It is normal to suffer some hardship.”

“The suffering I endured when I was young was a hundred times worse than what you are experiencing now!”

“Brother Ma, although I have already passed the Great Dao Embodiment Body Art to you, I cannot tell you how to cultivate it in detail, nor can I share the related insights, experiences, or cultivation experience.”

“You must figure everything out on your own.”

Li Fan cupped his fists. “Obtaining such a divine art is already an immense favor. How could I dare hope for anything more?”

“I will not disturb Brother Nangong any further today. I shall return and cultivate.”

After some polite exchanges, Li Fan bid farewell to Nangong Hongzhao and returned to his own residence.

His official position was nominally even higher than Nangong Hongzhao’s. Yet the area of his residence was actually somewhat smaller than his counterpart’s.

Li Fan did not mind. He transformed the residence into a replica of the summit of Jieli Mountain.

Sitting cross-legged on the ground, he officially began cultivating the [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art].

His pathetic display before Nangong Hongzhao had naturally been intentional. He had merely been looking for an excuse to leave.

Given the delicacy of Li Fan’s perception of the Great Dao, the speed at which he cultivated this art would likely far exceed anything Nangong Hongzhao could imagine.

Moreover, Li Fan was not simply following the cultivation method mechanically.

Li Fan now possessed the ability to modify cultivation methods according to his own unique circumstances.

“Great Dao Embodiment Body.”

“And within my heart, a projection of the Great Dao has already begun to emerge.”

“Therefore...”

“There is no need for the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth to descend. I only need to contemplate it within my mind, allowing the Dao strings to transform from the illusory into the tangible.”

“Then I can borrow the might of the Great Dao to temper my physical body.”

Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly. The miniature Dao Net within his divine sense subsequently began to radiate waves of faint light.
